tādṛgrūpo’bhavad bālaḥ sṛṣṭirakṣāpahārakṛt | 
bhrūśuṃḍī taṃ nirīkṣyaiva svayopāsyaṃ gajānanam ||29||
luluṃṭha bhūmau premṇā sa pratipatti parāṅmukhaḥ | 
nanarta paramānando romāṃcita śarīravān ||30||
dehabhāvaṃ prapadyātha praṇanāma yathāvidhi | 
pūjayāmāsa vidhivadupacāraiḥ pṛthakpṛthak ||31||
ūce rājā mahāpuṇyaṃ phalitaṃ pūrvajanmajam | 
devabhaktabhavaṃ saukhyaṃ dṛṣṭamadya mayā’dbhutam ||32||
ubhābhyāṃ praṇipatyātha pupūja ca yathāvidhi | 
mahāsanagatau tau tu parasparamathocatuḥ ||33||
āliṃgya daśadordaṃḍaiḥ paramātmā jaharṣa ca | 
sagadgadau nirīkṣyaitau sāśru rājā tato’dhyabhūt ||34||
gajānana uvāca | 
tava niṣṭhā mayā’jñāyi rājñā cāpi niveditā | 
ata eṣa dhṛto veṣo daśadordaṃḍamaṃḍitaḥ ||35||
yathā yathā jano’nyo’pi dhyātvā mām bhajate mune | 
tathā tathā’haṃ rūpāṇi karomi ca dadāmi ca ||36||
vāṃchitaṃ tasya lokasya viśvāsena bhajatyasau | 
duṣṭabhārabhayākrāntā dharitrī satyalokagā ||37||
brahmāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ yātā tenāhaṃ pratibodhitaḥ | 
ādityāśca vareṇāhaṃ jātaḥ kaśyapanandanaḥ ||38||
bhuvo bhāraṃ hariṣyāmi śakrādīṃśca nije pade | 
sthāpayiṣyāmi daityānāṃ nāśaṃ kṛtvā’pyanekadhā ||39||
tavotkaṭamanuṣṭhānaṃ dṛṣṭvā’hamīdṛśo’bhavam | 
devāntakaṃ saha bhrātrā hatvā yāmi nijaṃ padam ||40||
ṛṣiruvāca | 
dṛṣṭvā tavāṅghriyugalaṃ sarvalokasukhāvaham | 
sarvakleśaharaṃ deva vandyaṃ kāṃkṣitadaṃ prabho ||41||
kṛtakṛtyaḥ pavitraśca jāto’haṃ tava darśanāt | 
varaṃ me dehi viśvātman tena tṛpto bhavāmyaham ||42||
yadā yadā’haṃ dhyāyāmi rūpeṇānena vighnapa | 
tadā tadā me pratyakṣo bhavethāḥ karuṇānidhe ||43||
āśāpūraka ityevaṃ nāma te khyātimetu ca | 
gajānana uvāca | 
yadā yadā vāṃchase tvam tadā yāsyāmi te’ntikam ||44||
bhaktibhāvādidaṃ nāma tvayoktaṃ khyātimeṣyati | 
ka uvāca | evaṃ varau samākarṇya praṇanāma mudā muniḥ ||45||
praṇate tu munau tasmin punaḥ proce gajānanaḥ | 
nidhāya mastake hastam śanaistasya dvijasya saḥ ||46||
yadyatte vāṃchitaṃ brahmaṃstatsarvaṃ siddhimeṣyati | 
avismṛtiśca bhavitā mama nityaṃ mune tava ||47||
ka uvāca | 
evamuktā bālarūpaścikrīḍa sa vināyakaḥ | 
so’pi padmāsanagato dhyānamevānvapadyata ||48||
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ cakruḥ sarvalokāstadādbhutam | 
nirīkṣya bālarūpasya caritaṃ paramātmanaḥ ||49||
kāśirājo’vadattatra dhanyo’haṃ jagatītale | 
darśanaṃ prāptavānasya brahmādīnāṃ sudurlabham ||50||
taṃ ca bālaṃ kare dhṛtvā nināyāntargṛhaṃ mudā | 
bhojayāmāsa svādvannaṃ śāyayāmāsa pūrvavat | 
ājñāṃ gṛhītvā suṣvāpa svayaṃ ca pūrvavanmune ||51||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite saptadaśo’dhyāyaḥ ||17||

<adhyāya 18 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca | 
apare’hani saṃprāpte kā kathā samapadyata | 
tāṃ me kathaya lokeśa śṛṇvastṛpyāmi na kvacit ||1||
ka uvāca | 
śṛṇuṣvāvahito brahman kathayāmi samāsataḥ | 
vināyakakṛtāṃ divyāṃ kathāṃ pāpāpanodinīm ||2||
udite tu divānāthe nityakarma samāpya tau| 
krīḍārthamagamadbālo rājā bhadrāsanaṃ tayā ||3||
daityo’tha brāhmaṇo bhūtvā jyotiḥśāstraviśāradaḥ | 
tālīyaṃ pustakaṃ vāme mālāṃ dakṣe kare dadhat ||4||
pāṭalaṃ paridhānaṃ ca śirovasraṃ mahattaram | 
gopīcandana sambhūtāṃstilakān dvādaśāpi ca ||5||
atidīrghatamaśśmaśrurājagāma nṛpāntikam | 
samīpamāgataṃ yāvattāvadrājā nanāma tam ||6||
utthāya taṃ samīpe sa svāsane saṃnyaveśayat | 
prapaccha kuśalaṃ tasmai kāryaṃ cāgamanaṃ kutaḥ ||7||
kiṃ ca te nāma viprendra kiṃ jñānama kiṃ ca te tapaḥ | 
vada satyaṃ mune kṛtvā kṛpāṃ mayi kṛpānidhe ||8||
ka uvāca | 
evaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ sa tenāśu dattvāśīrvacanaṃ purā | 
anukrameṇa sakalaṃ nijagāda mahāmune ||9||
hemajjyotirvidityevaṃ nāma me nṛpanandana | 
gandharvalokādāyāto vastaṃ tava samāśraye ||10||
bhūtaṃ bhavadbhaviṣyaṃ ca śāntikāni ca vedmyaham | 
praśnāṃśca kathayiṣyāmi yānyāṃstvaṃ pṛcchase’nagha ||12||
ariṣṭānyatra jātāni jñātāni tāni me nṛpa | 
bhāvi te duṣṭacihnaṃ ca vijñāyāhaṃ samāgataḥ ||12||
tvadāśrayeṇa vastuṃ me vāṃcchā’sti nṛpasattama | 
nṛpa uvāca | 
kiṃnimittamariṣṭāni jāyante’tra mahāmune ||13||
agre’pi kāni bhāvīni satyameva vadasva me| 
tava pratītau jātāyāṃ sthāpayiṣye nije gṛhe ||14||
vṛttiṃ ca kalpayiṣyāmi yogakṣemakarīṃ tava | 
gaṇaka uvāca | 
yāvadvināyakaste’sti gṛhe kaśyapanandanaḥ ||15||
tāvadvighnāni jāyante yato vighnādhipastu saḥ | 
asaṃcāre vanaṃ cainaṃ tyaktvā yāhi nṛpātmaja ||16||
na bhaviṣyati vighnaṃ te nagare vā gṛhe’pi ca | 
asmin sthite jalaṃ sarvaṃ nagaraṃ plāvayiṣyati ||17||
tasminkathaṃcinnaṣṭe tu parvatā vāyunoditāḥ| 
cūrṇayiṣyanti nagaraṃ nṛpa nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ||18||
etatkathaṃ na jānīṣe pūrvaṃ nāsannupadravāḥ | 
rājñā viśvasitavyaṃ no kapaṭe lubdhake’śucau ||19||
atiśūre svokaṭe ca svātmapātaṃ prapaśyatā | 
ete ghnanti ca rājānaṃ rājyakāmā mahāśayāḥ ||20||
etat kathayituṃ yāto jñātvā rājyacyutiṃ tava | 
hitāhitamavaśyaṃ hi vaktavyaṃ jānatā nṛpe ||21||
vivicya nṛpa me vākyaṃ yathecchasi tathā kuru | 
ka uvāca | 
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ rājā taṃ paryabhāṣata ||22||
rājovāca | 
atītānāgatajñānāduktaṃ sarvaṃ tvayā mune | 
mithyaiva bhāsate mahyaṃ tatvajñāne prapaṃcavat ||23||
tvaduktapratyaye jāte dāsyāmi vetanaṃ tava | 
na jñāto bālakaścāyaṃ tvayā gaṇakasattam ||24||
brahmāṇaṃ kamalākāntamaparaṃ śūlinaṃ haram | 
janayiṣyati vāṃchā ced brahmāṃḍāni bahūni saḥ ||25||
yadi te niścayo vākye svakīye brāhmaṇottama | 
nayainaṃ gahanaṃ ghoraṃ tyaktvainaṃ punarehi ca ||26||
lokadveṣakṛto’nena nihatā balavattarāḥ| 
svadeṣṭāraṃ kathamayaṃ na jāne sthāpayiṣyati ||27||
aniṣṭamasya manasi na vayaṃ cintituṃ kṣamāḥ | 
anena nagarī rājyaṃ trātamutpātato bahuḥ ||28||
karotīndramanindraṃ vā’śaktaṃ śaktaṃ laghum gurum | 
uccaṃ nīcaṃ tathā nīcamuccamīśamanīśvaram ||29||
saṃśrutya vāṇīṃ nṛpatergaṇako’bhūdruṣāruṇaḥ | 
rājānaṃ punarārebhe vaktuṃ kiṃcidavāḍ||mukha ||30||
mayā tava hitaṃ proktamaniṣṭaṃ tava bhāsate | 
na jātu laṃghayetko’pi bhāvisṛṣṭuṃ tathetarat ||31||
nṛpate darśayainaṃ me bālakaṃ lakṣaṇāni te | 
kathayiṣye tato bālān sarvānākārayannṛpaḥ ||32||
agre vināyakaḥ prāpto dhāvamānāstato’khilāḥ | 
gaṇakaṃ sa namaskṛtya papraccha kuta āgataḥ ||33||
sāmudralakṣaṇajño’si jyotiḥśāstraviśāradaḥ | 
bhūtabhāvibhaviṣyajña vada me bhāgyajaṃ phalam ||34||
ka uvāca| 
sadhairyaṃ bālavākyaṃ tadākarṇya kapaṭī dvijaḥ | 
tarkayāmāsa manasi kathaṃ svasti gatirbhavet ||35||
anena nihatā duṣṭā balino’nekarūpiṇaḥ | 
śiśorhastaṃ gṛhītvā sa vadati sma śubhāśubham ||36||
caturṣu divaseṣu tvaṃ kūpamadhye patiṣyasi | 
cennirgataḥ kathaṃccitvaṃ sindhau majjanameṣyasi ||37||
tato’pi jīvitaścettu dhvāntamagno bhaviṣyasi | 
tato’pijīvite śailastvayi ko’pi patiṣyati ||38||
mahāntau kālapuruṣau jīvantaṃ bhakṣayiṣyataḥ | 
etānyariṣṭāni tava bhaviṣyanti na saṃśayaḥ ||39||
pratīkāraṃ bravīmyatra nirbhayārthaṃ kuruṣva tam | 
tato’nyatra mayāsārthaṃ yāhi dinacatuṣṭayam ||40||
punaratrānayiṣyāmi śapatho me tacāṅghrijaḥ | 
niśamya vācaṃ bālo’sau jagāmāntagṛhaṃ śanaiḥ ||41||
rājño hastāt pragṛhayāśu mudrikāṃ ratnanirmitām | 
kṛtvā tāṃ svakare bālo vadati sma dvijaṃ prati ||42||
gaṇakāśu vada tvaṃ no gataṃ vastu mahattaram | 
kena nītaṃ tadā prāptaṃ bhūyātte pratyayo bhavet ||43||
ityuktaḥ sa tu bālena vicārya janasaṃsadi | 
babhāṇa hāsyavadano mudrikā yadi labhyate ||44||
mamaiva yadi deyā sā tadedānīṃ bravīmyaham | 
tatheti bālakenokte tavaiva karageti sā ||45||
itthamukto jihmaveṣadhāriṇā vāḍavena sā| 
tayā mantritayā bālo nyavadhīttaṃ hradi kṣaṇāt ||46||
sa tayā bhinnahṛdayo vajrāhata ivācalaḥ | 
nipapāta dharāpṛṣṭhe cālayan vasudhāṃ bhṛśam ||47||
patatā tasya dehena cūrṇitaṃ nagaraṃ kiyat | 
kāśirājo janāḥ sarve āścaryaṃ menire tadā ||48||
jaharṣurdevatā sarvāḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ sucakrire | 
svargabhūvādyaghoṣairdyaurvyāptā’bhūd bhūtalaṃ tadā ||49||
janā ūcuḥ | 
kathaṃ jñāto’nena duṣṭo brāhmaṇo brahmaveṣabhṛt | 
rājñā’pi na kṛtaṃ daivād vākyaṃ tasya durātmanaḥ ||50||
ayaṃ bālo na mantavyo bhūbhāraharaṇe’maraḥ| 
avatīrṇaḥ kaśyapasya gṛhe ’sau karūṇānidhiḥ ||51||
mudrikāghātamātreṇa kathaṃ prāṇān jahau bali| 
ityuktvā pupūjuḥ sarve nemustaṃ tuṣṭuvurjaguḥ ||52||
dadau dānāni viprebhyo rājā rājīvalocanaḥ | 
dvijacāraṇa bandibhyo dīnebhyaścāpyanekaśaḥ ||53||
nāgarān mānayitvā tān visasarja tataḥ sabhām | 
bālo’pi bālakaṃ rantuṃ yayau pūrvavadajñavat ||54||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite kapaṭi daityavadho nāmāṣṭādaśodhyāyaḥ ||18||

<adhyāya 19 prāraṃbhaḥ>
 
ka uvāca | 
bālakena hataṃ śrutvā vrāhmaṇaṃ veṣadhāriṇam | 
narāntako mahādaityo kūpakaṃ kandarāsuram ||1||
preṣayāmāsa balinaṃ brahmalabdhavaraṃ tadā | 
datvā bahūni vastrāṇi ratnāni vividhāni ca ||2||
daitya uvāca | 
gaccha taṃ sumuhūrtena hantuṃ bālaṃ tamāśuca | 
nānopāyairmāritānāṃ kuru tanniṣkṛtiṃ balāt ||3||
ka uvāca | 
evaṃ tena samājñaptāvasurau kūpakandarau | 
senayā ca suraṃgiṇyā gavyūtiṃ sampratasthatuḥ ||4||
nivṛtya senāṃ ca tata īyaturbhṛśa harṣitau | 
vicāraṃ cakraturmārge kūpo’haṃ kūpatāmiyām ||5||
bālo bhavaṃ krīḍamānaṃ mayi taṃ kṣipa yatnataḥ | 
maṃḍūkarūpaḥ kūpastho bhakṣayiṣyāmi tatkṣaṇāt ||6||
antardhānaṃ kariṣyāvaḥ kṛtvā kāryaṃ mahattaram | 
evaṃ tau niścitamatī kāśirājapuram mahat ||7||
prāpaṃturabhavat kopo mahānnṛpa nṛpājire | 
kandaro bālarūpo’bhūdrantuṃ teṣu mano dadhe ||8||
rājā’tha dadṛśe kūpaṃ sujalam taṃ jaharṣa ca | 
yāvat paśyati madhye’sau maṃḍūkaṃ taṃ dadarśa ha ||9||
nadantaṃ maṃjulām vāṇīṃ pītavarṇaṃ bhayānakam | 
kandaro bālamadhyastho jagāda taṃ vināyakam ||1 0||
bahiryāhi mahābāho paśya kūpaṃ manoharam | 
dadarśa kūpaṃ bālaiḥ sa bahirāgatya tatkṣaṇāt ||1 1||
atyantaṃ sundaraṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ kūpaṃ bālakaiḥ saha | 
cikrīḍe vividhairbhāvairnānā nilayanairapi ||1 2||
madhyāhne jalamadhyasthāḥ siṣiṃcuste parasparam | 
majjanonmajjanaṃ cakru kārayantaḥ parasparam ||1 3||
uḍḍānam jalamadhye te dūrādāgatya cakrire | 
evaṃ ramitvā te tatra gṛhān gantum samudyatāḥ ||14||
tāvadvināyako’paśyajjalamadhye nijaṃ vapuḥ | 
jānaṃstartuṃ jale sarvān paśyannāsīdbahiḥ purā ||15||
tāvannunoda tam duṣṭo jalamadhye sa kandaraḥ | 
agādhaṃ tajjalaṃ jñātvā loḍayāmāsa līlayā ||16||
svecchayā ramaṇo lakṣmyā kṣīrābdhimiva vāḍavaḥ |
adho yāti muhūrtam sa upariṣṭāttathaiva ca ||17||
māyāmāsthāya sa gaṇo majjituṃ samajāyata | 
caraṇau hastakamale cikṣepa bahudhā śiśuḥ ||1 8| | 
lulupuḥ sarvaśo bālāstāvattena dhṛto balāt | 
caraṇe kandaro nītastatkūpa talamādarāt ||1 9||
amajjayadbalāt taṃ ca muṃca mumaceti so’bravīt | 
vināyako’pi tam proce mucamuṃceti māmapi ||2 0||
ubhau samabalau tau tu majjanonmajjane ratau | 
hiraṇyakaśipurlakṣmī nṛsiṃhāviva durdharau ||2 1 ||
adhasthādbahukālaṃ tau gatau jñātvā’khilārbhakāḥ | 
cakrurārtasvaraṃ duṣṭaṃ mṛto bāla iti sphuṭam ||22||
apaśabdaṃ tathā śrutvā yayuḥ strīvṛddhabālakāḥ | 
kāśirājo’pi cukrośa kiṃ kāryamiti cābravīt | |2 3 ||
agādhanīre kūpe’smin kathaṃ jīvetsa bālakaḥ | 
ko’pyūce cāparaḥ prāha śīghraṃ niḥsārito yadi ||24||
upāyo bhavitā kaścijjīvamāyāsya niścitam | 
yena yatprārthyate dāsye tadasmai jīvitaṃ svakam ||25||
niṣkāśyatāṃ bahirbālo vegena ca balena ca | 
iti rājavacaḥ śrutvā kūpamadhye gatāśrayaḥ ||26||
magnāste daityakṛtayā māyayā nāparo janaḥ | 
avartaṃtu manaścakre tato rājā śuśoca tam ||27||
rājovāca | 
kiṃ mayā’kāri duritaṃ yena me duḥkhamīdṛśam | 
kathaṃ bālo mayā’’nītaḥ sukhārthaṃ duḥkhadobhavat ||28| | 
darśayiṣye kathaṃ tasya pitṛbhyāṃ mukhameva ca | 
janāya vā kathamidaṃ kathamasya mṛtirbhavet ||2 9||
nirastānyat ariṣṭāni mahānti bālakena ha | 
kathamasma vaśaṃ yāto na jāne devaceṣṭitam ||3 0||
evaṃ sarveṣu śocatsu strīvṛddhabālakeṣu ca | 
maṃḍūkarūpī kūpastho vyādāya mukhamāsthitaḥ ||3 1||
tāvadevāgato bālo noditaḥ kandareṇa saḥ | 
tenāpi nodito daityo daityāsye nyapatatkhalaḥ ||32||
bhakṣayāmāsa kūpastaṃ matsyo matsyabhivālaghuḥ | 
gilito bāla iti sa nijarūpaṃ samāsthitaḥ ||33||
kūpo’pi gaganaspardhāṃ cakre ślāghyāṃ yaśīrjitām | 
sarveṣāmeva daityānāmānṛṇyaṃ gatavānaham ||34||
ityujjaharṣa manasā matvā devamavikramam | 
kandarastu ruṣāviṣṭo vidāryāsya mahodaram ||35||
nirgamya vegamāsthāya sa bālo’ntarhitaḥ kṣaṇāt | 
vidāritaṃ me jaṭharamiti kūpo’pi kandaram ||36||
adaśat kaṃṭhadeśe sa jīvagrāhaṃ ruṣā tadā | 
evaṃ paraspara vadhāt patitau vihvalo bhṛśam ||37||
pādahastāvamardena cūrṇitaṃ nagaram kiyat | 
yathā sundopasundau tu paraspara vadhān mṛtau ||38||
parasparavadhāttadvan mṛtau tau kūpakandarau | 
rājabhṛtyairapākṛṣya tyaktau tau nagarādbahiḥ ||39||
tatkṣaṇādajire kūpo pidadhe sa vināyakaḥ | 
dadṛśe pūrvavat krīḍan sarvabālayuto janaiḥ ||40||
āścaryaṃ menire tatra bālā rājā janā api | 
ūcuḥ parasparaṃ kecit kāpaṭyaṃ yena yujyate ||41||
āmūlān naśyate so’tha svayameva na saṃśayaḥ | 
pataṃgo dīpanāśārthamāyāti naśyate yathā ||42||
asya cālayituṃ śakto na syātkālo’pi roma ca | 
dṛṣṭaṃ bahuvidhaṃ cāsya sāmarthyamavatāriṇaḥ ||43||
jetumīśaṃ gataḥ kāmaḥ syayaṃ bhasmatvamāgataḥ | 
kāśirājastu tacchrutvā satyaṃsatyamiti bruvan ||44||
dadau dānāni viprebhyo visasarja prapūjya tān | 
vināyakaṃ namaskṛtya gatā lokā nijān gṛhān ||45||
kecidāliṃgya taṃ bālaṃ yayūrājñā prapūjitam | 
devāśca puṣpavarṣāṇi mumucustuṣṭuvurjanāḥ ||46||
ka uvāca | 
evaṃ na jñāyate brahman kāryākāryaṃ marudbalam |
māyārasvarūpaṃ puṃbhāgyaṃ rākṣasācaraṇaṃ mune||47||
avatāraguṇā vāpi na jñeyā paramātmanaḥ ||48||
iti gaṇeśa purāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe kūpakandaravadhonāmaikonaviṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||19||

<adhyāya 20 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca | 
kāśirājagṛhe brahman vivāhaḥ sa kadā’bhavat | 
tanmamācakṣva bhagavan vistarāccaturānana ||1||
ka uvāca | 
ekaikaṃ naśyate’riṣṭamaparaṃ yāti vai punaḥ | 
asminnaṣṭe kariṣyāmītyevaṃ yāvadvicāryate ||2||
kūpe ca kandare naṣṭe trayo’pyanye udīritāḥ | 
andhako’mbhāsurastuṃgo rākṣasāḥ krūradarśanāḥ ||3||
jighāṃsayā gatā bālaṃ tato yuddhamavartata | 
śrutvā yeṣāṃ tu saṃgrāmam brahmādyāḥ prāpalan bhayāt ||4||
diggajā marditā yaistu vibudhānāṃ tu kā kathā| 
kadāyaṃ kaśyapasuto bhavitā dṛṣṭigocaraḥ ||5||
nāśayāmo’nekadhā tamiti vyavasitā bhṛśam | 
yāvantaḥ preṣitā vīrāstāvanto nidhanaṃ gatāḥ ||6||
vayamenaṃ haniṣyāmo’jitvā yāmo na vai gṛhān | 
vahnirūpaṃ samāsthāya dāhayāmo muneḥ sutam ||7||
andhakastvabravīttatra tamasā vyāpya khaṃ diśaḥ | 
andhakāraṃ kariṣyāmi dharāyāmatha sarvataḥ ||8||
tataḥ parasparadṛśirna syātteṣāṃ kadācana | 
ambhāsuro’bravīttatra mahīṃ sarvāṃ jalena ha ||9||
āpuraṃ plāvayiṣyāmi samantānna gatirbhavet | 
tuṃgo’bravīdahaṃ tuṃgo girirbhūtvā purīṃ tu tām ||10||
āloḍya cūrṇayiṣyāmi sapakṣa iva parvataḥ | 
andhakāre jale vahnau parvate sarvataḥ sthite ||11||
ko’pi gantuṃ na śaknoti kathaṃ bālo gamiṣyati | 
evaṃ niścitasaṃkalpā jagarjuste trayo bhṛśam ||12||
teṣāṃ garjitaśabdena kampitaṃ bhuvanatrayam | 
tatyajurnijavelāntaṃ sāgarāḥ kṣubhitodakāḥ ||13||
tato raviṃ samācchādya tasthau tatrāndhakāsuraḥ | 
andhakāre mahāghore na prājñāyata kiṃcana ||14||
akasmādrajanī prāptā vyavasāyaparairjanaiḥ | 
snānāsaktairjapāsakterhomāsaktairnṛbhistadā ||15||
tapaḥsu vedaghoṣeṣu vivāhopanayādiṣu | 
kīrtaneṣu purāṇeṣu dvijadevārcanādiṣu ||16||
saktairnānāvidhairlokerniśā prāptā vratādiṣu | 
jajalpuḥ kimu vindhyo’yaṃ ruṇaddhi sūryamaṃḍalam ||17||
athavā pralayo bhāvī voparāgaḥ samāgataḥ | 
sabhāyāmūcurāsīnaṃ rājānamiti paṃḍitāḥ ||18||
tarkayanti yāvadeva gāvo’guḥ svagṛhāntaram | 
asaṃbhāvitavelāsu cakrurdīpān gṛhe gṛhe ||19||
kathamastamitirjātā na pāko na ca bhojanam | 
vyasmayanta tadā nāryo duduhurgāśca kāścana ||20||
dīpikābhiḥ kāṣṭhadīpairvyavahāraṃ janāstadā | 
cakrurduḥkhena kāryāṇi vāṇijo brāhmaṇādayaḥ ||21||
sevakānāṃ mahānandaḥ kāmināṃ cābhavattadā| 
sālasānāṃ prasuptānāṃ nidrālūnāṃ tathaiva ca ||22||
evaṃ kṛte cāndhakena nāmnā cāmbhāsurastadā | 
megharūpeṇa vavṛṣe dhārā hastikaropamāḥ ||23||
śampāprakāśato lokāḥ kṣaṇamīkṣāṃ pracakrire | 
jaladhārā bhayātsarve gṛhamadhye sthirāstadā ||24||
prāsādā nyapatankecid gṛhāṇāṃ bhittikā api | 
antaḥ kecidbahiḥ kecinmṛtā janacayāstadā ||25||
jhaṃjhāvāta hatā vakṣā patitā dharaṇītale | 
saudāminībhirnirdagdhā drumā gṛhacayā bahū ||26||
uttīrābhirnadībhiḥ sā samudrairiva saṃplutā | 
nagarī sarvalokāḍhyā sarvasatvasamākulā ||27||
ka uvāca | 
pralayaṃ daityamāyāṃ ca jñātvā sa karuṇākaraḥ | 
vināyako’karonmāyā balena vaṭamuccakaiḥ | ||28||
nirmame tatkṣaṇādeka latāgulmaiḥ suśobhitam | 
śatayojana vistīrṇaṃ jaṭāśākhā samanvitam ||29||
pakṣirūpeṇa tatrāsau sthito gaganalehinī | 
pakṣau bhūmau pratiṣṭhāpya spṛśaṃ ca śirasā namaḥ ||30||
prakāśayāmāsa purīṃ pītvā caṃcvā jalaṃ mahat | 
kareṇāśoṣayadvanya karīva palvalodakam ||31||
janā hardhāddhi jagadurgato vighno gataṃ jalam | 
gate’ndhakāre dadṛśurvaṭaṃ sāndrataraṃ ca tam ||32||
pakṣiṇaṃ cādbhutākāraṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ na ca vai śrutam | 
sarve janā yayustatra nyagrodhe sthātumutsukāḥ ||33||
aśvairgajairathairuṣṭraiḥ śibikābhirnṛbhistu taiḥ | 
strībhirdāsaiḥ kāśirājo vaṭasyādhaḥ samāyayau ||34||
paśavaḥ sārameyāśca biḍālā vana gocarāḥ| 
vināyakaprabhāveṇa na vṛṣṭistatra no tamaḥ ||35||
cakruḥ sarve pūrvavatte varṇāḥ karma yathāvidhi | 
paurāḥ sadvyavasāyeṣu samasajjanta pūrvavat ||36||
sarveṣāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ kartuṃ pakṣirūpamadhārayat | 
jagadīśo gaṇeśo nu na jānīmo’tra kiṃcana ||37||
vistārya pakṣān vṛṣṭiṃ yo nyaṣedhīddaitya kāritām | 
vṛṣṭiṃ solkāṃ sakarakāṃ svayaṃ sehe sukhena saḥ ||38||
iti lokāstarkayitvā sthitā nirvyākulāstadā | 
ekādaśagatā ghasrā evaṃ tatra sthitasya ca ||39||
nājñāsīt ko’pi caritaṃ vināyaka kṛtaṃ dvija | 
dṛṣṭvā vināyakaṃ bālaṃ krīḍantaṃ bālayūthagam ||40||
tato daityo kṣīṇaśaktī niśceṣṭau samapadyatām | 
tuṃgo jagarja bahudhā nādayan pradiśo diśaḥ ||41||
girirūpaṃ samāsthāya haniṣye pakṣiṇaṃ kṣaṇāt | 
bruvannevaṃ babhūvāśu parvato bhūttadaiva saḥ ||42||
saronadī samāyuktaḥ paṃcayojana vistṛtaḥ | 
divyoṣadhībhirdīptābhirbhāsayan gaganaṃ diśaḥ ||43||
nānāpakṣisamāyukto vāḍavāśramamaṃḍitaḥ | 
sapakṣaṃ parvataṃ dṛṣṭvā patamānaṃ sa pakṣirāṭ ||44||
uḍḍīya paribabhrāma vārayāmāsa tajjalam | 
ghūrṇayan pakṣavātena jaṃgamājaṃgamam jagat ||45||
prasthānipatitā bhūmau parvatānāmitastataḥ | 
caṃcvā dadhre sa tuṃgādriṃ kāśyapiḥ phaṇinaṃ yathā ||46||
saha tenābhraman khe’sau lakṣayan dānavāvubhau | 
andhakaṃ caraṇaikena pareṇāmbhāsuraṃ ca saḥ ||47||
atikramya bhuvarlokamiyāya sa mahākhagaḥ | 
atibhramaṇakhinnāste taptāśca sūryaraśmibhiḥ ||48||
nipeturvyasavo bhūmau trayaḥ parvatasannibhāḥ| 
patantaścūrṇayāmāsurvanānyupavanāni ca ||49||
draṣṭuṃ daityaśarīrāṇi strībālasahitā janāḥ | 
yayuste nagarābhyāśe vīkṣyāścaryaṃ paraṃ yayuḥ ||50||
khaṃḍāni taccharīrāṇāṃ gaṃḍaśailā ivābabhuḥ | 
nirasya māyāṃ daityānāṃ  tato lokā visiṣmire ||51||
vaṭaṃ tam devarūpeṇa dadṛśurna ca vai punaḥ | 
antarhite vaṭe pakṣirūpaṃ tatyāja so’rbhakaḥ ||52||
tato vināyakaṃ bālaṃ kāśirājo liliṃga ha | 
pṛṣṭvā kuśalasaṃpraśnaṃ nāgarāśca nṛpāśca tam ||53||
pupūjaḥ paramaprītyā vighnanāśaṃ vināyakam | 
praśaśaṃsurmudā devam kaśyapasya sutaṃ janā ||54||
na jānīmo vayaṃ devaṃ māyāṃ daityakṛtāṃ parām | 
sāmarthyaṃ te guṇāṃścāpi yatra vedā vikuṃṭhitā ||55||
līlayā rakṣitaḥ sarve mocitā bahusaṃkaṭāt | 
vātāgatā vināśaṃ ca utpātāścaiva vṛṣṭayaḥ ||56||
bhāti bimbaṃ raveḥ samyagandhakāre gate layam | 
manāṃsi sarvalokāṃnā prasannāni mudā tadā ||57||
nadyaḥ prasannasalilāḥ sarvamāsīdyathā purā | 
vavarṣuḥ puṣpavarṣāṇi devadeve vināyake ||58||
nānāvādyaninādaiste’laṃkṛtaṃ puramāviśan | 
dadurdānāni viprebhyo devo naḥ prīyatāmitī ||59||
śāntihomaṃ vidhāyāsau nṛpo’dād godhanaṃ bahu | 
visṛjya sarvān bubhuje vināyakayuto nṛpaḥ ||60||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite daityatrayavadhonāma viṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||20||

<adhyāya 21 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
śṛṇu dvija mahāścaryaṃ kathyamānaṃ mayā tava | 
vināyakasya caritaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ nṛṇām ||1||
ambhāsurasya yo mūrdhā sa gato nijamandiram | 
bhramarāyā niraikṣiṣṭa sakhī sā tāṃ nyabodhayat ||2||
prasuptāṃ kāṃcane tapte sotthāyāṃgaṇamāyayau | 
pautraṃ śiro nirīkṣyaiva mūrchitā patitā bhuvi ||3||
nighnatī nijavakṣaḥ sā karābhyāṃ śokanirbharā | 
nipatat bhūṣaṇā khinnā nāgāmṛṣṭeva padminī ||4||
vibhinnavalayā chinna kūrpāsā tanumadhyamā | 
dṛśyādṛśya mahadgātrā luṃṭhatī bhuvi vihvalā ||5||
niṣidhyamānā sakhibhirbhrātṛbhiḥ svajanairapi| 
trimuhūrteḥ prabuddhā sā kiṃcid vaktuṃ pracakrame ||6||
utthāya ca karābhyāṃ tu nijaghāna punaḥ śiraḥ | 
bhramaryuvāca | 
yeneyaṃ pṛthivī sarvā trāsitā sāmarāvatī ||7||
mūrdhnā sahasraṃ śeṣasya bhrūkaṭākṣena kampitam | 
yena rājye’bhiṣiktau tau devāntaka narāntakau ||8||
yasya kṣveḍitamātreṇa rodasī kampitā bhṛśam| 
sa kathaṃ patitaḥ kutra nihataḥ kena vā sutaḥ ||9||
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā kampitaḥ kālaḥ sa kathaṃ nidhanaṃ gataḥ| 
evamākrandatī dīnā vivatseva payasvinī ||10||
evaṃ vilapatīṃ tāṃ tu nyaṣedhayat sakhījanaḥ | 
na dṛṣṭvā sakhi kenāpi mṛtena saha vai mṛtiḥ ||11||
atiśokena śoko’gnyaḥ prāpyate mūḍhajantubhiḥ | 
yadi snehena putra tvam śocase’sya hitaṃ kuru ||12||
cārānpreṣaya vṛttāntagrahaṇāyāsya sarvataḥ | 
dahyatāṃ mastako mantrairevamasya hitaṃ kurū ||13||
suhradāmaśru patitaṃ pretasya tanmukhe patet | 
dahatyenaṃ na natattyājyamityāhuḥ paramarṣayaḥ ||14||
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ sovāca tāṃ sakhīṃ ruṣā | 
ayaṃ mūrdhnā mahābhāge sthāpyatāṃ yatnataḥ sakhi ||15||
ānayiṣye’diteḥ putramastakaṃ saha tena ca | 
dāhayiṣyāmyamuṃ tasmāt sthāpyatāṃ tailamadhyataḥ ||16||
sā rākṣasī rākṣasavaryayuktā samāyayau rājapurīṃ nihantum | 
vināyakaṃ taṃ nijarūpagopinī pataṃgapatrīva dhanaṃjayāntikam ||17||
āgatā’ditirūpeṇa bhāsayantī diśo daśa | 
ati lāvaṇyanilayā sarvālaṃkāra śobhinī ||18||
dāḍimī bījadaśanā bimboṣṭhī tanumadhyamā | 
vilasat padmanayanā muktādāmalasat kucā ||19||
vīrā mumuhire dṛṣṭvā tādṛśīṃ nṛpasadmagām | 
manasā kāmayanti sma yadāliṃgana cumbane ||20||
tarkayāmāsurapare rambhā kiṃ nu tilottamā | 
menakā vā ghṛtācī vā nāgī yakṣī śivā nu kim ||21||
urvaśī vā ratī rājñī kaśyapasyāditirnu kim | 
jñātvā tu rājapatnīṃ tām praṇanāmāditiṃ mudā ||22||
pūjayāmāsa saubhāgyadravyairbhūṣāṃ śukādibhiḥ | 
uvāca ca sucārvaṃgī premagadgadayā girā ||23||
mahābhāgyena dṛṣṭāsi tvaṃ devajananī mayā | 
vināyakaprasādena kvānyathā darśanaṃ tava ||24||
rājñīmevaṃ bruvantīṃ tu sāśrukaṃṭhā’vadadvacaḥ | 
aditiruvāca | 
mama bālo bahudinaṃ sthāpitaḥ kutra vartate ||25||
atyutkaṃṭhatayā subhru darśanārthamihāgatā | 
strīṇāṃ svabhāvaṃ jānāsi māyā vyākula cetasām ||26||
śokasaṃtaptagātrā’haṃ tamāliṃgāmi cārbhakam | 
tatsaṃsargasuśītāṃgī prāpsye saṃtoṣamuttamam ||27||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sādarā sā sasambhramā| 
gaveṣituṃ tu taṃ bālaṃ rājñī dūtānacodayat ||28||
anye cakhyuḥ kāśirājaṃ kaśyapastriyamāgatām| 
sa snātvā gṛhamāgatya tāṃ dṛṣṭvā harṣamāpa ha ||29||
tām praṇamya mahābhaktyā proce baddhāṃjalirnṛpaḥ | 
me rājyaṃ janma pitarau dhanaṃ dṛṣṭiḥ śrutaṃ tapaḥ ||30||
dṛṣṭvā śaktirdevamātā jagajjananakāriṇī | 
na śaktirvartate samyag varṇituṃ te guṇānmama ||31||
bālo vināyakaste’yaṃ śakrādadhika vikramaḥ | 
avadānānyanekāni kṛtāni bahudhā suraiḥ ||32||
aśakyāni mayā vaktuṃ rakṣasāṃ nidhanāni ca | 
tasyāpi varṇituṃ nāsti guṇānāṃ śaktiralpikā ||33||
viśrāmyatāṃ kṣaṇaṃ mātarmama bhāgyaṃ mahattaram | 
padanena hatā māyābalamāśritya dānavāḥ ||34||
aparikṣitena vapuṣā bālaste vasate sukham | 
tvaṃ kimarthamihāyātā yātā cet sthīyatāṃ kṣaṇam ||35||
nivṛtte’sminvivāhe tu prāpayiṣye ubhāvapi|  
sovāca | 
kimidaṃ bhāṣase rājanviyogādasya me’bhavat ||36||
mahānkhedo na ca sukhaṃ lebhe kvāpīha kiṃcana | 
ka uvāca | 
evaṃ vadatyāṃ tasyāṃ sa āgato’sau vināyakaḥ ||37||
preṣito bālakaiḥ sarvairāgatā jananī tava | 
sā liliṃga tato bālamaśrupūrṇā mudā jagau ||38||
cirakālaṃ sthito’syatra māṃ vihāyāti niṣṭhura | 
tvadviyogena saṃtaptā’’gatā sarvaṃ vihāya ca ||39||
jīvitāśāṃ parityajya tvadarthe tapa ācaram | 
atikleśena labdho’si tāna kleśānvetti ceśvaraḥ ||40||
vinā tvayā kṣaṇo me’to yugakalpasamo’bhavat | 
evaṃ lāghava vākyā’sau duṣṭabhāvena bhāminī ||41||
atipremabalātsā taṃ kaṭideśe dadhāra ha | 
so’pi tacceṣṭitaṃ jñātvā’’gastasyāḥparicintayan ||42||
gūḍhābhisandhistāṃ proce pūrvaṃ kiṃ me tvayāhratam | 
evaṃ tasminbruvatyeva laḍḍukaṃ sā tato dadau ||43||
atte’sminpunaranyaṃ sā mahāviṣayutam dadau | 
atte’sminpunaranyaṃ sa yayāce laḍḍukaṃ balāt ||44||
kāśirājaśca patnī ca prārthayāmāsatustadā | 
uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha mātastvaṃ sabālā bhojanaṃ kuru ||45||
yāvaduttiṣṭhate sā tu tāvadruddhā’rbhakeṇa sā| 
girīndrasāravapuṣā sā tato vihvalābhavat ||46||
muṃca muṃceti taṃ prāha mātā’haṃ tava bālaka | 
bahukālena dṛṣṭuṃ tvāmāgatā snehapāśataḥ ||47||
kathaṃ cūrṇayituṃ mām tvamudyato’si giriryathā | 
karābhyāṃ nunude sā taṃ tāvannidrāṃ yayo ca saḥ ||48||
prasārya caraṇau hastau śvāsocchvāsaparāyaṇaḥ | 
iti rājā nyaṣedhattāṃ vihvalāṃ bhārapīḍitām ||49||
mā nidrābhaṃgamasyāśu kuru tvaṃ jananī satī| 
uddhartuṃ bālakaṃ keciccakruḥkaruṇayā manaḥ ||50||
utthāpituṃ cālituṃ vā sarve nāśaknuvaṃstadā | 
prārthayāmāsurapare bālaṃ rājīvalocanam ||51||
anukrośaṃ kuru śiśo jananī te mariṣyati | 
cālayāmāsa caraṇau haste sā’thaśirodharām||52||
kimidaṃ kriyate bāla pitaraṃ kiṃ vadiṣyasi | 
evaṃ vadatsu lokeṣu sā mṛtā’tha niśācarī ||53||
daśayojana dehena vyāpitaṃ bhūtalaṃ tayā | 
palāyanaparāḥ kecittāṃ dṛṣṭvā’tibhayānakām ||54||
yayuḥ kecicca tāṃ dṛṣṭuṃ vicitrajanavṛttayaḥ | 
rājā ca vismitastatra janaḥ sarve vilokyatām ||55||
kathaṃ kapaṭarūpeyamāgatā bālaghātinī| 
ṛṣipatnīsvarūpeṇa jñātā na rākṣasīti ca ||56||
atyadbhūtamaṃ jñānamasya sāmarthyaṃ ca vilokitam | 
iti bruvāṇāstu narāstatsamīpaṃ mudā yayuḥ ||57||
bālamutthāpya tasyāstu puṣpabhārasamaṃ jaguḥ | 
apaneyeti te’pyāhurduṣṭā duṣṭasvarūpiṇī ||58||
parasyāniṣṭamicchedyaḥ sa eva nidhanaṃ vrajet | 
tuṣṭuvaḥ paripūjyainaṃ kāśirājo janā api ||59||
ṛṣayo lokapālāśca bhaktyā devaṃ vināyakam | 
nāthastvamasi devānāṃ manuṣyoragarakṣasām ||60||
yakṣagandharvaviprāṇāṃ gajāśvaratha pakṣiṇām | 
bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyasya buddhīndriyagaṇasya ca ||61||
harṣasya śokaduḥkhasya sukhasya jñānamohavoḥ | 
arthasya kāryajātasya lābhahānyostathaiva ca ||62||
svargapātālalokānāṃ pṛthivyā jaladherapi| 
nakṣatrāṇāṃ grahāṇāṃ ca piśācānāṃ ca vīrudhām ||63||
vṛkṣāṇāṃ saritāṃ puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ bālajanasya ca | 
utpattisthiti saṃhārakāriṇe te namo namaḥ ||64||
paśūnāṃ pataye tubhyaṃ tatvajñānapradāyine | 
namo viṣṇusvarūpāya namaste rudrarūpiṇe ||65||
namaste brahmarūpāya namo’nantasvarūpiṇe | 
mokṣaheto namastubhyaṃ namo vighnaharāya te ||66||
namo’bhaktavināśāya namo bhaktapriyāya ca | 
adhidaivādhibhūtātmaṃstāpatraya harāya te ||67||
sarvotpātavighātāya namo līlāsvarūpiṇe | 
sarvāṃntaryāmiṇe tubhyaṃ sarvādhyakṣāya te namaḥ ||68||
adityā jaṭharotpanna vināyaka namo’stu te| 
parabrahmasvarūpāya namaḥ kaśyapasūnave ||69||
ameyamāyānvita vikramāya, māyāvine māyika mohanāya | 
ameyamāyāharaṇāya māyāmahāśrayāyāstu namo namaste ||70||
evaṃ stutvā tu te sarve yayuḥ svaṃ svaṃ gṛhaṃ mudā| 
tyaktvā tām rākṣasīṃ chittvā khaṃḍaśo dūrataḥ purāt ||71||
ya idaṃ paṭhate stotraṃ trisandhyotpātanāśanam| 
na bhavanti mahotpātā vighnā bhūtabhayāni ca ||72||
trisandhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhetstotraṃ sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt | 
vināyakaḥ sadā tasya rakṣaṇaṃ kurate’nagha ||73||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite rākṣasīvadho nāmaikaviṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||21||

<adhyāya 22 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
aparasmin dine rājā prātaḥsnānakṛtodyamaḥ | 
paurāḥ sarve vitarkyaivaṃ nāyaṃ devo na mānuṣaḥ ||1||
na devaścet kathaṃ paṃca mahānto devadurdharāḥ | 
ajeyāḥ śakraharibhirhatā daityā mahābalāḥ ||2||
devaeva bhaved bālamadhye’yaṃ krīḍate laghu | 
iti tarkya tatastāvadāgatā rājadarśanam ||3||
rājā’pi bhadramagamaduvāca sakalān prati| 
nirūpayantu yatkāryaṃ tadvaḥ siddhiṃ prayāsyati ||4||
kimarthaṃ prātareveha paurā yūyaṃ samāgatāḥ | 
ta ūcurna rahasyaṃ yat svābhipretamṛte vacaḥ ||5||
tvayā’yaṃ hi samānīto muniputro vināyakaḥ | 
nāsya dāsyamabhūnno hi kiṃcit kvāpi kadācana ||6||
tvayā tu pūjito’nekavāraṃ svaśaraṇe sthitaḥ | 
yadyattava gṛhe yāti tattannaḥ preṣayasyaho ||7||
ayaṃ tu na kathaṃ rājana preṣito nu vināyakaḥ | 
rājovāca | 
samyaguktaṃ yatra janā vibhajya bhujyate tu yat ||8||
amṛtaṃ yāti ca viṣaṃ viṣamanyad bhavettu tat | 
asminyasya bhaved bhaktirdeve vā mānuṣe’pi vā ||9||
sa enaṃ svagṛhe nītvā pūjayedbhojayedapi | 
janāstu trividhāḥ paurā rajaḥsatvatamo guṇaiḥ ||10||
parīkṣantenmarā duṣṭāḥ puṇyavanto bhajantyapi | 
kecidenaṃ vinindanti praśaṃsanti ca kecana ||11||
yasya svabhāvo yādṛksyātsa tathā vartate naraḥ | 
na jahāti ghṛṣyamāṇaścandanaḥ svasugandhitām ||12||
kasturī kardamayutaḥ palāṃḍuvā nijaṃ guṇam | 
bhavatāṃ yadi bhaktiścedananyā’sminsute muneḥ ||13||
nīyatāṃ pūjayansvāśu bhojanāya priyāya ca | 
parīkṣārthaṃ na neyo’sau janako jananī mama ||14||
mayā kathaṃ narā vācyā nītvainaṃ pūjayan tviti | 
asya śaktiṃ dṛḍhāṃ dṛṣṭvā mama bhaktirviśiṣyate ||15||
nāyaṃ cenmatpurī kvatyā kṛto yūyaṃ kva cāpyaham | 
iti rājavacaḥ śrutvā punarūcuśca nāgarāḥ ||16||
yathā vadasi kalyāṇa tattathā na tadanyathā | 
asmākaṃ kāryasiddhiryā tāṃ kuruṣva janādhipa ||17||
nītvemaṃ svagṛhe bālaṃ pūjayāmo yathārhataḥ | 
etasminnantare bālo’vadattān sadasisthitān ||18||
jānannantargataṃ teṣāṃ sarvasākṣī jagadguruḥ | 
vināyaka uvāca | 
kimarthaṃ mām prārthayanto bhavanto’ti mahattarāḥ ||19||
ahaṃ bālo munisuto matpūjanaphalaṃ nu kim | 
bhaviṣyati na jāne’haṃ bhavatāṃ vyayakāriṇām ||20||
vivāho vratabandho vā yajño vā’nyo mahotsavaḥ | 
tadā bhavadgṛhaṃ yāsye havyaṃ kavyaṃ yadā bhavet ||21||
asaṃkhyātā bhavanto hi kathameko gṛhaṃ vraje | 
vakeśinīṃ kimarthaṃ vā gāṃ dhokṣyatha bhavadvidhāḥ ||22||
vāṃchitārthī kathaṃ yācettyaktvā kalpadrumaṃ drumam | 
anyat kṣudraṃ vāṃchitaṃ vā tadvadyūyaṃ samutsukāḥ ||23||
ka uvāca | 
vināyakavacaḥ śrutvā śreṇīmukhyā jaguḥ punaḥ | 
asminvivāhe sampanne na sthāsyati kṣaṇaṃ bhavān ||24||
rājñaḥ prasādāddṛṣṭo’si bhaktiṃ na saphalīkurū | 
avāptakāmasya ca te sṛṣṭi sthityantakāriṇaḥ ||25||
sarvāntaryāmiṇaḥ sarvaṃ cittavṛttī vijānataḥ | 
kartuṃ vāpyanyathā kartumakartuṃ vā kṣamasya ha ||26||
cidānandasya naivāsti pūjābhirnaḥ prayojanam | 
bhaktipriyo deva iti nigamaṃ na vṛthā kurū ||27||
niśamyeti vacasteṣāṃ tānūce’tha vināyakaḥ | 
evaṃ cedbhavatāṃ bhaktistadā yāsye nṛpājñayā ||28||
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ sarve’pi svagṛhān yayuḥ | 
maṃḍapān vidadhuḥ kecit kecidvastragṛhāṇi ca ||29||
toraṇān vidadhurbhaktyādarśaśobhādbhutāni ca | 
bhājanāni mahārhāṇi sugandha candanāni ca ||30||
dravyāṇi ca sugandhīni mṛganābhibhavāni ca | 
phalāni ca vicitrāṇi vāsāṃsyābharaṇāni ca ||31||
pakvānnāni vicitrāṇi paṃcāmṛtayutāni ca | 
sthāpitā mūrtayo nānāmuktāmālā vibhūṣitāḥ ||32||
evaṃ gṛhe gṛhe sarve sāmagrīṃ cakrurutsukāḥ | 
eko’bhūnnagaraprānte brāhmaṇo vedaśāstravit ||33||
śukla nāmnā śuklavṛttiḥ śānto dāntaḥ kṣamānvitaḥ | 
śrautasmārta karmanidhirbrahmaniṣṭhotithipriyaḥ ||34||
yasya patnī mahābhāgā vidrumā nāma nāmataḥ | 
nispṛhā jñānasampannā sarvāvayavasundarā ||35||
daridrā jñānaniṣṭhā sā patiprāṇā pativratā | 
tasyā gṛhagataṃ dṛśyaṃ nabhastārāṃkitaṃ sphuṭam ||36||
na svarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ bhājanaṃ rītijaṃ na ca | 
gaurā valkalasaṃvītā tejojvālā suśobhanā ||37||
yadaṃgatejasā vyāptaṃ dṛśyamānaṃ na dṛśyate | 
tena vṛttena tuṣṭā sā gahaśobhāvidhāyinī||38||
tasyāstoṣeṇa tuṣṭo’bhūdvinayena ca sevayā| 
svasthaḥsthito jñānaniṣṭhastiṣṭhatyakṛtabhojanaḥ ||39||
kadācidbhikṣituṃ yāto vāritastu gṛhegṛhe | 
mahotsavaṃ prakurvadbhirvināyakakṛte janaiḥ ||40||
yallabdhaṃ tena tuṣṭo’sau bhāryāmetyā’bravīdidam | 
śrūyatāṃ patni nagare vārito’haṃ gṛhegṛhe ||41||
āyāsyati pratigrahaṃ pūjārthaṃ sa vināyakaḥ | 
yo’vatīrṇo munigṛhaṃ bhūbhāra haraṇodyataḥ ||42||
āyāsyati gṛhaṃ cetsa pūjārthe yatnamācara | 
sā’bravīnmahatīṃ pūjāṃ tyaktvā’nnaṃ tvadgṛhe katham ||43||
bhavatāṃ so’kiṃcanānāṃ kathaṃ vā gṛhameṣyati | 
gandhapuṣpaṃ paryuṣitaṃ pakvaṃ kandaphalaṃ mune ||44||
bhaviṣyati kṛtaṃ samyagasamyak svalpamapyaho| 
kiṃ vā prayojanaṃ tasya gṛheṇa bhavatāṃ prabho ||45||
iti priyāvacaḥ śrutvā punarūce muniḥ priyām | 
sa mām bhaktaṃ vijānāti dīnānātha prabhuḥ prabhuḥ ||46||
bhaktipriyo’sti devo’yaṃ na lobhavaśago vibhuḥ| 
arṇasā ca palāśena puṣpeṇa prīyate hi saḥ ||47||
suvarṇānicayārvāpi na hi dambhāti samarpitaiḥ | 
ityākarṇya vacastasya vidrumā punarabravīt ||48||
evaṃ cettarhi yat siddhaṃ tadetasmai nivedyatām | 
tato’ṣṭādaśa dhānyānām kṛtvā piṣṭaṃ papāca sā | ||49||
odanaśca kṛto jīrṇaṃ tandulānāṃ jalādhikaḥ | 
muṣṭipiṣṭaṃ parṇapuṭe paktvā bhuṃkte dinedine ||50||
tattena dinamānena dravyeṇa cailamānayat | 
gandhākṣataṃ ca puṣpāṇi dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ mahārtike ||51||
vanyaṃ phalaṃ valkalaṃ ca sthāpayāmāsa caikataḥ | 
śuṣkāmalaka khaṃḍāni mukhavāsārthameva ca ||52||
prasārya darbhanicayaṃ susikte svāṃgaṇe dhvajam | 
cakāra kaśipū nāsau dhyānaniṣṭhobhavan muniḥ ||53||
naivedyaṃ vaiśvadevaṃ ca kṛtvā pūrvaṃ prayatnataḥ ||54||
iti śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite nāma dvāviṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||22||

<adhyāya 23 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
sa bālo bālamadhye tu cikrīḍe kautukānvitaḥ | 
rājā bhadrāsanagato nṛtyaṃ sarvajanaiḥ saha ||1||
yāvatpaśyati veśyānāṃ tāvadevāgatāvubhau | 
devalokātsado ramyaṃ sanakaśca sanandanaḥ ||2||
udbhāsitaṃ tayordīptyā sūryāgnyoriva tatsadaḥ | 
āsanādudatiṣṭhatsa nṛpatiḥ sahasekṣya tau ||3||
sthiraḥ sthāpya pādapadme sāṣṭāṃgaṃ praṇanāma tau | 
baddhāṃjaliruvācātha adya dhanyaṃ kulaṃ mama ||4||
rājyaṃ jñānaṃ ca dehaśca patnīputrādikaṃ ca yat | 
ityuktvā pāṇinā dhṛtvā svāsane copaveśya tau ||5||
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirapūjayata bhaktitaḥ | 
tau ca viśrāmayāmāsa pādasaṃvāhanādibhiḥ ||6||
punaḥ punaḥ praṇamyāsau vaktuṃ samupacakrame | 
rājovāca | 
munīnāṃ nyastadaṃḍānāṃ nispṛhāṇāṃ ca sarvadā ||7||
kāryalipsā na kācitsyādājñāmīhe tathāpyaham | 
tapo rājyaṃ kulaṃ śīlaṃ sārthakaṃ kartumudyataḥ ||8||
tāvūcatuḥ | 
avāptasarvakāmo’sau parabrahmasvarūpavān | 
līlāvatārī bhagavān kaśyapasya sutastava ||9||
gṛhe nānākautukāni kurute’dbhutavikramaḥ | 
śrutvā’vadānaṃ tasyātra draṣṭumāvāṃ samāgatau ||10||
anyathā śakrabhavanatyāge kiṃ naḥ prayojanam | 
gṛhe yasya kalpataruḥ so’nyaṃ ki yācayiṣyati ||11||
tasyoṅghrikamalaṃ natvā gacchāvaḥ svasthalaṃ nṛpa | 
evaṃ tayorāgatayoḥ śrutvā vākyaṃ vināyakaḥ ||12||
vadatorāgataḥ krīḍāṃ tyaktvā modaka hastakaḥ | 
paṃcakhādyamayaṃ bhakṣya modakaṃ līlayādadhat ||13||
tayostaṃ darśayāmāsa nṛpo hāsyaparo mṛdu | 
so’yaṃ samāgato devo yaddarśanasamutsukau ||14||
āgato yacca kartavyaṃ kurutaṃ tadyatheṣṭataḥ | 
taṃ nirīkṣya munī tau tu parasparamathocatuḥ ||15||
anena dūṣito nūnaṃ kaśyapo munisattamaḥ | 
tatputratāṃ samāsādya svācāraṃ tyajatā bhṛśam ||16||
spṛṣṭāspṛṣṭavicāro’sya bhakṣyābhakṣyavidhirna ca | 
darśane sparśane cāsya mahāndoṣo bhavetkhalu ||17||
kathaṃ svaniyamāṃstyaktvā sthitaḥ kṣatriyaveśmani | 
svadharme naḥ sthitānāṃ tu darśanenāsya kiṃ bhavet ||18||
śuśrāva tāṃ giraṃ devo nṛpasannihitastadā | 
uvāca vākyaṃ vākyajño vācaspatirivāparaḥ ||19||
kva gataṃ bhavato jñānaṃ vṛthā śramavatormunī| 
svargaṃ tyaktvā kathamiha saṃprāptau prākṛtāviva ||20||
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ tasya nṛpatiṃ tāvathocatuḥ | 
māyāmayasya devasya māyayā mohito bhṛśam ||21||
dehyanujñāṃ gamiṣyāvo nijamāśrama maṃḍalam | 
tato nṛpatiśārdūlo dṛḍhabhakti praṇoditaḥ ||22||
sthīyatāṃ śrūyatāṃ vākyaṃ madīyamityuvāca
tau| bhaktyā ca prārthayāmāsa snātvā bhuktvā’nugamyatām ||23||
niśamya prārthanāṃ tasya mocaturmunipuṃgavau | 
na bhujyete rājakīyamannam tasmād vrajāvahe ||24||
tadanujñāṃ samāsādya maṇikarṇī tato gatau | 
vināyakaḥ sarvabālairanuyāto mudā raman ||25||
snātvā gataḥ śuklagṛhaṃ jñātvā tadbhaktimādṛtaḥ | 
yathā dhāvati gaurvatsaṃ yathā mātā rudacchiśum ||26||
yathā kṛṣṇaḥ sabhānītāṃ draupadīṃ śaraṇāgatām | 
dṛṣṭvā tau dampatī devaṃ pādayorvinipetatu ||27||
ānandāśrūṇi muṃccantau vācā gadgadayā girā| 
liliṃgaturmudā devaṃ nanṛturharṣanirbharau ||28||
na vindatuḥ prakartavyaṃ dehātītā vivāsatuḥ | 
romāṃccāṃccitagātrau tau bāṣpadhārāmamuṃccatām ||29||
muhūrtāllabdhasaṃjñau tau nematurjagadīśvaram | 
tata ūce munirdevaṃ dīnanātheti yattava ||30||
vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu nāma pāpaharaṃ śubham | 
sārthakaṃ kṛtamadyaiva janma naḥ pādadarśanāt ||31||
prāsādanicayāṃstyaktvā gataḥ parṇakuṭīmimām | 
dhanyo vaṃśo madīyo’dya janma jñānaṃ tapo vayaḥ ||32||
apūjayattatastaṃ sa kuśāsanagataṃ muniḥ | 
prakṣālya caraṇadvandvaṃ jalaṃ tacchirasā’dadhat ||33||
gandhākṣataṃ puṣpamālāṃ dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ dadāvatha | 
dūrvāṃkurān śamīpatraṃ sutailaṃ ketakaṃ śubham ||34||
khādyaṃ svalpaṃ puraḥ sthāpya phalamāraṇyakaṃ tathā| 
puṣpāṃjaliṃ mantrapūtāṃ dattvā nattvā sa lajjitaḥ ||35||
kadannaṃ kathamasmai taddeyamityeva cintayan | 
abhiprāyaṃ viditvā taṃ devadevo vināyakaḥ ||36||
yāce mātaḥ kimannaṃ te gṛhe taddīyatāṃ mama | 
bhaktyā dattaṃ kadannaṃ me’dhikaṃ syādamṛtādapi ||37||
viṣaṃ bhavati yadattaṃ dambhenāśraddhayānvitam | 
vistīrṇamodanaṃ śuṣkaṃ bhāskarīṃ bahudhānyajām ||38||
āsthāpayat purastasya munipatnī hriyānvitā | 
tailaṃ ca sakalaṃ dṛṣṭvā prāhasaṃstaṃ ca bālakāḥ ||39||
abhakṣayatsa pakvānnasadṛśaṃ paramādarāt | 
pratigrāsaṃ jalaṃ pītvā jaghāsa balavattaram ||40||
tatastenodanaḥ pātraṃ nikṣipto muninā’khilaḥ | 
tajjalaṃ calitaṃ dikṣu bālo roddhuṃ na cāśakat ||41||
tato’bhavaddaśabhujo bubhuje caudanaṃ ca taiḥ | 
āścaryaṃ dadṛśurlokā hasanto hastatālataḥ ||42||
apūrvaḥ pācito’yaṃ nu jānudaghnajalena yaḥ | 
ityūcurbubhujurbālā rucyā pāyasakalpayā ||43||
sākaṃ tena ca vairbhuktaṃ gatāste devatulyatām | 
aśocannitare paścādye’hasanvivṛtai radaiḥ ||44||
kolāhalo mahānāsīnnagare kva vināyakaḥ | 
kecidūcuḥ śuklagṛhe bhunakti śīrṇamodanam ||45||
roddhuṃ kṛtā daśabhujo odanaṃ ca bhunakti taiḥ | 
bhukte tasminviśuddhe sa śuṣkamāmalakaṃ dadau ||46||
mukhaśuddhiṃ tena cakre jagadātmā vināyakaḥ | 
prasannaḥ śuklamavadat prīto bhāvena te’nagha ||47||
varānvṛṇu mahābhāga matto yānabhikāṃkṣase | 
śukla uvāca | 
sarvānnāgarikāṃstyaktvā bhoktuṃ rūkṣānnamāgataḥ ||48||
ayameva varo mahayaṃ yajjātaṃ tava darśanam | 
prabhuvākyānurodhena yāce bhaktiṃ tvayi sthirām||49||
ante mokṣaṃ ca me dehi yena nāvartate punaḥ | 
na saṃsārasukhe cittaṃ ramate te vināyaka ||50||
ka uvāca | 
evamastviti taṃ procya pūrvavad dvibhujaḥ śiśuḥ | 
gṛhaṃ tasmai śakragṛhādvaraṃ sadratnakāṃcanam ||51||
atyuttamaṃ svarūpaṃ ca jñānaṃ sampadamarpayat | 
anujñāto yayau tābhyāmanyatra bālakairvṛtaḥ ||52||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacaritre śuklavarapradānaṃ nāma trayoviṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||23||

<adhyāya 24 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
vyalokayaṃstaṃ nagare tasmin sarve mahājanāḥ | 
vidhāya pūjāsāmagrīṃ bhojanasyāpyanekadhā ||1||
gṛhe gṛhe rājagṛhe sarvāmātyagṛhe’pi ca | 
gaveṣayanti sma janāstadā kaśyapanandanam ||2||
apṛcchanta janāḥ sarve kvacidṛṣṭo vināyakaḥ | 
neti neti śrutirvakti yaṃ sa kasyākṣigocaraḥ ||3||
bhavennālokito’smābhiriti tatrāvadajjanāḥ | 
puraprānte tu ye tatra te’śṛṇvajjanavākyataḥ ||4||
sa cāgataḥ śuklagṛhaṃ tataste tadgṛhaṃ yayuḥ | 
nāpaśyaṃstamathāpṛcchaṃstenoktaṃ sa ito gataḥ ||5||
sa tu māyāmayo bālo janānvaṃcitumutsukaḥ | 
visṛjya bālakān paścād dvāreṇāgatya suptavān ||6||
kecittaṃ dadṛśuḥ suptaṃ haryakṣamiva gahvara | 
kecittaṃ krodhāvaśagā ninindurbālako laghuḥ ||7||
piśāca iva kiṃ yāto daridrasya gṛhaṃ prati | 
īśaścet sarvabhāvajñaḥ sarveṣāṃ priyamācaret ||8||
utthāpya taṃ kare dhṛtvā yāhīti śaraṇaṃ śubham | 
saphalaṃ kuru naḥ sarvaṃ tvadarthamupakalpitam ||9||
gataḥ khedo bhramaṇajo dṛṣṭvā tvaccaraṇāmbujam | 
yāhi yāhi gṛhānnastvaṃ bhoktuṃ bālagaṇaiḥ saha ||10||
sa ūce sāmprataṃ bhuktaṃ punarūcurjanāstu tam | 
daridrasya gṛhe kiṃ te bhuktaṃsyādbhaikṣyavartitaḥ ||11||
nāpo’pi pibate kvānnaṃ kathaṃ bhuktaṃ tvayā vibho| 
gaṇeśa uvāca | 
udaraṃ paripūrṇaṃ me yātuṃ śaktiścanāsti me ||12||
ka uvāca | 
tataḥ sarve viditvā taṃ bhuktaṃ śuklagṛhe janāḥ| 
nirāśā bhagnasaṃkalpā ruṣṭā dīnā vicetasaḥ ||13||
sambhārā vyarthatāṃ yātā nānāvakeśārtha saṃcitāḥ |
bubhujustān svayaṃ duṣṭā dāmbhikā bhaktivarjitāḥ ||14||
ye bhaktāste nirāhārāstasthurdhyānaparāyaṇāḥ | 
evaṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu jñātvā dhyānasthiteṣu saḥ ||15||
eko nānāṣvarūpo’bhūd ghaṭākāśa iva sphuṭam | 
jalapūrṇeṣu kumbheṣu ravirnānā yathekṣyate ||16||
kvacit paryaṃkaśayanaḥ kvacit japaparāyaṇaḥ | 
kvaciddānarataḥ kvāpi bhojanāya samutsukaḥ ||17||
kvacit pāṭhyate śiṣyān sāṃgavedaṃsahārthakam | 
kvacit vyākurute śāstraṃ kvacicca paṭhati svayam ||18||
evaṃ nānāsvarūpaiḥ sa nānāgṛhagato babhau | 
ayamasmad gṛhaṃ yātaḥ pūrvamityabhimenire ||19||
tailamudvartanaṃ snānaṃ pūjanaṃ bhojanaṃ daduḥ | 
evaṃ sarve jahraṣire kāśirājasamanvitam ||20||
dṛṣṭvā devaṃ paramayā mudā sādhuvināyakam | 
pupūjurbhojayāmāsurdvijān suhṛdgaṇānapi ||21||
paṃcāmṛtaṃ ca pakvānnaṃ vyaṃjanaṃ pāyasaudanau | 
anyadrucikaraṃ sarvaṃ bubhajuste yathāruci ||22||
evaṃ dvijeṣu deveṣu kāśirāje suhṛtsvapi | 
āśīrvatsu sabhāviṣṭaḥ kāśirājo nvapṛcchata ||23||
māyayā muniputrasya mohito’nekadhākṛteḥ | 
rājovāca | 
vināyako gato bhoktuṃ nānāgṛha itisphuṭam ||24||
ayaṃ me nikaṭe’pyasti ko nu bhuṃkte gṛhe gṛhe | 
kvacicca śibikārūḍho yāti bhoktuṃ vināyakaḥ| ||25||
gajārūḍhaḥ kvacidyāti hayāruḍhaśca kutracit | 
evaṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu pūjāvyagreṣu bālake ||26||
tatastau nagarīṃ yātau tatra tatra vināyakam| 
apaśyatāṃ bhuktavantaṃ parāvṛtya samīyatuḥ ||27||
yatra yatra gṛhe yattau tatra tatra vināyakam | 
nirvṛtya sakalaṃ nityaṃ bhikṣārthaṃ paryagacchatām ||28||
śayānaṃ śayane divye bhakṣayantaṃ phalāni ca | 
carvayantaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ kvacitgandhādicarcitam ||29||
kvacinnṛtyaṃ prapaśyantaṃ gaṇikānāṃ gaṇānvitat | 
krīḍantamakṣaiḥ kutrāpi kvacid buddhibalena ca ||30||
nānālaṃkārasaṃyuktaṃ divyavastrānvitaṃ kvacit | 
paṭhantaṃ ca japantaṃ ca dhyānena tamapaśyatām ||31||
stūyamānaṃ kvacid bhaktai stuvantaṃ jagadīśvaram | 
darśayantaṃ vibhūtiṃ svāṃ bhaktyānekaśaḥ kvacit ||32||
yuvānaṃ ramayantaṃ svāḥstriyo’tyanta manoramāḥ | 
cikitsayantaṃ vṛddhaṃ ca sevantaṃ rasamuttamam ||33||
riṃgaṃtaṃ bhakṣayantaṃ ca mṛdaṃ kutracidaṃgaṇe | 
pariśrāntaṃ pibantaṃ kaṃ krīḍābhiḥ kandukādibhiḥ ||34||
evaṃ tau kṣudhitau śrāntau paryaṭantau gṛhe gṛhe | 
sarvatra dṛṣṭvā taṃ devaṃ parasparamathocatuḥ ||35||
atrāsti nirmalaḥ śuklo brāhmaṇo nagare śuciḥ | 
yāvo bhoktuṃ tasya gṛhe ityuktvā tatra jagmatuḥ ||36||
tatrāpi soṃ’gaṇagato dṛṣṭastābhyāṃ vināyakaḥ | 
nagare nātra bhoktavyaṃ na tu śeṣaṃ vināyakāt ||37||
ityuktvā bahirāyātau puro dṛṣṭvā vināyakam | 
tiryag gatidharau bhūtvā tato’pi samapaśyatām ||38||
prācyāṃ pratīcyāṃ ca diśi tamevetau samanvataḥ | 
adhaścordhvaṃ sarvagataṃ vināyakamapaśyatām ||39||
vināyakasvarūpaṃ ca tau punarviśvarūpiṇam | 
sarvaṃ vināyakamayaṃ vastumātramapaśyatām ||40||
athābhāvayatāṃ śambhuṃ viṣṇuṃ tau bhṛśavismitau | 
apaśyatāṃ hradi tadā taṃ ca taṃ ca vināyakam ||41||
nimīlya nayane cāntastameva munisattamau | 
udghāṭaya nayane cāpi svānta enamapaśyatām ||42||
kirīṭinaṃ kuṃḍalinaṃ muktāmālāmbarāṃgadam | 
kaṭisūtreṇa haimena mudrikābhiśca bhūṣitam ||43||
siṃhārūḍhaṃ daśabhujaṃ siddhibuddhiyutaṃ śubham | 
bhālacandraṃ mṛgamadatilakaṃ nāgabhūṣaṇam ||44||
sūryakoṭiprabhaṃ sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārakāriṇam| 
tyaktvā bhedaṃ tattvavidau nematuścaraṇāmbujam ||45||
nutaḥsma parayā bhaktyā devadevaṃ vināyakam | 
tasyaiva karuṇāprāpta prabodhau tau munīśvarau ||46||
 iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe vināyakabhojanakathanaṃ nāma caturviṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||24||

<adhyāya 25 prāraṃbhaḥ>

tāvūcatuḥ | 
sarveṣāṃ kāraṇānāṃ tvaṃ kāraṇaṃ kāraṇātigaḥ| 
brahmasvarūpo brahmāṃḍakāraṇaṃ vyāpakaḥ paraḥ ||1||
pāsīdaṃ sṛjase viśvaṃ tvameva harase’nagha | 
nānārūpairarūpastvaṃ nānāmāyā balānvitaḥ ||2||
tvameva paṃcabhūtāni yakṣagandharva rākṣasāḥ| 
kastvāṃ stotuṃ samarthaḥ syāccarācarasvarūpakam ||3||
neti neti bravīti sma tvadrūpājñānataḥ śrutiḥ | 
āvām vimohitau jñātuṃ neśāthe rūpamuttamam ||4||
mahimānaṃ na jānīvo’nekarūpasya te vibho | 
kṛtakṛtyo bhavatpādadarśanātsvaḥ prabho’dhunā ||5||
nānāvatārairbhūbhāraṃ harase’nantaśaktitaḥ | 
ka uvāca | 
evaṃ tayoḥ stutiṃ śrutvā prītyā provāca tāvubhau ||6||
vināyako bālarūpī kautukī māyiko’pi ca | 
tatvajñau matprasādācca sarvajñau ca bhaviṣyathaḥ ||7||
evaṃ datvā varaṃ devastatraivāntaradhīyata | 
tatastau mūtimatulāṃ kṛtvā samyagvināyakīm ||8||
kṛtvā prāsādāmatulaṃ ratnakāṃcana saṃyutam | 
sthāpayāmāsaturubhau sanakaśca sanandanaḥ ||9||
ākhyāṃ ca cakratustasyā varado’yaṃ vināyakaḥ | 
gaṇeśakuṃḍamiti ca kṛtaṃ tābhyāṃ sarovaram ||10||
atra snātvā pūjayedyo varadaṃ taṃ vināyakam | 
sa kāmānakhilān prāpya bhuktvā bhogānanekaśaḥ ||11||
avāpya putrān pautrāṃśca vidyāmāyuryaśo mahat | 
dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ yaśaścāpi tatvajñānaṃ ca śāśvatam ||12||
ante vaināyakaṃ dhāma yāti nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ | 
ka uvāca | 
yatra devāḥ sagandharvā yakṣāścāpsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ ||13||
dṛṣṭvā sampūjya deveśaṃ tatraivāntarhitāḥ kṣaṇāt | 
saṃprāptavismayau tau ca jagmaturdivamuttamam ||14||
kāśirājo’pi tatratya sāmātyaḥ saprajastu tam | 
nānādravyairalaṃkārairvastrairuccāvacairapi ||15||
pakvānnaiścāpyanekaiśca phalairnānāvidhairapi | 
ratnairmuktāphalaiḥ puṣpairdakṣiṇābhiranekaśaḥ ||16||
pupūja parayā bhaktyā brāhmaṇaiḥ saha mantrataḥ | 
brāhmaṇān pūjayāmāsa vastrālaṃkāra kāṃcanaiḥ ||17||
āśiṣaḥ parigṛhyaiva nagaraṃ punarāgamat | 
tathaiva tāḥ prakṛtayaḥ pupūjustaṃ vināyakam ||18||
tathaiva brāhmaṇān pūjya jagṛhurāśiṣaḥ parāḥ | 
tataḥ prajāstaṃ pupūjuryathāśakti vināyakam ||19||
sarve te hṛṣṭamanasā puraṃ praviviśustadā | 
iti te sarvamākhyātaṃ vināyakaviceṣṭitam ||20||
sarvapāpapaharaṃ puṇyaṃ kimanyacchrotumicchasi | 
muniruvāca | 
śrutaṃ caritraṃ śuklasya śuklabhikṣāratasya ca ||21||
dhātusparśavihīnasya gṛhaṃ yātau vināyakaḥ | 
bhujairdaśabhirādāya hyodanaṃ śithilaṃ jalaiḥ ||22||
jagdhvā tyaktvā śramaṃ tṛpto dāridryaṃ ca vyanāśayat| 
dattaṃ gṛhaṃ tu śuklasya śakrasya nagarādvaram ||23||
sampado’pi tathā dattā yāḥ santi hyalakādhipe | 
svarūpaṃ ca tathā dattaṃ yannāsti makaradhvaje ||24||
jñānaṃ tasmai tathā dattaṃ yannāsti caturānane | 
atra kiṃ kāraṇaṃ brahmaṁstanme vyākhyātumarhasi ||25||
ka uvāca | 
samyakpṛṣṭaṃ tvayā vyāsa parameśakathā yataḥ | 
punāti prāśnikaṃ śrotṝnvaktāraṃ bhaktitaḥ śrutā ||26||
mano me hi sthitaṃ vaktuṃ tatpṛṣṭaṃ hi tvayā vibho | 
samyakte’haṃ vadiṣyāmi bhaktigamyasya ceṣṭitam ||27||
sarvajñaḥ sarvadarśī ca sarvagaḥ sarvacittavit | 
bhaktyaiva tuṣyate devo bhāvāhi toṣakāraṇam ||28||
bhaktyāhrataṃ palāśaṃ ca jalaṃ puṣpaṃ manoharam | 
tenaiva parituṣṭo’sāvātmānaṃ ca prayacchati ||29||
dambhena helayā vāpi lajjayā’pi parasya vā | 
mahādhanaṃ mahāvittaṃ ratnāni vividhāni ca ||30||
kāṃcanaṃ rājataṃ muktā dattaṃ sarvaṃ vṛthā bhavet | 
śamīpatreṇa tuṣṭo’sau vyādhāyādāt salokatām ||31||
prasaṃgato’pi dattena bhāvaṃ kṛtvā dṛḍhaṃ hṛdi| 
anāyāsena labdhena tasmādbhaktirgarīyasī ||32||
 iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite bhaktipraśaṃsā kathanaṃ nāma paṃcaviṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||25||

<adhyāya 26 prāraṃbhaḥ>

muniruvāca | 
kiṃ karma kṛtavān vyādhaḥ kathaṃ patraṃ samarpitam | 
kiṃ nāmāsa kathaṃ prāptaḥ sālokyaṃ tadvadasva me ||1||
ka uvāca | 
vidarbhadeśe nagara mahiṣākhyaṃ babhūva ha | 
vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu kubera nagaropamam ||2||
bhīmo nāmābhavad vyādho nagare māṃsavikrayī| 
pareṣāṃ doṣakathane doṣo yadyapi vartate ||3||
praśne kṛte tu vaktavyo yāthātathyānna matsarāt | 
sa bhīmo doṣabahulo bāṇakodaṃḍavānvane ||4||
śarapūrṇeṣudhī khaḍgī cāpaṃ bibhracca śastrikām | 
avadhīt prāṇino nityaṃ kuṭumbabharaṇe rataḥ ||5||
pathikānbrāhmaṇāṃścāpi nirdayo nirjane vane| 
kadācinnagare tasmin samārabdho mahotsavaḥ ||6||
prātareva vanaṃ yāto bahumāṃsajighṛkṣayā | 
vikrayecchurdravyalubdho kuṭumbasyāpi poṣakaḥ ||7||
jaghāna mṛgayūthāstānmāṃsabhārayutaḥ puram | 
yāvatpraviśate tāvadāgato rākṣaso mahān ||8||
manuṣyagandhahṛṣṭo’sau yayau tasyāntikaṃ kṣaṇāt | 
piṃgākṣo nāmataḥ piṃganetraḥ sarvabhayaṃkaraḥ ||9||
manuṣyaśvāpadāśī sa duṣpūra iva havyabhuk | 
cakampe sa ca taṃ dṛṣṭvā vyādho bhūmāvathāpatat ||10||
patitāni ca śastrāṇi netre’pyācchādite balāt | 
unmīlya nayane’paśyacchamīvṛkṣaṃ samīpataḥ ||11||
āruroha ca bhīmastaṃ rākṣaso’pi samāruhat | 
tāvaccikṣepa śākhāṃ sa tattpatraṃ vāyuneritam||12||
papāta gaṇanāthasya vāmanāsthāpitasya ha| 
mastake tena tuṣṭo’sāvubhābhyāṃ pūjitaḥ sphuṭam||13||
vāmanāya varāndātuṃ sphuṭo yobhūd gajānanaḥ | 
baliṃ vijetukāmāya kaśyapasyātmajāya saḥ ||14||
atyantaṃ tuṣyate devo dūrvayā ca śamīdalaiḥ| 
ratnakāṃcanasaṃyuktā divyavastrasamanvitā||15||
dūrvāṃkuravinā pūjā niṣphalā jāyate nṛṇām | 
śamīpatreṇa vā sā syātsaphalā nānyathā kvacit ||16||yajñadānavratairanyaistapobhirniyamairapi | 
tathā na prīyate devaḥ kāṃcanai ratnasaṃcayaiḥ ||17||
dūrvābhiśca śamīpatrairyathā suprīyate vibhuḥ | 
tayostuṣṭo devadevaḥ preṣayadyānamuttamam ||18||
svarūpadhāribhiryuktaṃ kiṃkarairnijalokataḥ| 
teṣāṃ nirīkṣaṇānnaṣṭāḥ pāpaparvatarāśayaḥ ||19||
kṛpayā ca gaṇeśasya tayo rākṣasabhīmayoḥ | 
yathāgnikaṇasamparkānnaśyante tṛṇaparvatāḥ ||20||
vyaktvā deho divyadehau gṛhītvā yānamāsthitau | 
dūtaiḥ sampūjitau nānāvastrabhūṣāvilepanaiḥ ||21||
nānāvāditra nirghoṣaiḥ strīgandharvaiyutaṃ mahat| 
nītau tau devanikaṭe nematustau vināyakam ||22||
aśeṣa pāpa nirmuktau prāpatustau sarūpatām | 
ākalpaṃ sthāpitau tena gaṇeśena nijasthale ||23||
evaṃ bhāvapriyo devaḥ prīyate bhaktibhāvataḥ | 
tathaiva muṣṭimātrānnātsantuṣṭo’yaṃ dvijasya saḥ| 
śuklasya pradadau divyamalakāsadṛśaṃ gṛham ||24||
tasmāddūrvā śamīpatrairvinā pūjā vṛthā bhavet | 
na mahatyā dāmbhikayā pūjayā prīyate tathā ||25||
bhaktibhāvena kṛtayā svalpayā’sau gajānanaḥ ||26||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bhīmarākṣasamokṣaṇaṃ nāma ṣaḍviṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||26||

<adhyāya 27 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
pūrvajanmani vyādho’sau ka āsīdrākṣasoḥpi saḥ |
kiṃ śīlaṃ kiṃsamācāra stanmamācakṣva nābhija ||1 ||
anuṣṭhānaṃ kṛtaṃ tena vāmanena kathaṃ prabho|
kathaṃ tasmai varo datto gajānanasvarupiṇā ||2||
loko gaṇeśasya kathaṃ pṛthaganyatra tiṣṭhati |
kathaṃ śamī priyā tasya sarvametadvadāśu me ||3 ||
ka uvāca |
śṛṇu vakṣyāmi te sarvaṃ yadyatpṛṣṭaṃ tvayā mune |
rājā’sītpūrvajanuṣi sa vyādho vedapāragaḥ ||4||
sarva śāstrapravaktā ca sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ |
yajvā vinīto hrīmāṃśca devatātithipūjakaḥ ||5| |
gajāśvarathapattīnāṃ saṃkhyā yasya na vidyate |
dātā śuraśca medhāvī samṛddho maghavāniva ||6||
dhvajaśca śatrujidyasya pradhānau buddhimattarau |
yato’dharīkṛtaḥ kāvyo girāṃ patirapi sphuṭam ||7||
yau tau kṣititale jetuṃ samarthau śastratejasā |
patnī sulakṣaṇāsyāsīnnāmnā ca madanāvatī ||8||
pativratā dharmaśīlā sumukhī sarvavedinī |
na tayā sadṛśī kācittriṣu lokeṣu kāminī ||9||
dīnānāthavṛddhajane kṛpāluratithiṣvapi |
yajatordadatoreva ratayoḥ pūjane satām ||1 0||
tayorvṛttena tutuṣuḥ paurā jānapadā janāḥ |
amitrā api gṛhṇanti ṣaḍguṇān sādhusaṃmatān ||11 ||
evaṃ sa puṣyakīrtiḥ saṃśaśāsa pṛthivīmimām |
bahusaṃvatsaraṃ daivātkāladharmamupeyivān ||1 2||
mariṣyamāṇaścakre’sau gosahasraṃ yathāvidhi |
mahādānānyapi dadau daśāṣṭau cetarāṇi ca ||1 3||
mṛte’sminnagarī sarvā śuśoca nṛpatiṃ tadā |
aputratvātsahaivāsya patnī sahagatiṃ yayau ||14||
mantriṇau sarva saṃskārānkārayāmāsaturdvijaiḥ |
ekādaśāhe dadadurnānā dānāni bhaktitaḥ ||15||
gate māse mattrayato rājyāhaṃ kulajaṃ naram |
dhīmantaṃ satvasampannaṃ śūraṃ satyaparākramam ||1 6||
durdharṣasya sutaṃ tasya dāyādasya mahābalam |
kṣetrajaṃ sāmyanāmānaṃ rājñāśiṣṭaṃ ca tāvubhau ||17||
amātyau niścayaṃ kṛtvā rājyaṃ dātuṃ tu tasya ha |
paurajānapadān sarvān brāhmaṇān paripṛccha ca ||18||
śubhe muhūrte lagne ca nānā sambhāra saṃcayaiḥ |
kārayāmāsaturubhāvabhiṣekaṃ dvijaiḥ saha ||1 9||
muniruvāca |
kathaṃ kṣetrāt samutpannaḥ kiṃjātīyaḥ pitāmaha |
sa sāmbastanmamācakṣva savaṃjño’si kṛpānidhe ||2 0||
ka uvāca |
durdharṣeṇa kṛtā yatnāḥ putrārthaṃ bahudhā mune |
na ca jātaḥ suputro’sya duṣṭadaivayutasya ha ||2 1 ||
tasya patnyā pramadayā kaivartāsakta cittayā |
janitaḥ sumuhūrte sa na jñāto jārajastviti ||22||
yāvanti rājacihnāni tāvanti dadatuśca tau |
nivedya kośasahitaṃ sarvaṃ rājyaṃ sarāṣṭrakam ||23||
amātya padaniṣṭau tau jātāvāstāṃ yathā purā |
sa rājyaṃ prāpya durddharṣasuto mattaḥ śriyā’bhavat ||24||
strīmāṃsamadirāsakto matto gaja ivālasaḥ |
durācārarato nityaṃ nayamārga parāṅamukhaḥ ||25 ||
ādāya dhanināṃ sarva dhanaṃ tānniravāsayat |
apamānaṃ cakārāśu brāhmaṇānāṃ satāmapi ||2 6||
bahiścakāra tān sarvān vṛttimācchidya sāntatim |
śiṣṭaḥ sma prakṛtī nītiṃ na tadvākyaṃ samādade ||27||
vāraṃvāraṃ yadā tau taṃ śiṣṭa’ sma karuṇāyutau |
amātyau sa babandhāśu nigaḍābhyāṃ ruṣānvitaḥ ||28||
śikṣāpayantau pitarau tasya taṃ durhṛdaṃ sutam |
yat prasādāt tvayā prāptaṃ rājyaṃ nihatakaṃṭakam ||2 9||
vinigrahaṃ kathaṃ sādhyostayostvaṃ kuruṣe khala |
anādṛtya tu tadvākyaṃ pīyūṣamiva pāmara ||30||
śṛṃkhalābhyāṃ nibaddhau tau kārāgāre’dhyavāsayat |
atiduṣṭo duṣṭabuddhināmā’bhūttat sakhā’niśam ||3 1||
tameva sacivaṃ cakre dattvā’śvaṃ kāṃcanaṃ gajam |
ratnāni divyavāsāṃsi rāṃkvāstaraṇāni ca ||32||
dāsī dāsān asaṃkhyātān pānānyannaṃ ca nārikama |
na karotyasya vākyaṃ yastasya chetsye śiro balāt ||33||
ityuktvā sarvalokeṣu sāmbo’ntaḥpuramāyayau ||34||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite saptaviṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||27||

<adhyāya 28 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
sa rājakāryaṃ kurute svayaṃ bhuṃkte varastriyaḥ |
rāṣṭre śṛṇoti rāmāṃso’pati vā sapatiṃ tathā ||1||
balādānayate bhoktuṃ krandatāṃ rudanāmapi |
na vā gaṇayate jñātibhedaṃ viṣayalampaṭaḥ ||2||
yā connatakucā nārī tasya dṛṣṭipathaṃ gatā |
tāmāliṃgyatyasau kāmī cumbatīmāṃyamatyapi ||3||
paradravyāṇi gṛhṇāti striyo’pi sa balāt punaḥ |
so’pi tādṛgguṇo mantrī duṣṭabuddhipathe rata ||4||
evaṃ durācāraratau na kanyāṃ na ca mātaram |
na varjayantaḥ sambhoge bhaginīmapi nirghṛṇo ||5||
na brahmahatyāṃ manuto na strībālavadhaṃ tathā |
evaṃ pāpasamācārau pāmasya parvatāviva ||6||
āstāmubho duṣṭabuddhī rājāmātyau suduḥsahau |
na brāhmaṇo yatirvāpi tadgṛhe yāti bhikṣitum ||7||
na rāṣṭre nāma tasyāpi gṛhṇāti prakṛterapi |
ekadā mṛgayārthaṃ tau yātaḥ sma gahanaṃ vanam ||8||
ghnataḥ sma mṛgayūthāni pakṣiyūthānyanekaśaḥ |
tāni prāpayya nagaramaśvārūḍho svayaṃ punaḥ ||9||
vaināyakaṃ mahāsthānaṃ pathi tau paśyataḥ sma ha |
yasmin virājite mūrtirjīṇā vaināyakī śubhā ||1 0||
sthāpitā rāmapitrā sā putrārthaṃ kurvatā tapaḥ |
bhaktyā sākṣātkaro yatra devadevo vināyakaḥ ||1 1||
daśākṣareṇa mantreṇa dhyāyatā bahu vāsaram |
dattvā tasmai varān devo vidhate vāṃchitānapi |12||
tato vasiṣṭhahastena teneyaṃ sthāpitā dṛḍhā |
varadeti ca nāmāsyā vasiṣṭhena kṛtaṃ tataḥ|
asya darśanato nṛṇāṃ purūṣārthāścaturvidhaḥ |
smaraṇāt pūjanādvāpi setsyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||13||
evaṃ vasiṣṭhavākyena sthānaṃ tadbhuvi paprathe |
gaṇeśabhajanenāsya smaraṇenārcanena ca ||14||
rāmalakṣmaṇabharataḥ śatrughnasahitāḥ sutāḥ |
jātā lokeṣu vikhyātāḥ sarvajñāḥ śūrasaṃmataḥ ||15||
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā mahāramyaṃ prāsādaṃ rājanirmitam |
uttare turaśvapṛṣṭādrājāmātyau pupūjatuḥ ||16||
patraiḥ puṣpairnematustaṃ sarvapāpāharaṃ vibhum |
pradakṣiṇī kṛtya vibhumīyatuḥ kṣaṇamātrataḥ ||17||
etadeva paraṃ puṇyaṃ daivājjātaṃ tayostadā |
evaṃ pāpasamācārau rājyaṃ kṛtvā’tha mamṛtuḥ ||18||
badhvā pāśairyāmyadūtairnītau tau śamanāntikam |
citraguptaṃ samāhūya papraccha sa śubhāśubham ||19||
tenoktaṃ na tayorasti puṇyaleśo raveḥ suta |
pāpānāṃ gaṇanā nāsti dūtāṃścaivedamabravīt ||2 0||
badhyatāṃ badhyatāmetau lohadaṃḍena pātyatām |
kuṃḍe vīcimaye paṃkti sahasraparivatsarān ||21||
ekaṃ kasmin krameṇaitau kuṃḍe bhuktvā’ghasaṃcayān |
kṣipyatāṃ mṛtyuloke ca nīcayoni gatāvubhau ||22||
anayoḥ puṇyaleśo’sti dūtāḥ śṛṇuta tatkṛtim |
prasaṃgādarcito dṛṣṭo deva ābhyāṃ gajānanaḥ ||23||
tena puṇyena so’pyetā vuddhariṣyatī tatra vai |
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ dūtairbaddhau hatau dṛḍham ||24||
kumbhīpāke śoṇitode niraye raurave’pi tau |
nikṣiptau kālakūṭe ca kramaśaḥ śatavatsarān ||25||
tāmisre cāndhatāmisre pūyaśoṇita kardame |
kaṃṭake tu kṣatāṃgau tau saṃtaptā taptavāluke ||26||
vikṣatau kṛmibhistatra bhṛśaṃ sūcīmukhairubhau |
asipatravane ghore tato nītāvubhāvapi ||27||
yatra śastrābhi ghātena varṣma bhidyati tāpinām |
tatastaptaśilāyāṃ tau niṣpiṣṭau ghanaghātataḥ ||28||
bhuṃjaṃtau narakānekaviṃśatiṃ bahuvāsaram |
na duḥkhaṃ śakyate vaktuṃ śeṣeṇāśeṣatastayoḥ ||29||
evaṃ kṛti sahastrāṇi bhuktvā bhogānanekaśaḥ |
nistīrṇabhogau tau pāpaśeṣeṇa bhuvamāgatau ||3 0||
eko jātaḥ kākayonau kauśiko’bhūtparo’pi ca |
tata eko darduro’bhūdaparaḥ saraṭo’bhavat ||3 1||
tata eko viṣadharo’paro’bhūd vṛściko’pi ca |
tatrāpi kurvataḥ pāpaṃ nānālokānvidaṃśataḥ ||32||
śvamārjārayoryonau jātau nakula sūkarau |
vṛkajambūkayoryonau jātau ghoṭakagardabhau ||33||
tata uṣṭra gajau jātau tato nakra mahājhaṣau |
tato vyāghramṛgau jātau tato vṛṣabha māhiṣau ||34||
evaṃ nānāyonigatau jātau cāṃḍālakīṭakau |
rākṣasībhillayoryonau tataścānte samīyatuḥ ||35||
piṃgākṣo durbuddhiriti nāmnā khyātau ca bhūtale |
ājanmakṛtapāpasya kathyate bahudoṣatā ||36||
yadā tau rājaprakṛtī āstāṃ pāparato purā |
ekaṃ puṇyaṃ tayorāsīn mṛgayāyāṃ prasarpatoḥ ||37||
vināyakasya namanaṃ darśanaṃ ca pradakṣiṇā |
arcanaṃ phalapuṣpādyaistuṣṭa āsīd gajānanaḥ ||38||
agādhaṃ tattayoḥ puṇyamāsīttajjanma saṃcitam |
yadā purā rākṣaso’sau bhīmaṃ bhakṣitumāgataḥ ||39||
sa ārūḍhaḥ śamīvṛkṣaṃ tatpatrāṇi ca mastake |
patitāni gaṇeśasya tuṣṭo’bhūtsa dvayorapi ||40||
divyadehaṃ tayordattvā svarlokaṃ prāprayadvibhuḥ |
etatte kathitaṃ pūrva janmakarma mayā tayoḥ ||41||
śamīpatrārcanādeva tuṣṭo’bhūtsa gajānanaḥ |
yathā’yaṃ sthāpitastena vāmanena tadapyaham||42||
kathayāmi mune sarvaṃ tadanuṣṭhānameva ca |
yathā ca gaṇanātho’sya prasanno varado bhavat ||43||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite bhīmarākṣasayorjanmavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭāviṃśatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||28||

<adhyāya 29 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca ||
mayājñaptaḥ kaśyapo’sau sṛṣṭiṃ nānāvidhāṃ kuru | 
savinīto mahājñānī bhūtabhaviṣyavit ||1||
ativaiśvānararavitejāḥ sutapasāṃ nidhiḥ | 
sṛṣṭisāmarthyasaṃprāptau tatāpa sumahattapaḥ ||2||
ṣaḍakṣareṇa mantreṇa dhyātvā devaṃ gajānanam | 
divyavarṣasahasrānte sa devo varado’bhavat ||3||
īpsitānasya sarvānsa dadau nānāvarāñśubhān | 
tato’sau manasā yadyadakalpayata lāghavāt ||4||
tattadagre paśyati sma varadānaprabhāvataḥ | 
caturdaśasu patnīṣu ṛtudānādanekadhā ||5||
cakāra tejasā sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam | 
tāsu śreṣṭhe ca dve patnyau dityaditīti nāmataḥ ||6||
adityā’pi tapastaptaṃ viṣṇuprīṇanahetave | 
saṃvatsarāṇāmayutaṃ tatastuṣṭo janārdanaḥ ||7||
jagatyāḥ pālanāyāsau dharmasaṃsthāpanāya ca | 
śakrāditatsutānāṃ ca duṣṭānāṃ nigrahāya ca ||8||
putratāmagamattasyāstayā pūrvaṃ prasāditaḥ | 
atha sā svāminaṃ prāha ditirhomāya sādaram||9||
ṛtuṃ me dehi bhagavanbādhate madanānalaḥ | 
ityākarṇya vacastasyā munistāṃ prāha sādaram ||10||
ayaṃ kālo ghorarūpo homakāla upasthitaḥ| 
kṣaṇamācara dhairyaṃ tvaṃ paścādraṃsye tvayā saha ||11||
asminkāle bhavedduṣṭamapatyaṃ devadharmadhruk | 
iti svāmivacaḥ śrutvā haste dhṛtvā nanāma tam ||12||
uvāca raktanayanā dagdhukāmā jagattrayam | 
idānīmeva me vāñchā na kṛtā cettvayā mune ||13||
dehatyāgaṃ kariṣyāmi vihvalā madanāgninā ||
yathā tathāsthāṃ daivānme apatyaṃ devadharmadhruk||14||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā śaraṇāgatavatsalaḥ| 
dadāvṛtuṃ tadā tasya homaṃ snātvā cakāra ha ||15||
tataścāsyāḥ suto jajñe hiraṇyākṣo mahābalaḥ | 
hiraṇyakaśipuścobhau ceratustapa utkaṭam ||16||
pañcākṣaraṃ mahāmaṃtraṃ pitustau bahuvatsaram | 
ekāṅguṣṭhasthitau vāyumātrabhakṣau savalmikau ||17||
athāyayau mahādevo varaṃ dātuṃ kṛpānidhi| 
tuṣṭuvatustatastaṃ tau bhaktyā devaṃ trilocanam ||18||
kṛtāñjalipuṭau cobhau praṇipātapuraḥsaram | 
yathāśakti yathājñānaṃ taddarśanasphuranmatī ||19||
yo devo vṛṣavāhano daśabhujaścandrārddhanāgānvito 
nānārūpagaṇairgirīndratanayāyukto jagatkāraṇam | 
yo vyāghrājinakṛttimāṃstrinayano 
yaḥ śūladhṛkvāmahā bhaktecchāparipūraṇaikanilayo dharmārthamokṣapradaḥ ||20||
iti stutvā mahādevaṃ sarvavyāpinamīśvaram | 
namaścacakraturdevaṃ bhaktavāñchāsuradrumam ||21||
tāvuvāca tatastuṣṭo vṛṇutaṃ varamityasau | 
vṛṇutastau varāndevādamṛtiṃ yakṣarakṣasaḥ ||22||
kinnarairmānuṣaśreṣṭhaḥ piśācaiścāraṇādibhiḥ | 
na śastrairnārdraśuṣkaiśca jantubhirjalajairapi ||23||
sthāvarairjaṅmairanyairna divā na niśāsu ca | 
na pṛthivyāṃ na cākāśe nāntarāle tathoṣasi ||24||
tatheti tāvuvāceśastathā cāntardadhe haraḥ | 
tadaiva tāvajayatāṃ rodasī ca rasātalam ||25||
devasthānāni sarvāṇi balādākramya tau sthitau | 
hiraṇyakaśiporjātaḥ prahlādo nāma nāmataḥ ||26||
tanayo viṣṇubhakteṣu garīyānvaṃśakaṇṭakaḥ| 
japannārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ pitrā saṃtāḍito bhṛśam ||27||
anekayātanāyāṃ sa rakṣito’nekadhā’munā | 
jale sthale viṣādagnerviṣṇunā karuṇāvatā ||28||
hiraṇyakaśipuḥ sāyaṃ sarvalokabhayaṃkaraḥ | 
nārasiṃhena vapuṣā nakharaiḥ sa vidāritaḥ ||29||
vārāheṇa hataḥ pūrvo daṃṣṭrayā vasudhāhṛtā | 
virocanaḥ sutastasya balistasyābhavatsutaḥ ||30||
virocanaḥ sūryabhakto mukuṭaṃ prāptavāṃstataḥ | 
ūcivāṃstaṃ ca saptāśvaḥ parahasto yadā spṛśyet ||31||
mukuṭasya tadā dhvaṃso bhavitā nātra saṃśayaḥ | 
sa sūryavaradānena cakre tribhuvanaṃ vaśe ||32||
cyutasthānāstato devā acyutaṃ śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ | 
sa cintāṃ mahatīṃ kṛtvā niścayaṃ nādhyagacchata ||33||
tatastena dhṛtaṃ rūpaṃ sarvayoṣidvaraṃ striyaḥ | 
tena taṃ ramayāmāsa bahukālaṃ virocanam ||34||
tataḥ sa surataṃ kartumudyato’bhūtkubuddhimān | 
tatastaṃ sudatī prāha mamaikā vāsanā’sti bhoḥ ||35||
abhyaṃgaṃ kuru pūrvaṃ tvaṃ tato hi raṃsyase mayā | 
iti śrutvā sa tadvākyaṃ kāmāsakto’nvamodata ||36||
uttārya mukuṭaṃ sātha mūrdhni tailaṃ vyamardayat | 
sa tadā śatacūrṇo’bhūttatsārūpyaṃ sa āptavān ||37||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite virocanavadho nāmaikonatriṃśo’dhyāyaḥ ||29||

<adhyāya 30 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
balistu haribhaktatvānna kiñcidanvatapyata | 
vedavedāṅgavitprājñaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ ||1||
paranindāparadravyaparadrohaparāṅmukhaḥ| 
dātā yajvā mānayitā mānyānāṃ paramādarāt ||2||
gacchanvadansvapanbhuñjaṃstiṣṭhannapi japanpiban | 
dhyāyati sma hariṃ nityaṃ bhaktyā saṃsthiracetasā ||3||
ekadā śukramāhūya paripūjya yathāvidhi | 
papraccha taṃ balirnītiṃ śakro bhuṅktte’marāvatīm ||4||
kathaṃ ca bhāgo nāsmākaṃ bhrātṛtve satataṃ sthite | 
iti me saṃśayaṃ prājña chettumarhasyaśeṣataḥ ||5||
śukra uvāca | 
tava pūrvairetadarthaṃ kṛtaṃ vairaṃ nirantaram | 
suraiḥ sārddhaṃ mahābhāga te sarve nāśamāpitāḥ ||6||
devairviṣṇusahāyaistu nānāmāyābalāśrayāt | 
tyaktvā vairaṃ bhavānsādhu yajatāṃ śatasaṃkhyayā ||7||
tatpuṇyasya balādaindraṃ padaṃ pūrṇamavāpsyasi| 
baliruvāca | 
samyagukttaṃ tvayā brahmannītiśāstraviśārada ||8||
sāmno dānavidherbhedājjaghanyo nigraho mataḥ | 
so’pi puṇyabalātsidhyettasmātpuṇyaṃ dṛḍhaṃ matam ||9||
ka uvāca | 
evaṃ sa niścayaṃ kṛtvā vasiṣṭhādīnsamāhṛyat | 
bhṛgvārdīśca munīnpūjya sumuhūrte samārabhat ||10||
yajñaṃ mahāsamārambhaṃ sarvānandakaraṃ param | 
sarve te yajñakuṇḍāni prācīsādhanapūrvakam ||11||
cakruśca kārayāmāsuryajñasambhārasaṃcayān | 
vedikāmaṇḍapādīṃśca bhūmiśodhanapūrvakam ||12||
kṛtvā’bhyudayikaṃ śrāddhaṃ svastivācanapūrvakam | 
mātṝṇāṃ pūjanaṃ kṛtvā sthāpayāmāsurādarāt | 
mantrairnānāvidhairviprāḥ sarvā maṇḍapadevatāḥ ||13||
rejāte dampatī tatra śvetavastrāvalaḍ||kṛtau | 
amātyarājasuhṛdāṃ paurāṇāṃ ca gaṇairvṛtau ||14||
sammānitāḥ striyo’paśyangavākṣastaṃ mahotsavam | 
anvārabdhā brāhmaṇāste pūrvāgḍaāni nyavartayan ||15||
ālabhanta paśuṃ vedakalpavākyānusārataḥ | 
tattaddevāya tanmantrairjuhṛti sma vidhānataḥ ||16|| 
yajñavāṭe caturdvāraṃ sarveṣāmanivārite| 
āyānti yānti sarve’pi pūjitāstrividhā janāḥ ||17||
vivadante mahāvādairekato viduṣāṃ janāḥ | 
nṛtyantyapsaraso’nyatra paṭhante vaidikāḥ kṛtaḥ ||18||
gāyanti vaiṣṇavāḥ śaivā mṛdaṅagatālavādanaiḥ | 
bhuṃjate brāhmaṇāḥ svecchā bhojanaṃ ṣaḍrasaiḥ kvacit ||19||
kathayanti kathā nānākathāḥ śṛṇvanti modakāḥ| 
evaṃ suvitate yajñe vasiṣṭhādyā maharṣayaḥ ||20||
vasordhārāṃ sumahatīṃ pātayāmāsuragniṣu | 
uttarāṃṅgāni saṃpādya rathe tau sthāpya dampatī ||21||
kartuṃ cāvabhṛthasnānaṃ sarve lokā viniryayuḥ | 
nānāvāditra nirghoṣairvarabandīravairapi ||22||
vedaghoṣairbahuvidhaiḥ sāmagānairharipriyaiḥ | 
snātvā’’gatya hariṃ so’tha toṣayāmāsa tāndvijāna ||23||anekaratnanicayairdhanairvastrairanekaśaḥ | 
gobhiraśvairgajairgandhairicchāviṣayapūraṇaiḥ ||24||
ekonaśatamevaṃ te tasya yajñānapārayan | 
śate yajñe samārabdhe śakraścintāṃ parāṃ yayau ||25||
kanīyāṃsaṃ sa śaraṇaṃ svapadabhraṃśaśaṅakayā| 
kṣīrasāgaramadhyasthaṃ śeṣaśāyinamīśvarama ||26||
mayā saṃvāhitapadaṃ sevyamānaṃ surairapi| 
gīyamānaṃ nāradena vīṇāhastena sādarama ||27||
tumburupramukhairanyairapsarogaṇakinnaraiḥ 
astaudasau harirdṛṣṭvā taṃ hariṃ sveṣṭasiddhaye| 
praṇamya sampuṭaṃ kṛtvā pāṇyorbhāvena sādaram ||28||
śakra uvāca | 
namo’nantaśakte namo viśvayone namo viśvabhartre namo viśvakartre | 
namo daityahantre namo’nekarūpa namaḥ saktacittārthadāyāstu nityam ||29||
ka uvāca | 
evaṃ stutvā’bravīdenaṃ vāsavo baliceṣṭitam | 
balirvirocanasuto vikhyāto bhuvanatraye ||30||
grahīṣyati mama padaṃ makhācchataguṇādayam | 
yāvatasya bhavedvāṇī sarvaṃ jñātaṃ tu viṣṇunā ||31||
tata ūce sa bhagavānindraṃ saṃkaṭacintayā | 
mama bhakto baliḥ śakra tapasvī vijitendriyaḥ ||31||
tapasaḥ phalametena bhoktavyaṃ śrutivākyataḥ| 
na jāyate vṛthā karma śubhaṃ vā yadi vā’śubham ||33||
sādhayiṣye bhavatkāryaṃ sthirī kuru mano hare | 
tataḥ sa garbhatāṃ pede adityā jaṭhare vibhuḥ ||34||
suṣūve navame māsi sā’ditistanayaṃ śubham| 
prabhayā tasya te dīpā niṣprabhatvamupāgatan ||35||
snātvā śītajalenāśu jātakarmaṃ yathāvidhi | 
cakāra kaśyapo dhīmānsvastivācanapūrvakam ||36||
kṛtvā pitramahāyajñaṃ mātṛpūjanapūrvakam | 
dadarśa bālakaṃ paścātprāśayitvā ghṛtaṃ madhu ||37||
hrasvaṃ pṛthulamūrdhānaṃ laghupādaṃ bṛhattanum | 
divyajñānasamāyuktaṃ divyaṃ dehaṃ caturbhujam ||38||
nānālaṃkārasaṃyuktaṃ jñātvā viṣṇumathānamat | 
uvāca ca prasannātmā tapo vaṃśo’dya cakṣuṣī ||39||
jñānaṃ tathā’’śramo dhanyo dharā svargo rasātalam | 
dhanyastaddarśanādete saccidānandarūpiṇaḥ ||40||
ka uvāca | 
evamākarṇya tadvākyamuvāca jagadīśvaraḥ| 
adityāstapasā tuṣṭaḥ putratvamahamāgataḥ ||41||
hartuṃ bhūbhāramatulaṃ jyeṣṭhakāryaṃ ca sādhitum| 
ityuktvā prākṛto bhūtvā’rudatstanyaṃ papāvatha ||42||
nāmaniṣkramaṇaṃ karma cakre’nnaprāśanaṃ muniḥ | 
tṛtīye caulamabde sa paṃcame vratabandhanam ||43||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite vāmanāvatārakathanaṃ nāma triṃśo’dhyāyaḥ ||30||

<adhyāya 31 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
vedāṃśca caturaḥ sāṅgānadhyāpayata taṃ muniḥ | 
ekasmindivase tātaṃ vāmanaḥ paryapṛcchata ||1||
kenopāyena devānāṃ padaprāptirbhaviṣyati | 
bhūbhāraharaṇaṃ tanme yathāvadvaktumarhasi ||2||
kaśyapa uvāca | 
upadeśaṃ kariṣyāmi mantrasya siddhidasya te | 
ṣaḍakṣarasya vighneśatoṣakasya mamātmaja ||3||
tasminprasanne vighneśe jagatsthityantakāriṇi | 
anekabrahmāṃḍavidhau sarvakāraṇakāraṇe ||4||
sarve kāmāḥ prasidhyanti tadarthe yatnamācara | 
ka uvāca | 
evamuktvā dadau tasmai sumuhurte mahāmanum ||5||
tadaiva calitastasmādāśramāddharṣasaṃyutaḥ | 
praṇamya tamanujñāpya jagāma tapase śiśuḥ ||6||
bhramatā dadṛśe tena vidarbhe sthānamuttamam | 
latāvṛkṣasamākīrṇaṃ kāsārapariśobhitam ||7||
tatra padmāsanaṃ kṛtvā jajāpa taṃ manuṃ śubham | 
ṣaḍakṣaraṃ varṣamātraṃ nirāhāro jitendriyaḥ ||8||
nirvāṇaṃ tasya tajjñātvā’’virāsītsiddhibuddhiyuk ||9||
mayūravāhano devaḥ śuṇḍādaṇḍavirājitaḥ | 
vilasaddaśadordaṇḍo ratnamālā vibhūṣitaḥ ||10||
āśīviṣalasannābhirnānālaṃkāraśobhitaḥ| 
āgato dṛḍhabhaktaṃ taṃ dhenurvatsamivādarāt ||11||
vāmanastaṃ puro dṛṣṭvā sarvatejobhibhāvinam | 
sarvasiddhikaraṃ devaṃ sarvavighnanivāraṇam ||12||
nunāva parayā bhaktyā devadevaṃ gajānanam ||13||
vāmana uvāca | 
avyaktaṃ vyaktahetuṃ nigamanutanuṃ sarvadevādhidevaṃ 
brahmāṇḍānāmadhīśaṃ jagadudayakaraṃ sarvavedāntavedyam | 
māyātītaṃ svavedyaṃ sthitivilayakaraṃ sarvavidyānidhānaṃ 
sarveśaṃ sarvarūpaṃ sakalabhayaharaṃ kāmadaṃ kāṃntarūpam ||14||
taṃ vande vighnarājaṃ vidhiharamunibhiḥ sevyamānaṃ 
sanāgaistejorāśiṃ trisatyaṃ triguṇavirahitaṃ tattvamasyādibodhyam | 
bhaktecchopāttadehaṃ nijajanasukhadaṃ tattvabuddhiprakāśaṃ 
sāmbādyaiḥ stūyamānaṃ sakalatanugataṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ tam ||15||
ka uvāca | 
iti stuto devadevaḥ prasannaḥ prāha vāmanam | 
varaṃ varaya tuṣṭo’yaṃ yatte manasi vartate ||16||
stotreṇa tapasā caiva parituṣṭo dadāmi tat | 
vāmana uvāca | 
na te svarūpaṃ vidurabjajādyā brahmarṣayo ye sanakādikādyāḥ | 
dṛṣṭo’si sa tvaṃ jagatāmadhīśa tvattaḥ kamanyaṃ varamadya yāce ||17||
athāpi te vākyabhidā bhayāttvāṃ yāce varaṃ taṃ mama nātha dehi | 
parājayo me na bhavetkadāpi kārye’khile vighnabhayaṃ na ca syāt ||18||
udyato’sti baliḥ śakrapadamattuṃ kratorbalāt | 
sa ca māṃ śaraṇaṃ yātastasya siddhiryathā bhavet ||19||
tathā kuru jagatkartaryadi tuṣṭo’si me’nagha | 
gajānana uvāca | 
etatte bhavitā sarvaṃ smaraṇānmama suvrata ||20||
setsyanti sarvakāryāṇi mahānti ca laghūni ca | 
ka uvāca | 
evamuktvā gate deve vāmano’kārayacchubhām | 
kāśmīropalajāṃ so’tha sthāpayanmūrtimuttamām ||21||
caturbhujāṃ trinayanāṃ śuṇḍādaṇḍavirājitām | 
prasannaṃkarihastābhyāṃ bhaktānāmabhayapradām ||22||smaraṇāddarśanāddhayānātpūjanātsarvakāmadām | 
prāsādaṃ kārayāmāsa ratnakāṃcananirmitam ||23||
tatraikaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ sthāpya grāmaṃ datvā dhanāni ca | 
trikālaṃ pūjane tasya kārayāmāsa tena saḥ ||24||
āgataḥ svagṛhaṃ natvā pitaraṃ sarvamabravīt | 
tapaśca tatphalaṃ cātha yajñaṃ gantumapṛcchata ||25||
kaśyapenābhyanujñāto vāmano darbhadaṇḍabhṛt | 
sūtrakṛṣṇājinadharo mekhalī yajñamāyayau ||26||
hrasvākṛtiṃ muniṃ dṛṣṭvā munayo vismitāstadā| 
nāyaṃ muniryatastejo dṛśyate paramādbhutam ||27||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā paramāyāntaṃ balirutthāya satvaram | 
ko’si tvamiti papraccha natvā taṃ kuta āgataḥ ||28||
kiṃ vāṃcchasi vada vibho pitā mātā ca kastava | 
vasatistava kutrāsti tata ūce sa vāmanaḥ ||29||
na smare pitarau rājannanāthaṃ viddhi māṃ kṛśam | 
trailokye vasatirme’sti yāce bhūmiṃ tribhiḥ padaiḥ ||30||
mitāṃ kartuṃ parṇakuṭīṃ śaktiścedasti dīyatām | 
ka uvāca | 
iti vākyaṃ muneḥ śrutvā hradi jñātvā kṛpāyutam ||31||
taṃ dātumudyataṃ bhūmimūce kāvyo baliṃ tadā | 
śukra uvāca | 
na brāhmaṇo harirayaṃ kapaṭaṃ rūpamāsthitaḥ ||32||
hariṣyati tribhuvanaṃ padatrayamiṣeṇa tu | 
mā dīyatāmasya kiñciditi satyaṃ bravīmi te ||33||
tamuvāca mahādaityo mūḍha kiṃ vadase vacaḥ | 
hariścedvimukho gacchetsarvaṃ me sukṛtaṃ nayet ||34||
asmātpātraṃ kimanyatsyādasmai dattamanantakam | 
ityuktvā taṃ punaḥ prāha vāmanaṃ daityapuṃgava ||35||
brahmandattā mayā bhūmiḥ saṃkalpaḥ kriyatāmiti | 
vāmanaḥ prāha rājānaṃ saṃkalpayitumudyataḥ ||36||
tāvaddhārāṃ nirudhyaiva sthitaḥ śakro’nyadehataḥ | 
dadau śalākāṃ tatpātre bhagnanetro bahirgataḥ ||37||
tataḥ pāṇitale tasya vāmanasya jalaṃ dadau | 
smṛtvā gaṇeśaṃ vavṛdhe vāmano harṣanirbharaḥ ||38||
mūrdhnyākramya svargalokamekapādena rodasī | 
pātālānyapareṇāpi dattaṃ tanmastake’param ||39||
tata ūce baliṃ devo gaccha tvaṃ dharaṇītalam | 
tvāṃ vinā’haṃ kathaṃ yāmītyevamāha balistu tam ||40||
ityākarṇya vacastasya punastaṃ prāha vāmanaḥ | 
sānnidhyaṃ mama tatrāpi bhavitā tvadanugrahāt ||41||
indro māṃ śaraṇaṃ yātaḥ priyaṃ kāryaṃ hi tasya me | 
tavāpi mama bhaktasya priyaṃ paścādbhaviṣyati ||42||
aindre pade nivṛtte’sya padaṃ yāsye tavaiva tat | 
tato brahmādayo devāstuṣṭuvurvāmanaṃ vibhum ||43||
mumucuḥ puṣpavarṣāṇi vādyāni vavaduḥ surāḥ | 
pupūjuste’pi sarve ca jaguśca nanṛtuḥ pare ||44||
tataścāntardadhe devo vāmano’nantavikramaḥ | 
svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ gatā devā yathāpūrvaṃ mudānvitāḥ ||45||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ tena kṛtā mūrtirvikhyātā’bhūjjagattraye | 
gajānanasya devasya sarveṣāṃ kāmadā nṛṇām ||46||
ityetatkathitaṃ sarvaṃ prasaṅagādbaliceṣṭitam | 
lāghavaṃ vāmanasyāpi mahimā sumukhasya ha ||47||
adoṣākhye pure tena sthāpitasya mahātmanā | 
daśabāhoḥ prasiddhasya devadakṣiṇabhāgataḥ ||48||
priyatvaṃ tasya śamyāste kathayiṣye’dhunā sphuṭam ||49||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite ekatriṃśo’dhyāyaḥ ||31||

<adhyāya 32 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
śṛṇuśvāvahito brahmannitihāsaṃ purātanam| 
priyavratāyākathayatsvayameva gajānanaḥ ||1||
gajānana uvāca | 
śamīpatrasya mahimā śaṇuṣvārya mahābalam | 
na yajñairna ca dānaiśca vrataḥ koṭiśatairapi ||2||
na japaiḥ pūjanairvāpi mama toṣastathā bhavet | 
na padmairnānyakusumaiḥ śamīpatrairyathā bhavet ||3||
śamīti kīrtanādeva pāpaṃ naśyati vācikam | 
smaraṇānmānasaṃ pāpaṃ sparśanātkāyajaṃ tathā ||4||
nityaṃ tatpūjanāddhyānādvandanāccaiva bhaktitaḥ | 
nirvighnakaramāyuṣyaṃ jñānaṃ pāpakṣayo’pi ca ||5||
vāñchāsiddhiracāpalyaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ | 
muniruvāca | 
ko’sau priyavrato rājā kiṃśīlaḥ kutra vā’bhavat ||6||
kathaṃ vai tasya kathitā guṇāḥ śamyā mahātmanā | 
enaṃ me saṃśayaṃ brahmankṛpayā nuda suvrata ||7||
ka uvāca | 
atrāpyudāharantīmamitihāsaṃ purātanam | 
śaṃkarasya ca saṃvādaṃ pārvatyā saha putraka ||8||
pārvatyuvāca | 
kathaṃ śamī priyā deva sarvajña jagadīśvara | 
gajānanasya me śaṃsa yadi te mayyanugrahaḥ ||9||
śaṃkara uvāca | 
śṛṇu priye pravakṣyāmi kathāmekāṃ śucismite | 
yathā priyā gaṇeśasya śamīti jñāsyate tathā ||10||
priyavrato nāma rājā pūrvamāsīnmahāmatiḥ | 
satyavāndharmasaṃpanno dharmātmā śaṃsitavrataḥ ||11||
tejasvī ca vadānyaśca vedaśāstrārthatattvavit | 
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannaścaturaṅgabalādhikaḥ ||12||
pālayāmāsa pṛthivīṃ prajāḥ putrānivaurasān | 
kīrtiśca prathamaṃ bhāryā prabhā tasyāparā’bhavat ||13||
dhūrtaśca kuśalastasya prakṛtī saṃbabhūvatuḥ | 
kuśalau nītiśāstreṣu ṣaḍānanaparākramau ||14||
buddhyā parākrameṇāpi rasā sarvā vaśīkṛtā | 
sa tu rājā vaśaṃ nītaḥ prabhayā tasya bhāryayā ||15||
krīḍate’harniśaṃ so’tha nānālaṃkāraśobhayā | 
yauvanākrāntavapuṣā rambhādibhyo’tiramyayā ||16||
kṣaṇamātraṃ na sahate viyogaṃ sa tayā saha | 
dhikkaroti sadā jyeṣṭhāṃ tadvaco na śṛṇotyapi ||17||
ruṣā nirīkṣeta tāṃ sa tanmṛtiṃ cintayatyapi| 
svayaṃ na bhāṣate kiñcinna gṛhṇāti tayā’rpitam ||18||
tatastasyābhavatputraḥ prabhayāṃ patisannibhaḥ | 
jātakarma ca rājā’sya kṛtvā dharmasanekadhā ||19||
nāma cāsyākarotpadmanābhiritthaṃ dvijeritam | 
śuklapakṣe yathā candro vavṛdhe so’pi bālakaḥ ||20||
buddhimānkuśalo yuddhe pitṛto’pyadhiko’bhavat | 
paṃcame vratabandhaṃ ca cakārāsya nṛpastadā ||21||
priyavrato mahābuddhirmadrāṇāmadhipo balī | 
pāṃcālarājakanyāṃ sa bhāryāṃ tasyāpyamelayat ||22||
ativaibhavayuktau tau vivāhaṃ cakratustayoḥ | 
kadācidasya sā patnī jyeṣṭhā dāsītvamāpitā ||23||
pādasaṃvāhanaṃ kartumāgatā bhartṛsannidhim | 
nipapāta mahīpṛṣṭhe sapatnyā caraṇāhatā ||24||
duḥkhitā rudatī hrīṇā jagāma nijamandiram | 
kamahaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāyāṃ ko me duḥkhaṃ vyapohayet||25||
sapatnyā’mānitāyā me trātā syāddhariravyayaḥ | 
smṛtamātrastu yastrātā draupadyā karuṇānidhiḥ ||26||
yāṃ naiva manute bhartā na ko’pi manute’pi tām | 
ato na jīvitenārthaḥ śoṣayiṣye kalevaram ||27||
pibe hālāhalaṃ vāpi pate vāpīṃ jalānvitām| 
na mano niścayaṃ yāti tasyā vihvalacetasā ||28||
etasminnantare daivāddevalo brāhmaṇottamaḥ | 
purohitaḥ samāyātaḥ sa jagāda gajānanīm ||29||
upāsanāṃ kleśahantrīṃ sarvakāmaphalapradām | 
sā tadaiva prapadyāśu mūrtiṃ mandāranirmitām ||30||
gajānanasya saṃpūjya snātvā śuddhadine tu sā| 
upaviṣṭā pūjayituṃ parameṇa samādhinā ||31||
tyaktvā haraṃ hariṃ cāmbāṃ tejoyuktaṃ paraṃ ravim | 
sarvaiḥ saṃpūjyate devadeva ādau gajānanaḥ ||32||
vicāryamāṇe paṃcānāṃ bhedo naivopalabhyate | 
yo bhedenekṣate caitānsa yāti narakānnaraḥ ||33||
eka eva parānandapūrṇo’sau parameśvaraḥ | 
svecchayā paṃcadhā jāto lokānugrahakāraṇāt | 
eka eva pumānyadvatputramātulanāmabhiḥ ||34||
sā samyak ṣoḍaśabhistamupacāraiḥ pupūja ha| 
dūrvāpuṣpairdakṣiṇābhirnamaskārairanekadhā||35||
kṛtvā karapuṭaṃ sā tu tuṣṭāva jagadīśvaram| 
kīrtiruvāca | 
tvameva jagadādhārastvameva sarvakāraṇam ||36||
tvameva brahmā viṣṇuśca bṛhadbhānustvameva hi| 
candro yamo vaiśravaṇo varuṇo vāyureva ca ||37||
tvameva sāgarā nadyo latākusumasaṃhatiḥ | 
sukhaṃ dukhaṃ tayorhetustvaṃ nāśastvaṃ vimocakaḥ ||38||
iṣṭavighnakaro nityaṃ mahāvighnakaro virāṭ | 
tvameva putralakṣmīdastvameva sarvakāmadhuk ||39||
tvameva viśvayoniśca tvameva viśvahārakaḥ | 
tvameva prakṛtistvaṃ vai puruṣo nirguṇo mahān ||40||
tvameva śaśirūpeṇa sarvamāpyāyase jagat | 
tvameva bhūtabhavyaṃ ca bhāvibhāvātmakaḥ svarāṭ ||41||
śaraṇyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ tāpanaḥ paraḥ | 
karmakāṇḍaparo yajvā jñānakāṇḍaparaḥ śuciḥ ||42||
sarvavettā sarvavidhiḥ sarvasākṣī ca sarvagaḥ | 
sarvavyāpī sarvaviṣṇuḥ sarvasatyamayaḥ prabhuḥ ||43||
sarvamāyāmayaḥ sarvamantratantravidhānavit ||44||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite nāma dvāviṃśo’dhyāyaḥ ||32||

<adhyāya 33 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
evaṃ sā’nudinaṃ pūjya tuṣṭāva taṃ vināyakam | 
ekasmindivase tasyāḥ sakhyo dūrvārthamāyayuḥ ||1 ||
śukramāsavaśānnāpuḥ kvāpi dūrvāṃkurānśubhān | 
śamīpatrāṇi bhūyāṃsi gṛhītvā tu tadantikam ||2||
āyayuḥ paramaśrāntā dūrvābhāvaṃ nyavedayan | 
śamīpatraiḥ pupūjainaṃ sarvopacārapūrvakam ||3||
dūrvāṃ vinā nirāhārā’tiṣṭhatsā niyame ratā | 
tasya tuṣṭiḥ parā jātā śamīpatraiḥ samarpitaiḥ ||4||
aśeta tatpuraḥ svapnamapaśyatsā mahādbhutam | 
abdānte saiva mūrtistāmimāṃ vācamuvāca ha | ||5||
varaṃ dadāmi te subhru śamīpatraiḥ sutoṣitaḥ | 
sā novāca yadā kiṃcitkīrtiḥ sā tāṃ punarjagau ||6||
mūrtiruvāca | 
vaśyaste bhavitā kānto bhogāśvānekaśaḥ śubhāḥ | 
tvadbhaktinirataḥ putro bhaviṣyati mahābalaḥ ||7||
kṣipraprasādanetyevaṃ kuru nāmāsya sundaram | 
turye’bde’sya viṣānmṛtyurbhaviṣyati punaḥ kṣaṇāt ||8||
gṛtsamado dvijobhe’tyaṃ punastaṃ jīvayiṣyati | 
rājyakartā cirāyuśca dharmaśīlo bhaviṣyati ||9||
idaṃ stotraṃ paṭhedyastu mama saṃtoṣakāraṇam | 
tasya vaśyo bhavedrājā kimanyaḥ prākṛto janaḥ | 1 0||
putravāndhanasaṃpanno vedavedāṅapāragaḥ | 
buddhiśca vardhate tasya medhāvṛddhirbhaveddṛḍhā ||11 ||
vācchitāṃllabhate so’rthāṃstrisandhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhedidam | 
mama bhaktirdṛḍhā tasya mokṣaścānte bhaviṣyati ||1 2||
evaṃ dattvā varānsvapne sa devo’pidadhe kṣaṇāt | 
jāgṛtā sāśrunayanā vismitā mānase’vadat ||1 3||
dīnānāthena mahatī kṛpā mayi niyojitā | 
anugraho mahānmahyaṃ yaddattaṃ darśanaṃ svayam | | 14||
vināyakena devena pāvitā paramādarāt | 
adyāhaṃ tena devena sarvabhūtānukampinā ||15||
iyameva parā siddhiridameva tapaḥphalam | 
ayameva paro lābho yadavocanmayā saha ||16||
ka uvāca | 
tataḥ prabhāte snātā sā paramānandanirbharā | 
pupūja parayā bhaktyā gaṇeśaṃ varadaṃ śubham ||17||
samāpya niyamaṃ mūrtiṃ pūrvasthāne nyaveśayat ||18||
purohitaṃ samānīya pūjayāmāsa bhaktitaḥ | 
gaṇeśaṃ manasā dhyātvā vadantī nāmamālikām ||1 9||
smarantī tadvarānkīrtiḥ samayaṃ ca pratīkṣatī | 
tataḥ kālena mahatā prabhā sā niṣprabhā’bhavat ||20||
raktapittavatī jātā prārabdhādīśvarecchayā | 
karau pādau ca nāsā ca tasyāḥ kṣarati nityaśaḥ ||21||
atibībhatsarūpāṃ tāṃ tatyāja manasā patiḥ| 
na vakti nekṣate jñātvā prayatnānniṣphalānbahūn ||22||
tataḥ kīrtergṛhaṃ yātastapasā sundarākṛteḥ | 
kṛpayā gaṇarājasya svayaṃ vaśyo’bhavacca saḥ ||23||
sa gṛhītvā kare tāṃ tu maṃccakaṃ samupānayat | 
cikrīḍa svecchayā kīrtyā tanniṣṭhastatparāyaṇaḥ ||24|||
na viyogaṃ kṣaṇaṃ tasyāḥ sahate nṛpasattamaḥ | 
sā’pi bhogānalaṅkārānanantānbubhuje satī ||25||
sā’pi garbhavatī jātā kālena suṣuve sutam | 
śubhe muhūrte rājā’sau dadau dānānyanekaśaḥ ||26||
putrajanmaprahaṣṭaḥ sanbrāhmaṇebhyo yathārhataḥ | 
dikṣu sthāpya dvijānpacca jātakarma cakāra saḥ ||27||
kṣipraprasādanetyevaṃ nāma cakre tato’sya ha | 
tutoṣa putraṃ dṛṣṭvaiva nānālaṅkārasaṃyutam ||28||
atiprītimatī kīrtirabhavatputradarśanāt| 
prabhā ca duḥkhitā putraṃ sapatnyā vīkṣya sundaram ||29||
eṣa eva bhavedrājā jyeṣṭhaputro’yamityuta | 
dadhyodanaṃ tu saviṣaṃ kīrtiputrāya sā dadau ||30||
sa tu vihvalatāṃ yāto vivṛttanayanaḥ kṣaṇāt | 
prabhaiva ca mahāśabdaṃ kṛtvā’rodīdbhṛśāturā ||31||
tasyā śabdaṃ samākarṇya kīrtistamagamattadā | 
tamudantaṃ sā pataye nyavedayata sādaram ||32||
bhiṣajo’pi dadustasmā auṣadhāni nṛpājñayā | 
parīkṣya viṣabādhāṃ te tasmai rājñe nyavedayan ||33||
na guṇāya bhavanti sma nānāmantrauṣadhāni ca | 
prabhā tu dhikkṛtā bhartrā nirastā dūrato gṛhāt ||34||
kīrtistaṃ bālakaṃ gṛhya sakhībhiḥ kānanaṃ yayau | 
nagarādyojanaṃ yāti tāvadbālo mamāra saḥ ||35||
aṃke kṛtvā tu taṃ bālaṃ ruroda śokavihvalā | 
mūrchitā patitā bhūmau lateva vāyuneritā ||36||
cyutālaṃkaraṇādhvasta kaṃkaṇā mastakāṃcalā | 
kṣaṇena kiṃcidāśvastā sasmāra dviradānanam | |37||
utpateḥ smaratī bālaceṣṭitaṃ nighnatī svahṛt | 
daivāttenaiva mārgeṃṇa prayayau munisattamaḥ ||38||
nāmnā gṛtsamadaḥ sādhuḥ sākṣātsūrya ivāparaḥ | 
gaṇeśabhaktapravarastapasāṃ paramo nidhiḥ ||3 9||
sa tasthau karuṇāviṣṭaḥ sā kīrtiḥ praṇanāma tam | 
uvāca śvasatī sā taṃ gaṇanāthaprasādajaḥ ||40 ||
ayaṃ bālo mama mṛto jīvayainaṃ tapobalāt | 
kṣipraprasādana iti nāma devājñayā kṛtam ||4 1 ||
sapatnyā duṣṭabhāvena viṣamasmai samarpitam | 
tato’nena samaprāpi mune maraṇamīdṛśam ||42 ||
na satāṃ darśanaṃ vyarthamiti yāce tapodhana | 
śaraṇaṃ saṃtyāgaṃ gehante sādhavo janāḥ ||33 ||
