kaśyapasyātmajo bālaścañcalo bahulaṃ purā | 
idānīmatra kiṃ tena kartavyaṃ kucareṇa me ||19||
idānīṃ sarvasainyāni nāśayiṣye tavāgrataḥ | 
evaṃ bruvati daityendre kāśirājo‘śṛṇodvacaḥ ||20||
tato vināyakaṃ rājā bodhayāmāsa sādaram | 
kāśīrājovāca | 
bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyajña kathaṃ māyāṃ na budhyase ||21||
gāndharvī daityaracitāṃ kathaṃ sakto‘si tatra vai | 
devāntakenābhicarānnirmitāṃ rākṣasīmiva ||22||
sā sainyaṃ nāśayiṣyati te sāvadhānamanā bhava | 
ka uvāca | 
evaṃ nṛpavacaḥ śrutvā sāvadhānamanā vibhuḥ ||23||
buddhvā tajjñānadṛṣṭyaiva sarvaṃ māyāmayaṃ tadā | 
śaradvayaṃ viniṣkṛṣya mantrābhyāṃ parimantrya ca ||24||
ghaṇṭāstreṇa khagāstreṇa śrotrākarṣaṇasaṃbhramāt | 
vināyako mumocātha tau bāṇau vegavattarau ||25||
garjantau ghanavadrukmapuṃkhai mārutaveginau | 
sahasācchādayantau ca sahasrakaramaṇḍalam ||26||
ghaṇṭānādaḥ samabhavanmahānsarvavimohanaḥ | 
tato ghaṇṭāsvanāñśrutvā prottasthuḥ sarvasainikāḥ ||27||
siddhayaḥ sarvavīrāśca yuyudhuḥ śatrapāṇayaḥ| 
aparastasya sainyeṣu mukto bāṇaḥ khagāstrataḥ ||28||
tataḥ khagāḥ prādurāsannānārūpā mahaujasaḥ | 
pakṣavātena teṣāṃ tadgāndharvaṃ layamāgatam ||29||
andhakāraṃ yathā sūryasāratherudayānmune | 
tataste pakṣiṇaḥ sarve babhakṣurdaityasainikān ||30||
pakṣāghātahatāḥ kecitkeciccañcvagravikṣatāḥ | 
kecidbhītyaiva patitā daityāḥ prāṇānvihāya ca ||31||
hāhākāro mahānāsīddaityasainyeṣu sarvaśaḥ | 
tato devāntako roṣātkhaḍgāstraṃ prāhiṇottadā ||32||
tasya dhvaniṃ samākarṇya cukṣubhe sainyasāgaraḥ | 
diggajāśca tataḥ khaḍgā asaṃkhyātā viniḥsṛtāḥ ||33||
vahnimantaśca saṃghaṭṭātsenayorubhayorapi | 
agnidagdhāstadā devā nipeturdharaṇītale ||34||
khagāśca nihatāḥ kecitkeciddagdhāstathāgninā | 
apare yayurantarddhi diśaścātha cakāśire ||35||
asaṃkhyakhaḍgaprabhayā senācakṣurmahomuṣā | 
khaḍgavṛṣṭyā vinihatā devāḥ peturdharātale ||36||
keṣāñcidbāhavacchinnāḥ keṣāñcinmastakāḥ karāḥ | 
jaṭhare jānubhāge ca pṛṣṭhabhāge‘parā hatāḥ ||37||
nipeturdevapuṃjeṣu mṛteṣu ca gatāsavaḥ | 
aṣṭasiddhikṛtāḥ sarvāḥ senā evaṃ vināśitāḥ ||38||
asṛṅanadyo daśa diśo yātāḥ pretavahāstadā| 
vināyakastato dṛṣṭvā daityasyādbhutavikramam ||39||
bāṇaṃ niṣkāśya sudṛḍhaṃ vajrāstreṇābhimantrya ca | 
ākrandanena mahatā daityasainye mumoca tam ||40||
parvatāśca drumāstasya śabdena patitā bhuvi | 
tadastrānalasaṃyogāddigdāhaḥ samajāyata ||41||
dedīpyamānaṃ sahasācchādayanravimaṇḍalam | 
pakṣiṇo nihatā dagdhāstejasā tasya kecana ||42||
khaṇḍitaṃ tena sahasā khaḍgāstraṃ tatsahasraśaḥ | 
nirdadāha tadā daityasenāṃ vajrairanekaśaḥ ||43||
sahasraśo nihanyante ekayā vajradhārayā| 
yato yato‘gamaddaityastatra tatrāpatacca tat ||44||
cucūrṇa mastakānpādānhastānskandhānurūnapi | 
dharāṃ bhittvā gatā daityāstānapyatha jaghāna tat ||45||
evaṃ sarve hatā daityā vajraistīkṣṇaḥ sahasraśaḥ | 
tato devāntakaṃ jagmustāni vajrāṇi sarvaśaḥ ||46||
so‘pi bāṇaṃ samādāya mantrayāmāsa yatnataḥ | 
raudrāstreṇātha saṃyojya dhanuṣaḥ karṣaṇena tam ||47||
sasarja parasainyeṣu śivanāmāṃkitaṃ śubham | 
nādayangaganaṃ so‘tha diśaśca vidiśo’pi ca ||48||
vamannagnikaṇāndikṣu pralayānalasannibhaḥ | 
bhūlokā devalokāśca bhayādyātā diśo daśa ||49||
vināyakasya sainyeṣu mahākolāhalo hyabhūt | 
tasminnipatite bāṇe puruṣo ghoradarśanaḥ ||50||
niḥsṛto bhīṣaṇamukhastrailokyaṃ grāsayanniva | 
jaṭilo dīrghahastaśca dīrghapādo mahodaraḥ ||51||
dharādharo vyomagoṣṭho girijihvo bhayānakaḥ | 
vajrāstraṃ bhakṣayāmāsa kṣaṇātsa puruṣo mahān ||52||
vināyakaṃ hantukāmo yayau tasyāntikaṃ kṣaṇāt | 
tato vināyakaḥ śīghraṃ brahmāstraṃ samāyojayat ||53||
mantreṇa śatasaṃkhyena sāyake vegavattare | 
ākarṇakarṣaṇāddevo mumoca sahasā ca tam ||54||
cakampe tasya śabdena tāreṇa bhuvanatrayam | 
visphuliṃgairdiśo dagdhā na prājñāyata kiṃcana ||55||
tato‘pi puruṣo jajñe tādṛśo’tibhayānakaḥ | 
tāvubhau yuddhato vyomni parasparajayaiṣiṇau ||56||
nānāvidhaṃ mallayuddhaṃ cakratustau mahābalau | 
kṣaṇenāntarhito cobhau na dṛśyate‘tha kutracit ||57||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe‘strayuddhaṃ nāmāṣṭaṣaṣṭitamoghyāyaḥ ||68||

< adhyāya 69 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
tato devāntako‘tīva vismito’tarkayattadā| 
yathā yathā mayā māyā kriyate‘sya nivṛttaye ||1||
tathā tathāpyayaṃ bālo darśayatyeva pauruṣam | 
kadāyaṃ nidhanaṃ yāyātkadā svapsye gataśramaḥ ||2||
ityevaṃ cintayāviṣṭo dhanuḥ sajyamathākarot | 
abhimantrya śaraṃ ghoraṃ visasarja vināyake ||3||
sa sāyako bāṇavarṣamanantaṃ kṛtavānvibhau|
śaktiṃ ca nirmame ghorāṃ trailokyagrāsasāhasām ||4||
dadarśa vighnarājastā tadaṃke daityapuṃgavam | 
varṣantaṃ śarajālāni tīkṣṇāni subahūnyapi ||5||
tato‘ṣṭasiddhayaḥ śoghramuḍḍīya balavattaram | 
dadhrustāṃ sahasā śaktimāninyustāṃ vināyakam ||6||
ānīyamānā sā hastānniḥsṛtyātha palāyitā | 
roṣāttābhirdaityapatirānāyi sa kacagrahāt ||7||
aṇimānaṃ tato daityo jaghāna muṣṭinā dṛḍham| 
muṣṭighātaṃ samāsādya mūrchitā nipapāta ha ||8||
laghimā garimā cātha vaśimā taṃ mahāsuram | 
yāvallattāprahāraistu nijaghnustāvadeva hi ||9||
daityena vidhṛtāḥ vegāccaraṇeṣu pṛthakpṛthak | 
yāvadāsphālayetpṛthvyāṃ niḥsṛtāstāvadeva tāḥ ||10||
prākāmyaṃ caiva bhūtiśca jaghnatustaṃ tato balāt | 
papāta bhūtale daityo vamannagniṃ mukhāttataḥ ||11||
kṣaṇāllabhya tata saṃjñāmāruroha hayaṃ dṛḍham | 
śastrapāṇirvāyuvego nijaghāna vināyakam ||12||
dṛḍhāsighātāddevo‘tha kiṃcinmūrcchāmavāptavān | 
nimeṣātsuvidhāno‘ bhūdadhāvaddaityanāyakam ||13||
hiraṇyakaśipuṃ viṣṇuryathā vṛtraṃ śacīpatiḥ | 
śaraṃ ca kamalaṃ pāśamaṃkuśaṃ ca catuḥkaraiḥ ||14||
bibhrāṇaḥ śuśubhe vīraśriyā paramayā jvalan | 
garjitvā ghanavaddevo jaghāna daityapuṃgavam ||15||
caturbhirāyudhairvegānna cacāla tathāpi saḥ | 
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ prāpa dṛṣṭavā śastraṃ vṛthārthatām ||16||
vajrātsārataraṃ mene daityadehaṃ vināyakaḥ | 
tato devo rākṣasasya dhūmrākṣyasya mahāyudham ||17||
vajraṃ saṃcūrṇayansūryamaṇḍalādāgataṃ tu yat | 
tadādāyāhanattena daityaṃ tacchatadhā‘bhavat ||18||
na romāpi cacālāsya tadapyāścaryamuttamam | 
tato nānāśastraghātairjaghnatustau parasparam | |19||
mastake pṛṣṭhabhāge ca hṛdaye bāhumaṇḍale | 
tato śastravighātena jāto vahnirvasundharām ||20||
dadāha na ca tau bhītau yuddhamevānvapadyatam | 
niśīthe tamasā vyāpte virāmaṃ na samīyatuḥ ||21||
tataḥ kṛtrimadīptyā tau parasparamayudhyatām | 
tato māyāṃ raudraketuścakre suravimohinīm ||22||
aditiṃ sundarāṃ kṛtvā daityahaste nyaveśayat | 
padmanetrāṃ pīnakucāṃ kuṅkumāraktamālikām ||23||
muktahārāṃ suvalayāṃ divyāṃśukavirājitām | 
lāvaṇyalaharīṃ rukmavilasaddivyakañcukām ||24||
vināyakaṃ nirīkṣyaiva ruroda daityahastagā| 
dhāva dhāveti taṃ prāha trāsitā‘haṃ kimīkṣyase ||25||
evaṃ bruvatyā daityo‘syāściccheda kañcukīṃ balāt | 
aṃśukaṃ ca cakarṣāsyā madenāviṣṭacetasā ||26||
uccairuvāca devaṃ sā kva gatā puruṣārthatā | 
lokalajjābhayānmāṃ tvaṃ niḥsnehāśu vimocaya ||27||
vināyako nirīkṣyaitāṃ bāṣpakaṇṭho ruṣānvitaḥ| 
na sasmāra vicāraṃ sa śastrāṇi jagaluḥ karāt ||28||
śuśoca mama māteyaṃ kathamasya kare gatā| 
dhigjanma yasya janani gatā‘vasthāṃ duratyayām ||29||
devānāṃ jananī caiva saṃprāptā duṣṭasaṃgatim | 
kāśirājo‘pi taṃ dṛṣṭvā śocantaṃ gaṇanāyakam ||30||
svayaṃ śuśoca bahudhā loko‘pi nagare sthitaḥ | 
tato devāntako devaṃ jagarhe bahudhā tadā ||31||
dhigjanma pauruṣaṃ te‘dya na prāṇaṃ tyajase katham | 
nirlajjo‘si mukhaṃ loke darśayasyeva yo’dhunā ||32||
tvatsamīpaṃ haniṣye‘syāḥ śiraḥ kāyātkhalādhunā| 
ka uvāca | 
iti taṃ niṣṭhuraṃ vākyaṃ śrutvā devo vināyakaḥ ||33||
tarkayāmāsa manasi satyameva vadatyayam | 
prāṇatyāgāya kiṃ kāryaṃ viṣaṃ vā pāśabandhanam ||34||
udare śastrasaṃghātaḥ kāryo vā mṛtyuhetave | 
itthaṃ yāvaccintayate duḥkhaśokasamanvitaḥ ||35||
tāvadākāśavāṇīṃ sa śuśrāva ca vināyakaḥ | 
sovāca | 
māyeyaṃ racitā deva daityena duṣṭabuddhinā ||36||
avadhehi raṇaṃ yatto bhūtvā jahi nijaṃ ripum | 
tataḥ sa sāvadhāno‘bhūjjñātvā māyāmayīṃ tu tām ||37||
jaharṣa ca mahābuddhirdaityaṃ hantuṃ pracakrame | 
smṛtvā ca tadvaraṃ śambhudattaṃ tasmai durātmane||38||
uṣaḥkālaṃ vinā sarvaśastrāstrāṇi vṛthā tvayi | 
evaṃ tasya varaṃ jñātvā prātaryuddhāya niryayau ||39||
daityau‘pi purato’paśyadyuddhānte taṃ vināyakam | 
āraktanayanaṃ bhrājanmukuṭaṃ kuṇḍalojvalam ||40||
dantaprakāśaruciraṃ muktādāmavibhūṣitam | 
divyāmbaraṃ sudhāmānaṃ vyomaspṛkpuṣkaraṃ vibhum ||41||
dṛṣṭvā devāntako rūpaṃ bibhāya ca visasmare | 
arddhaṃ naraśarīraṃ kimarddhaṃ gajasamaṃ param ||42||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe ekoṇasaptatitamodhyāyaḥ ||69||

< adhyāya 70 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
evaṃ vadati yāvatsa bhayabhrāntaḥ surāntakaḥ | 
tāvaddevo dadhārainamutsaṃge laghubālavat ||1||
kṛtvā padmāsanaṃ cāru gaṇeśaḥ svaprabhāvataḥ | 
uvāca cainaṃ daityendraṃ varaṃ smara nijaṃ śubham ||2||
sa daityo radanaṃ cāsya dhṛtvā hastadvayena ha | 
dolayāmāsa dehaṃ svamantarikṣe muhurmuhuḥ ||3||
yāvatpapāta bhūpṛṣṭe bhaṃktvā dantaṃ surāntakaḥ | 
tāvaddadhāra taṃ devaḥ svadantaṃ lāghavādvibhuḥ | ||4||
ahanattena dantena mastake taṃ surāntakam | 
jagarja ca mahārāvairgarjayanvidiśo diśaḥ ||5||
cālayanpṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ pātālānyakhilāni ca | 
dvijāghātena śatadhā‘kāri deho’sya tatkṣaṇāt ||6||
asṛgvṛṣṭiḥ papātāśu gaganānmeghavṛṣṭivat | 
utpātaṃ menire lokāḥ sarve bhūtalavāsinaḥ ||7||
daityadehagataṃ jyotistaṃ viveśa vināyakam | 
paśyatāṃ sarvadevānāṃ pradhanālokitaṃ punaḥ ||8||
dehastu nipapātāsya dharāpṛṣṭe triyojanam | 
cūrṇayanvṛkṣasaṃghātānparvatānpādapairyutān ||9||
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā gatiṃ tasya sainikāśca diśo daśa | 
yātāśca katicinnāśaṃ prāptāstaddehapātataḥ ||10||
mumucuḥ puṣpavarṣaṃ te devāḥ svasthānamāgatāḥ | 
devadundubhayo nedurnṛpadundubhinisvanaiḥ ||11||
diśaśca vimalā āsanvavurvātāḥ sukhāvahā | 
tejāṃsi vahnerlokānāṃ prasannāni tadā‘bhavan ||12||
pratikūlavahā nadya āsansanmārgagāstathā | 
tataḥ śakrādayo devā munayastaṃ pupūjire||13||
tuṣṭuvuḥ parayā bhaktyā devadevaṃ vināyakam | 
vimocitā vayaṃ bandhāddevāntakakṛtādvibho ||14||
upendra iva devendra kāryaṃ yasmātkṛtaṃ tvayā| 
upendra iti nāmnā tvaṃ khyāti loke gamiṣyasi ||15||
vayaṃ svasvādhikāreṣu nirātaṃkā vasāmahe | 
svāhāsvadhāvaṣaṭkārā bhaviṣyanti gṛhe gṛhe ||16||
evamuktvā namaskṛtya devaṃ te ca pradakṣiṇam | 
anujñātā yayuḥ sarvaṃ svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ mudānvitāḥ ||17||
munayaśca tadā devairhṛṣīkeśeti nāma ca | 
kṛtvā natvā yayuḥ svaṃ svamāśramaṃ harṣanirbharāḥ ||18||
tataḥ sarve dharādhīśāḥ saṃpūjya ca praṇamya ca| 
ūcurvināyakaṃ devamuddhṛtā dharaṇī tvayā ||19||
daityabhāra bharākrāṃtā tasmāttvaṃ dharaṇīdharaḥ | 
ityuktvā te gatāḥ svasvapattanāni tadājñayā ||20||
tataḥ paścātpaśyati sma kāśirājo vināyakam | 
siṃhārūḍhaṃ bālarūpaṃ krīḍantaṃ bālakaiḥ saha ||21||
bālo‘pi taṃ nṛpaṃ dṛṣṭvā liliṃga paramādarāt | 
ubhāvānandabharitau muṃcato‘śrūṇi netrataḥ ||22||
tata ūce nṛpo devaṃ mahadbhāgyaṃ mamoditam | 
brahmādīnāmagamyaṃ yatparaṃ brahma sanātanam ||23||
tanme dṛggocaraṃ nityaṃ pūrvapuṇyaphalodayāt | 
viśvasya kāraṇānāṃ ca kāraṇaṃ tadvivarjitam ||24||
vedāntavedyaṃ sajjotirjyotiṣāmapi bhāsvaram | 
nānārūpamarūpaṃ yadbālarūpeṇa me gṛhe ||25||
krīḍate svecchayā pṛthvībhārahāri manoharam | 
ka uvāca | 
evaṃ śrutvā stutiṃ tasya pramṛjyāśrūṇi vighnarāṭ ||26||
uvāca na kṣaṇaṃ tvatto yāmi dūraṃ kadācana | 
tato rājā pupūjainamupacārairanekaśaḥ ||27||
tato vāditranirghoṣairbandiśabdairvimiśritaiḥ |  
surāntakavadhāddharṣayuktaiḥ senācaraiḥ saha ||28||
vināyakaṃ bālarūpaṃ stuvadbhi svapuraṃ yayau | 
visṛjya sarvalokāṃśca dattvā vāsāṃsi caikaśaḥ ||29||
tāmbūlāni ca keṣāṃcitpuraskṛtya vināyakam | 
viveśa svagṛhaṃ ramyaṃ harṣanirbharamānasaḥ ||30||  
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṃḍe bālacarite purapraveśo nāma saptatitamo‘dhyāyaḥ ||70||

<adhyāya 71 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca | 
aparasmindine brahmanrājā bhadrāsanaṃ gataḥ | 
amātyānvīramukhyāṃśca vṛddhāṃśca suhṛdo dvijān ||1||
ākārya ca namaskṛtya jagāda hadi saṃsthitam | 
rājovāca | 
vivāhāya tu putrasya samāhūto vināyakaḥ ||2||
utpātā bahavo jātāste ca tena nirākṛtāḥ | 
adityai ca mayā proktaṃ śīghraṃ te tanayaṃ śubham ||3||
ānayiṣye tatra bahu divasā jagmurajjasā | 
svastheṣu triṣu lokeṣu vivāho‘sya vicintyatām ||4||
amātyā ūcuḥ | 
samyaguktaṃ mahārāja vilambo vighnakāritaḥ | 
jāto vivāhakārye‘smingate’sminsa vidhīyatām ||5||
vināyakaprasādena sarvamavyākulaṃ jagat | 
dūrasthānāṃ ca suhṛdāṃ preṣyantā lagnapatrikaḥ ||6||
dūtāṃśca sarvasaṃbhārānkartuṃ grāmeṣu yujyatām | 
ka uvāca | 
iti prakṛtivākyāni śrutvā lokāḥ sabhāsadaḥ ||7||
sādhūktamiti tatrocustato rājā jaharṣa ca | 
jyotirvidbhirlagnadinaṃ niścikāya tadaiva saḥ ||8||
preṣayāmāsa suhṛdo dūtānākārituṃ śubhān | 
sāmagrīṃ kārayāmāsa dūtairāptairyathāvidhi ||9||
tato magadharājo‘pi sutāmādāya tāṃ purīm | 
āyayuḥ sarvasuhṛdo nānādigbhyaḥ samāgatāḥ ||10|| 
upāyamāni bhūrīṇi daduste‘nyonyamādarāt| 
devatāsthāpanaṃ tau ca cakratuḥ sumahotsavān ||11||
sāṃgopāṃgavivāhaṃ tau samyaksaṃpādya yatnataḥ | 
toṣayāmāsaturviprānsarvalokāndhanādibhiḥ ||12||
visṛjya suhṛdaḥ sarvāṃstato rājā vināyakam | 
bhūṣayāmāsa vividhairbhūṣaṇairaṃśukairvaraiḥ ||13||
abhyajya bhojayāmāsa svādvannaṃ saha bandhubhiḥ | 
tato rathaṃ samāruhya saha tena nṛsattamaḥ ||14||
yayau vāditranirghoṣaiḥ kaśyapāśramamuttamam | 
tato nāgarikāḥ sarve hitvā kṛtyāni niryayuḥ ||15||
bhojanaṃ paṭhanaṃ nidrā vyāsaṃga tatyajustadā | 
akṛtvā svasvaveṣaṃ te tvarayā niryayuḥ purāt ||16||
tato bālasahasrāṇi rurudhustaṃ vināyakam | 
asmāṃstyaktvā kathaṃ yāsi kathaṃ niṣṭhuratāṃ gataḥ ||17||
gamiṣya iti pūrvaṃ naḥ kathaṃ noktaṃ prasaṃgataḥ| 
abhuktvā‘smadgṛhe yāsi kathaṃ taṃ nijamāśramam ||18||
kaścidbālo dadhārāsya pādapadmaṃ rudanmuhuḥ | 
kaścitsnehātsamāliṃgya dadhāra karapaṃkajam ||19||
paurāṃganāstathaivainaṃ bālā mugdhāḥ sayauvanāḥ |
viparyasya nijā bhūṣā āyayustaṃ vilokitum ||20||
yathā pāthonidhiṃ sarvāṃ varṣākāle samudragāḥ |
yānti haṃsā yathā muktāsamūhaṃ maruto harim ||21||
atiprītiyutāḥ procuḥ kathaṃ yāsi vināyaka |
akasmātsnehamutsṛjya kathaṃ niṣṭhuratāṃ gataḥ ||22||
ka uvāca |
rathādavatatārāsau dṛṣṭvā tānnṛpasaṃyutaḥ |
pariśrāntāndhāvamānānskhalataḥ patatoʼpi ca||23||
tānuvāca tataḥ sarvānmuhūrtaṃ tvarayā janāḥ |
nṛpeṇa sahitaḥ śīghramāgato nagarādbahiḥ ||24||
idānīṃ prārthaye sarvānna tyājyā karuṇā mayi |
aparādhāstu kṣantavyā mayā vaḥ sadane kṛtāḥ ||25||
na ca tyājyaḥ paricayo yadi vo darśanaṃ bhavet |
evaṃ tadvākyamākarṇya paurāḥ sarve śucānvitāḥ ||26||
romāṃcāṃcitamātrāste procurgadagadayā girā |
janā ūcuḥ |
na māyā māyinasteʼsti sarvasya jagataḥ pituḥ ||27||
na pitā tyajate bālānsāparādhānapi dhruvam |
na candra uṣṇatāṃ yāti suvarṇanīlatāmapi||28||
kathaṃ niṣṭhuratāṃ yātaḥ kathaṃ gantuṃ samutsukaḥ |
pūrvameva na cāgāścettadā śoko na saṃbhavet||29||
corayitvā kathaṃ yāsi sarveṣāṃ no manāṃsi ca |
kathaṃ kāryaṃ kariṣyāmo nīteṣu no manassu ca||30||
jale naṣṭe jalacarāḥ kathaṃ jīvanti tadvada |
prāṇe gate śarīreṇa kiṃ prayojanamasti naḥ ||31||
kastatra pratikāraḥ syādikṣuścetsravate viṣam |
tasmānno naya yatra tvaṃ yāsi tatra svakiṃkarān||32||
tadvacaḥ parikarṇyāsau proce gadgadayā girā |
muṃcannetrāśru bahulaṃ sarvānstrīvṛddhabālakān||33||
deva uvāca |
na khedaḥ sarvadā kāryo na viyogo mama kvacit |
sarvāntaryāmiṇoʼpāyahīnasyānandarūpiṇaḥ ||34||
na cittasya samādhānaṃ bhavedvai cintanena me |
mama mūrti mṛdā kṛtvā pūjayantu gṛhe gṛhe||35||
saṃkaṭaṃ vo yadā tu syātsmartavyoʼhaṃ tadā janāḥ |
sākṣātkāraṃ pradāsyāmi nāśayiṣyāmi saṃkaṭam||36||
ka uvāca |
vacanaṃ tatsamākarṇya nananduḥ sarvanāgarāḥ |
nemuḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya jayaśabdai pratuṣṭuvuḥ ||37||
gṛhītvājñāṃ yayuḥ sarve paurāḥ svaṃ svaṃ niveśanam |
vināyakoʼpi śīghraṃ sa rathārūḍho yayau puraḥ ||38||
kāśirājena sahito mātṛdarśanalālasaḥ |
sa dadarśa parāvṛtyaṃ mukhaṃ paurakumārakān||39||
ākrandatastānuvāca punaryāsye vajantu hi |
cintā na kāryā satyaṃ vo bravīmi nānṛtaṃ kvacit||40||
evaṃ tānsa parāvṛtya kṣaṇātsvāśramamāptavān||41||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe vināyakāśramaṃ pratigamanaṃ nāmaikasaptatitamodhyāyaḥ ||71||

<adhyāya 72 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
pūrvameva ca dūtena kathitoʼsau vināyakaḥ |
adityai kāśirājena rathasthena sahāgataḥ ||1||
tataḥ sā tadviyogena dhāvamānā yayau puraḥ |
autsukyatvaritā veda nāṃcalaṃ patitaṃ svakam||2||
dṛṣṭvā tāṃ muniputroʼpi rathātsprakandya satvaraḥ |
āyayau māturabhyāśamāliliṃga ca tāṃ mudā||3||
ānandāśru mumocāsau soʼpi bāṣpaṃ cirāgataḥ |
muhūrtamekabhāvaṃ tau gacchetāṃ payasī iva||4||
dadau snehātsvasthacittā stanapānaṃ mudāʼditiḥ |
pramṛjya netre provāca śrāntoʼsi bahuvāsaram,||5||
tato nanāma tāṃ rājā jagau gadgadabhāṣayā |
rājovāca |
nītvā vināyakaṃ devaṃ yātāstu bahuvāsarāḥ ||6||
ato na mātaḥ kopaṃ tvaṃ mayi kartumihārhasi |
viyogaṃ nāsya soḍhuṃ ca na śaktoʼhaṃ munipriye||7||
na tṛptirasti pīyūṣe naudāsinyaṃ nidhāvapi |
dine dine navaprema vardhayatyeṣa no gṛhe||8||
utpātā bahavoʼnena pure no vinivāritāḥ |
asaṃkhyātā hatā daityā devāśca mudamāpitāḥ,||9||
bahulā ca kṛtā kīrtiḥ sthāpitā dharmasetavaḥ |
pauruṣaṃ ca tathāʼnena kṛtaṃ nendrādibhiḥ kṛtam||10||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ sa sarvavṛttāntaṃ kathayāmāsa sādaram |
tatoʼditistu dadhyannaṃ bhrāmayitvāʼtyajadbahiḥ ||11||
duṣṭadṛṣṭinipātasya śāntaye bālakopari |
tato bālaṃ sa nṛpatiṃ nināyāśramamaṇḍalam||12||
kaśyapo hi bahiryāto dṛṣṭvā putraṃ sa bhūpatim |
tau taṃ ca nematurbhaktyā baddhāṃjalipuṭāvubhau||13||
āliliṃga munistau ca mūrdhnyāghrāya mudā tadā |
uvāca ruddhakaṇṭhoʼsāvaṃke kṛtvā vināyakam||14||
kāśirāja nocitaṃ te bālaṃ nītvā vilambanam |
śīghramevānayiṣyeʼhamityuktvā nītavānkatham||15||
viyogenāsya taptānāmaṃgānāṃ meʼdhunā nṛpa |
jātā śītalatā puṇyāddarśanādasya sāmpratam||16||
ka uvāca |
tata ūce kāśirājo nipīya munibhāṣitam |
upaviśyāsane ramye prāpyānujñāṃ munernṛpaḥ ||17||
nṛpa uvāca |
asyaiva māyayā jātā satyatā me munīśvara |
madgṛhaṃ deva devena svakīyamiti niścitam||18||
avāptakāmo devoʼyaṃ svakāmavaśago mune |
saṃpādya mama putrasya vivāhaṃ samupāgataḥ ||19||
ka uvāca |
tasya daityavadhādīni karmāṇyasmai nyavedayat |
tato jaharṣaturubhau munipatnī muniśca saḥ |
jñātvā parākramaṃ tasya svaputrasya guṇānbahūn,||20||
tataste bhojanaṃ cakruḥ ṣaḍrasānnena sādaram |
dattvāśīrvacanaṃ tasmai visasarja nṛpaṃ muniḥ ||21||
soʼpi tānpraṇanāmātha prādakṣiṇyena niryayau |
prāpyānujñāṃ netrayugmānmuṃcannaśru suduḥkhitaḥ ||22||
smaranguṇagaṇaṃ tasya snehanirbharamānasaḥ |
śīghraṃ prayāto nagaraṃ vādyaghoṣasamanvitaḥ ||23||
sarve nāgarikā bālā vināyakadidṛkṣayaḥ |
āgatā dadṛśustatra tato duḥkhitamānasāḥ ||24||
nirīkṣya taṃ kāśirājaṃ yayuḥ svaṃ svaṃ niketanam |
aparedyuḥ sarvapaurāḥ papracchustaṃ nṛpaṃ mudā||25||
yāsya ityuktavāndevo nāgataḥ sa kathaṃ nṛpa |
tvaṃ tu niṣṭhurabhāvena tyaktvā taṃ kathamāgataḥ ||26||
rājovāca |
atyantaṃ prārthito deva uktavānmāṃ munipriyaḥ |
manmūrtisthāpanaṃ kṛtvā sevadhvaṃ sarva eva mām||27||
viyogo na ca sarvāntaryāmiṇā vaḥ kathaṃcana |
tataste kārayāmāsurmūrtiṃ dhātumayīṃ śubhām||28||
caturbhujāṃ trinayanāṃ sarvabhūṣaṇabhūṣitām |
śūrpakarṇāṃ gajamukhīṃ sarvāvayavasundarām||29||
ḍhuṇḍhirājeti nāmnā tā sthāpayāmāsurādarāt |
ṛtvigbhirbrāhmaṇairanyairvedaśāstraviśāradaiḥ ||30||
prāsādaṃ paramaṃ kṛtvā pupūjuste dine dine |
yena yenaiva kāmena pūjayedyo vināyakam||31||
taṃ taṃ dadāti tasmai sa bhaktyā saṃpūjito vibhuḥ |
evaṃ nānāmūrtidharo bhrājate sma vināyakaḥ ||32||
viśveśvare svanagaraṃ yāte sarvasuraiḥ saha |
divodāse cāvimukte kāśirāje sukhaṃ sthite||33||
vināyako muniṃ prāha kaśyapaṃ mātaraṃ ca tām |
ahaṃ te putratāṃ yātastapasārādhitaḥ purā||34||
bhūbhāraśca hṛtaḥ samyaghatau daityau mahābalau |
trailokyapīḍakau duṣṭau devāntakanarāntakau||35||
devāśca sādhavaścaiva rakṣitāḥ sthāpitāḥ pare |
idānīṃ tu gamiṣyāmi nijalokaṃ cirantanam||36||
ka uvāca |
śrutvā vākyamubhau khinnau bāṣpakaṇṭhau tamūcatuḥ |
kadā te darśanaṃ deva punarāvāṃ bhaviṣyati||37||
tataḥ sa mātaraṃ prāha darśanaṃ me bhaviṣyati |
bhavānyā mandire mātaḥ satyaṃ me priyabhāṣitam||38||
evaṃ śrutvā devavākyaṃ punaryāvadvadatyasau |
tāvadantarhito devastatastau khinnamānasau||39||
kārayāmāsaturmūrtiṃ prāsādaṃ cātiśobhanam |
vināyaketi nāmāsya cakraturbhaktitatparau||40||
tasyāṃ mūrtau dhyātamātro nityaṃ darśayate vibhuḥ |
svātmānaṃ sarvagaṃ nānārūpiṇaṃ sa vināyakaḥ ||41||
gṛtsamada uvāca |
evaṃ kīrte samākhyātaṃ caritaṃ te mayā śubham |
vināyakasya devasya śravaṇātsarva siddhidam||42||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ sarvopadravanāśanam |
sarvakāmapradaṃ sarvapāpasaṃcayanāśanam||43||
punaste kathayiṣyāmi sindhudaityavadhāya saḥ |
avatīrṇaḥ śivagṛhe mayūreśvarasaṃjñitaḥ ||44||
bālyātprabhṛtyadbhutāni nānākarmāṇi yoʼkarot||45||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe vināyakacaritrakathanaṃ nāma dvisaptatitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||72||

<adhyāya 73 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
kathaṃ brahmañśivagṛhe mayūreśvara saṃjñitaḥ |
avatīrṇaḥ kimarthaṃ vā kiṃ ca taccaritaṃ pitaḥ ||1||
mayūreśvarasaṃjñā ca kathaṃ jātā vināyake |
etanme sarvam ācakṣva śṛṇvaṃstṛpyāmi na kvacit||2||
ka uvāca |
tretāyuge mahādaityaḥ sindhunāmāʼbhavatpurā |
sa hatastena balināʼvatīrṇena śivālaye||3||
atrāpyudāharantīmamitihāsaṃ purātanam |
śaunakasya ca saṃvādaṃ rājñā ca cakrapāṇinā||4||
maithile viṣaye rājā gaṇḍakīnagare śubhe |
cakrapāṇiriti khyātaḥ sākṣādviṣṇurivāparaḥ ||5||
na guṇā varṇituṃ śakyāḥ śeṣeṇāsya kadācana||6||
tejasā lopayanmānaṃ lāvaṇyena ca manmatham |
matyā bṛhaspatiṃ skandaṃ vikrameṇa balena ca||7||
yeneyaṃ sakalā pṛthvī kṣaṇena vaśavartinī |
kṛtā sarve ca rājānaḥ sevāyāṃ viniyojitāḥ ||8||
turaṃgāṇāṃ gajānāṃ ca padātinā jayaiṣiṇām |
rathānāṃ ca na yasyāsti saṃkhyānaṃ jagatītale||9||
sākṣāllakṣmī sthirā yasya gṛhe darśanagocarā |
ratnakāṃcanamuktābhiraniśaṃ bhāsayaddiśaḥ ||1 0||
bhadraṃ bhadrakaraṃ yasya lokanāmalakopamam |
yasyāmātyau mahābuddhī sāmbaścaiva subodhanaḥ ||1 1||
sevantau svāmikāryārthe tṛṇīkṛtya svajīvitam |
yasyāsītpramadā ramyā nāmnogrā cāruhāsinī||12||
kumudāni vikāsante divā yanmukhacandrataḥ |
anekabhūṣādīptyā yā nāśayitvāʼkhilaṃ tamaḥ ||1 3||
pātivratya guṇāndraṣṭuṃ yānti sarvāḥ pativratāḥ |
evaṃ rājā budho mānyaḥ sadā viṣṇuparāyaṇaḥ ||14|||
purāṇaśravaṇe sakto dharmaśāstraparāyaṇaḥ |
santatyā rahitaḥ soʼtha duḥkhamāpa divāniśam||15||
asatyaṃ jāyate yadyattattannaśyati tatkṣaṇāt |
anekavratadānāni yajñāṃśca kṛtavānbahūn||16||
tataḥ sa rājā kasmiṃścitsamaye nijabhāryayā |
ākārya prakṛtī paurānvirakto rājyasaṃpadi||17||
uvāca sarvāndaṇḍe ca kośaṃ rājyaṃ tyajeʼdhunā |
kimaputrasya rājyena svargahīnasya meʼdhunā||18||
yāni karmāṇi ca mayā putrārthe caritāni ca |
īśvarārpaṇabuddhyā cetkṛtāni syuryadā tadā||1 9||
muktiḥ syādubhayoḥ pūrvajanmakarmakṣayoʼpi ca |
gatamāyurvṛthā lokā vanaṃ yāsye mudāʼdhunā||20||
sarvaṃ prakṛtisātkṛtvā yūyaṃ tadvākyakāriṇaḥ |
gamiṣyeʼhaṃ vanaṃ lokāstapastaptuṃ hitāya vai||2 1||
kadācidiṣṭasiddhiścetpunaryāsye nijāṃ purīm |
dhruvamājñāṃ mama punaḥ prayacchatādhunā janāḥ |
evaṃ śrutvā nṛpavacaḥ sarve duḥkhitamānasāḥ ||22||
aśrudhārāḥ pramuṃcaṃtaḥ procustaṃ nṛpasattamam |
paurā ūcuḥ |
jananī janakastvaṃ naḥ kathaṃ niṣṭhuratāṃ gataḥ |
kimarthaṃ tyajase nastvamaparādhaṃ vinā prabho||23||
vinā bhavantaṃ no janma vṛthaiva mātaraṃ śiśoḥ |
vayamapyanuyāsyāmo yatra yāti bhavān prabho||24||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ vadatsu lokeṣu nṛpeṇāthāgato muniḥ |
śaunako muniśārdūlo jātavedā ivāparaḥ ||25||
vedavedāṃgaśāstrāṇāṃ vaktā trailokyaviśrutaḥ |
śakrādisuravandyo yo bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyavit||26||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā purato rājā nanāmotthāya cāsanāt |
svāsane tamupāveśya pupūja parayā mudā||27||
bhuktavantaṃ ca viśrāntaṃ pādasaṃvāhanādibhiḥ |
cakrapāṇirathovāca puṇyaṃ kiṃ phalitaṃ mama||28||
yajjātaṃ darśanaṃ teʼdya sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham |
sarvakāmapradaṃ nṛṇāṃ durlabhaṃ pāpakarmaṇām||29||
tataḥ proce muniścakrapāṇiṃ nṛpatisattamam |
saṃtuṣṭaḥ parayā bhaktyā praśrayeṇa damena ca||3 0||
muniruvāca |
mā cintāṃ kuru rājendra mā ca rājyaṃ parityaja |
putraste bhavitā samyak madvākyānnātra saṃśayaḥ ||3 1||
mayā vāṇī noktapūrvāʼnṛtā hāseʼpi karhicit |
ka uvāca, evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ jahṛṣe rājasattamaḥ ||32||
alaṃkārāndadau tasmai ratnakāṃcananirmitān |
vastrāṇi ca mahārhāṇi na ca jagrāha vai muniḥ ||3 3||
punaḥ provāca nṛpatiṃ vayaṃ valkaladhāriṇaḥ? |
niḥspṛhāḥ sarvabhogeṣu sarvabhūtahite ratāḥ ||34||
sṛṣṭisaṃhārakaraṇe samarthāḥ karuṇābdhayaḥ |
sādhūnāṃ darśanaratāḥ samaloṣṭāśmakāṃcanā||35||
vidvatsu naiva kamalā sthānaṃ dhatte kadācana |
tasmānnedaṃ grahīṣyāmi kāṃcanaṃ vasanaṃ śubham||3 6||
tīrthayātrāprasaṃgena tvāmahaṃ samupāgataḥ |
dināni ca vyatītāni bahūni tava darśane||37||
ka uvāca |
punastau nematuḥ samyagdampatī śaunakaṃ munim |
papracchaturupāyaṃ taṃ santānotpādane kṣamam||38||
sarvavratatapoyajñānmatvā dānāni vai vṛthā |
tatoʼbravīnmuniḥ sauraṃ vrataṃ sarvārthadaṃ nṛṇām||39||
anekajanmapāpānāṃ śamanaṃ putrapautradam |
muniruvāca |
māsamātraṃ vrataṃ kāryamārabhya bhānusaptamī||40||
kṛtvāʼbhyudayikaṃ śrāddhaṃ mātṛpūjanapūrvakam |
abhyarcya gaṇanāthaṃ ca svastivācya dvijarṣabhān||41||
suvarṇakalaśai sthāpyaḥ sauvarṇaṃ ravimaṇḍalam |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirbhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ ||42||
raktacandanamiśraiśca taṇḍulaiḥ kusumairapi |
raktairnānāvidhai ratnaiḥ phalaiśca vividhairapi||43||
arghyairdvādaśasaṃkhyaiśca namaskāraiḥ parikramaiḥ |
stutibhiḥ prārthanābhiśca prārthayetparameśvaram||44||
tato lakṣanamaskārānkurvīta kārayīta vā |
pratyahaṃ lakṣaviprāṃstu bhojayetparayā mudā||45||
gāmekāṃ pratyahaṃ dadyādvedajñāya kuṭumbine |
brahmacaryeṇa tiṣṭhecca sapatnīko nṛpottama||46||
dīnāndhakṛpaṇebhyaśca dadyādannaṃ dayānvitaḥ |
māsānte sarvasaṃbhārānbrāhmaṇāya samarpayet||47||
evaṃ vrate kṛte rājanputraste matprasādataḥ |
bhaviṣyati mahākhyātaḥ sūryabhaktiyutaḥ śuciḥ ||48||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ vrataṃ samādiśya śaunakoʼntarhitastadā |
tataścakāra nṛpatiryathādiṣṭaṃ vrataṃ tu tat||49||
patnyā saha vinītātmā sūryabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |
brāhmaṇānbhojayāmāsa yathecchaṃ lakṣasaṃkhyayā||5 0||
upavāsayutaḥ patnyā māsamātraṃ babhūva saḥ |
godānaṃ ca namaskārāṃścakre cākārayaddvijaiḥ ||5 1||
sūryamantraṃ japannityaṃ tannāmasmaraṇaṃ sadā |
tataḥ kadācittatpatnī rātrau svapne dadarśa tama||62||
nijabhartṛdvijarūpaṃ savitāraṃ manoramama |
cakame kāmarūpaṃ taṃ madanāgniprapīḍitā||53||
uvāca paritaptāṃgī kāmo me bādhate bhṛśam |
ṛtuṃ me dehi bhartastvaṃ no cenmṛtyurbhavenmama||54||
soʼpi vandhyaṃ viniścitya tatpatiṃ kāmukāṃ ca tām |
svapna eva bhartṛrūpī savitā tāmṛtuṃ dadau||55||
sā prabuddhā tataḥ prāha samutthāpya nijaṃ patim |
niyamasthena kiṃ brahmaṃstvayā me ṛturarpitaḥ ||56||
sa uvāca tatastāṃ tu nāhaṃ vratamanāḥ śubhe |
upavāsarataḥ kṣīṇo rarāma ravibhaktimān||57||
tataḥ punaḥ prāha satī nānyaṃ jāne kathaṃcana |
tvadrūpeṇaiva dhyāyāmi savitāraṃ vrate sthitā||58||
ṛtujena ca dagdhāʼhaṃ vahnināʼntargatena ha |
tataḥ punaścakrapāṇirūce tāṃ priyabhāṣiṇīm||59||
nṛpa uvāca |
tuṣṭoʼsau savitā kānta namaskāraiśca bhojanaiḥ |
brāhmaṇānāṃ gavāṃ dānairupavāsairjapairapi||60||
āvāṃ siddhiṃ dadau samyak putrastava bhaviṣyati |
ka uvāca |
dine dine garbhavṛddhau tasyāstāpoʼbhivarddhate||61||
sā mumurṣuścandanāni sośīrāṇi niṣevate |
lilepāṃge ca karpūraṃ tāpo nāsyā śaśāma ca||62||
na cārdravastrapavanaistataḥ sā sakhibhiḥ saha |
tatyāja sindhutīre taṃ garbhaṃ gatvā mahattaram||63||
āyayau sātha viśrāntā sakhibhirnijamandiram |
nivedya bhartre tyaktaṃ taṃ gṛhakāryaratāʼbhavat||64||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe sindhūtpattivarṇanaṃ nāma trisaptatitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||73||

<adhyāya 74 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
tyakte garbhe tayā sindhau bāloʼjani mahābalaḥ |
tejasvī vikarālāsyo dīrghabhālastrilocanaḥ ||1||
raktakeśajaṭābhāraścakrapāṇistriśūlabhṛt |
bālaśabdena tasyātha cakampe bhuvanatrayam ||2||
ājānubāhurākrāntumiyeṣa viṣṭapatrayam |
cukṣubhe sāgarastena yādobhirmunisattama ||3||
tatra sthitastu bāloʼsau samudramupaśoṣayat |
samudra uvāca |
jīvanāya mama sutaṃ neṣya enaṃ nṛpālayam ||4||
ka uvāca |
ityānināya taṃ bālaṃ samudro nṛpamandiram |
dampatyoḥ purataḥ sthāpya proca evaṃ dvijastadā ||5||
tava patnyāʼtyāji garbho duḥsahatvānmamodare |
ugro bālastato jātoʼkhilalokabhayaṃkaraḥ ||6||
yasya prathamaśabdena kampitaṃ bhuvanatrayam |
netrābhyāṃ nekṣituṃ śakyoʼta evainamupānayam ||7||
ityuktvā bālakaṃ muktvāʼ ntardadhe sāgarastadā |
tata enaṃ karābhyāṃ sā kaṭau dadhre nṛpāṃganā ||8||
sthāpayitvā dadau premṇā stanapānaṃ mudānvitā |
ubhāvānanda yuktau tau dampatī putradarśanāt ||9||
yathā ciraṃ yoganiṣṭho labdhvā brahmāmṛtaṃ param |
tata ākārayāmāsa brāhmaṇānsuhadoʼpi ca||10||
jyotirvidbhirvicāryāsya sindhurityabhidhāṃ śubhām |
rājñī cakre nṛpaścāsya jātakarmaṃ yathāvidhi ||11||
kṛtvā dadau tato dānamanekaṃ harṣanirbharaḥ |
anekebhyo dvijātibhyo vastrāṇi vividhāni ca ||12||
dāpayāmāsa nagare patākābhiralaṃkṛte |
vādyatsu sarvavādyeṣu śarkarāṃ ca gṛhe gṛhe ||13||
gateṣu sarvalokeṣu raktāṃga iti nāma ca |
cakraturdampatī snehādathāmātyai samūcatuḥ ||14||
ugrāyāstanayaścāyamugramudrādharoʼpi ca |
ayamugrekṣaṇa iti nāmnā khyāto bhaviṣyati ||15||
vipraprasādana iti paurā nāma pracakrire |
tataḥ sarve paurajanā upadā vividhāstadā ||16||
dadurnṛpāya soʼpyetāndāpayāmāsa tatkṣaṇāt |
vavṛdhe bālakaḥ soʼtha śuklapakṣe yathā śaśī ||17||
yathāʼgnirvāyuvegena vardhate kṣaṇamātrataḥ |
tathā svatejasā bālaḥ pravṛddho gaganaṃ spṛśat ||18||
krīḍanneva sadā bālo vṛkṣāngṛhagatānbahūn |
utpāṭya pātayāmāsa vāmahastatalena ha ||19||
araṇye krīḍatā tena parvatāṃścūrṇitā drumāḥ |
uḍḍīya candrahariṇaṃ kṣaṇaṃ jagrāha karhicita ||20||
jalāvatāramārge tu ruddhe kvāpi kareṇunā |
muṣṭighātena bibhide gaṇḍamasyā papāta sā ||21||
evaṃ tasyādbhutaṃ karmaṃ dṛṣṭvā lokā visismire |
jaharṣa jananī cāsya pitā jñātvāʼtimānuṣam ||22||
evaṃ pravṛddho bāloʼsau sindhunāmā mahābalaḥ |
uvāca pitaraṃ yāsye tapastaptumahaṃ nṛpa ||23||
anuṣṭhityā svargalokaṃ bhūlokaṃ ca rasātalam |
ākramiṣye vṛthāyurmai gacchatīti matirmama ||24||
evamākarṇya tadvākyamūcatuḥ pitarau ca tam |
utkarṣaṃ svasya putrasya nityaṃ prārthayate pitā ||25||
jananī sarvadevebhyo vratadānādināpi ca |
evamājñāmavāpyaiva natvā tau sa yayau vanam ||26||
tatra bhramandadarśātha kāsāraṃ jalajairyutam |
tatra sthātuṃ manaścakre viviktamiti harṣitaḥ ||27||
ekāṃguṣṭhena bhūmiṣṭha ūrdhvabāhū raviṃ smaran |
śukropadiṣṭaṃ taṃ mantraṃ japanparvataniścalaḥ ||28||
dakṣiṇe jānuni sadā vāmapādaṃ nidhāya ca |
aṃjaliṃ hṛdi vinyasya dhyāyandinamaṇiṃ tadā ||29||
śītavātātapajalavṛṣṭīnāṃ sahano dṛḍhaḥ |
vāyumātrāśanastasthau valmīkākrāntavigrahaḥ ||30||
asthimātrāvaśiṣṭoʼpi japatyeva mahāmanum |
evaṃ tasya vyatītāya śaradāṃ dvisahasrakam ||31||
tasya sindhoḥ śarīrotthāstepurbhāso raviṃ tadā |
evamugraṃ tapo dṛṣṭvā pratyakṣoʼbhūddivākaraḥ ||32||
uvāca paramaprītoʼnuṣṭhānena tavādhunā |
varaṃ varaya cittasthaṃ dāsyāmi jīvitāvadhi ||33||
sindhustadākarṇya vaco bhāṣitaṃ bhānunā svayam |
dehabhāvaṃ gatauʼpaśyatpurato bhāskaraṃ prabhum ||34||
natvā tatpādakamalaṃ baddhāṃjalipuṭoʼbravīt |
namaste dīnanāthāya namaste sarvasākṣiṇe ||35||
namaste tridaśeśāya brahmaviṣṇuśivātmane |
namaste viśvavandyāya namaste viśvahetave ||36||
namaste vṛṣṭibījāya sasyotpādanahetave |
parabrahmasvarūpāya sṛṣṭisthityantahetave ||37||
guṇātītāya gurave gruṇakṣobhavidhāyine |
sarvajñāya jñānadātre sarvasya pataye namaḥ ||38||
dhanyaṃ me janma deveśa vaṃśo me janakoʼpi ca |
jananī ca tapaścāpi yajjātaṃ tava darśanam ||39||
varadaśceddineśa tvaṃ dehi me sarvatoʼmṛtim |
tava prasādātsaṃgrāme jayeyaṃ sarvadevatāḥ ||40||
vartamānāddevaṇānna me mṛtyurbhavediti |
evaṃ tasya varāñśrutvā parituṣṭo vibhāvasuḥ |
uvāca nijabhaktaṃ tamanuṣṭhānabhṛśaṃ kṛśama ||41||
sūrya uvāca |
na bhayaṃ vidyate devayonibhyo nṛbhya eva ca |
na tiryagbhyo na nāgebhyo na divā na niśi kvacit ||42||
noṣaḥkāle na sandhyāyāṃ mama vākyādbhaviṣyati |
maraṇaṃ te nṛpasuta gṛhāṇāmṛtabhājanam ||43||
idaṃ yāvatkaṇṭhagataṃ tāvanmṛtyurna te bhavet |
niṣkāśayedidaṃ yaste tasmānmṛtyurbhaviṣyati ||44||
devo yoʼvataretkoʼpi dhunvankeśāgrato divam |
yasyāṃguṣṭhanakhāgre syurbrahmāṃḍānāṃ hi koṭayaḥ ||45||
sa tvāṃ haniṣyati vibhuranyasmādabhayaṃ tava |
madvarasya prasādena savaṃ tṛṇamayaṃ tava ||46||
trailokyarājaṃ te dattaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ nānāvarāndattvāntardadhe savitā tadā ||47||
sopyānandasamāyukto jagāma nijamandiram |
jananī ca pitā tasya mūrdhnyāghrāyāpaturmudam ||48||
ūcatustaṃ tadā putraṃ virahātreʼnnavarjitau |
cintayā kṛśatāṃ yātau paśya putradaśāmimām ||49||
tayoḥ pādau praṇamyāha sa putrau harṣanirbharaḥ |
prasannaḥ savitā mahyaṃ trailokyasvāmitāmadāt ||50||
sādhayiṣye tadvarāttāṃ na cintāṃ kartumarhatha ||51||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe varapradānaṃ nāma catuḥsaptatitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||74||

<adhyāya 75 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
buddhimantaṃ sutaṃ jñātvā saviturvaradarpitam |
rājyaṃ tasmai dadau sarvaṃ deśakośabalānvitam ||1||
pitā vanaṃ yayau tasya svātmasādhanalālasaḥ |
sa tu pitrābhiṣiktaḥ sanrājyaṃ kartuṃ mano dadhe ||2||
śreṇīmukhyānsamāhūya prakṛtīradhikāriṇaḥ |
āsthāpayatsvādhikāre mānayitvāṃśukādibhiḥ |
ḍiṇḍiraveṇa svājñāyā bhaṃge doṣamaghoṣayat ||3||
digjayārthaṃ tato vīrānājñāpayata satvarama |
tejasāʼhepayatsūryamatibhīṣaṇavigrahaḥ ||4||
vīrā agre prayānti sma vikarālāruṇānanāḥ |
nagnanānāśastradharā rajaśchāditabhāskarāḥ ||5||
tato gajāścaturdantā nānāvarṇavibhūṣitāḥ |
yayurmahāśṛṃgalinaḥ kampayanto vasundharām ||6||
mahāmātrasamārūḍhāścaṃcalāḥ parvatā iva |
nānāvarṇadhvajayutā diggajānbhettumicchavaḥ ||7||
ghaṇṭāghoṣeṇa mahatā nādayanto digantaram |
aśvārohā yayuḥ paścānnānālaṃkaraṇānvitāḥ ||8||
asaṃkhyatūṇadhanuṣāṃ śastrāṇāṃ maṇḍalānvitāḥ |
sindhudaityaścamūmadhye rarāja hayapṛṣṭhagaḥ ||9||
muktāmālālasatkaṇṭho dhanurbāṇalasatkaraḥ |
khaḍgakheṭadharo gacchaṃścūrṇayandrumaparvatān ||10||
yadyannagaramuddiśya yāti daityo mahābalaḥ |
tasya tasyādhipaṃ vīrā dhṛtvā ninyurnijādhipam ||11||
svakīyaṃ nāyakaṃ cihnaṃ mudrāṃ tatra nyaveśayan |
tatoʼnye śaraṇaṃ yātā dāsabhāvamupāgatāḥ ||12||
karadāṃstānsa kṛtvaiva rarakṣa svapade balāta |
evaṃ sarvānvaśaṃ cakre daityakoṭyastatoʼnvayuḥ ||13||
śumbho niśumbho vṛtraśca pracaṇḍaḥ kāla eva ca |
kadambāsuranāmā ca śambaraḥ kamalāsuraḥ ||14||
kolāsuroʼbravīttatra yathā tripuradaityarāṭ |
vijitya lokatritayamadhikārānhi no dadau ||15||
tathā yacchādhikārānno jitvā trailokyamojasā |
tasminhate śivenādya dṛṣṭoʼsi prabaloʼsuraḥ ||16||
na te parākramatulāṃ kṛtāntoʼpi labhetkvacit |
tavājñāṃ kartumicchāmastava sevāṃ mahābala ||17||
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ saṃtuṣṭaḥ sindhudaityarāṭ |
dadāvaśvāngajāṃstebhyo vastrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni ca ||18||
sindhuruvāca |
prabalastu tadāʼhaṃ syāṃ yadā jeṣyeʼmarāvatīm |
daraviṣṇusatyalokānpātālāni ca sapta ca ||19||
ka uvāca |
sādhu sādhviti sarveṣu bruvatsu sarvaṃdaityapāḥ |
jagarjuṃśca jaharṣuśca brahmāṇḍaṃ paryakampayan ||20||
na bhūpayuddhātkaṇḍurno hyaśamaddevayuddhataḥ |
śamaṃ yāsyatgrato jagmuḥ śīghramevāvrajandivam ||21||
rurudhuste śakrapurīṃ brāhmaṇā iva vittadam |
madhyaṃ viviśurapare luṇṭanto ratnasaṃcayān ||22||
kolāhalo mahānāsīddevānāṃ tatra garjatām |
tataḥ sabhāmadhyagato harirdūtamukhāttataḥ ||23||
śuśrāva daityābhigamaṃ veṣṭikāṃ ca nijāṃ purīm |
śīghramairāvatārūḍho vajrahastoʼmarānvitaḥ ||24||
āyayau tena daityena yoddhukāmaḥ surādhipaḥ |
kecidūcuḥ surāstatra nāyaṃ yuddhakṣamo hare ||25||
vinā ramāpatiṃ cāsya na samo dṛśyate kvacit |
yāvadvadatsu teṣvevaṃ tāvadaittho mahābalaḥ ||26||
śakraṃ suragaṇairyuktamavidhyaccharavṛṣṭibhiḥ |
tāvaddevagaṇāḥ kecitpalāyanaparā yayuḥ ||27||
tatoʼdhāvanmahendroʼsau garjamāno ruṣānvitaḥ |
grasandaityagaṇānsarvānvajrodyatakaroʼrihā ||28||
ahanaddaityarājaṃ taṃ mastake vajrato dṛḍham |
sa mūrchāṃ mahatīṃ prāyānmuhūrtātpunarutthitaḥ ||29||
uvāca ca hariṃ gaccha svakṣayaṃ na kṣayaṃ vraja |
mama muṣṭiprahāreṇa kālo mṛtyuṃ gamiṣyati ||30||
tatra kā gaṇanā teʼsti nāśaṇottaddharirvacaḥ |
roṣāviṣṭo mahādaityastato muṣṭiprahārataḥ ||31||
bibhedairāvatakaṭaṃ rudhiraughapravarṣitam |
tata uḍḍīya daityastaccaturdantānadhārayat ||32||
apātayadagajapatimāścaryaṃ harirāyayau |
dadhāra ca hariṃ pāde pothayadyāvadeva tam ||33||
tāvatsūkṣmeṇa vapuṣā nirgato hastamadhyataḥ |
jagāma dūradeśaṃ sa manasā praśaśaṃsa tam ||34||
naitādṛśaṃ balaṃ dṛṣṭamatiṣṭhaṃ cenmṛtoʼbhavam |
tatoʼmaragaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ taṃ hariṃ hariḥ ||35||
jagāma vapuṣāʼnyena visṛjyairāvataṃ gajam |
sindhūdaityastu sendreṣu deveṣu vidruteṣu ca ||36||
indrāsanamārūḍho daityavṛndena veṣṭitaḥ |
dadau padāni devānāṃ daityebhyaḥ sakalāni saḥ ||37||
tataḥ śumbhādayo daityāḥ sthitāsteṣu nirākulāḥ |
namasyantoʼsurapatiṃ praśaṃsanto balādhikam ||38||
nānāvāditranirghoṣairnādayaṃtastriviṣṭapam ||39||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe suraparājayo nāma paṃcasaptatitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||75||

<adhyāya 76 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
vaikuṇṭhe sukhamāsīnamāgatoʼmarasaṃyutaḥ |
harirhariṃ namaskṛtya nijagāda prayojanam ||1||
indra uvāca |
kiṃ na jānāsi govinda sindhudaityakṛtāmimām |
āpadaṃ noʼmarāvatyāṃ duṣṭairākramaṇaṃ kṛtam ||2||
tena sārddhaṃ kṛtaṃ yuddhaṃ yathāśakti suraiḥ saha |
na sa jetuṃ kṣamo daityoʼtastvāṃ śaraṇamanviyām ||3||
na tvāṃ vinā gatirnoʼsti sarvadā tvaṃ gatirhi naḥ |
nirdālayainaṃ no dehi sthānāni jagadīśvara ||4||
ka uvāca |
śrutvā harivaco viṣṇuścintāścaryasamanvitaḥ |
uvāca taṃ na bhīḥ kāryā pravijyeṣyeʼsuraṃ kṣaṇāt ||5||
evamuktvā hṛṣīkeśo ruroha nijavāhanam |
taduḍḍānena ca tadā cakampe bhuvanatrayam ||6||
patitā avanau vṛkṣāḥ pakṣivṛndasamanvitāḥ ||
kirīṭakuṇḍaladharo vanamālāvibhūṣitaḥ ||7||
kastubhārcirlasadvakṣāḥ kastūrītilakojjvalaḥ |
śaṃkhacakragadāpadmahasto yātoʼmarāvatīm ||8||
jñātvā devagaṇānprāptāngaruḍāsanasaṃyutān |
yuddhāyājagmurasurā nānāśastradharāstadā ||9||
dhanuścakradharo daityaḥ satūṇo hayasaṃgataḥ |
sindhurapyāgato roṣādyoddhukāmo mahābalaḥ ||10||
tataḥ kubero varuṇo vāyuragniḥ purandaraḥ |
somo mitraśca bhaumaśca nāsatyau madanoʼpi ca ||11||
tatoʼbhavad dvandvayuddhaṃ sindhudaitye puraḥ sthite |
pracaṇḍo varūṇenājau kamalena ca yakṣarāṭ ||12||
vṛtreṇa śatayajvā ca niśumbhaḥ pavanena ca |
śumbhoʼpi yuyudhe tatra viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā ||13||
vahniścaṇḍena muṇḍena somaśca yuyudhe bhṛśam |
kadambena ca bhaumaśca madanena ca śambaraḥ ||14||
nāsatyau kāladaityena sarva eva ca sainikāḥ |
śastrairastraiḥ prajaghnuste marmasthānāni cāsakṛta ||15||
kecidyuyudhire mallalīlayā yuddhadurmadāḥ
parasparaṃ śastraghātaiḥ kecittatra prapedire ||16||
mṛtā mumūṣaṃvaḥ kecidvyaṃgāścaiva valgire |
jayaṃ parājayaṃ tena prāpuḥ kvacitkameva ca ||17||
tato vṛtrāsuraḥ śakramahananmuṣṭighātataḥ |
mastakaṃ mastakenaiva nijaghnaturathaujasā ||18||
evaṃ hastena hastaṃ ca pādaṃ pādena jaghnatu |
vakṣaḥsthalena vakṣaśca tato vajreṇa soʼhanat ||19||
jaghāna vṛtra śakraṃ taṃ soʼpatanmūrchito bhuvi |
yāvatpalāyati puro vamanraktaṃ mukhādbahu ||20||
tataḥ papāta bhūpṛṣṭhe hato mūrchā gato bhṛśam |
punaḥ saṃjñāmavāpyaiva vajrakalpena muṣṭinā ||21||
jaghāna vṛtraḥ śakraṃ taṃ soʼpatanmūrcchito bhuvi |
yāvatpalāyati suro vamanraktaṃ mukhādbahu |
tāvaddaityā yoddhukāmāḥ samīyuḥ sarvatodiśam |
dṛṣṭavā parākramaṃ teṣāṃ maghavāntardadhe tadā ||22||
evaṃ ye ye dvandvayuddhaṃ kurvanto devatāgaṇāḥ |
te sarve bhagnadarpāstu palāyanamakurvata ||23||
evaṃ deveṣu bhagneṣu garuḍaṃ prāhiṇoddhariḥ |
cakradīptyā svadīptyā ca bhāsayanvidiśo diśa ||24||
ahanaccakradhārābhirdetyaśreṇīranekaśa |
kecidbhagnānanā daityā kecidbhagnaśirodharā ||25||
kecicca śatadhā jātā śīrṇajānūrubāhava |
kecicca śaraṇaṃ yātā na mamāra janārdanaḥ ||26||
sarve mahābalā daityā muktiṃ harihatā gatā|||
medomāṃsavahā nadyaḥ sadyastatra pravartitāḥ ||27||
tataḥ śaṃkhaninādena hari sarvaṃ vyanādayat |
evaṃ jayati goviṃde sarve śumbhādayoʼsurāḥ ||28||
tameva yoddhumāyātā dvandvayuddhaṃ vihāya tat |
virāḍrūpeṇa bhagavāndhṛtvā tāṃścaturoʼsurān ||29||
bhiṇḍipālena pāṣāṇaṃ yathā kṣipati mānavaḥ |
tathā caṇḍaṃ ca muṇḍaṃ ca niśumbhaṃ śumbhameva ca ||30||
bhrāmayitvāʼkṣipaddūre madhye kṛtvā saridvarām |
te mūrcchāṃ paribhūyaiva punaśca vāhinīṃ gatāḥ ||31||
hṛdi pracaṇḍaṃ pṛṣṭhe tu vṛtraṃ muṣṭiprahārataḥ |
jaghāna hariravyagraḥ kālaṃ kamalameva ca ||32||
bhaumāsuraṃ ca śirasi kadambaṃ cakraghātataḥ |
kolāsuraṃ ca gadayā vivyādha hṛdi mādhavaḥ ||33||
tataḥ sindhuragāttūrṇaṃ kṛtakolāhalāravaḥ |
digantānnādayanproce balaṃ te darśitaṃ hare ||34||
mamāpi pauruṣaṃ paśya na ca gantuṃ tvamarhasi |
na me dṛṣṭigato jīvan ripuryāti kadācana ||35||
bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyajña kiṃ pūrvaṃ na vicāritam |
yasya śabdena ca bhṛśaṃ kampate bhuvanatrayam ||36||
tatpuraḥ kathamāyātaḥ khadyota iva bhūṣaṇam |
tatoʼmaragaṇāḥ procurvalgantaṃ sindhudaityapam ||37||
śūrā na hi prajalpanti darśayantyeva pauruṣam |
asmākaṃ na harerājñā no cettvaṃ śatadhā bhaveḥ ||38||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe ṣaṭsaptatitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||76||

<adhyāya 77 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā krodhādvahnikaṇānvaman |
yathā siṃho gajānīkaṃ yāti tadvadyayau surān ||1||
sindhurmuṣṭiprahāreṇa jaghāna balasūdanam |
papāta sa dharāpṛṣṭhe vātāhata iva drumaḥ ||2||
bhāladeśe kuberaṃ ca hanau ca varuṇaṃ yamam |
pṛṣṭha deśe jaghānāsau cakrāghātena daityarāṭ ||3||
tāludeśe pāvakaṃ ca manmathaṃ lattayāʼhanat |
anilaṃ pādaghātena śanaiścaramapothayat ||4||
somaṃ bhaumaṃ bhrāmayitvā tatyāja bhūtale balāt |
pṛṣṭhabhāgeʼhanaccaiva sanakaṃ ca sanandanam ||5||
nāsatyau nāradaścāpi kutrāpi ca palāyitāḥ |
tataḥ palāyitā sarve dṛṣṭvā vīryaṃ surāstadā ||6||
patitā mūrchitāḥ keciddaityaścakreṇa mādhavam |
muṣṭideśe jaghānāsau cakraṃ tasyāpatadbhuvi ||7||
mādhavo gadayā mūrdhni daityeśaṃ tamatāḍayat |
vaṃcayitvā ca śatadhā gadāṃ svāṃ ca vyacikṣipat ||8||
jñātvā parākramaṃ tasya proce taṃ madhusūdanaḥ |
varayasva varaṃ daitya yatte manasi vartate ||9||
naitādṛśo mayāʼdarśi puruṣārthoʼsure kvacit |
tatoʼbravīddaityapatiḥ paramāhlādasaṃyutaḥ ||10||
yadi tuṣṭoʼsi deveśa yadi deyo varo mama |
gaṇḍakīnagare me tvaṃ parivārayuto hare ||11||
sārvakālaṃ vasa vibho nānyaṃ yāce varaṃ param |
tata ūce mahāviṣṇuḥ sthāsyāmi nagare tava ||12||
datto varo yato nūnaṃ tatoʼsmi vaśagastava |
tataḥ sa satyaloke ca kailāse vaiṣṇave pade ||13||
āsthāpayaddaityapatīnsvayaṃ śākre padeʼvasat |
tatrāpyanyaṃ sthāpayitvā ramāpatiyutaḥ svayam ||14||
vādyandundubhinirghoṣaiḥ svapurīṃ gaṇḍakīṃ yayau |
nuvanti bandinastaṃ tu nedṛśoʼbhūtpumānkvacit ||15||
yena viṣṇurgṛhaṃ svīyaṃ jitvāʼnāyi surānbahūn |
dadṛśurnāgarāstasya samīpe varuṇaṃ harim ||16||
kuberaṃ devamukhyāṃśca tataḥ svabhavanaṃ yayuḥ |
tataḥ proce hariṃ daityo gaṇḍakīnagare sukham ||17||
viharasvāmaraiḥ sārddhaṃ tataḥ soʼpi tathāʼkarot |
parito dūrato daityo daityānanyānnyaveśayat ||18||
tato devā hariṃ procuḥ kiṃ kṛtaṃ garuḍadhvaja |
tyaktvā svavikramaṃ kiṃ tvaṃ sthitoʼtyānandanirbharaḥ ||19||
kathaṃ kārāgṛhe prāptā mṛtyulokaṃ gatāḥ katham |
kathamanto bhavedasya bhogasya jagadīśvara ||20||
tata ūce hariḥ sarvānkālo hi duratikramaḥ |
kālena jāyate sarvaṃ hṛsate vardhateʼpi vā ||21||
tasmātkālaṃ pratīkṣadhvaṃ kāla enaṃ grasiṣyati |
evaṃ jitvā trilokīṃ sa mahābalaparākramaḥ ||22||
tato daityo jagau sarvaṃ vṛttāntaṃ pitarau mudā |
āśiṣaṃ dadatustau ca jñātvā pauruṣamasya ha ||23||
tato daityo duṣṭamatirbhūtale samaghoṣayat |
devadvijagavāmarcā kriyate yena kenacit ||24||
sa hantavyoʼthavā sādhurāneyo mama sannidhau |
pratimā yatra yatra syāttāṃ bhaṃktvā nikṣipejjale ||25||
mamaiva pratimāṃ kṛtvā pūjayantu gṛhe gṛhe |
prābodhayansindhuvaco dūtā evaṃ sthale sthale ||26||
bhaṃktvā prāsādamūrtīśca toʼyegādhe ca cikṣipuḥ |
sindhośca pratimāṃ kṛtvāʼsthāpayanparamādarāt ||27||
pūjārthaṃ rākṣasāścāpi tato yātā nijaṃ patim |
ūcurvaināyakaharaharibhānuramādikāḥ ||28||
pratimāḥ pravibhajyāśu kṣiptāḥ sarvā mahājale |
tavaiva pratimāstatra sthāpitā rākṣasā api ||29||
pūjārthaṃ sthāpitāḥ svāminnāgatāśca tavāntikam |
evaṃ dharmasya sarvasya vicchittiḥ samajāyata ||30||
yajñadānasvadhāsvāhāvaṣaṭkārānna cākarot |
devadvijagurūṇāṃ ca pūjanaṃ kva ca nābhavat ||31||
meruṃ yātā ṛṣigaṇāḥ kecidbhraṃśamupāgatāḥ |
evaṃ trailokyaprabalā daityā jātāśca rākṣasāḥ ||32||
vilīnā nidhanaṃ prāptāḥ sādhavo devatāgaṇāḥ |
yathā devagaṇā mokṣaṃ sindhoḥ prāptāḥ śṛṇuṣva tat ||33||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe saptasaptatitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||77||

<adhyāya 78 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
sarve nigṛhitā devāḥ sindhunā prabhaviṣṇunā |
tasyaiva ca vadhopāyaṃ cintayāmāsurutsukāḥ ||1||
hariruvāca |
sarveṣāṃ saṃmataṃ jñāya kartavyaḥ kāryaniścayaḥ |
tasmātsarve bruvantvadya mataṃ yasya yathā bhavet ||2||
ka uvāca |
iti śakravacaḥ śrutvā bruvanbrahmādayaḥ surāḥ |
īśvaraḥ sarvakartā‘sti sa kalyāṇaṃ kariṣyati ||3||
sa yena tuṣyate devaḥ sa upāyo vidhīyatām |
sa enaṃ paribhūtvaiva padāni naḥ pradāsyati ||4||
tato vaktupradhāno‘sau bṛhaspatirabhāṣata |
svalpayā pūjayā sadyaḥ prasanno jāyate vibhuḥ ||5||
carācaraguruḥ sadyaḥ sarvaiḥ prārthyo‘surāntakaḥ |
devā ūcuḥ |
ko devaḥ prārthanīyaste mato vācaspate vada ||6||
tasya tuṣṭiṃ kariṣyāmaḥ svapadaprāptaye‘khilāḥ |
gururuvāca |
sṛjate pāti yo viśvaṃ hanti rūpatrayātmakaḥ ||7||
abījo bījabhūtaścāśeṣavācāmagocaraḥ |
nityo brahmamayo jyotisvarūpaḥ śāstragocaraḥ ||8||
anādimadhyanidhano nirguṇo yo nirāmayaḥ |
bahurūpaikarūpaśca yasya nāmākhilā janāḥ ||9||
gṛhītvā sarvakāryeṣu siddhiṃ yānti nijepsitām |
saṃkaṣṭaṃ harate bhaktyā pūjitaḥ sa vināyakaḥ ||10||
ārādhayantu sarve taṃ sa vaḥ siddhiṃ vidhāsyati |
māghasya kṛṣṇapakṣo‘yaṃ saṃpravṛtto’dhunā surāḥ ||11||
caturthī bhaumayuktā‘sya priyā vighna harasya ha |
sa eva prakaṭībhūya dāsyate svaṃpadāni vaḥ ||12||
sindhuṃ sa saṃnihatyaiva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
yadyatkāmayate yo yastattatsarvaṃ pradāsyati ||13|| 
devā ūcuḥ |
samyaguktaṃ guro vākye yena tṛptā vayaṃ mune |
mahāsrotovahānnastvaṃ karṇadhāroʼsi sāmpratam ||14||
ka uvāca |
tataste śakravaruṇakuberamadhusūdanāḥ |
gurubhaumaśaśīnāśca yamāgnyanilakādayaḥ ||15||
pañcāmṛtaṃ gandhamālyaṃ śamīdūrvāśca pallavān |
phalānyaraṇyajātāni vividhāni ca mṛttikāḥ ||16||
aśarkarāḥ samādāya gaṇḍakīṃ tāṃ nadīṃ yayuḥ |
maṇḍapaṃ vipulaṃ kṛtvā bhaktvā vṛkṣānanekaśaḥ ||17||
latābhiḥ kadalīstambhaiḥ succhāyaṃ ca suśītalam |
snātvā nityakriyāḥ kṛtvā mūrtīścakru suśobhanā ||18||
siṃhārūḍhā daśabhujā daśāyudhavirājitāḥ |
vaināyakīḥ karikarā nānābhūṣāvirājitāḥ ||19||
siddhibuddhiyutāḥ pārśve kirīṭakuṇḍalojjvalāḥ |
pītavastraparīdhānāḥ sarpabhūṣāvirājitāḥ |
tasminmaṇḍapamadhye tāḥ sthāpayitvā yathāvidhi |
pupūjuḥ parayā bhaktyā ṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ ||20||
pañcāmṛtaiḥ śuddhajalairvastragandhādidīpakaiḥ |
naivedyairvividhaiścaiva phalairārārtikaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||21||
evaṃ saṃpūjya te mantraṃ jepuḥ savitṛtuṣṭaye |
astaṃ yāte savitari sandhyāṃ kṛtvāʼstuvanvibhum ||22||
sarve ūcuḥ |
dīnanātha dayāsindho yogihṛtpadmasaṃsthita |
anādimadhyarahitasvarūpāya namo namaḥ ||23||
jagadbhāsa cidābhāsa jñānagamya namo namaḥ |
munimānasaviṣṭāya namo daityavighātine ||24||
trilokeśa guṇātīta guṇakṣobha nabho namaḥ |
trailokyapālana vibho viśvavyāpinnamo namaḥ ||25||
māyātītāya bhaktānāṃ kāmapūrāya te namaḥ |
somasūryāgninetrāya namo viśvambharāya te ||26||
ameyaśaktaye tubhyaṃ namaste candramaulaye |
candragaurāya śuddhāya śuddhajñānakṛte namaḥ ||27||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ stuvatsu deveṣu tejorāśirabhūtpuraḥ |
sarve hatadṛśo devā vismitā abhavaṃstadā ||28||
tatastatkṛpayā devaḥ saumyatejā ajāyata |
tataste dadṛśurdevaṃ siṃhārūḍhaṃ vināyakam ||29||
daśāyudhadharaṃ tāvadbhujaṃ divyakirīṭinam |
nānābhūṣābhirāmaṃ ca muktādāmavibhūṣitam ||30||
divyagandhānulepāḍhyaṃ vyālabaddhodaraṃ vibhum |
kṣudraghaṇṭākvaṇatpādaṃ kastūrītilakojjvalam ||31||
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā surā devaṃ namaskṛtyocire tadā |
yaścintito gurorvākyātsa evāyaṃ vināyakaḥ ||32||
sa eva dṛṣṭaḥ sākṣāttu vāṅamanoʼgocaro vibhuḥ |
janma dhanyaṃ dṛśirdhanyā tapo dānaṃ ca naḥ surāḥ ||33||
tata ūce surāndevastṛptoʼhaṃ bhavatāṃ stavaiḥ |
pūjayā caiva bhaktyā ca saṃkaṣṭīvratatoʼpi ca ||34||
saṃkaṣṭaharametacca stotraṃ khyātaṃ bhaviṣyati |
yaḥ paṭhetprayato nityaṃ sa me mānyo bhavetsurāḥ ||35||
tasya darśanataḥ sarve naśyeyuryakṣarākṣasāḥ |
bhogānbhunakti vividhānante mokṣapadaṃ labhet ||36||
śaṇvantu me punarvākyaṃ sindhudaityena pīḍitāḥ |
śaraṇaṃ me prapannāśca yajñavedādivarjitāḥ ||37||
niruddhā gaṇḍakīdeśe svadhāsvāhāvivarjitāḥ |
tato hyasya vadhaṃ kartumavatāntaraṃ mama ||38||
girijāyā gṛhe devā bhaviṣyāmi ca sāmpratam |
mayūreśvaranāmnāʼhaṃ khyātiṃ yāsye tadā ca vaḥ ||39||
padānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca prāptiḥ sindhau hate mayā |
bhaviṣyati na saṃdeha yataḥ kṛtayuge surāḥ ||40||
siṃhārūḍho daśabhujastejorūpī vināyakaḥ |
ṣaḍbhujaḥ śaśivarṇaśca tretāyāṃ barhivāhanaḥ ||41||
mayūreśvaranāmā ca dvāpare raktavarṇavān |
caturbāhurākhuvāho bhūtvā cāhaṃ gajānanaḥ ||42||
tataḥ kaliyuge prāpte śyāmavarṇo dṛṣanmayaḥ |
dhūmaketuriti khyāto bhaviṣyāmi surā aham ||43||
śīghrameva kariṣyāmi bhavatāṃ vāñchitaṃ surāḥ |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ nivedya tāndevastatraivāntardadhe vibhuḥ ||44||
ānadat ca surā prāpuḥ svakāryaṃ kṛtaniścayāḥ |
ya idaṃ paramākhyānaṃ śaṇuyācchrāvayecca yaḥ ||45||
paṭhecca parayā bhaktyā dhyātvā devaṃ vināyakam |
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti pretya brahmamayo bhavet ||46||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe surardhivarapradānaṃ nāmāṣṭasaptatitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||78||

< adhyāya 79 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
nirjitānsindhunā devāṃ jñātvā śambhurnijasthalāt, saptakoṭigaṇākīrṇastrisandhyākṣetramabhyagāt ||1||
svāhāsvadhāvaṣaṭkāravedādhyayanavarjitāḥ |
nivasanti bhayātsindhorgautamādyā maharṣayaḥ ||2||
dṛṣṭvā te tryambakaṃ devaṃ parivavruḥ samantataḥ |
yathā tīrthaṃ vipravarā bālakā jananīṃ yathā ||3||
praṇamya taṃ prapūjyaiva procurdhanyatamā vayam |
anuṣṭhānaṃ dṛśirjanma dhanyaṃ jñānaṃ yato haraḥ ||4||
agocaro dṛṣṭigato daṇḍakāraṇyamāgataḥ |
duritaṃ vilayaṃ yātaṃ puṇyaṃ ca phalitaṃ mahat ||5||
idānīṃ na ca duḥkha no dṛṣṭe deve bhaviṣyati |
yathodite divānāthe na dhvāntaṃ dṛśyate kvacit ||6||
tatoʼbravīnmahādevaḥ sarvāṃstānmunipuṃgavān |
śiva uvāca |
sindhunāʼkrāmi trailokyaṃ kṛtāṃ devavinigrahaḥ ||7||
munīnāmapi keṣāṃcittena khinnaṃ mano mama |
viśrāntiṃ na labhe sthāne tatoʼhamiha cāgataḥ ||8||
bhavatāṃ darśane nāhaṃ parituṣṭo mudānvitaḥ |
idānīmavakāśaṃ saparivārasya dīyatām ||9||
atipuṇyena vaḥ prāptaṃ darśanaṃ pāpanāśanam |
yatrasthitvā paraṃ dhyānaṃ kariṣye jagadīśituḥ ||10||
ka uvāca |
iti śūlivacaḥ śrutvā procuḥ sarve maharṣayaḥ |
sarveśvarasya te deva dātā sthānasya ko bhavet ||11||
kalpadrumasya kaḥ kāmaḥ kenāsau paripūryate |
kṣīrārṇavatṛṣā kiṃ vā pallavena gamiṣyati ||12||
tavaiva rūpaṃ pṛthivī tava vaśyāʼtra sarvadā |
tvamīśaḥ sarvalokasya yathecchasi tathā kuru ||13||
darśayāmaḥ surapate sundaraṃ tava cāśramam |
nānāvṛkṣalatākīrṇaṃ ramyavāpīsarojalam ||14||
yādobhiḥ pakṣibhiryuktaṃ ghanacchāyaṃ suvistṛtam |
svādukandaphalopetaṃ mṛdughāsasamanvitam ||15||
yadīcchāʼtra vasa tryakṣa paripālaya noʼkhilān |
ka uvāca |
tatrāvasanmahādevo gaṃgaurīgaṇairyutaḥ ||16||
kailāsādadhikaṃ mene munerāśramamaṇḍalam |
tadāśrayeṇa te sarve gautamādyā maharṣayaḥ ||17||
cerustapāṃsi vividhānyāsaṃste vigatajvarāḥ |
śivopi tapa ārebhe gaṃgāgaurisahāyavān ||18||
tato gaṇāśca sāmagrīṃ tasya cakrurmahātmanaḥ |
kasmiścitsamaye gaurī pṛcchati sma sadāśivam ||19||
tvameva kartā hartā tvaṃ pātā viśvasya śaṃkara |
aṣṭamūrtiramūrtistvaṃ sarvaśaḥ sarvabhāvataḥ ||20||
sarvaśreṣṭhatamastvaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ sarvakāmadaḥ |
aṣṭānāṃ karmaṇāṃ deva phaladātāʼkhilārthavit ||21||
tvattaḥ śreṣṭhatamaḥ koʼsti yaṃ tvaṃ dhyāyasi taṃ vada |
devāśca munayo nāgā hā yakṣamānuṣarākṣasāḥ ||22||
yatsattayāʼkhilaṃ viśvaṃ tvattaḥ śreṣṭhatamastu kaḥ |
trayastriṃśatkoṭidaiveḥ pūjyase siddhasādhakaiḥ ||23||
ka uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā śivo vacanamabravīt |
śiva uvāca |
samyakpṛṣṭaṃ tvayā devi tuṣṭo vākyena teʼnaghe ||24||
śaṇuṣvāvahitā devi vistareṇa bravīmi te |
adyāpi na kathaṃ jñāto yaṃ dhyāyāmi sureśvari ||25||
tatsvarūpaṃ nirūpiṣye tava prīticikīrṣayā |
lokānāmupakārāya saṃsārottāraṇāya ca ||26||
yo devaḥ sarvabhūteṣu gūḍhaścarati viśvakṛt |
yoʼnantaśīrṣānantaśrīranantacaraṇaḥ svarāṭ ||27||
anantaśrutinetraścānantanāmā guṇātigaḥ |
anantarūpī yo devo vedakartāʼkhilārthadaḥ ||28||
yoʼnantaśaktiviśvātmāʼnupameyaḥ purātanaḥ |
nopamā śeṣacandrābdhivyomnāṃ nārāyaṇasya ca ||29||
bhāratasya ca vedasya vyāsasya ca munerapi |
yato jīvā anantāśca jāyanteʼmbumuco yathā ||30||
jaladhārā yathāʼgnervā visphuliṃgā bhavanti hi |
brahmāviṣṇuśivādīnāṃ guṇatrayapradaḥ prabhuḥ ||31||
sṛṣṭipālanasaṃhārānājñāpayata tairiha |
tato guṇavibhāgena gaṇeśa iti paprathe ||32||
tamimaṃ paramātmānaṃ parātparataraṃ vibhum |
guṇeśamaniśaṃ dhyāye parabrahmasvarūpiṇam ||33||
gauryuvāca |
tuṣṭāʼhaṃ tava vākyena pratyayo me kathaṃ bhavet |
taṃ guṇeśaṃ kathaṃ sākṣātkaromi ca bhajāmi ca ||34||
tamupāyaṃ vada vibho yadi tuṣṭoʼsi śaṃkara |
ka uvāca |
iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā punarūce maheśvaraḥ ||35||
śiva uvāca |
ekaniṣṭhatayā yāvattapasārādhito na te |
tāvatpratyakṣatāṃ devi kathaṃ yāsyāti sa prabhuḥ ||36||
devyuvāca |
kathaṃ mayā tapaḥ kāryaṃ kenopāyena vā vibho |
yadi tuṣṭoʼsi deveśa vada satyaṃ mamānagha ||37||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ tadādaraṃ buddhvā provāca girijāṃ haraḥ |
yena tasyā bhavetprīto guṇeśo guṇavallabhaḥ ||38||
ekākṣaramanuṃ tasyai dadau samyaggharastadā |
dvādaśādvaṃ kuru tapastatastuṣṭo bhavedvibhuḥ ||39||
sākṣātkāraṃ guṇeśaste pradāsyati na saṃśayaḥ |
tadaiva sā namaskṛtya girīśaṃ hṛṣṭamānasā ||40||
jagāma tapase gaurī mantradhyānaparāyaṇā |
jīrṇāpurāduttare tu lekhanādi manoharama ||41||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe gaurīmantrapradānaṃ nāma navasaptatitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||79||

<adhyāya 80 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
tatra sā kānanaṃ dṛṣṭvā ramyaṃ puṣpajalānvitam |
tasthau padmāsanagatā nāsāgranyastalocanā ||1||
gaṇeśadhyānaniratā prāṅmukhī japatatparā |
ekākṣarasya mantrasya śuṣkadruma iva sthitā ||2||
na phalaṃ na jalaṃ mūlaṃ parṇaṃ kandaṃ na mārutam |
atti sā niścalā tatra nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ śritā ||3||
evaṃ sā dvādaśābdāni vyatītāni tato vibhuḥ |
kṛpayāʼʼvirabhūtprīto guṇaśo guṇavallabhaḥ ||4||
kirīṭakuṇḍaladharo daśabāhustriśalūbhṛt |
bhālacandraḥ śaṃkhacakramuktāmālāvibhūṣitaḥ ||5||
akṣamālā ca kamalaṃ kastūrītilakaṃ dadhat |
madhye nārāyaṇamukho dakṣiṇe ca śivānanaḥ ||6||
vāme brahmamukhaḥ śeṣe padmāsanagato vibhuḥ |
tatphaṇāmaṇḍalacchāyaḥ kundakarpūrasannibhaḥ ||7||
uvāca jagadambāṃ tāṃ yaṃ dhyāyasi divāniśama |
guṇeśo guṇeśa iti soʼhaṃ te gataḥ ||8||
tava niṣṭhāṃ ca bhaktiṃ ca tada ugramavekṣya ca |
mama svarūpaṃ vakṣyāmi prītastava varānane ||9||
trayastriṃśatkoṭideveṣvadhiko na ca matparaḥ |
guṇatrayavibhāgācca guṇeśa iti māṃ viduḥ ||10||
trideho guṇataścāhaṃ saṃtuṣṭastapasā ca te |
varaṃ varaya mattastvaṃ yatte manasi vartate ||11||
asādhyamapi dāsyāmi trailokyeʼsminmaheśvari |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā harṣagadgadaniḥsvanā ||12||
unmīlya nayane gaurī paśyati sma puro vibhuma |
guṇeśaṃ triguṇeśaṃ ca tritanuṃ praṇanāma sā ||13||
uvāca janma niṣṭhā ca tapo mantro haroʼpi ca |
adya dhanyatamo jāto dṛṣṭavā padapaṃkajam ||14||
adya siddhiḥ parā prāptā sākṣāddṛṣṭo yato bhavāna |
na kāṃkṣeʼnyavaraṃ kaccittvadvākyaṃ na ca laḍghaye ||15||
atastvaṃ putratāṃ yāhi mama prītikaro bhava |
nirantaraṃ darśanaṃ te sevanaṃ pūjanaṃ bhavet ||16||
iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā guṇeśo harṣanirbharaḥ |
uvāca girijāṃ tāṃ sa bhaviṣyāmi sutastava ||17||
vāñchitaṃ pūrayiṣyāmi lokānāṃ ca tavāpi ca |
evamuktvā guṇeśastāmantardhānamagātkṣaṇāt ||18||
na dadarśa yadā devī guṇeśaṃ taṃ kṣaṇāttadā |
amanyata mayā dṛṣṭaḥ svapnā sukhadaḥ kṣaṇam ||19||
īśvarasyopadeśena dṛṣṭo devoʼkhilārthadaḥ |
na soḍhuṃ virahaṃ tasya śaktāsmīti vyadhācchivā ||20||
guṇeśapratimāṃ kṛtvā sthāpayāmāsa sādaram |
prāsādaṃ kārayāmāsa caturdvāraṃ suśobhanam ||21||
girijātmajeti tannāma sthāpayāmāsa sundaram |
siddhikṣetramiti khyātaṃ bhaviṣyati nṛṇāmiha ||22||
anuṣṭhānavatāṃ siddhirbhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ |
iti dattvā varaṃ tatra saṃpūjya ca yathāvidhi ||23||
pradakṣiṇā namaskārānkṛtvā ca dvijapūjanam |
dattvā dānāni tebhyaśca gṛhītvāśīrvaco dvijāt |
trisaṃdhyākṣetramagamadyogasthaṃ dadṛśe haram ||24||
tatpādapaṃkaje svīyaṃ śiraḥpadmaṃ nyadhācchivā |
uvāca nijavṛttāṃtamājñāṃ prāpya vibho tava ||25||
upadeśaṃ manoścāpi dvādaśādvaṃ kṛtaṃ tapaḥ |
avāyubhakṣayā svāmiṃstatastuṣṭo guṇeśvaraḥ ||26||
uvāca paramaprīto bhāvaṃ jñātvā paraṃ mama |
tavodareʼvatāraṃ tu kariṣyāmyacalātmaje ||27||
vāṃchite ca kariṣyāmi devānāṃ ca tavāpi ca |
evamuktvā kṣaṇenāsau tatraivāntardadhe vibhuḥ ||28||
tatoʼhaṃ paramaprītā mūrtiṃ prāsādameva ca |
vidhāya sthāpayitvā tāṃ tavāntikamupāgatā ||29||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ śrutvā priyāvākyaṃ prahṛṣṭamānaso haraḥ |
protphullanayanaḥ prāha dhanyāʼsi girijātmaje ||30||
sākṣāddṛṣṭo guṇeśaste gṛhe soʼvatariṣyati ||31||
haniṣyati mahādaityaṃ bhūbhāraṃ ca hariṣyati |
iṃdrādilokapālānāṃ svapadāni pradāsyati ||32||
etanmama vaco devi na ca vismartumarhasi |
ka uvāca,एvamuktvā śivo deva āliliṃga śivāṃ tadā ||33||
ānaṃdāśrūṇi muṃcaṃtau romāṃcitatanū ubhau |
tiṣṭhaṃtau paramāhlādayutāvambāmaheśvarau ||34||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍeʼśītitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||80||

<adhyāya 81 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
tataḥsā pārvatī hṛṣṭā sakhījanamagānnijam |
vṛttāntaṃ sarvamācakhyau hṛṣṭarūpoʼpi soʼbhavat ||1||
tatatprabhṛti sā devī guṇeśamānasāʼbhavat |
paśyati smānyabālaṃ sā guṇeśoʼyaṃ bhavediti ||2||
gṛhītuṃ dhāvati tadā tanmātrā sā niṣidhyate |
na kvāpi viśramaṃ yāti guṇeśadhyānatatparā ||3||
tannāma japate nityaṃ tanmayaṃ lokate jagat |
papraccha ca sakhīḥ sarvā guṇeśo yāsyate kadā ||4||
pitrā me kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ vratamīśāptaye śubham |
pārthivasya gaṇeśasya pūjanaṃ ca yathāvidhi ||5||
tena vrataprabhāveṇa prāptāʼsmi śaṃkaraṃ priyam |
punastadācariṣyāmi guṇeśaprāptayeʼdhunā ||6||
tato bhādracaturthyāṃ sā kṛtvā mūrtiṃ mudānvitā |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pupūja ca gajānanīm ||7||
ṣaḍbhirdhyānādikaḥ paṃcāmṛtarvastrairanekaśaḥ |
yajñopavītagaṃdhaiśca dhūpadīpairyathāvidhi ||8||
nānāpuṣpaiśca naivedyairnānāvidhaphalairapi |
tāmbūlairārdrapūgaiśca lavaṃgādisamanvitaiḥ ||9||
dūrvābhiśca śamīpatrairdakṣiṇābhiranekaśaḥ |
ārārtikyairvicitraiśca mantrapuṣpaiḥ stavairapi ||10||
pradakṣiṇāprārthanābhirbrāhmaṇānāṃ ca pūjanaiḥ |
parituṣṭā mūrtirasau pārthivī cetanāmagāt ||11||
tadaiva prabhayā tasyāḥ koṭisūryaprabhā jitā |
kalpānalārcirvijitāʼnantacandraprabhā hṛtā ||12||
hatanetraprabhā gaurī mūrchitā patitā bhuvi |
muhūrtātsāvadhānā sā provāca jagadīśvaram ||13||
kiṃ mayā vikṛtaṃ devapūjanena ca tarkaye |
abhicāroʼthavā kena prayuktoʼyaṃ na vedmi tam ||14||
kathaṃ me viphalo yatno jāto deva kṛpānidhe |
ka uvāca |
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ tasyāḥ saumyatejā abhūtprabhuḥ ||15||
sā tadaivābhavaddhṛṣṭā bālaṃ vīkṣya purogatam |
asaṃkhyavaktranayanamasaṃkhyāṃghriśirodharam ||16||
asaṃkhyamukuṭaṃ sūryacandrāgnilocanaṃ vibhum |
muktāmaṇimayīṃ mālāṃ bibhrantaṃ dīptimatparam ||17||
anekāyudhahastaṃ ca sarvāvayavasundaram |
dṛṣṭvā tu pārvatī rūpaṃ pavitraṃ paramādbhutam ||18||
atarkayaccetasā sā nimīlya nayane śubhe |
kampamānāṃgā yaṣṭiḥ sā kartavyaṃ nābhyapadyata ||19||
cintitaṃ kiṃ mayā dṛṣṭamariṣṭamadbhutaṃ nu kim |
unmīlya nayaneʼpaśyaddivyadṛṣṭirdharātmajā ||20||
tāvacchivopadiṣṭaṃ tadavedīcca jagāda ca |
pārvatyuvāca |
asyaiva māyayā bhrāntā na jānāmi vibhuṃ prabhum, ||21||
papraccha taṃ tataḥ kastvaṃ kuta āgamanaṃ tava |
kimarcha tvaṃ guṇeśaścedbhavitāsi vadasva mām ||22||
ka uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvāʼvadatsa puruṣo mahān |
svanena nādayansarvā diśo vidiśa eva ca ||23||
deva uvāca |
yaṃ dhyāyasi divārātrāvanuṣṭhānavatī śubhe |
soʼhaṃ guṇeśaste geheʼvatīrṇaḥ paramaḥ pumān ||24||
parātparataro yasmādavadaṃ tvā varārthinīm |
yāsye te putratāṃ devi yatkṛtyaṃ me śṛṇuṣva tat ||25||
ubhayoḥ sevanaṃ kāryaṃ sindhudaityavināśanam |
amarāṇāṃ padaprātiṃ kṛtvā yāsye nijaṃ padaṃ ||26||
ka uvāca |
pītvā vākyāmṛtaṃ tasya prītā sā girijātmajā |
uvāceha paraṃ bhāgyaṃ paramaṃ me tapaḥphalam ||27||
nāyakoʼnantakoṭīnāṃ brahmāṇḍānāṃ vibhurmayā |
dṛṣṭaścidānandaghano yasmātsṛṣṭamanekaśaḥ ||28||
paṃcabhūtāni brahmeśahariścandrendra bhāskarāḥ |
nakṣatrāṇi ca gandharvā yakṣā munigaṇā drumāḥ ||29||
śailāḥ pakṣigaṇāḥ sarve bhuvanāni caturdaśa |
sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ yasmātsacetanamacetanam, ||30||
sa tvaṃ mama kumāratvaṃ prāptoʼsīti viḍambanam |
atha tvāṃ prārthayedeva prākṛto bhava sāmpratam ||31||
yena te lālanaṃ kuryāṃ sevanaṃ paramādarāt, ka uvāca |
yāvadvadati saivaṃ tu dadarśa puratastu tam ||32||
ṣaḍbhujaṃ candrasubhagaṃ locanatrayabhūṣitam |
sunāsa subhru vadanaṃ sthūlavakṣasamīśvaram ||33||
dhvajāṃkuśordhvarekhābjacihnitaṃ pādapaṃkajam |
koṭisphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ koṭicandraprabhaṃ vibhum ||34||
hematārānibhānkeśāndadhantaṃ bālarūpiṇam |
kurvantaṃ prathamaṃ śabdaṃ kampayantaṃ vasundharām ||35||
janāḥ sthānāni maryādāṃ tatyajustasya śabdataḥ |
nīrasā vṛkṣanicayāḥ sapallavacayāstadā ||36||
jātā gāvo bahukṣīrāstrailokyaṃ jahṛṣe punaḥ |
devadundubhayo neduḥ puṣpavṛṣṭirathāpatat ||37||
dṛṣṭvā bālaṃ tu girijā hastābhyāṃ jagṛhe mudā |
snāpayāmāsa payasā taptena snehabhāvataḥ ||38||
prasnutau pāyayāmāsa stanau paramaharṣitā |
śivoʼpi jahṛṣe tena śiśunā parameṇa ha ||39||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe gaṇeśāvirbhāvonāmaikāśītitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||81||

<adhyāya 82 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
tataḥ śivagaṇāḥ śambhuṃ vṛttāntaṃ taṃ nyavedayan |
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ devaṃ śivā putrasamanvitā ||1||
jātā putrāvalokārthaṃ tato yāhi nijaṃ sthalam |
evaṃ gaṇavacaḥ śrutvā tutoṣa girijāpatiḥ ||2||
āgatya vīkṣya taṃ bālamadbhutaṃ śaśisannibham |
sphaṭikādrinibhaṃ kundadhavalaṃ kañjalocanam ||3||
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ lebhe provāca ca vacastadā |
śiva uvāca |
nāyaṃ bāloʼnādisiddho jarājanmavivajitaḥ ||4||
līlāvigrahavāneṣa svaprakāśo guṇātigaḥ |
śuddhasattvamayaḥ sarvajīveśo bhuvaneśvaraḥ ||5||
munidhyeyoʼkhilādhāro brahmabhūtoʼkhilapradaḥ |
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā karābhyāṃ sa bālamādatta śaṃkaraḥ ||6||
lālayitvā hṛdi sthāpya sarvahṛtsthoʼpi yaḥ sadā |
punarūce ca girijāṃ śivoʼśivaharo vibhuḥ ||7||
paramātmā guṇātītaḥ putratāṃ te samāgataḥ |
parānuṣṭhānato devi sākṣāddṛṣṭo vibhustvayā ||8||
ka uvāca |
tato viprānsamāhūya pūjayitvā gaṇādhipam |
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā ca mātṛpūjanapūrvakam ||9||
kṛtvāʼbhyudayikaṃ śrāddhaṃ prāśayitvā ghṛtaṃ madhu |
abhimantrya śiśuṃ bhūmiṃ girijā taṃ stanaṃ dadau ||10||
gautamādīnpūjayitvā dadau dānānyanekaśaḥ |
tataste tadanujñātā nāma procurmaharṣayaḥ ||11||
ayaṃ te bālatāṃ yātoʼnādisiddhoʼkhileśvaraḥ |
nirmātā sarvalokānāṃ carācaragurorguruḥ ||12||
māyāvī vedavedāntāgocaro madhyage ravau |
avatīrṇaḥ sumuharte kīrtimāviṣkariṣyati ||13||
bhādraśuklacaturthyāṃ tu śaśivāre ca vāyume |
siṃhalagne paccaśubhagrahayukteʼbhavatsutaḥ ||14||
śrīmānparākramayutoʼdbhutakarmā mahābalaḥ |
bhaviṣyati ca lokānāṃ bhaktānāmatisaukhyadaḥ ||15||
ekādaśadine śambho kuru nāmāsya sundaram |
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā gatāḥ sarve munayo nijamandiram ||16||
haro bālaṃ vinikṣipya girijāśayane śubhe |
visṛjya lokānakhilānbahirāyānmudānvitaḥ ||17||
tataḥ śeṣo yayau tatra phaṇācchāyāmathākarot |
nīrajaskāṃ dharāṃ cakrurmeghāstatra sukho vavau ||18||
māruto mālatīgandho nanṛtuścāpsarogaṇāḥ |
jagurgandharvasaṅaghāśca tuṣṭuvuścāraṇā vibhum ||19||
tato munigaṇāḥ sarve mṛnmūrti cakruruttamām |
maṇḍapaṃ śobhanaṃ kṛtvā kadalīpuṣpamaṇḍitam ||20||
pupūjuḥ parayā bhaktyā padmāsanagatā mudā |
guṇeśeti guṇeśeti jepuḥ sarve nirantaram ||21||
ahorātroṣitāḥ sarve rātrau ca jāgarānvitāḥ |
yathāśakti dvijānbhojya cakruste pāraṇaṃ dvijāḥ ||22||
evaṃ daśadine viprānbhojayāmāsurādarāt |
gṛhe gṛhe mahotsāhāṃścakrurdevasya tuṣṭaye ||23||
ekādaśe tu divase sarve munigaṇāḥ śivam |
āhūtā āyayurnāma kartuṃ śivasutasya ha ||24||
mānitāstena te samyakkārayāmāsuruttamam |
sarveśatvādguṇeśeti sarvavighnaharaṃ śubham ||25||
śivoʼpi tānmunīnprāha samyaṅanāma samīritam |
sarvārambheṣu pūjyoʼyamiti tasmai varaṃ dadau ||26||
pūjitā munayaḥ sarve dattvā dānāni sarvaśaḥ |
ājñāṃ gṛhītvā śambhoste svasvāśramapadaṃ yayuḥ ||27||
antargṛhagatā devī pupūje devatāḥ svayam |
tadādi sā tithiḥ khyātā guṇeśasya varapradā ||28||
tasyāṃ mahotsavaḥ kāryaścaturthyāṃ svaśubhāptaye |
mṛnmayīṃ pratimāṃ kṛtvā pūjayecca yathāvidhi ||29||
kṛtvā maṇḍapikāṃ cārumupoṣya jāgṛyānniśi |
modakāpūpalaḍḍūkaiḥ pāyasaiḥ pūjayedvibhum ||30||
aparasmindine viprānbhojayecca yathāvidhi |
ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyākānyathāśakti ca dakṣiṇām ||31||
dattvā tebhyo namaskṛtya paścādbhojanamācaret |
yo na pūjayate cāsyāṃ gaṇeśaṃ mṛnmayaṃ naraḥ ||32||
sa vighnairabhibhūtaḥ sannānārogai prapīḍyate |
na tasya darśanaṃ kuryātpatitasyeva karhicit ||33||
jāte tu darśane tasya gaṇeśaṃ nāma saṃsmaret |
caturthyā mahimānaṃ no na śakyaṃ sunirūpitum ||34||
karmāṇi tu guṇeśasya yathāmati nirūpaye |
guptarūpāḥ sindhudūtāḥ śaṃkarālayasaṃsthitāḥ ||35||
tamudantaṃ tu te gatvā prāvadansindhave tadā |
samāsīnāya yodhaiśca bahubhirveṣṭitāya ca ||36||
sthāpayitvāʼpsaronṛtyaṃ gānaṃ gandharvasaṃbhavam |
dūtā ūcuḥ |
dakṣiṇe daṇḍakāraṇye trisandhyākṣetrasaṃjñite ||37||
gatvā tatra dṛṣṭavanto bhagavantaṃ śivābhidham |
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇāṃ munīnāmāśramānbahūn ||38||
ekasmindivase bālaṃ śivā prāsūta ṣaḍbhujam |
lāvaṇyakośaṃ trailokyeʼnupamaṃ mahasojjvalam ||39||
daśāhassu mahotsāhairgateṣu munipuṃgavāḥ |
cakrurnāma guṇeśeti tasya bālasya śaṃkaraḥ ||40||
vayaṃ vāditranirghoṣairbadhirā iva tasthitama |
yadi hantuṃ gamiṣyāmaḥ saptakoṭigaṇāstadā ||41||
ekaikasya balaṃ ghoraṃ parvatonmūlanakṣamam |
nihanyuḥ sahasāʼbhyetya vṛttāntaṃ ko nivedayet ||42||
ka uvāca |
eva vadatsu dūteṣu śuśruve vyomavāk tadā |
utpannaḥ kvāpi te hantā sindho sāvadhatāṃ vraja ||43||
sindhuruvāca |
vadhyatāṃ vadhyatāṃ koʼyaṃ brūte duṣṭavacoʼtra ha |
ka uvāca |
keciduḍḍīya patitā dhāvayitvāʼpare yayuḥ ||44||
tāvaddaityo mahāmūrcchāṃ saṃprāptaḥ patito bhuvi |
cintāmagno mlānamukho nirutsāho hataprabhaḥ ||45||
uvāca viskhaladvāco muhūrtātprāpya cetanām |
trailokyakaṇṭakasyāpi kaṇṭakaḥ ka upasthitaḥ ||46||
kathaṃ hanyādgajaḥ siṃhaṃ maśako vātha vāraṇam |
trayastriṃśatkoṭidevā nirjitāḥ kṣaṇamātrataḥ ||47||
tasya me maraṇaṃ kasmājjāyeta vyomavāṅmṛṣā |
ṛtāʼstu vā gamiṣyāmi bhakṣituṃ tamahaṃ ripum ||48||
evamuktvā kṣaṇārdhātsa bhadrāsanagatoʼbhavat |
sarve vīrāstato yātāḥ śrutvā vārtāṃ tathāvidhām ||49||
tvameva mṛtyormṛtyuśca tatra mṛtyuḥ kathaṃ bhavet |
ityūcuste bhavedvāpi taṃ kyaṃ ghātayāmahe ||50||
svarge bhūmau tale vāpi gagane vā vasedyadi |
dehyājñāṃ no gamiṣyāmo daityāstānabravīdatha ||51||
sakhīnāṃ sevakānāṃ ca pītvā vākyāmṛtaṃ param |
prītoʼhaṃ yāntu śīghraṃ tu yatra me sa ripurbhavet ||52||
anekamāyayā hatvā ripuṃ mama nivedyatām |
ka uvāca, evamājñāṃ gṛhītvā te saṃkhyātītā yayustataḥ ||53||
trisandhyākṣetramāsādya guptarūpeṇa cāvasan |
gaurīsutaṃ tatra kutra māyayā hanma ityuta ||54||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe dvyaśītitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||82||

<adhyāya 83 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
śuklacandra iva brahmanvavṛdhe sa dine dine |
tato himālayaḥ prāyācchrutvā gaurīsutaṃ śubham ||1||
alaṃkārānsamādāya mūlyaratnasamanvitān |
ajire taṃ nirīkṣyaiva dhāvayitvā yayau sutā ||2||
āliliṃga mudā gauro bahukālāgataṃ ca tam |
ānandāśrūṇi sāʼmuñccatsoʼpi sā taṃ tatoʼvadat ||3||
gauryuvāca |
kathaṃ niṣṭhuratāṃ yāto vṛttāntaṃ mama karhicit |
na gṛhṇāsi na vā svīyaṃ preṣayasyapi nirdaya ||4||
himavānuvāca |
satyaṃ vadasi gauri tvaṃ kaṇṭhe prāṇā mama sthitāḥ |
atastvaddarśanākāṃkṣī prāptoʼhaṃ haravallabhe ||5||
vatse dhenumano yadvattadvattvayi mano mama |
ka uvāca |
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ sā dadāvāsanaṃ śubham ||6||
upaviṣṭoʼbravīdvākyaṃ punargirivaraḥ sutām |
śruto nāradavākyena putraste paramādbhutaḥ ||7||
tatastaṃ draṣṭumāyātaḥ sarvavighnaharaṃ śubham |
ka uvāca |
pārvatī bālamādāya pituraṃke nyaveśayat ||8||
soʼlaṃkārairalaṅkṛtya mahimānaṃ tatoʼbravīt |
sa uvāca |
ayaṃ bālo mahāmāyaḥ kariṣyati mahīmimām ||9||
niṣkaṇṭakāṃ yathā candraḥ śītalāṃ svakarairimām |
ayaṃ devagaṇānsarvānsvasthāne sthāpayiṣyati ||10||
kariṣyatyaṅghrisevāṃ te śiśorasya munīśvarāḥ |
etasya sarvarūpāṇi sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca ||11||
ayameva sadā dhyeyo brahmādīnāṃ sahasradṛk |
sahasrāṅaghriḥ sarvajagatkāraṇānāṃ ca kāraṇam ||12||
sahasrāsyoʼnantamūrtiḥ samaṣṭivyaṣṭirūpavān |
trailokye nedṛśo bālo dṛṣṭā kvāpi śubhāyavān ||13||
ahaṃ tanmayatāṃ yāto dṛṣṭvā tatpādapaṃkajam |
yathāʼmbhasi payaḥ kṣiptaṃ yāti tanmayatāṃ kṣaṇāt ||14||
asya yatnaṃ kuru śubhe nidhireṣa purātanaḥ |
ka uvāca |
mastake mukuṭaṃ cāsya svāṃgade bhujayorapi ||15||
paṃkajaṃ hṛdi śrutyośca kuṇḍale ratnabhūṣite |
kaṭisūtra kaṭau cāṃḍaghryormahārhāḥ kṣudraghaṇṭikāḥ ||16||
evaṃ dattvā bhūṣaṇāni nāma cakre śubhaṃ giriḥ |
herambeti mahāvighnaharaṃ bhaktābhayapradam ||17||
tato bhuktvā gṛhītvājñāṃ yayau svasthānamādirādra |
ekasmindivase bālaścikrīḍa nijamandire ||18||
gṛdhrāsuro gṛdhrarūpī mahābalaparākramaḥ |
pakṣavātena yasyādricayaścūrṇatvamīyivān ||19||
gaṇḍaśailāśca vṛkṣāśca pādāghātena tasya ca |
sapakṣacchāyayā bālamācchādya kheʼbhramattadā ||20||
nayanāni ca sarveṣāṃ pakṣāghātarajaḥkaṇaiḥ |
ācchādayanmahāśabdaiḥ śrotre badhiratāmiyet ||21||
tataścaṃcvā tu taṃ bālaṃ gṛhītvā gṛdhrapuṃgavaḥ |
vyomamārgaṃ gato dūraṃ garuḍo bhujagaṃ yathā ||22||
babhrāma gagane duṣṭoʼjānanbālaparākramam |
tatoʼpaśyadgirisutā bālaḥ kutra kuto gataḥ ||23||
nāpaśyaccatvare taṃ tu tataḥ śokākulāʼbhavat |
kena nīto mama suto duṣṭena ca durātmanā ||24||
upaviṣṭā tatoʼpaśyadvyomni gṛdhramukhe sutam |
mūrcchitā patitā bhūmau dhāva dhāveti cābravīt ||25||
mama prāṇā gamiṣyanti vinā putraṃ sureśvara |
kathaṃ mamopari vyoma patitaṃ śaṃkaraḥ katham ||26||
naiṣṭhuryaṃ paramaṃ prāpto mayyeva jagadīśvaraḥ |
dvādaśābdaṃ tapastaptaṃ nirāhāratayā mayā ||27||
karuṇābdhiḥ svaprakāśoʼvatīrṇoʼbhūnmamālayam |
sa gato nidhirūpo meʼbhāgyāyāḥ pāpasaṃcayāta ||28||
gate bāle gataṃ sarvaṃ sukhamātyantikaṃ mama |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ tasyāṃ tu śocantyāṃ śuśucustatsakhījanāḥ ||29||
tataste tatsamādhānaṃ cakrurjñānena yuktibhiḥ |
anādinidhane bāle na śokaṃ kartumarhasi ||30||
svaprakāśeʼvatīrṇe te bahvanuṣṭhānayogataḥ |
kutra vā mṛnmayī mūrtiścetanāmagamadvada ||31||
sthirā bhava kṣaṇena tvaṃ drakṣyase svasutaṃ śubhe |
antakasyāntako bālaḥ punareṣyati niṣṭhayā ||32||
sakhījane vadatyevaṃ tāvadbālaḥ svamuṣṭinā |
dadhāra cañcu balavānyathā śvāso na niryayau ||33||
śvāsarodhādgatāsuḥ sa gṛdhrastena samanvitaḥ |
nipapāta mahīpṛṣṭhe vajrāhata ivācalaḥ ||34||
patamāno gṛdhradeho daśayojana vistṛtaḥ |
cūrṇayāmāsa gehāni taccitramabhavadbhṛśam ||35||
caityavṛkṣeṣu bhagneṣu yayuḥ pakṣigaṇā diśaḥ |
tato dṛṣṭvā tu taṃ bālamavadatsa sakhīgaṇaḥ ||36||
ayamakṣatadehastu patito girijāsutaḥ |
ka uvāca |
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ tasya tvaritā bālamādade ||37||
gaurī nidhāya hṛdaye paramānandamāpa ca |
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ prāpya dadau dānānyanekaśaḥ ||38||
gatamasya mahāriṣṭamagre na syāditi dvijā |
namaskṛtya dvijānprāyānmandirābhyantaraṃ śivā ||39||
daśayojanavistāraḥ kvāsuro balavattaraḥ |
kva bālaḥ komalatanurlaghurūpo jaghāna tam ||40||
etāvato balamidamagre kiṃ vā kariṣyati |
ityuvāca sakhīṃ gaurī stanapānaṃ dadau mudā ||41||
ayaṃ laghurna mantavyo yathā vahnikaṇo dahet |
mahāntaṃ kāṣṭhanicayaṃ tathāʼnena hatoʼsuraḥ ||42||
tatastaddehakhaṇḍāni gaṇā dūre nicikṣipuḥ |
śṛṇuyādidamākhyānaṃ soʼsurairnābhibhūyate ||43||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe tryaśītitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||83||

<adhyāya 84 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
māse dvitīye gaurī taṃ sāyamabhyajya bālakam |
sthāpayāmāsa dolāyāṃ gāyati sma mudā bahu ||1||
tāvadākhū samāyātau māyārūpadharau sutam |
tau kṣemakuśalau daityau tadvadhākāṃkṣiṇāvubhau ||2||
nakhadaṃṣṭrāyudhau ghorau mandirābhyantaraṃ gatau |
yudhyantau pālanābhyāśe sphuradaṃgaruhau khalau ||3||
yayoścītkāraśabdena śrutayo badhirīkṛtāḥ |
pādāghātena bhūḥ sarvā kṛdakṣetrasamā kṛtā ||4||
tato yaṣṭiṃ gṛhītvā sā bhīṣayāmāsa tāvubhau |
yanaparau cobhāvupariṣṭādgatau tadā ||5||
yudhyantau patitau ghorau bālakopari tāvubhau |
urasthalābhighātātsa bubudhe bālakottamaḥ ||6||
kṛtvā karkaśaśabdaṃ tu sā bibhāya tadā śivā |
bālabhāvena hastau sa cālayāmāsa bhītitaḥ ||7||
tadāghātādubhau naṣṭajīvitau patitāvadhaḥ |
vamantau rudhiraṃ vaktrāddṛṣṭvā bhrāntā śivāʼbhavat ||8||
kicijjīvitaśeṣau tau bahirnītau gaṇaistadā |
mokṣaṃ jagmaturāghātādgaṇeśakarasaṃbhavāt ||9||
taddehau patitau ghorau daśayojanavistṛtau |
kolāhalo mahānāsīddṛṣṭvā dehau tathā gatau ||10||
gaṇānāṃ ca sakhīnāṃ ca pārvatyā sumahāṃstadā |
vidhvastā āśramā vṛkṣāḥ parvatā bhuvanāni ca ||11||
patadbhyāṃ taccharīrābhyāṃ nirjiyābhyāṃ mahāntyapi |
chittvā chittvā tu taddehau gaṇā ninyurbahistadā ||12||
pārvatī bhayavitrastā bālamādāya satvaram |
pāyayāmāsa snehena stanyaṃ bālakavatsalā ||13||
ṛṣipatnyaḥ samāyātā pārvatīmūcireʼnaghāḥ |
puṇyaṃ mahattu te gauri yena vighnaṃ vinaśyati ||14||
dvimāsikena bālena hatau duṣṭau mahāsurau |
agre tribhuvaneʼnena sadṛśo na bhaviṣyati ||15||
mātā rākṣasabhūratra yatnaḥ kāryaḥ śiśāviha |
neṣyanti rākṣasā guptā asurā māyinaḥ pare ||16||
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā gatā devyo gaṇāstāṃ tu samūcire |
ayaṃ tu te śive bālo haniṣyati dine dine ||17||
asmābhistu kiyatkālaṃ bahiḥkāryā hatāsurāḥ |
tatastu munayo jagmū rakṣāṃ cakrurathārbhake ||18||
dadau dānāni tebhyaḥ sā visasarja praṇamya tān |
tatastṛtīye māse tu svasvakāryarate jane |
madhyāhne pārvatī bālaṃ gṛhītvā maṃcakeʼsvapat ||19||
tatparīcārikāḥ suptā kāścitkāryāntare sthitāḥ |
cakruḥ kāściddvāri vārtāmetasminnantareʼsuraḥ ||20||
kūrākhyaścotuveṣaṇa viveśa girijālayam |
vaṃcayitvā lokadṛṣṭiṃ śveva duṣṭo mahābalaḥ ||21||
sa nirīkṣya śivāṃ suptāṃ jahṛṣe manasā tadā |
yāvannidrāti girijā tāvanneṣye śiśuṃ tvaham ||22||
lāghavānmaṃcakārūḍho bālamādāya mastake |
maṃcakādhaḥ papātāśu cukrośa bālakastadā ||23||
tataḥ prabuddhā girijā biḍālāsyagataṃ śiśum |
nirīkṣya dhāva dhāveti prākrośadbhayavihvalā ||24||
nimīlya nayane mūrcchāmagādduḥkhena bhītitaḥ |
kaṇṭhe daśati yāvatsa tāvadbālo dadhāra tam ||25||
karṇayorubhayordorbhyāṃ svabhāvātpādatāḍanāt |
nyapātayacchirodeśe maṃcakādho mahāsuram ||26||
sa bhinnahṛdayoʼdhāvadbahirgatvā papāta ha |
mahāvātena vṛkṣāttu pakvaṃ kiṃcitphalaṃ yathā ||27||
durgandhaṃ rudhiraṃ tasya patitaṃ dharaṇītale |
ācchādayannāsikāṃ sā girijā śokavihvalā ||28||
ādāya tasmai prādātsā stanapānaṃ śiśostadā |
āgatā bālaśabdena sarvāstāḥ paricārikāḥ ||29||
spṛśanti sma tadāṃgāni pratyekaṃ kautukānvitāḥ |
ajarāmaratāmasya niścitya manasā tu tāḥ ||30||
sa tu dīrgho biḍāloʼbhūttadā dvādaśayojanam |
kiyatyaścūrṇitāḥ sakhyaḥ kiyatyaḥ paricārikāḥ ||31||
gaṇāścaiva samāyātā otuṃ dṛṣṭvā tathāvidham |
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ cakrurbālakasya parākrame ||32||
kva daityo māyiko duṣṭaḥ kva bālacaraṇāhatiḥ |
indrādīnāmavadhyā ye hanyanteʼnena līlayā ||33||
ityuktvājñāṃ gṛhītvā te gaṇā yātā nijaṃ padam |
kecicca balino daityamākṛṣya tatyajurbahiḥ ||34||
kaṭideśe gṛhītvainaṃ sāʼviśannijamandiram |
tataścutarthe māse tu munipatnyaḥ samāgatāḥ ||35||
nānāsaubhāgyadravyāṇi gṛhītvottarage ravau |
abālāśca sabālāśca pārvatyāmaṃdiraṃ śubham ||36||
tāsāmāsanasatkārāndadau dākṣāyaṇī tadā |
namaskaroti tā nārīḥ kaṭau bālaṃ pragṛhaya ca ||37||
parasparaṃ samarcantyāṃ haridrākuṃkumādibhiḥ |
bhūmau saṃsthāpya bālāni gatimantyagatīni ca ||38||
pārvatīyastatasteṣu krīḍate smāpi bālakaḥ |
tena sarve śuśubhire śaśinoḍugaṇo yathā ||39||
dṛṣṭvā tā munipatnyastamūcurgirisutāmiti |
dhanyāʼsi gauri putreṇa śivadattena bhāsvatā ||40||
tava bālasya sānnidhyādbhāsante noʼpi bālakāḥ |
nidhisaṃgena hematvaṃ lohenāpi samāpyate ||41||
evaṃ kautukayuktāstā ānarcuśca parasparam |
haridrābhiḥ kuṃkamekṣukhaṇḍacandanagṛṃjakaiḥ ||42||
tilairguḍaistālapatrairnānāparimalairapi |
kāścidaṃge haridrāṃ ca mukheṣu ca lilimpire ||43||
etasminantare daityo nāmnā bālāsuro mahān |
bālarūpī samavayāstanmadhye krīḍituṃ yayau ||44||
cikrīḍa pārvatīyena yudhyanniva vinodataḥ |
yathāsiṃhena kroṣṭā tu mahiṣo vā gajena ca ||45||
kaṇṭhena kaṇṭhaṃ saṃghṛṣya petaturbhuvi dāruṇau |
mastake caraṇābhyāṃ soʼhanadduṣṭo gajānanam ||46||
kacādānāya hastābhyāṃ vicakarṣa balādamum |
svayaṃ ca garjate duṣṭo vṛddhaḥ kroṣṭā yathā niśi ||47||
dṛṣṭvā tathāvidhau tau sā girijā prāha tāḥ striyaḥ |
kasyarṣerdṛḍhabāloʼyaṃ tāḍayitvā śiśuṃ mama ||48||
krandateʼsau khara iva prahare prahare khalaḥ |
arbhakāṇāṃ svabhāvoʼyamityupekṣitavatyasau ||49||
tau tu bhūmigatau bālau luṇṭhataḥ smānuvatsalau |
dhṛtvā parasparaṃ keśānākarṣantaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ ||50||
hasantau hāsayantau ca bālakāṃśca munistriyaḥ |
jānannapi mahādaityaṃ khelati sma vināyakaḥ ||51||
tato bālāsuro duṣṭo hastābhyāṃ balavattaram |
jīvagrāhaṃ kaṇṭhadeśe dadhāra gaṇanāyakam ||52||
tathā taṃ soʼpi jagrāha ruddhaśvāsoʼsuroʼbhavat |
vihvalaṃ daityaputraṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā duścittatāṃ striyaḥ ||53||
yayuśca cakruśurbālo nihatoʼnena niścitam |
dhāvamānā yayuḥ kāścinnāśakanmocituṃ hi tau ||54||
striyaśca puruṣāḥ sarve tatastāmambikāṃ śubhām |
ūcurvimocayainaṃ tvaṃ muniputro mariṣyati ||55||
tataḥ sā pārvatī bālaṃ muṃca muṃceti sāʼbravīta |
asminmṛte śapiṣyanti munayo nastaponvitāḥ ||56||
jīvadānātparaṃ puṇyaṃ nāsti brahmāṇḍagolake |
gamiṣyati mahāśaktistava teṣāṃ tu śāpataḥ ||57||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ yāvatprārthayate girijā tāvadeva hi |
netradvāreṇa tatprāṇā gatā budbudavatkṣaṇāt ||58||
dadṛśustaṃ tataḥ sarve daśayojanamāyatam |
karālavaktraṃ patitaṃ cūrṇayitvā mahādrumān ||59||
tataḥ striyo yayurbhītā bālamādāya satvarāḥ |
vṛkavṛndaṃ hi dṛṣṭvaiva dhenavoʼjā yathā bhayāt ||60||
girijāpi svakaṃ gṛhaya bhrāmayitvā sutaṃ mudam |
dattvā stanaṃ kārayitvā śāntikaṃ dvijapuṃgavaiḥ ||61||
dadau dānāni tebhyaḥ sā jagṛhe āśiṣo bahu |
na jāne āsurīṃ māyāṃ kiyanto yānti nāśitum ||62||
tataste vilayaṃ yānti mama bālānmahākhalāḥ |
īśvare sānukūle kaḥ pīḍituṃ kṣamate janam ||63||
ka uvāca |
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ munipatnyo yayuśca te |
gaṇāstaṃ dūrataḥ kṣiptvā snātvā svaṃ dhāma lebhire ||64||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe bālāsuravadho nāma caturaśītitamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||84||

< adhyāya 85 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
śrutvā munimukhātputraṃ girijāyā mahābalam |
paṃcame māsi saṃprāpte marocirvīkṣituṃ yayau ||1||
bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyajñaḥ samaloṣṭhāśmakāṃcanaḥ |
vedaśāstrārthatattvajño nyastasvaparavibhramaḥ ||2||
saṃhāre rakṣaṇe sṛṣṭau manasā jagataḥ kṣamaḥ |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā pādayostasya patitā sakhibhiḥ saha ||3||
upaveśyāsane pādau kṣālayāmāsa bhaktitaḥ |
tajjalaṃ pāvanaṃ pītvā siṣecāntarguhaṃ madā ||4||
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pupūjeśvarabhāvanā |
uvāca pārvatī tuṣṭā labhyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ padaṃ hi te ||5||
muniruvāca |
ahamātmamanā devi na vrajāmi hi kutracit |
akasmāddhyānaviṣayastava putro babhūva me ||6||
tasya te darśanākāṃkṣī śibasyāpyahamāgataḥ |
ka uvāca |
evamākarṇya sā vākyaṃ tasyarṣestanayaṃ svakam ||7||
atiprītimanā gaurī tasyāṃke saṃnyaveśat |
ūce sā taṃ mahābhāgyaṃ phalitaṃ darśanāttava ||8||
puṇyaṃ ca paramaṃ prāptaṃ śubhamagre bhaviṣyati |
muniruvāca |
gauri teʼyaṃ suto brahmasvarūpo bhāsate mama ||9||
ṣaṭcakrabhedanaratā yoganiṣṭhā maharṣayaḥ |
vāyubhakṣyā upāsante yaṃ te soʼyaṃ tavātmajaḥ ||10||
guṇatrayabibhedena tritanustrikriyākaraḥ |
yatsattayātha śeṣoʼpi vahate mastake bhuvama ||11||
aṇurūpaḥ sthūlarūpaḥ saptaviṃśatitattvakṛt |
cetanācetanaṃ viśvaṃ yadrūpaṃ sarvakarmakṛt ||12||
yuge daśabhujaḥ pūrve siṃhārūḍho vināyakaḥ |
dvitīye ṣaḍbhujo barhivāhanaḥ śaśisannibhaḥ ||13||
mayūreśa iti khyāto nānādaityanibarhaṇaḥ |
tṛtīye tu yuge devo raktavarṇaścaturbhujaḥ ||14||
gajānana iti khyāto dhūmravarṇaḥ kalau yuge |
dhūmaketuriti khyāto dvibhujaḥ sarvadaityahā ||15||
etasya rakṣaṇaṃ gauri daityebhyaḥ kuru sarvadā |
gauryuvāca |
eṣoʼticapalo bālo daityānhanti bahūnapi ||16||
agre kiṃ karma karteti na jāne munisattama |
daityā nānāvidhā duṣṭāḥ sādhudevadruhaḥ khalāḥ |
atoʼsya kaṇṭhe kiṃcittvaṃ rakṣārthaṃ baddhumarhasi ||17||
muniruvāca |
dhyāyetsiṃhagataṃ vināyakamamuṃ digbāhumādye yuge
tretāyāṃ tu mayūravāhanamamuṃ ṣaḍbāhukaṃ siddhidam |
dvāpāre tu gajānanaṃ yugabhujaṃ raktāṃkarāgaṃ vibhuṃ
turyetu dvibhujaṃ sitāṃgaruciraṃ sarvārthadaṃ sarvadā ||18||
vināyakaḥ śikhāṃ pātu paramātmā parātparaḥ |
atisundarakāyastu mastakaṃ sumahotkaṭaḥ ||19||
lalāṭaṃ kāśyapaḥ pātu bhrūyugaṃ tu mahodaraḥ |
nayane bhālacandrastu gajāsyatvoṣṭhapallavau ||20||
jihvāṃ pātu gaṇakrīḍaścibukaṃ girijāsutaḥ |
vācaṃ vināyakaḥ pātu dantānrakṣatu durmukhaḥ ||21||
śravaṇau pāśapāṇistu nāsikāṃ cintitārthadaḥ |
gaṇeśastu mukhaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ pātu devo gaṇaṃjayaḥ ||22||
skandhau pātu gajaskandhaḥ stanau vighnavināśanaḥ |
hṛdayaṃ gaṇanāthastu herambo jaṭharaṃ mahān ||23||
dharādharaḥ pātu pārśvaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ vighnaharaḥ śubhaḥ |
liṃgaṃ guhyaṃ sadāʼpātu vakratuṇḍo mahābalaḥ ||24||
gaṇakrīḍo jānujaṃghe ūrūmaṃgalamūrtimān |
ekadanto mahābuddhiḥ pādau gulphau sadāʼvatu ||25||
kṣipraprasādano bāhū pāṇī āśāprapūrakaḥ |
aṃgulīśca nakhānpātu padmahastoʼrināśanaḥ ||26||
sarvāgāṃni mayūreśo viśvavyāpī sadāʼvatu |
anuktamapi yatsthānaṃ dhūmraketuḥ sadāʼvatu ||27||
āmodastvagrataḥ pātu pramodaḥ pṛṣṭhatoʼvatu |
