te ūcu |
tyaktvāʼsmāndaityajaṭhare mṛtānkvāsti gato bhavān ||42||
tvatsmṛtestasya jaṭhare jīvitā nirgatāstataḥ |
mayūreśa uvāca |
sarvavyāpī sarvagataḥ sarvavettāʼkhileśvaraḥ ||43||
na tyaktavānkadācidvo mā cintāṃ kartumarhatha |
ka uvāca |
tato yayau mayūreśo bālakaiḥ pariveṣṭitaḥ ||44||
keciddhāvanti purato nānāśabdakarā bhṛśam |
kecicchatradharāḥ keciddaṇḍacāmaradhāriṇaḥ ||45||
dhūlidhvajaṃ samālokya yayurmunigaṇā bahi |
śikhyārūḍhaṃ mayūreśaṃ bālakai pariveṣṭitam ||46||
ūcuḥ parasparaṃ sarve munayo vismayānvitāḥ |
gṛheʼsminśiśavaścāmī kutoʼnyeʼsya samīpataḥ ||47||
tāṃśca tānmīlitāḥ sarveʼpaśyaṃte devarūpiṇaḥ |
vicāreṇa ca tānsarve parabrahmasvarūpiṇaḥ ||48||
ānandahradamagnāste na viduḥ svaparaṃ punaḥ |
nāpaśyaṃste punastāṃstānbhrāntā iva vyalokayan ||49||
māyayā mohitāḥ sarve svānsvānbālānvyalokayan |
kaścitpitaramāsādya paṭhati sma yathā purā ||50||
kaścinmātaramāsādya pibati sma stanaṃ mudā |
āliliṃgātha jananīṃ pitaraṃ vāpi kaścana ||51||
bhrātaraṃ tāḍayitvāʼnyo ruroda tāḍanādiva |
umāpi tanayaṃ dṛṣṭvāliṃgya prādātstanaṃ mudā ||52||
kathaṃ cirataraṃ yāta iti krodhādabhāṣata |
ādāya haste girijā mayūreśaṃ gṛhaṃ yayau ||53||
sarve te munayo jagmuḥ śiśubhiḥ svaṃ svamāśramam ||54||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe bhagāsuravadho nāma śatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||100||

<adhyāya 101 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
tatastu daśame varṣe sukhāsīnaṃ maheśvaram |
girijāsahitaṃ vāme saptakoṭigaṇānvitam ||1||
nṛtyattaṃ taṃ mayūreśaṃ paśyantaṃ svāṃgaṇe śiśum |
āyayurmunayaḥ sarve gautamādyāḥ praṇamya tam ||2||
ūcuḥ prabuddhamatayo mahādevaṃ mahaujasam |
yāvatkālaṃ śivātra tvaṃ mayūreśasamanvitaḥ ||3||
sthitoʼsi tāvaddaityānāmanantānāṃ samāgamaḥ |
vayaṃ tena prapīḍyāmoʼnyatra yāhi śivādhunā ||4||
vayaṃ vānyatra gacchāmo yadyājñā te bhaveddhara |
śiva uvāca |
bhavatsaṃgena ca mayā navavarṣāṇi saukhyataḥ ||5||
atikrāntāti vighnāni mayūreśena tāni ca |
bhavatāṃ gamaneʼsmākaṃ sthitvā kinnu prayojanam ||6||
ahameva prayāsyāmi sthalamanyannirākulam |
ka uvāca |
iti śambhuvacaḥ śrutvā munayastaṃ praṇamya te ||7||
jaya deva mayūreśetyuktvā svasthānamāyayuḥ |
śivoʼpi gaṇasaṃyukto vṛṣārūḍhoʼmbikāyutaḥ ||8||
barhyārūḍhaṃ mayūreśaṃ puraskṛtvā mudānvitaḥ |
nānāvāditranirghoṣairnādayanviṣṭapaṃ tataḥ ||9||
anvayurmunayaḥ sarve mayūreśaṃ śivānvitam |
patnyoʼpi bālakai sārddhaṃ snehagadgadabhāṣibhiḥ ||10||
rajasā vyomani tate na prājñāyata kiṃcana |
parāvṛtya śivaḥ sarvāndakṣiṇābhimukho yayau ||11||
dadṛśurmārgamadhye taṃ dvādaśākṣauhiṇīyutam |
kamalāsuramāyāntaṃ dhūlidhūsaritāmbaram ||12||
gajāśca purato yānti rathāśvapādacāriṇaḥ |
na jñāyate ravestejaḥ śastradīptyātha kiṃcana ||13||
kolāhalo mahānāsīdgajāśvarathapattinaḥ |
nānāvidhānāṃ śastrāṇāṃ saṃghaṭṭasvanamiśritaḥ ||14||
dadṛśustaṃ mahādaityaṃ śivacārāḥ puro gatāḥ |
śaṃkāsurasya taṃ bandhuṃ nānābhūṣāvirājitam ||15||
nānāyudhadharaṃ procurāgatya tu śivaṃ gaṇāḥ |
pādāghātena tasyāśu kampate kamaṭhādayaḥ ||16||
munayoʼgrecarāḥ peturbhayabhītāstamabruvan |
mayūreśa mahābhāga kathaṃ rakṣasi noʼkhilān ||17||
ka uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā prāvadatsa guṇeśvaraḥ |
vidyamāne śive cintā na kāryā munipuṃgavā ||18||
mayūreśo haraṃ prāha namaskṛtya tataḥ punaḥ |
deva uvāca |
mahāsenāyuto daityo nāmnāʼsau kamalāsuraḥ ||19||
yadi teʼnugraho me syādyuddhāyāśu vrajāmyaham |
śiva uvāca |
samyaguktaṃ tvayā putra hṛdayānandanaṃ vacaḥ ||20||
dvādaśākṣauhiṇīsenāyutoʼsau daityapuṃgava |
ekākī kathamāyāsi saptakoṭigaṇairyutaḥ ||21||
vajryāpnuhi jayaṃ śīghraṃ jahi śatruṃ mahābalam |
deva uvāca |
tava prasādāttrailokyaṃ daheyamiti me matiḥ ||22||
kiyanto na hatā daityā na jñātāste tvayā nu kim |
asya kā gaṇanā svāminnāyāsye jayavānaham ||23||
ka uvāca |
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ śambhūrāliṃgya taṃ dadau |
triśūlaṃ putrahaste tanmūrdhni nyasya karaṃ śubham ||24||
ājñāṃ dadau svaputrāya gaṇayuktāya śaṃkaraḥ |
kṣveḍitairjayaśabdaiśca nādayansa diśo daśa ||25||
prayayau pramathairyukto vighnahā yuddhalālasaḥ |
vṛṣārūḍhaḥ śivāyukto haroʼpi draṣṭumanvagāt ||26||
dṛṣṭvāʼpārāṃ daityasenāṃ mayūreśaḥ śarīrataḥ |
nirmame bahulāṃ senāṃ tato yuddhamavartata ||27||
kālānurūpāste vīrā brahmāṇḍāśanatatparāḥ |
meroradhikamānāste śabdataḥ śṛṃgapātinaḥ ||28||
parasparaṃ nijaghnuste tumule saṃyuge tataḥ |
andhakāro mahānāsīdrajasācchāditā diśaḥ ||29||
vismayaṃ cakrire daityā idānīmayamekalaḥ |
anekatāṃ kathaṃ yātastasmādeṣa paraḥ pumān ||30||
bhūbhāraṃ hartukāmoʼyamavatīrṇaḥ śivālaye |
soʼpi dīrghataraṃ rūpaṃ dadhāra sahasā vibhuḥ ||31||
dṛṣṭvā dīrghaṃ daityarūpaṃ yogamāyāsamāvṛtaḥ |
daśāyudhadharo devo daśahasto mahābalaḥ ||32||
mayūreśaśca daityaśca yudhyamānau virejatuḥ |
nānāśastrapraharaṇairnā nāyuddhaviśāradaiḥ ||33||
vyastāvyastamayudhyanta gaṇāḥ senācarāśca te |
muṣṭiprahārairdantaiśca śastrāstrairitaretaram ||34||
keṣāṃcinmastakā bhagnā apareṣāṃ mukhāni ca |
jānujaṃghāvirahitā nipeturapare raṇe ||35||
garjayanto daśadiśaḥ śastrāstraghātanisvanai |
svāmisvanāmato jaghnurandhakāre rajaskṛte ||36||
kabandhāmārayaṃstatra parānsvānapi saṃmukham |
daityasenācarā bhagnā daityamevānvayustadā ||37||
asaṃkhyātānmṛtānprāhurnadyustu raktasaṃbhavāḥ |
daitya uvāca |
indrādayo lokapālā brahmādyā devatāgaṇāḥ ||38||
nirjitā yena teneha ko yuddhaṃ kurtumutsahet |
bhūgolaṃ nyubjatāṃ netuṃ koʼnyaḥ śakto bhaviṣyati ||39||
ka uvāca |
ityuktvāndolayāmāsa śastrāndolanato jagat |
krodhasaṃraktanayano mārayāmāsa daityarāṭ ||40||
mayūreśagaṇānsarvānnānāvāhanahetikān |
śiraḥpādānkṛtī hastānbhittvāʼpātayadāhave ||41||
utkarṣaṃ tasya taṃ dṛṣṭvā mayūreṣo yayu puraḥ |
svargaṃ ca garjayanvyoma daśaśastrairdviṣoʼhanat ||42||
asaṃkhyātā hatā daityā muktiṃ prāpuḥ sudurlabhām ||43||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe daityasenāvadho nāmaikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||101||

<adhyāya 102 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
vinaṣṭe bahudhā sainye rurohāśvaṃ sa daityarāṭ |
khaḍgapāṇiryayau yoddhuṃ mayūreśaṃ ruṣānvitaḥ ||1||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tu mayūreśo ruroha svaṃ śikhaṇḍinam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā śīghramāyāntaṃ pannagāstraṃ sa daityarāṭ ||2||
japtvā mantraṃ ca śatadhā bhārgavādiṣṭamādarāt |
sasarja pannagāstraṃ tattejasā jajvalurdiśaḥ ||3||
sarpasainyairaveṣṭyanta devasenācarā narāḥ |
mṛtāḥ kecidgatāḥ kecitpātitāścāpareʼvanau ||4||
nananda devastaṃ daityamativīrya parākramam |
sādhu yudhyasi daityendra nedṛśaḥ koʼpi vīkṣitaḥ ||5||
evamuktvā ripuṃ devo garuḍāstramavāsṛjat |
tataḥ palāyitāḥ sarpāḥ prottiṣṭhaṃśca camūcarāḥ ||6||
cakrāstramasṛjaddevo daityasenānibarhaṇam |
tena kecinmṛtā daityāḥ kecicchinnāṅghrimastakāḥ ||7||
vibhinnajānujaṅghāśca chinnagulphāḥ pareʼpatan |
garjanti dhāva dhāveti pitarmātaḥ suteti ca, ||8||
muktiṃ yātā mayūreśaṃ dṛṣṭvā prāṇāṃ jahaśca ye |
evaṃ senā nirastāʼsya tato daityo ruṣāʼdravat ||9||
khaḍgapāṇī ruṣā garjanvegenāyādgajānanam |
tathāvidhaṃ ripuṃ dṛṣṭvā paraśuṃ jagṛhe vibhuḥ ||10||
yayau daityaṃ mahāvegamasṛjatparaśuṃ ca tam |
sa dahaṃstejasā vyoma diśaḥ pakṣigaṇānapi ||11||
acchinaddaityahastaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ ca śatadhā javāt |
sa cāpaṃ sahasā gṛhya sajyaṃ kṛtvā śarānbahūn ||12||
asaṃkhyātānbalādvidhyaṃśchādayāmāsa sainikān |
gaganaṃ ca diśaḥ sarvā jagarja ghananisvanaḥ ||13||
śarāghātena balavānnivārya śarapaṃjaram |
andhakāre bāṇamaye nājñāsītkopi kaṃcana ||14||
mayūreśoʼtha śīghraṃ tadāśvaṃ bhūmāvapātayat |
tatoʼntarikṣe tiṣṭhantsa proce ca gaṇanāyakam ||15||
aśvo nipātitastasya paśya me kautukaṃ mahat |
śikhaṇḍinaṃ haniṣyāmi paśyataste guṇeśvara ||16||
ubhayatra niṣaṃgasthāñśarānbhūmau vyacikṣipat |
cāpamākṛṣya karṇāntaṃ cikṣepa śarasaṃcayān ||17||
mayūreśasya senāyāṃ mārayansarvasainikān |
vavarṣa śarajālāni varṣākāle ghanā yathā ||18||
kiyanto bhinnasarvāgāṃ kecitprāṇāṃ jahuḥ surāḥ |
evaṃ naśyatsu vīreṣu mayūreṣo ruṣānvitaḥ ||19||
kamalāsurabāṇaistu viddhaṃ tyaktvā śikhaṇḍinam |
pāśaṃ kareṇa jagrāha tolayitvā sa sādaram ||20||
jagarja nādayanvyoma tatyāja mahasā yutam |
sa pātayandaityagaṇāndadāha ripusainikān ||21||
kaṇṭhe ca nyapatattasya daityasenādhipasya ha |
rurodha śvāsamasyāsau daityo rūpāntaraṃ śritaḥ ||22||
mūrdhnā saṃchādayāmāsa tatkṣaṇādravimaṇḍalam |
andhakāre ghoratame na prajñāyata kiṃcana ||23||
uvāca samukho bhūtvā mayūreśaṃ ruṣānvitaḥ |
mayā ki yudhyase bāla gaccha mātaḥ stanaṃ piba ||24||
ramasva bālakairno cenmariṣyasi mamāgrataḥ |
mama cākrandanenaiva kampate bhuvanatrayam ||25||
pāde bhūmau sthāpyamāne śeṣoʼpi calate hyadhaḥ |
mama muṣṭiprahāreṇa cūrṇībhūtā mahācalāḥ, ||26||
nakhāgreṇa śirasteʼhaṃ gamayiṣye rasātalama |
ka uvāca |
evamākarṇya dubuddhervākyaṃ proce gajānanaḥ |
piśācavanmadyapavatkiṃ valgasi mamāgrataḥ ||27||
na jayaṃ prāpnute kvāpi devadvijavinindakaḥ |
mama cedbhavitā krodho jvālayedbhuvanatrayam ||28||
yaśaste khyāpituṃ loke yuddhyāpi harṣanirbharaḥ |
no cedhuṃkaramātreṇa nītaḥ syādyamasādanam ||29||
ka uvāca |
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ daityaḥ krodhānalākulaḥ |
śveḍitaṃ pratiśabdena nādayanvidiśo diśaḥ ||30||
tadā garbhā nipatitāstasya śabdena tatkṣaṇāt |
pramathādyā gaṇāḥ kecinmūrchitā dharaṇīṃ gatāḥ ||31||
ākarṇaṃ dhanurākṛṣya mumoca kamalāsuraḥ |
śaravṛṣṭiṃ punā raudrāṃ mayūreṣe baleʼpi ca ||4644||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe kamalāsurasaṃgrāmo nāma dvyuttaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||102||

<adhyāya 103 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
utkarṣaṃ tasya daityasya dṛṣṭvā devastvarānvitaḥ |
āyayau purato daityaṃ jaghāna śaravṛṣṭibhiḥ ||1||
nyavārayata daityoʼpi svaśarairativegitaiḥ |
tatastutoṣa bhagavānmayūreśo guṇāgraṇī ||2||
darśayāmāsa tasmai sa viśvarūpamanantakam |
daśadikṣu mayūreśaṃ dadarśa kamalāsuraḥ ||3||
vismitaśchādya nayane hṛdi taṃ paridṛṣṭavān |
tataḥ palāyanaparo yāvadgacchati daityarāṭ ||4 ||
śikhāmākramya devoʼpi dhṛtvā provāca taṃ punaḥ |
tiṣṭha yudhyasva daityendra smara vākyāni tāni ca ||5||
punardadarśa daityastamekameva puraḥ sthitam |
adhāvatsahasā yoddhuṃ jagarja ca mahābalī ||6||
daśāyudhadharo devo bodhayāmāsa taṃ tadā |
bibhedāsya śarīraṃ sa vighnarāṭ śastraghātataḥ ||7||
yatrāsya nyapatadraktabindustatrāsuroʼbhavat |
tadrūpastadbalaścaivamasaṃkhyātāstadāʼsurāḥ ||8||
nānāśastrairathāstraiste taṃ jaghnuḥ śaravṛṣṭibhiḥ |
tataḥ parāvṛto devo mayūreśo ruṣā jvalan ||9||
sārddhakoṭitrayamitaiḥ siddhibuddhī tadā balaiḥ |
īyatuḥ paramakruddhe tato yuddhamavartatata ||10||
ūcaturgaṇarājaṃ te dehi bhakṣyaṃ kṣudhāpaham |
ta ūce bhakṣataṃ devo daityāsṛgjanitānbahūn ||11||
ityukte te tadā tena mayūreśena satvaram |
babhakṣaturbhūtagaṇaistāndaityānakhilānapi ||12||
devo jaghāna khaḍgena taṃ daityaṃ kamalāsuram |
punaśca śataśo jātā daityāstadraktasaṃbhavāḥ ||13||
babhakṣustānapi tato pibanraktaṃ samantataḥ |
tataḥ khinno mayūreśaḥ śūlamudyamya vegavān ||14||
tatyāja sahasā daitye yayau sa pradahandiśaḥ |
cūrṇayanparvatānvyoma nādayanbhāni pātayan ||15||
vegena patito daityadehaṃ bhittvā dvidhāʼkarot |
mastakastasya patito bhīmāya dakṣiṇe taṭe ||16||
kṛṣṇāyā uttare tīre tasthau tatra guṇeśvaraḥ |
sarve santuṣṭamanaso jayaśabdaistamabruvan ||17||
jaya deva mayūreśa kuru duṣṭavināśanam |
tatastatrāyayau gaurīpatirgaṇaparīvṛtaḥ ||18||
gautamādyaiśca munibhiḥ pārvatyā ca samāyutaḥ |
vādyatsu sarvavādyeṣu puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta khāt ||19||
āliṃgya pārvatī prādātstanapānaṃ mudā tadā |
munayastuṣṭuvuścainaṃ mayūreśaṃ sureśvaram ||20||
kamalākāntahṛdayahṛddayānandavardhana |
kamalākāntanamita kamalāsuranāśana ||21||
kamalāsevitapada jaya tvaṃ kamalāprada |
kamalāsanavandyeśa kamalākaraśītala ||22||
kamalāṃkasupādābja kamalāṃkitasatkara |
kamalābandhutilaka bhaktānāṃ kamalāprada ||23||
kamalāsūnuripuja kamalāsūnusundara |
kamalāpitṛratnānāṃ mālayā jaya śobhita ||24||
kamalāsurabāṇānāṃ kamalena nivāraka |
kamalākrāntakamalakośajitkarapaṃkaja ||25||
kamalāpatihastasthapadmakośanibhekṣaṇa |
sarvahṛtkamalānanda jaya sarvāghanāśana ||26||
kamalāṅakuśahastābja jayavighnaharāvyaya |
tvayā vinihataḥ pāpaḥ śakrādibhayado ripuḥ ||27||
vajracakrādyabhedyo yo munīnāmapi bhītidaḥ |
evaṃ stutvā pupūjaste gautamādyā maharṣayaḥ ||28||
daśabhirdorbhirāliṃgya pupūja giriśoʼpi tam |
tataste munayaḥ procurmayūreśaṃ ca śaṃkaram ||29||
atratvaṃ sarvadā tiṣṭha sarvadevagaṇairyutaḥ |
pūrayanbhaktakāryāṇi vārayanvighnasaṃcayāna ||30||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ taiḥ prārthitau devau sarvadevagaṇaiyutau |
pūrayantau bhaktakāmānharantau vighnasaṃcayāna ||31||
sthitau tatra mayūreśaśaṃkarau lokaśaṃkarau |
viśvakarmā vyadhāttatra mandiraṃ sundaraṃ tataḥ ||32||
asaṃkhyaśikharadvāramasaṃkhyāścaryasaṃyutam |
tatrābhūnnagaraṃ cāru sarvalokasamanvitam ||33||
mayūreśapuraṃ tasya nāma cakrurmaharṣayaḥ |
tapastepurmunigaṇā nānābharaṇasaṃśritāḥ ||34||
haroʼpi ca tapastepe girijāgaṇasaṃyutaḥ |
tasya dhyānaṃ pūjanaṃ ca smaraṇaṃ te vyadhurdvijāḥ ||35||
mayūreśaḥ punarbālaiḥ krīḍati sma yathā purā ||36||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe kamalāsuravadhonāma tryuttaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||103||

<adhyāya 104 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
ahaṃ prayātastaṃ draṣṭuṃ guptarūpo dvijottama |
adarśa tatra taṃ devamumayā sahitaṃ prabhum ||1||
snātvāsīnaṃ japantaṃ ca paraṃbrahmasanātanam |
ahaṃ tamastuvaṃ devaṃ mayūreśvarasaṃjñakam, ||2||
purāṇapuruṣaṃ devaṃ nānākrīḍākaraṃ mudā |
māyāvinaṃ durvibhāvyaṃ mayūreśaṃ namāmyaham ||3||
parātparaṃ cidānandaṃ nirvikāraṃ hṛdisthitam |
guṇātītaṃ guṇamayaṃ mayūreśaṃ… ||4||
sṛjantaṃ pālayantaṃ ca saṃharantaṃ nijecchayā |
sarvavighnaharaṃ devaṃ mayūreśaṃ… ||5||
indrādidevatāvṛndairabhiṣṭutamaharniśam|
sadasadavyaktamavyaktaṃ mayūreśaṃ… ||6||
nānādaityanihantāraṃ nānārūpāṇi bibhratam |
nānāyudhadharaṃ bhaktyā ma… ||7||
sarvaśaktimayaṃ devaṃ sarvarupadharaṃ vibhum |
sarvavidyāpravaktāraṃ mayūre… ||8||
pārvatīnandanaṃ śambhorānandaparivardhanam |
bhaktānandakaraṃ nityaṃ ma… ||9||
munidhyeyaṃ munīnutaṃ munikāmaprapūrakam |
samaṣṭivyaṣṭirūpaṃ tvāṃ ma… ||10||
sarvājñānanihantāraṃ sarvajñānakaraṃ śucim |
satyajñānamayaṃ satyaṃ ma… ||11||
anekakoṭibrahmāṇḍanāyakaṃ jagadīśvaram |
anantavibhavaṃ viṣṇuṃ … ||12||
tvaddarśanena pūtoʼhaṃ paramānandanirbharaḥ |
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ prāptastasya daityasya māraṇāt ||13||
yena śakroʼntako devā lokapālā jitā balāt |
sahasraśatayodhī yoʼsaṃkhyadeho raṇe hataḥ ||14||
tridhābhūtastristhaleṣu patitaḥ kamalāsuraḥ |
taṃ samāpūjayamahaṃ sarvatīrthajalaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||15||
kamaṇḍalugatairenonāśakairabhiṣecayam |
divyavastre divyagandhaṃ divyapuppamayīṃ śubhām ||16||
vanamālāmasya kaṇṭhe dattvā nāma vyadhāmaham |
vanamālīti lokeṣu vikhyātaṃ sarvamaṃgalam ||17||
pūjāvidhiṃ samāpyainaṃ pradakṣiṇāmatho vyadhām |
pādāghātena me tatra nyubjatāṃ gamitaḥ kṣaṇāt ||18||
nānātīrthajalairyuktaḥ sthāpito me kamaṇḍaluḥ |
yateʼhaṃ tajjalaṃ bhartuṃ tato māmabravīdvibhuḥ ||19||
mayūreśa uvāca |
idaṃ brahmakṛtaṃ stotraṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
sarvakāmapradaṃ nṛṇāṃ sarvopadravanāśanam ||20||
kārāgṛhagatānāṃ ca mocanaṃ dinasaptakāt |
ādhivyādhiharaṃ caiva bhuktimuktipradaṃ vidhe ||21||
sthiro bhava viriṃce tvaṃ nadīyaṃ sarvapāvanī |
kamaṇḍalubhavā nāmnā khyātā loke bhaviṣyati ||22||
darśanādvācikaṃ pāpaṃ sparśanācca manomayam |
snānācca kāyikaṃ sarvaṃ nāśayiṣyāti nimnagā ||23||
nirantaraṃ sevanācca mokṣameṣā pradāsyati |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ datte vare tena sasnustatra munīśvarāḥ ||24||
sadārāḥ saparīvārāḥ saptakoṭigaṇā aham |
tapastepustadā tatra munayaste mayā saha ||25||
tatoʼhaṃ māyayā tasya mohitaḥ prābruvaṃ bahūn |
jagatkartā jagadvandyaḥ śakrādisurapūjitaḥ ||26||
bālamekādaśābdaṃ tatkathaṃ praṇatavānaham |
arbhakaiḥ krīḍamānaṃ tamācārarahitaṃ śiśum ||27||
dhiṅme jñānaṃ mahatvaṃ ca paitāmahapadaṃ ca dhik |
imāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ vidhāyāhaṃ sthāsyāmi guptarūpadhṛk ||28||
ayaṃ cetparamātmā syādaparāṃ nirmametpunaḥ |
evaṃ niścitya manasā sṛṣṭimātmānameva ca |
antardhāya sthito yāvattāvaddevo gajānanaḥ ||29||
na dadarśa sakhīnbālāṃstatasteṣāṃ gṛhānagāt |
śūnyāndṛṣṭvā gṛhānsarvānkhinno vṛkṣānalokayat ||30||
na dadarśa drumāṃjantūnpaśupakṣigaṇānyadā |
tadā svargaṃ gato devo nāpaśyattatra tānsurān ||31||
gandharvānkinnarānyakṣānpitṛñśaśiravī na ca |
andhakāre gāḍhatame na prājñāyata kiṃcana ||32||
tatoʼsau praṇidhānena jñātvā brahmakṛtaṃ ca yat |
sāmarthyaṃ nijamāyāṃ ca āviścakreʼkhilātmakaḥ ||33||
brahmāṇḍaṃ nirmame devo mayūreśaḥ svalīlayā |
viśvaṃ carājaraṃ tatra mayūreśapurīmapi ||34||
yathāpūrvaṃ paśyati sma sarvaṃ tribhuvanaṃ vibhuḥ |
svaṃ ca bālaiḥ parivṛtaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ ca tathāvidham ||35||
śaṃkaraṃ ca bhavānīṃ tāmanuṣṭhānagatānmunīn |
tapanaṃ śaśinaṃ bhāni svargāndevagaṇānapi ||36||
pṛthivīṃ sarvavṛkṣāṃśca samudrānsaritoʼpi ca |
pātālāni mayūreśo dadarśa pūrvavadvibhuḥ ||37||
ka uvāca |
tatoʼhaṃ smṛtimāpannoʼpaśyandevaṃ cidātmakam |
kalākāṣṭhāmuhūrtādidinapakṣasvarūpiṇam ||38||
dṛṣṭvetthaṃ tamahaṃ natvā kṣamāpayamudāradhīḥ |
māsartuvarṣakalpādicarācarasvarūpiṇam ||39||
asaṃkhyabrahmāṇḍayutaromāṃcairupaśobhitam |
devarṣiyakṣagandharvasaritsāgararūpiṇam ||40||
manuṣyakinnaralatāvṛkṣapannagarūpiṇam |
dṛṣṭvetthaṃ tamahaṃ natvā kṣamāpamudāradhīḥ ||41||
punaḥ punarapaśyaṃ taṃ mayūreśaṃ kirīṭinam |
kuṇḍalābhyāṃ nūpurābhyāmaṃgadābhyāṃ virājitam ||42||
śeṣanābhiṃ sarpakaṇṭhaṃ divyamālāmbarāvṛtam |
divyasiṃhāsanagataṃ sarvadevairabhiṣṭutam ||43||
siddhibuddhisamāyuktaṃ vibhūtibhirupāsitam |
carācaramayaṃ sarvanamasyaṃ tatsvarūpiṇam ||44||
pratyekaṃ tatra cātmānaṃ tadrūpaṃ svaṃ vyalokayam |
tatastaṃ stutavānnatvā prārthayaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ ||45||
kṣamāparādhaṃ me deva tvanmāyāgarvitasya me |
prabhāvaṃ draṣṭukāmasya dīnasya śaraṇārthinaḥ ||46||
kṣaṇenānantabrahmāṇḍakāriṇe te namo namaḥ |
evaṃ vadantaṃ māṃ nāsāpavanātsvodareʼnayat ||47||
tatrāpaśyaṃ sarvalokaṃ yathā bahiḥ pradṛṣṭavān |
devaṃ tathāvidhaṃ cāpi koṭibrahmāṇḍaromakam ||48||
sarvaṃ carācaraṃ viśvaṃ yathā bhūmau yathā divi, ekasmādaṇḍataścānyadbrahmāṇḍāntaramāśiśam ||49||
tathaiva dṛṣṭavānsarvānevaṃ nānāṇḍamadhyagaḥ |
apaśyaṃ viśvamakhilaṃ svātmānaṃ devameva ca ||50||
tatakhinnoʼbruvaṃ devaṃ nāntaṃ paśyāmi te kvacit |
kṛpāṃ kuru mama vibho māyāmetāṃ nivāraya ||51||
tataḥ sa karuṇāviṣṭaḥ sarvamantardadhe kṣaṇāt |
punareva mayūreśamapaśyaṃ bālakairvṛtam ||52||
krīḍantaṃ pūrvavannatvāʼbruvaṃ māyāṃ na teʼvidam |
aparādhasahasraṃ me mātṛvattvaṃ kṣamasva ca ||53||
mayūreśoʼbravīnmāṃ hi mūrdhni hastaṃ niveśya ha |
deva uvāca |
na me krodho na bhinnā dhīḥ svīyaḥ para iti bhramaḥ ||54||
kasmādapi bhayaṃ me na na matto bhayamaṇvapi |
śrutveyaṃ vacanaṃ tasya mayūreśapuraṃ śivam ||55||
bhavānīmapi tatsūnumadrākṣaṃ munibālakaiḥ |
gṛhītvājñāṃ svakaṃ sthānamagamaṃ harṣanirbharaḥ ||56||
bhavānī bālakaināyānmandiraṃ sva ca bālakāḥ ||57||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe viśvarūpadarśanaṃ nāma caturadhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||104||

<adhyāya 105 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
tato bhādracaturthyāṃ tu te sarve munibālakāḥ |
mṛdamāhartumāyātā vimalāṃ tāmathānayan ||1||
svasvabuddhyā vidhāyāśu mūrtīrnānāvidhāḥ śubhāḥ |
siṃhārūḍhā mayūrasthāḥ kāścinmūṣakasaṃsthitāḥ ||2||
nānāyudhadharā nānādivyālaṃkārasaṃyutāḥ |
caturbhujā gaṇeśasya divyagandhāmbarāḥ śubhāḥ ||3||
bhaktyā paramayāʼpūjannupacāraiḥ pṛthakpṛthak |
maṇḍape tāḥ pratiṣṭhāpya puṣpādarśaprabhāyuji ||4||
padmapuṣpayutā mālā dadustāḥ sumanoharāḥ |
vādyatsu tūryaghoṣeṣu nagarī harṣamāpa sā ||5||
gaurī tasyāṃ janmatithau dvādaśābdikamarbhakam |
snāpayāmāsa ca dadau vāyanāni suvāsinīḥ ||6||
saṃpūjya vidhivadbhaktyā haridrākuṃkumādibhiḥ |
etasminnantare daityo viśvadeva iti śrutaḥ ||7||
bhagavadrūpamāsthāya śrīmudrāṃkitamāyayau |
śaṃkhacakra gadāpadmavanamālālasattanuḥ ||8||
tulasīdāmaruciro divyavastrānulepanaḥ |
kamaṇḍalu vāripūrṇaṃ veṇudaṇḍaṃ dadhatkare ||9||
gaurī tamatithiṃ dvāri tiṣṭhantaṃ prāha bhaktitaḥ |
kuta āgamyate brahmaṃstuṣṭāʼhaṃ tava tejasā ||10||
ityuktaḥ prāha tāṃ vipraḥ śrutavānasmi yatpurā |
taddṛṣṭvā nayanābhyāṃ te parituṣṭaṃ mano mama ||11||
kṣudhayā pīḍitaścāhaṃ bhavatīgṛhamāgataḥ |
pārvatyuvāca |
āsyatāṃ bhujyatāṃ svasthaṃ sa ca tatrāsane sthitaḥ ||12||
āsīnasya mayūreśo viṣṭareʼkṣālayattadā |
caraṇau tattīrthajalaiḥ siṣeca sarvatoʼmalaiḥ ||13||
saṃpūjya bhojayāmāsa pakvānnaiḥ pāyasairapi |
apośānakarastūṣṇīmāsīccintāturo dvijaḥ ||14||
umovāca dvijaṃ paścādbhujyatāṃ bhujyatāmiti |
yadi nānnaṃ manoyogyaṃ tadā dāsyeʼtivāñchitam |
vada tacchīghramānyeṣye śaṃkarasya prabhauvataḥ ||15||
dvija uvāca |
kṣīrārṇavasthitaṃ devaṃ dṛṣṭvā nityaṃ bhunajmyaham |
jalaṃ pibāmi taccādya vismṛtaṃ mohato mayā ||16||
umovāca |
pūrvameva kathaṃ nokto niyamaste dvijottama |
pātrādutthāya gantāsi kathaṃ sattvavilumpaka ||17||
śaṃkaraḥ kṣobhamāpanno na jāne kiṃ kariṣyati ||18||
mayūreśoʼbravīttatra yadi bhāvo dṛḍho hṛdi |
tadātraiva bhavetpadmanābherdarśanamāśu te ||19||
prāyoʼbhāgya mahaddṛṣṭaṃ yatpakvānnaṃ ca pāyasam |
tyaktvā vrajasi kutra tvaṃ mūḍhabhāvena vā dvija ||20||
bahujanmatapobhiryatkaṭākṣo bhuvi labhyate |
tayā te pariviṣṭānna kathaṃ tyajasi suvrata ||21||
brahmādīnāṃ na gamyā yā dṛṣṭā sākṣāttvayā śivā |
sarvalokasya jananī sarvayoṣidvarā hi sā ||22||
sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ śaktiḥ sā paramādbhutā |
taddarśanena devasya viṣṇorjātaṃ hi darśanam ||23||
cakorasyevāgnibhujo miṣṭānnaṃ naiva rocate |
tathā kiṃ te na roceta devānnamamṛtopamam |
mama vā sarvarūpasya darśaneʼsya dṛśirbhavet ||24||
dvija uvāca |
ramāpaterahaṃ dāso na namasyāmi kiṃcana |
yadi tvaṃ sarvarūpoʼsi yadi sāmarthyamasti te ||25||
tadā darśaya viśveśaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayam |
evaṃ buddhvā dṛḍhaṃ tasya niścayaṃ sa mayūrarāṭ ||26||
antardhāya prādurāsīnnārāyaṇasvarūpavān |
pītāmbaraḥ śaṃkhacakragadāpadmadharo vibhuḥ ||27||
nānālaṃkāraruciraḥ sarvālaṃkārasundaraḥ |
kaustubhena lasadvakṣā vanamālāvibhūṣitaḥ ||28||
śeṣaśāyī mahālakṣmyā saṃvāhitapadāmbujaḥ |
viśvadevaśca taṃ dṛṣṭvā nanāma paripūjya ca ||29||
yātastanmayatāṃ bhaktyā vismṛtya ca śarīratām |
uvāca ca cidānandaparipūrṇo dvijottamaḥ ||30||
adya dhanyaṃ janurmeʼdya jīvitaṃ ca sujīvitam |
adyeyaṃ nagarī dhanyā dhanyau tau pitarau mama ||31||
mayūreśopadeśena bhavānyā darśanena ca |
parātparasya viṣṇoste carācarasvarūpiṇaḥ ||32||
labdhaṃ darśanamatraiva sarvāntaryāmiṇo vibhoḥ |
nārāyaṇoʼpi taṃ bhaktaṃ bāhubhiḥ pariṣasvaje ||33||
uvāca ca prasannātmā bāṣpagadgadayā girā |
madarthaṃ tyaktavānbhuktiṃ kṣīrābdherahamāgataḥ ||34||
dṛṣṭvā te niścayaṃ bhaktiṃ dṛḍhāmavyabhicāriṇīm |
bhaktamārgaṃ prasiṃcāmi mārutoddhatakaṇṭakam ||35||
bhaktatṛṣānivṛttyarthaṃ jātoʼhaṃ jalarūpadhṛk |
nānārūpadharo jāto bhaktānāmārtināśanaḥ ||36||
yathā bhaktaḥ priyo nityamiti te darśayāmyaham |
abhūdbhādracaturthyāṃ hi mayūreśamahotsavaḥ ||37||
mūrtiṃ mahīmayīṃ kṛtvā barhyākhusiṃhagāṃ pṛthak |
nānālaṃkāraśobhāḍhyāṃ nānāraṃgaiḥ suśobhitām ||38||
anekaiḥ pūjya tāṃ samyagbālakairmunisaṃcayaiḥ |
kecidagre pragāyanti kecinnṛtyanti bhaktitaḥ ||39||
vācayanti purāṇāni pradakṣiṇaparāḥ pare |
etasminnantare pautro vasiṣṭhasya mahātmanaḥ ||40||
parāśara itikhyāta āyayau svecchayā muniḥ |
sa caturvārṣiko mūrtiṃ vidadhe bhaktitatparaḥ ||41||
śuṣkaparṇakṛtāṃ mālāṃ kṛtvā tasmai nyavedayat |
gandhabuddhyā kardamena tāṃ vyalepayadādarāt ||42||
miṣṭānnāni ca laḍḍūkāndakṣiṇāṃ ca phalāni ca |
nyavedayattadā patrairanekaiḥ kardamairapi ||43||
ṣaṃkalpaṃ līlayā cakraṃ jaṅghāvādyaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
muhūrtaṃ nartanaṃ kṛtvā ruroda bhakṣayeti ca ||44||
saṃprārthya mūrtiṃ bahudhā tataḥ sā cetanāṃ yayau |
yadyattena yaduddeśātsthāpitaṃ puratoʼbhavat ||45||
tattathaiva ca saṃjātaṃ tataḥ sāʼbhakṣayattadā |
laḍḍukānmodakānnānāpakvānnaṃ pāyasānvitam ||46||
viśvadevaśca taṃ dṛṣṭvā mithyābhūtaṃ yathārthavat |
mṛttikānnāni satyāni sarvaṃ puṣpaphalādikam ||47||
sacetanāṃ ca tāṃ mūrtiṃ ṣaḍbhujāṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ |
vyalokayadviṣṇurūpāṃ caturbāhuvirājitām ||48||
uvāca satataṃ devaṃ kimidaṃ mṛttikāśanam |
mūrtiruvāca |
bhaktyā yadarpitaṃ mahyaṃ sudhārasasamaṃ bhavet ||49||
abhaktārpitamamṛtaṃ viṣameva bhavenmama |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā viśvadevoʼbravītpunaḥ ||50||
kaste bhakto mama ca taṃ darśayasva sureśvara |
tatastasya karaṃ dhṛtvā bahirāyānmayūrarāṭ ||51||
gṛhe gṛhe pūjyamānaṃ devaṃ tasmai vyadarśayat |
śikhinaṃ susamārūḍhaṃ mayūreśamalokayat ||52||
tatrāpi paryapaśyatsa viśvadevayutaṃ ca tam |
nāyaṃ mama bhavatsvāmītyevaṃ buddhiṃ visṛjya tām ||53||
gṛhe gṛhe viśvadevo nārāyaṇamalokayat |
sarvatra sa dadarśātha pūjyamānaṃ guṇeśvaram ||54||
kvāpi kvāpi mayūreśaṃ dadarśa viṣṇurūpiṇam |
punarālokayaṃsta ttanmayūreśamalokayat ||55||
bhujaṃ svaṃ parigṛhyeva bahiryātaṃ svalīlayā |
ākṣipya hastaṃ sa prāyānnāyaṃ svāmī mameti ca ||56||
aṭadgṛhe gṛhe soʼtha pūjyamānaṃ dadarśa ha |
mayūreśaṃ vādyaghoṣairnṛtyagītairanekadhā ||57||
kvāpi dṛṣṭvā viṣṇurūpaṃ namaskartuṃ yathāvadha |
punarmayūreśarūpaṃ dadarśa sa śikhisthitam, ||58||
garuḍārūḍhamanyatra śeṣārūḍhaṃ tathā kvacita |
namaskartuṃ yadā yāti viśvadevo mudā yataḥ ||59||
tāvadbarhisamārūḍhaṃ paśyati sma punaḥ punaḥ |
bhuṃjantaṃ ca ramantaṃ ca svapantaṃ ca dadarśa saḥ ||60||
tataḥ khinno viśvadevo vasiṣṭhadvāramāgamat |
tatoʼbravīnmayūreśaḥ paśya me bhaktamuttamam ||61||
tataḥ parāśaraṃ soʼtha dadarśa purataḥ sthitam |
mṛnmayairupacāraistaṃ pūjayantaṃ guṇeśvaram ||62||
bhakṣayantaṃ ca laḍḍūkānmṛnmayāndevameva ca |
paśyati sma punarvyomni jale bhūmau tameva saḥ ||63||
kṣaṇaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ mayūreśaṃ kṣaṇādapi |
tataḥ svabhedabuddhiṃ svāṃ tatyāja bhrāntimeva ca ||64||
sarvabhāvena taṃ natvā stutvāʼbhedadhiyā punaḥ |
anujñāto mudā prāyādviśvadevaḥ svamāśramam ||65||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe viśvadevabhedabuddhinirāso nāmapaṃcādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||105||

<adhyāya 106 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
tatastrayodaśe varṣe namaskṛtya maheśvaram |
bhasmāṃgarāgaruciraṃ paṃcāsyaṃ digbhujaṃ śubham, ||1||
ruṇḍamālādharaṃ suptaṃ candraśekharamacyutam |
mayūreśoʼtha jagrāha śaśinaṃ tacchirogatam ||2||
krīḍanbahiḥ samāyāto bālakaiḥ parivāritaḥ |
suhṛdbhiḥ parinṛtyadbhirvadadbhiśca parasparam ||3||
etasminnantare tatra daityo maṃgalasaṃjakaḥ |
karālavadanaḥ kroḍīvijetā maramādbhutaḥ ||4||
varāharūpo balavāndantacchinnamahiruhaḥ |
vajrasārasaṭābhiḥ sa bhindanvyoma padairdharāma ||5||
aṃjanādririva śyāmo vahnikuṇḍanibhekṣaṇaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ vidrutā bālā varṣāghananibhasvanaḥ ||6||
na dṛṣṭaḥ sūkaraḥ kvāpīdṛśa ityabruvaṃśca te |
yāvanmārayate bālāṃstāvaddevo mayūrarāṭ ||7||
dadhāra dantayorduṣṭaṃ tadā sūkaradānavam |
athāpareṇa hastena dadhārādharapotrakam ||8||
pāṭayāmāsa sahasā vaṃśakhaṇḍamivārbhakaḥ |
pūrvadehaṃ samāsthāya daśayojanavistṛtam ||9||
patitaścūrṇayanvṛkṣāndārayandharaṇītalam |
tatastamabruvanbālā dhanyoʼyaṃ pārvatīsutaḥ ||10||
kṣaṇena nāśito daityo līlayāʼsau mahābalaḥ |
asya darśanatastrastā vayaṃ yātā diśo daśa ||11||
patamānena yeneha cūrṇitāni gṛhāṇi ca |
ka uvāca |
tataḥ śivo lalāṭe sve nāpaśyacchaśinaṃ yadā ||12||
krodhāsaṃraktanayano viṣṭapaṃ pradahanniva |
gaṇānūce ruṣāviṣṭo rakṣaṇaṃ kriyate katham ||13||
kena daityena nīto me lalāṭasthoʼmalaḥ śaśī |
ka uvāca |
tato līnā gaṇāḥ sarve kampamānā bhayāturāḥ ||14||
apare dhairyamālambya vadanti sma śivaṃ prati |
umākānta bhavatputro mayūreśvarasaṃjñakaḥ ||15||
krīḍituṃ bahirāyātastasya haste vilokitaḥ |
candrastena kadā nīto na jānīmo vayaṃ vibho ||16||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā proce ruṣṭo maheśvaraḥ |
kathaṃ nu kriyate rakṣā bhavadbhirbhakṣyatatparaiḥ ||17||
candro vā candrahartā vā yadyānītastadā śubham |
no cedbhasmīkariṣyāmi sarveṣāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||18||
tataste kṣubdhamanaso dhāvamānāstvarānvitāḥ |
mayūreśaṃ samabhetya prābhaṇanruṣṭacetasaḥ ||19||
yāhi duṣṭaṃ śivaṃ devaṃ candraṃ vā yaccha taskara |
gaṇavākyaṃ pariśrutya cukopa gaṇanāyakaḥ ||20||
na meʼsti gaṇanā kāpi bhavatāṃ tasya vā gaṇāḥ |
jagattrayajananyā me tanayasya prabhāviṇaḥ, ||21||
ka uvāca |
tasya śvāsena sarve te vātyā patramivoddhṛtāḥ |
petuḥ śivasya purato gaṇā dīnāstadāʼkhilāḥ ||22||
atiroṣānmahādevaḥ pramathādīnathābravīt |
ānīyatāṃ sa duṣṭātmā baddhvomātanayo laghuḥ ||23||
te tu śīghrataraṃ yātā yatra krīḍārataḥ śiśuḥ |
dadṛśustaṃ śiśugataṃ krīḍantamakutobhayam ||24||
veṣṭituṃ tānsamāyātānmohayitvā vināyakaḥ |
antarhitaścaturdikṣu gaṇāste taṃ vyalokayan ||25||
gṛhe gṛhe kānaneṣu nāpaśyaste vināyakam |
kvacidṛṣṭvā tu taṃ prāhurasmadagre kathaṃ bhavān ||26||
gantuṃ śakto brahmaloke sthitaṃ neṣyāma taṃ śivam |
evamantarhito dṛśyo vāraṃvāraṃ babhūva saḥ ||27||
tataḥ khinnāngaṇāndṛṣṭvā paramātmā kṛpānvitaḥ |
tasthau tatpurataḥ samyagdṛṣṭvā taṃ harṣanirbharāḥ ||28||
babandhurgirijāsūnuṃ ninyuste śaṃkaraṃ prati |
pṛthivībhārasadṛśamupaviṣṭaṃ tu te gaṇāḥ ||29||
na śekurutthāpayituṃ tato vismitamānasāḥ |
hatodyamā gaṇāḥ sarve śivametya samūcire ||30||
sarve vayaṃ samānetumekaṃ śaktā na śaṃkara |
ājñāpayāmāsa śivo nandinaṃ purataḥ sthitam ||31||
jhaṭityānaya gaccha tvaṃ mayūreśaṃ tu taskaram |
nandyuvāca |
śeṣaṃ sūryaṃ śaśāṃkaṃ ca haniṣyeʼhaṃ tavājñayā ||32||
kanīyaso na me kācidgaṇanāsti maheśvara |
ka uvāca |
ityuktvāʼgādvāyuvego bhaṃjanvṛkṣāṃśca parvatān ||33||
krodhasaṃraktanayanastīkṣṇaśṛṃgograsandivam |
uvāca taṃ mayūreśaṃ yāhi re tvaṃ śivaṃ prati ||34||
no cedadha nayiṣyāmi na samo hi gaṇairaham |
evaṃ vadati tasmiṃstu mayūreśo ruṣānvitaḥ ||35||
śavāsacakreʼkṣipattaṃ tu gamanāgamasaṃkule |
tatyāja taṃ dṛḍhaṃ śvāsādatikhinnaṃ śivāntike ||36||
vamantaṃ dhiraṃ vaktrātpṛthivyāṃ patitaṃ tu tam |
bruvantaṃ pauruṣaṃ nānā mūrchitaṃ dvimuhūrtataḥ ||37||
apaśyacca mayūreśaṃ jānubhāge sthitaṃ śivaḥ |
dedīpyamānaṃ vapuṣā divyabhūṣāsamanvitam ||38||
gaṇā ūcuḥ śivaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhālacandraṃ yathā purā |
lalāṭe te śaśī deva vṛthājñaptā vayaṃ śava ||39||
mayūreśaṃ gaṇāṃścāha śaśinaṃ vīkṣya mastake |
śiva uvāca |
śrāntāste ca gaṇāstvaṃ ca nandī cāpi mamājñayā ||40||
candra sthite lalāṭe me vṛthā yuddhamabhūddhi vaḥ |
pramathā ūcuḥ |
adyaprabhṛti deveśa svāmī noʼstu mayūrarāṭ ||41||
ka uvāca |
tatheti śiva ūce tāngaṇarājoʼbhavattu saḥ
natvā śivaṃ gaṇeśaṃ ca gaṇeśajananīmapi ||42||
praśaṃsayitvā deveśaṃ mayūreśaṃ tathāvidham |
garjantoʼtha gaṇā jagmurmudā svaṃ svaṃ niveśanam ||43||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe ṣaḍadhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||106||

<adhyāya 107 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
tataścaturdaśe varṣe gautamādyāḥ surarṣayaḥ |
āyayurgirijāgehaṃ nānāgaṇayutaṃ śivam ||1||
namaskṛtya ca tānambā nānāsanagatānpuraḥ |
pūjayitvā vidhānena provāca hṛdgataṃ vacaḥ ||2||
vighnabhītyā parityaktaṃ trisandhyākṣetramuttamam |
asya bālasya me vighnā atrāpi bahavoʼbhavan ||3||
diśantu vighnarahitaṃ karma vā sthānamuttamam |
munaya ūcuḥ |
indrayāge kṛte devi sarvavighnaṃ hariṣyati ||4||
ka uvāca |
tadeva kārayāmāsa maṇḍapaṃ bahuvistaram |
saṃbhārānkārayāmāsa śakrasaṃtoṣakārakān ||5||
ājñāṃ gṛhītvā śambhoḥ sā pārvatī harṣanirbharā |
kārayāmāsa vidhivadindrayāgaṃ maharṣibhiḥ ||6||
dhyānamāvāhanaṃ cakrurindrasyaite maharṣayaḥ |
pūpūjurgirijādeśātsāṃgamindraṃ gaṇānapi ||7||
kuṇḍe vahniṃ pratiṣṭhāpya mantraireva dvijarṣabhāḥ |
japānāṃ taddaśāṃśena juhuvustilapāyasam ||8||
praṇavādyairnamontaiśca svāhāntairmantrasaṃcayaiḥ |
śāntipāṭhānpaṭhanti sma caturvedabhavāndvijāḥ ||9||
etasminnantare bālaḥ krīḍitvā bālakaiḥ saha |
yajñavāṭamukho yāti daityau tu kalaviṃkalau ||10||
tāvatsaṃmukhatāṃ yātau māhiṣaṃ rūpamāsthitau |
mahāravau mahāśṛṃgau bhīṣayantau jagattrayam ||11||
saṭābhinnaghanau pucchāddārayantau girīnatha |
nāsānirodhapavanāccūrṇayantau drumānapi ||12||
umāsutaṃ hantukāmau yātau śīghraṃ tadantikam |
prāyudhyetāṃ tadā dvau tau mattāviva mahāgajau ||13||
tayoḥ śabdena gaganaṃ garjati sma yathā ghanaḥ |
jayaṃ bhaṃgaṃ krameṇobhau prāpatū rudhirokṣitau ||14||
vidrutā munibālāste tābhyāṃ bhītā vṛkādiva |
dūrāddadarśa girijāsutastau daityapuṃgavau ||15||
kathametau mahāduṣṭau haniṣyāmīti cintayan |
paśyatsu teṣu bāleṣu cikṣepa gagane ca tau ||16||
pucche gṛhītvāʼtibalau bhrāmayitvā punaḥ punaḥ |
muhūrtātpatitau pṛthvyāṃ tānvidārya drumānapi ||17||
śatadhā dehakhaṇḍāni tayorādanvṛkādayaḥ |
tataste munibālāstaṃ yayurbhaktyā pratuṣṭuvuḥ ||18||
yasya tattvaṃ na jānanti brahmādyā munayopi ca |
sarvāntaryāmiṇaste tu kathaṃ vidyāmahe vayam ||19||
bālyātyabhṛti daityāste nāśitāḥ koṭiśastvayā |
etau mahiṣarūpeṇa hantuṃ tvāṃ samupāgatau ||20||
mūṣakāviva saṃtyaktau pucche dhṛtvā svalīlayā |
yayoḥ śvāsena calitāḥ parvatāśca drumādharāḥ ||21||
ka uvāca |
mayūreśastataḥ prāyādyajñavāṭaṃ ramanraman |
dadarśendramakhaṃ ruṣṭo dhvaṃsayāmāsa tatkṣaṇāt ||22||
dūre kṣiptvā śakramūrtiṃ proce sarvānmunīśvarān |
vṛto munisutaiḥ sarvaiḥ kiṃ karma kriyateʼmalāḥ ||23||
kiṃ tuṣṭena pradātavyaṃ vāsavenākhilārthinā |
ajāprārthanayā kiṃ nu kāmadhenuphalaṃ labhet ||24||
sa ādaccheṣasāmagrī kṛtvā śānta hutāśanam |
munayo vismitahṛdo vīkṣya cāpalyamasya tat ||25||
umāniviṣṭahṛdayā nāśakattaṃ guṇeśvaram |
upālabdhuṃ śikṣituṃ vā vaktuṃ krodhena kiṃcana ||26||
umāyai sarvamākhyāya jagmuḥ svaṃ svaṃ niveśanam |
śrutvendre kṣubdhahdaye yatsyāttacca bhavatviti ||27||
svāpamāne parijñāte śakraścukrodha vai bhṛśam |
krodhasaṃraktanayano dahanniva jagattrayam ||28||
ākārya devanicayānuvāca vākyamuttamam |
indra uvāca |
mama yāgo munigaṇairārabdha paramādarāt ||29||
sa vidhvasto guṇeśena tasya drakṣyeʼdya pauruṣam |
ruṣṭe mayi bhavennaṣṭaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram ||30||
devā ūcuḥ |
tavājñā cenmayūreśaṃ baddhvānīmaḥ kṣaṇena ha |
śakra uvāca
mayūreśapure vahneḥ sthānaṃ māstu kvacittava ||31||
sarveṣāṃ jāṭharoʼpi tvaṃ līno bhava mamājñayā |
ka uvāca |
iti śrutvā vacastasya śakrasya ruṣitātmanaḥ ||32||
vahnirantarhitastvatra pureʼpi jaṭhareʼpi ca |
nāpaśyanmunayo vahniṃ mamanthurhomahetave ||33||
nāyātīti parijñāya teʼprakvaṃ jakṣurādarāt |
na ca tatpacyate tena vyathā bhavati durdharā ||34||
tataste munayo jagmurmayūreśaṃ kṛpākaram |
namaskṛtyābruvansarve śakrāhūto gatoʼnalaḥ ||35||
jāṭharoʼpi gato vahnirindrayāge hate tvayā |
ityākarṇya vacasteṣāṃ pratyekaṃ jaṭhareʼnalaḥ ||36||
soʼbhavatkṣudhayāviṣṭhāḥ sarve jātāstu tatkṣaṇāt |
mahānaseṣu kuṇḍeṣu tathaiva jvalitoʼbhavat ||37||
tato vāyuṃ nināyāśu śakraḥ kruddho mahābalaḥ |
pretabhūtā purī sarvā paṃcaprāṇe gateʼbhavat ||38||
paṃcaprāṇamayo devastatastānsamajīvayat |
tata indroʼbravītsūryaṃ tāpayaināṃ purīṃ bahu ||39||
tataḥ sūryoʼtapatsarvaṃ dvādaśādityarūpavān |
vāpīkūpataḍāgeṣu nadyāṃ jalamaśoṣayat ||40||
jajvāla pṛthivī sarvā puragrāmavanākarā |
tataḥ śīghraṃ mayūreśo megho bhūtvā vavarṣa ha ||41||
śamayāmāsa taṃ vahniṃ mṛtāṃśca samajīvayat |
tato harṣayutā lokāḥ sādhu sādhvityapūjayan ||42||
ka uvāca |
pratikūlaṃ kṛtaṃ yadyattatatsarvaṃ nirākṛtam |
jñātvā śakro yayau devaṃ mayūreśaṃ nanāma ca ||43||
baddhāṃjalipuṭo bhūtvā stotuṃ saveśvaraṃ vibhum |
śakra uvāca |
na vedā narṣayo jagmurbrahmādyāste gatiṃ vibho ||44||
sarvāntaryāmiṇaḥ sarvarūpasya brahmarūpiṇaḥ |
brahmādīnāṃ viśvasṛjāṃ kāraṇānāṃ ca kāraṇam ||45||
aparādhānkṣamasvādya tvāmahaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
mastakaṃ pādayornyasya prārthayāmāsa sādaram ||46||
ka uvāca |
nirvikalpaṃ manantasya jñātvā prāha guṇeśvaraḥ |
uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha bhadraṃ te pade tiṣṭha sukhaṃ svake ||47||
tataḥ saṃpūjya taṃ devaṃ namaskṛtya śacīpatiḥ |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya yayau haṣṭaḥ sannamarāvatīm ||48||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe saptādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||107||

<adhyāya 108 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca, tataḥ paṃcadaśe varṣe mayūreśoʼrbhakaiḥ saha |
gataḥ snātuṃ nadīṃ puṇyāṃ nāmnā dbrahmakamaṇḍalum ||1||
snātvā nityaṃ samāpyāśu mānasaiḥ ṣoḍaśaiḥ pṛthak |
upacāraiḥ pupūjaste hradisthaṃ gaṇanāyakam ||2||
kecitpadmāsanagataṃ kecidbarhigataṃ ca tam |
divyagandhairdivyavastrairdivyaiḥ puṣpairapūjayan ||3||
gārgyo munirmandire taṃ dhyātvā ratnānviteʼrcayat |
etasminnantareʼkasmātpidadhe dvāramasya tat ||4||
yāvaduddhāṭayatyeva tāvattaddṛḍhatāmagāt |
tatra ākrandanaṃ cakre sa ca te vālakā api ||5||
bālakā ūcuḥ
tvameva jananī tātaḥ kathamabhyantare sthitaḥ |
dattvā kapāṭaṃ deveśa noʼparādhānkṣamasva ca ||6||
ka uvāca |
etasminnantare daityo mahābalaparākramaḥ |
yasya śabdena mahatā kampate bhuvanatrayam ||7||
saṭābhinnaghano duṣṭo vyāghrarūpī samabhyagāt |
vyādāyaṃ vadanaṃ ghoraṃ grasanniva jagattrayam ||8||
dṛṣṭavā taṃ dudruvurbālāḥ krandantaste diśo daśa |
sa tu dvāraṃ vinirbhidya mayūreśaṃ tamāyayau ||9||
śārdūlasadṛśaṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvāʼyātsoʼpi taṃ kṣaṇāt |
papāla sahasā vyāghraḥ pṛṣṭhe lagno yayau ca saḥ ||10||
bhramansa tu vanaṃ yāto nikuṃje līya tasthivān |
devo vṛkṣaṃ samāruhya kuṃjamadhye papāta ha ||11||
ubhābhyāṃ vadanaṃ tasya karābhyāṃ jagṛhe dṛḍham |
anyābhyāṃ bahirānīya tataḥ paraśunāʼcchinat ||12||
nāsikāṃ śravaṇe pādau pucchamagrabhavāvubhau |
na mukhaṃ darśayetyuktvā visasarja guṇeśvara ||13||
nijaṃ rūpaṃ samāsthāya tathaiva vikṛtoʼbhavat |
evameva kariṣyāmi tvaddehamityuvāca tam ||14||
yayau nijālayaṃ daityo devaḥ svaṃ dhāma cāyayau |
tato munisutā bhremuḥ krandantaḥ kva mayūrarāṭ ||15||
atiśrāntā drumatale succhāye prāsvapaṃstadā |
anena yāsyati pathā drakṣyāmastatpadāmbujam ||16||
pādau dakṣiṇatasteṣāṃ dṛṣṭvā cukrodha bhānujaḥ |
atyāraktadṛśī roṣādbrahmāṇḍagrāsasāhasaḥ ||17||
baddhvā tānsvasthalaṃ yāto mayūreśastatoʼgamat |
nāpaśyattattaṭe bālānvismayaṃ paramaṃ yayau ||18||
cintāṃ paramikāṃ prāpto vinā taiḥ sa mayūrarāṭ |
na sukhaṃ kvāpi saṃprāyāttato munigaṇā yayuḥ ||19||
munaya ūcuḥ |
kvāsmākaṃ bālakā deva prātarnītāstvayāʼkhilāḥ |
vinā tairno gamiṣyanti prāṇāstvayyāgateʼdhunā ||20||
ka uvāca |
na kiṃcidūce tāndevo muṃcannaśru muhurmuhuḥ |
vicārya bhāskarīṃ yātaḥ purīṃ dūtairniveditaḥ ||21||
dūtā ūcuḥ |
koʼpi yāto yoddhukāmo lokatrayavināśakṛt |
ugrārāvo mahābāhurvāryamāṇo balādyamaḥ ||22||
yama uvāca |
brahmāṇḍaṃ cūrṇatāṃ yāti daṇḍāghātena yasya me |
tasya me purato yuddhaṃ kathaṃ bālo vidhāsyasi ||23||
deva uvāca |
tvamātmānaṃ varṇayase raṃkavadbhāṃsi me yama |
mama vakrabhuvā sarvaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ ca vinaśyati ||24||
evamuktvā mayūreśo yamaskandhaṃ ruroha saḥ |
pātayāmāsa mahiṣātpapātopari tatkṣaṇāt ||25||
parākramaṃ tasya buddhvā baddhāñjalipuṭo yamaḥ |
tuṣṭāva parayā bhaktyā mayūreśaṃ sureśvarama ||26||
yama uvāca |
na te rūpa vidurdevā brahmādyāḥ sanakādayaḥ |
neti neti ca vedāstvāṃ vadanti parameśvaram ||27||
sraṣṭāraṃ pālakaṃ saṣṭeḥ saṃhartāraṃ nijecchayā |
daityānāṃ sarvaduṣṭānāṃ nāśakaṃ sarvarūpiṇam ||28||
akasmādaparādhaṃ naḥ kṛtaṃ kṣantu tvamarhasi |
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā pupūjāsau vastrai ratnaiḥ phalairamum ||29||
bālānānīya taddhaste nivedya purataḥ sthitaḥ |
āliliṃgurmudā sarve parasparamathābruvana ||30||
bālā ūcuḥ |
śrāntāstvaddarśanārthāya baddhvā nītā yamena ha |
tvaddarśanādbandhamuktā yāsyāmo nijamandiram ||31||
ka uvāca |
tataste svāśramaṃ prāptā mayūreśapuraskṛtāḥ |
mayūreśapūraṃ yātā nānāvāditrasaṃkulam ||32||
nānādhvajapatākāḍhayaṃ puṣpamālāvibhūṣitama |
yamoʼpi pṛṣṭhato yāti draṣṭuṃ tatkautukaṃ mahat ||33||
saṃmukhaṃ tasya te sarve munayoʼpi yayustadā |
pupūjustuṣṭuvurdeva liliṃgurbālakānapi ||34||
uvāca devatānsarvānmunīnpūrvaṃ prakupyataḥ |
ānītā bālakā lokādyamasya kṣaṇamātrataḥ ||35||
munaya ūcuḥ |
sarvatra rakṣase tvaṃ hi sarvaśaktiḥ sadā ca naḥ |
ka uvāca |
ityuktvā tamanujñāpya svāśramānmunayo yayuḥ ||36||
yamoʼpi taṃ namaskṛtya svālayaṃ muditoʼgamat |
devoʼpi svālayaṃ prāpya harṣayāmāsa sādaram ||37||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe ravijagarvaparihāro nāmāṣṭādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||108||

<adhyāya 109 prāraṃbhaḥ>

kadācitpārvatī prāha sukhāsīnaṃ maheśvaram |
gatāni tu mahādeva varṣāṇi daśa paṃca ca ||1||
vivāhaścintyatāmasya mayūreśasya sāmpratam |
suśīlā sumukhī bālā mṛgākṣī haṃsagāminī ||2||
pikavāk sunasā tanvī vadhūḥ kvāpi vicāryatām |
manmathātuladehasya sarvāṃgasundarasya ha ||3||
ka uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sādhu sādhvityabhāṣata |
soʼcintayacchubhā kanyāṃ manasā nāpakutracit ||4||
tataścintāṃ parāṃ prāpa tāvannārada āgataḥ |
saṃpūjya copavaiśyenamāsane śambhurabravīt ||5||
śambhuruvāca |
āhlādaṃ paramaṃ prāptastavāgamanato mune |
cirāgatoʼsi ca mune dinaṃ sthātuṃ tvamarhasi ||6||
atisundarakāyasya tanayasya mamānagha |
gaveṣaya vadhū vipra yatastrailokyago bhavān ||7||
ka uvāca |
tathaiva pārvatī prāha vadhūṃ śīghraṃ vicāraya |
nārada uvāca |
na sthātuṃ śakyare svāminkarmaṇā dvimuhūrtakam ||8||
etadarthamahaṃ prāpto brahmaṇā preṣitaḥ śiva |
tava putraprabhāvaśca lāvaṇyaṃ vaya eva ca, ||9||
jñātaṃ dhātrā siddhibuddhī tasmai dātumabhīpsatā |
anasūyā śacī cāpi tayo rūpeṇa lajjite ||10||
saṃjñāʼbhūddaṇḍakāraṇye śilāʼhalyāpi sā hyabhūt |
jālandharasya yā patnī vṛndā mādhavamohinī ||11||
sāpi vṛkṣatvamāpannā yadrūpadarśanāddhara |
anye nānāvarā gauri nāgatā brahmaṇo hṛdi ||12||
naitādṛśaṃ kvāpi devi saundaryaṃ śauryameva ca |
tayoryogena ca tavaḥ sutaḥ śobhāmavāpnuyāt, ||13||
ratnakāṃcanayoryadvadyathā muktāpravālayoḥ |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ nāradavākyena sa tutoṣa haraḥ śivā ||14||
śiva uvāca |
samyaguktaṃ mune vākyaṃ vāṃchitārthakaraṃ śubham |
ka uvāca |
śivoʼpi tatkṣaṇādeva vṛṣārūḍhaḥ samāhvayat ||15||
indrādidevanicayāngautamādīnmunīnapi |
ardhāṃge pārvatīṃ gṛhaya yayau harṣasamanvitaḥ ||16||
śikhaṇḍinaṃ samāruhya mayūreśaḥ puro yayau |
nāradastu tapoyogādantarikṣacaro yayau ||17||
saptakoṭigaṇāḥ krīḍāṃ kurvantoʼbhiyayustadā |
nānāyudhadharā harṣādgarjayanto diśodaśa ||18||
vādyatsu sarvavādyeṣu rajasācchādite ravau |
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi munayoʼpi yayurmudā ||19||
gaṇḍakīnagaraṃ sarve gantukāmā vyalokayan |
gulvaṃ mārge rakṣasāṃ te saptakoṭigaṇānvitam ||20||
nabhasparśi mukhānāṃ ca sarvadā kāladhikkṛtām |
niśācarāḥ śuśruvuste senāyāstu ravaṃ dṛḍham ||21||
suptotthitāśca sannaddhā yayū raṇavidhitsayā |
rākṣasā ūcuḥ |
kasya yūyaṃ kuto yātha kṛta āgamanaṃ ca vaḥ ||22||
vijñānāṃ na pure gantuṃ dāsyāmaḥ svāminaśca naḥ |
mayūreśa uvāca |
svatantrā antakā daityarakṣasāṃ sādhupālakāḥ ||23||
ato yacchantu no mārgaṃ no cennāśaṃ vrajiṣyatha |
ka uvāca |
etasminnantare prāpto vikṛto hemanāmakaḥ ||24||
asuroʼthābravīddevamekāki prahitaḥ purā |
idānīṃ rākṣasaiḥ sārddhamādāsye jīvitaṃ tava ||25||
uvāca rākṣasānsoʼtha nānādaityā hatā balāt |
ahaṃ ca vikṛtoʼnena yatoʼhaṃ hanyate javāt ||26||
ka uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā cukrodha sa mayūrarāṭ |
muniputrānsamājñāpya yudhyadhvaṃ me hyanugrahāt ||27||
kuśānudyamya te harṣād yuddhaṃ kartuṃ samudyatāḥ |
uvāca pṛṣṭhato devo ghnantuṃ bālāḥ kuśairimāna ||28||
mantritāste kuṣāsteṣāṃ mastakānacchinanbahūn |
teṣāṃ śastrāṇi kuṇṭhāni jātāni tadanugrahāt ||29||
jāyante tāvadete tānkuśairjaghnustu rākṣasān |
khaṇḍaśaste nipeturvai chinnabāhurujānavaḥ ||30||
vyasmayanta ca rakṣāṃsi tṛṇairmṛtyuvrajanti kim |
evaṃ sarve hatā bālairakhilā rākṣasāstadhā ||31||
jagarjuste mayūreśaṃ prāpurnatvāpyapūjayan |
bālā ūcuḥ |
ājñayā nihatāḥ sarve rākṣasāstvatprasādataḥ |
punarājñāṃ naḥ prayaccha kariṣyāmo guṇeśvara ||32||
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ jagmurmunayo ye samāgatāḥ |
dṛṣṭvā bālahatāntsarvānrākṣasānkuśasaṃcayaiḥ ||33||
pārvatī vṛṣabhāttasmādavaruhayāliliṃga tam |
śivoʼpi prābravīddevaṃ dṛṣṭasteʼdya parākramaḥ ||34||
tava śastrahatāḥ pūrvaṃ nihatā bālakaiḥ kuśaiḥ |
brahmādīnāmagamyaste prabhāvo girijāsuta ||35||
na jñāyate mayā vāpi kiṃ kimagre kariṣyasi ||36||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe rākṣasavadho nāma navādhikaśatatamodhyāyaḥ ||109||

< adhyāya 110 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
jayaśālī mayūreśo yātyagre harṣanirbharaḥ |
pṛṣṭhato yānti bālāste tato vṛṣagataḥ śivaḥ ||1||
gautamādyā munigaṇā lagnasiddhyai yayustataḥ |
garjanto garjayantaśca diśaśca vidiśoʼpi ca ||2||
yojanāvadhi gaṇḍakyā avaruhya śikhaṇḍinaḥ |
atiṣṭhatsa mayūreśo mahārghāsanamuttamam ||3||
tataḥ sarvānupāveśya śivaṃ gaṇamuniśvarān |
vādyatsu sarvavādyeṣu tānāha sa mayūrarāṭ ||4||
sindhunā nihitā kārāgṛhe śakrādidevatā |
vinā tanmocanaṃ lagnasiddhirnaiva bhaviṣyati ||5||
atastanmocanārthāya preṣyātāṃ sindhave mahān |
buddhimānsa gṛhītvā cedāgacchettānsurānatha ||6||
tadā sāmnaiva sarveṣāṃ kuśalaṃ hi bhaviṣyati |
no cedyuddhena jeṣyāmo raṇe sindhuṃ mahābalam ||7||
mocayiṣyāma tāndevāṃstato lagnaṃ bhaviṣyati |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā prāhuḥ sarve guṇeśvaram ||8||
samyaguktaṃ mayūreśa sudhiyā sadṛśaṃ vacaḥ
bālabhāʼvepi te buddhirbṛhaspatisamā śubhā ||9||
puṣpadantaṃ mahābuddhiṃ nītiśāstraviśāradam |
preṣyatāṃ sindhunikaṭe devamocanahetave ||10||
vaktāraṃ balinaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ stotrasaṃtoṣiteśvaram |
ka uvāca |
tata ūce puṣpadanto mayūreśaṃ sureśvaram ||11||
mahimā te mayūreśa manovācāmagocaraḥ |
jānanti na janā māyāmohitāstava nityadṛk ||12||
bhūbhāraharaṇārtha tvamavatīrṇaḥ śivālaye |
na māṃ preṣaya tatra tvamanyaṃ preṣaya sāmpratam ||13||
apārasenayā yuktaṃ sindhudaityaṃ mahābalam |
uddhataṃ balinaṃ draṣṭumahaṃ śakyāmi na kvacit ||14||
yuddhameva kariṣyāmi tato dordaṇḍatejasā |
yadi sāmnā bhavetkāryaṃ yuddhaṃ tatra vṛthā matam ||15||
dānaṃ bhedo vṛthā daṇḍo nītireṣā sanātanī |
evamākarṇya tadvākyamuvāca pārvatīpatiḥ ||16||
samyaguktaḥ puṣpadanta nītimārgaḥ purātanaḥ |
krodhavānna kṣamaḥ sāmni kevalaṃ balavānparaḥ ||17||
dhariṣyati mahāduṣṭaṃ preṣitaścetṣaḍānanaḥ |
preṣito vīrabhadraścetkrodhameva kariṣyati ||18||
śṛṃgī cetpreṣitastatra saṃhāraṃ sa vidhāsyati |
pramathaḥ preṣito matto na jāne kiṃ kariṣyati ||19||
preṣitaścedbhūtarājo meṣayiṣyati tāṃ sabhām |
strīsaṃbhogarataḥ syāttu preṣito raktalocanaḥ ||20||
tatoʼbravīnmayūreśo nirasteṣu ca teṣu ca |
nandī samudragambhīro dhairye parvatasannibhaḥ ||21||
buddhyā bṛhaspatisamo jambhārisadṛśo balaṃ |
dhūrtaḥ parāśayajñātā preṣyatāṃ sāmahetave ||22||
śiva uvāca |
samyaguktaṃ mayūreśa jñātāsi guṇadoṣayoḥ |
nandine dīyatāṃ vastraṃ ratnāni vividhāni ca ||23||
tato dadau mayūreśastāni tasya śivājñayā |
ājñāpayāmāsa ca taṃ gaccha sindhuṃ mahābalam ||24||
yathā devā vimucyaṃte tathā nītirvidhīyatām |
sa tu natvā mayūreśaṃ sa śivāṃ śivameva ca ||25||
gaṇānsarvānmahāvākyaiḥ stutvā taṃ ca śivaṃ śivām |
uvāca buddhisaṃpanno diṣṭyocitamidaṃ vacaḥ ||26||
nandyuvāca |
sa mahāṃ jāyate loke yasya vaḥ syādanugrahaḥ |
atoʼhaṃ śreṣṭhatāṃ nītaḥ sādhayiṣye prayojanam ||27||
vaḥ prasādena bhūgolaṃ vidhāsye nyubjamaṃjasā |
śeṣaṃ sūryaṃ vo nikaṭe ānayiṣye na saṃśayaḥ ||28||
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā yayau nandī sindhudaityaṃ balānvitaḥ |
gataḥ sa nagaradvāraṃ manovegena dhairyavān ||29||
dhyāyangaṇeśaṃ śambhuṃ ca śivāṃ sarvārthasādhikām |
ṛtāṃ kartuṃ pratijñāṃ tāṃ prārthayāmāsa cetasi ||30||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe daśādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||110||

< adhyāya 111 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
prāyātsabhāṃ mahāramyāṃ dvārapālaniveditaḥ |
nandī nananda taṃ dṛṣṭvā daityaṃ sindhuṃ sabhāgatam ||1||
mahāvīraiḥ parivṛtaṃ nānāśastreṣu pāṇibhiḥ |
vārāṃganānṛtyarataṃ vījyamānaṃ nṛbhirmudā ||2||
vādyatsu sarvavādyeṣu nādanādita dikṣu ca |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ nandinaṃ kecinmenire ravimāgatam ||3||
mahākāyaṃ mahāvīraṃ vīkṣya keciccakampire |
jagarjurapare tatra bhīṣayanto balādamum ||4||
na bibheti mahādhairyānnandī svabalagarvitaḥ |
karasaṃjñārpitaṃ nandī bheje āsanamuttamam ||5||
sarve taṭasthatā yātāścitramā iva puttalā |
buddhvā tadiṃgitaṃ prāha nandī vācaspatiḥ paraḥ ||6||
nānāsabhā dṛṣṭapūrvāṃ nedṛśāstāsu mūḍhakāḥ |
yūyaṃ sarvetibalinaḥ śriyā paramaśobhitāḥ ||7||
sundarāḥ kāmasadṛśā buddhyā hīnā vṛkā iva |
sabhāyāmāgataḥ sādhurasādhurdurbalo balī ||8||
praṣṭavyo mānanīyaśca nītireṣā sanātanī |
na sā dṛṣṭā sabhāmadhye ato me vismitaṃ manaḥ ||9||
vṛthā sabhāsadaḥ sarve vṛthāʼmātyāśca nāgarāḥ |
nāyaṃ dharmo rājña eva sabhyānāṃ kṛtyameva tat ||10||
ka uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sindhuḥ provāca nandinam |
padmayoneriva tava matirdṛṣṭā guṇākara ||11||
kasya tvaṃ kuta āyātaḥ kimarthaṃ vā vṛṣeśvara |
tejasā vahnisadṛśaḥ paṃcāsya iva vikrame ||12||
nandyuvāca |
surabheḥ kāmarūpāyā apatyaṃ nandināmakam |
viddhi māṃ girijānāthavāhaṃ brahmāṇḍabhedinam ||13||
mayūreśo dharābhāraharo duṣṭanibarhaṇa |
avatīrṇaḥ śivagṛhe bālyātprabhṛti daityahā ||14||
śeṣo mūkoʼbhavattasya pratāpavarṇaneʼbjajaḥ |
samudramathanādyadvadratnajātaṃ bahiryayau ||15||
tadvannītiṃ vimathyādya kāryasiddhiṃ vicāraya |
ājñāṃ śṛṇu mayūreśapreṣitāṃ balavattarām ||16||
ājñābhaṃge tasya bhavedvināśo yasya kasyacit |
yataḥ sa sṛjate viśvaṃ pāti hanti carācaram ||17||
tvayā vinihitā kārāgṛhe devagaṇā balāt |
padāni teṣāṃ labdhāni bhāgyaṃ syātkimataḥ param ||18||
idānīṃ vairanirmokaṃ vicārya kartumarhasi |
mocayāśu surānsarvānājñayā svāmino mama ||19||
tripureṇa kṛtaṃ vairaṃ śivena saha tatkṣaṇāt |
hiraṇyakaśipuṃ stambheʼvatīrya hatavānhariḥ ||20||
tārakeṇa jitā devā atiṣṭhattatpade svayam |
skandena nihato yuddhe kṣaṇena tārako balāt ||21||
ato dattvā surānmatdyaṃ? tiṣṭha sthāne sukhaṃ ciram |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ nandivacaḥ śrutvā cukrodha sindhudaityarāṭ ||22||
uvāca raktanayano vamanniva hutāśanam |
sindhuruvāca |
cāturyaṃ bahudhā dṛṣṭaṃ tvadīyaṃ vṛṣanandana ||22||
prasaṃgaṃ naiva jānāsi yato vācaspaterapi |
ajñānātprakṛtārthasya vaco yāti vṛthārthatām ||24||
bālasya tava vākyena svāminasteʼpi vākyataḥ |
mocayeyaṃ kathaṃ devānyāvatsoʼpi na nirjitaḥ ||25||
pramāṇaṃ kintu te vākyaṃ tṛṇādavanacāriṇa |
kva te girīśastatputro vṛthā bhītiḥ pravarṣate ||26||
lāṃgale yojayāmyadya dautyena nāgato yadi |
yadi me vakratāṃ yāyādbhrukuṭhī krodhato vṛṣa ||27||
tadā tribhuvanaṃ naśyetkā tatra gaṇanā tayoḥ |
ruṣṭaḥ sṛgālaḥ kiṃ kuryādapriyaṃ siṃhanāgayoḥ ||28||
ka uvāca, evaṃ śabdaśarairviddho nandī krodhādathābravīt |
nandyuvāca |
viparītāṃ matiṃ teʼdya lakṣayāmya surā dhama ||29||
sannipātīva vikalaṃ valgase maraṇonmukhaḥ |
pūrvaṃ sāma prayoktavyamiti tenāhamīritaḥ ||30||
tvayi nītirvṛthā jātā upadeśo yathā khale |
yadi nindā prayuktā te mayūreśe śiveʼpi ca ||31||
kṣīyamāyustayā teʼdya na yuddhe jayameṣyasi |
idānīṃ hanmi tvāṃ mūḍha nājñāsti svāmino mama ||32||
nāyakena na hantavyo dūtastenāpi nāyakaḥ |
ityuktvā pātayandaityānnandī niśvāsavāyunā ||33||
krodhena tamapṛṣṭvaiva pratasthe harṣanirbharaḥ |
sānnidhyaṃ prāpya devasya mayūreśasya tatkṣaṇāt ||34||
dūrāddadarśa devoʼpi prāha nandī samāgataḥ |
praṇipatyābravīddevaṃ vṛttāntaṃ sarvamāditaḥ ||35||
nandyuvāca |
bahudhā bodhito daityo na svīcakre vaco mama |
nirbhartsito bahuvidhaṃ tenāhaṃ ca mayā ca saḥ ||36||
nyubje ghaṭe yathā bindustasminsarvaṃ vṛthā bhavet |
ka uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā prayātasya yathāgatam ||37||
jaharṣa sa mayūreśo daityamardanalālasaḥ |
ājñāpayatsa yuddhāya pramathādīngaṇāṃstathā ||38||
idānīṃ sindhunagare yuddhaṃ kartuṃ samutsukāḥ |
garjantu vīraśabdaiśca mocituṃ bandhanātsurān ||39||
hatvā sindhuṃ ca sabalaṃ mocituṃ cākhilānsurān |
munīnāṃ tvaramāṇānāṃ gantuṃ svāśramamaṇḍalam ||40||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe vicāravarṇanaṃ nāmaikādaśādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||111||

< adhyāya 112 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
śikhaṇḍinaṃ samāruhya mayūreśaḥ pare dine |
āyudhāni ca catvāri pragṛhya karapaṃkaje ||1||
jagarja ghanavadghoraṃ yayau raṇacikīrṣayā |
śivoʼpi garjayansahasā vṛṣārūḍho yayau tadā ||2||
saptakoṭigaṇāḥ paścātsannaddhā yayurojasā |
tato nandī prāha tatra na gantuṃ sahasāʼrhatha ||3||
gaṇānāṃ nāyake vīrabhadre ca mayi tiṣṭhati |
pramatha vā vidyamāne śakte hantuṃ suradviṣām ||4||
paśyantu sevakamahastato yāntu susaṃyugam |
ka uvāca |
bhāratīmevamākarṇya devo nandīritāṃ tadā ||5||
uvāca paramaprītaḥ sādhu sādhu tvayoditam |
parākramavataḥ sindhordraṣṭavastu parākramaḥ ||6||
iti yāmi puro nandinyūyaṃ yāntu sahaiva naḥ |
tatastu bhūtarājoʼtha puṣpadantoʼpi vīryavān ||7||
īyatuḥ puratastasya koṭisaṃkhyāyutā gaṇāḥ |
vīryaśriyā dīpyamānāḥ sarve vīrā madānvitāḥ ||8||
ākramanto digantānvai garjanto dhanavadbhṛśam |
bhāreṇa śeṣaśīrṣāṇi nāmayanto dharāmapi ||9||
krodhānalaṃ vamantaste prāpustāṃ gaṇḍakīṃ purīm |
daityagulme nipetuste daśakoṭyasurairyute ||10||
tatastatrābhavadyuddhaṃ tumulaṃ romaharṣaṇam |
śastrāstrairbāṇanicayairbhiṇḍipālaparaśvadhaiḥ ||11||
keṣāṃcinmastakā bhinnāḥ pādā hastāstathoravaḥ |
jānujaṃghāśca gulphāśca bhagnāḥ keʼpi gatāsavaḥ ||12||
nipatya punarutthāya prāyudhyansvaparaiḥ saha |
evaṃ nipatitā daityā asaṃkhyātā gatāsavaḥ ||13||
palāyya nirgatāḥ kecidācakhyuḥ sindhaveʼkhilam |
sabhāmadhye vīragaṇairupaviṣṭāya kecana ||14||
śastrāghātaiḥ sravadraktāḥ puṣpitā iva kiṃśukāḥ |
tatrāgatya mṛtāḥ kecitkeciddhairyāttamabruvan ||15||
nagaradvāragulme tu daśakoṭiniśācarāḥ |
nivṛttāḥ śastrasaṃghātairyuddhaṃ kṛtvā parasparam ||16||
yadā śailabhido yuddhamabhūttatra jayoʼbhavat |
upaviṣṭoʼsi kiṃ tūṣṇīṃ sindho svahitamācara ||17||
ārāmā ripubhirdagdhā gṛhāṇi ca pratolikāḥ |
andhakāre dhūlikṛte prāpalanvahnitejasā ||18||
bhuṃjanto nidritāḥ kecinniryayurnagarādbahiḥ |
bālānādāya papalustyaktvā bhogaṃ ca kecana ||19||
visrastavasanāḥ keciddhyānasthā vahninā hutāḥ |
tatrāpi nidhanaṃ jagmurnānāśastraprahārataḥ ||20||
dhanaṃjayaṃ sarvadikṣu vīkṣya paurā bhayāturāḥ |
menire pralayaṃ prāptaṃ dahyate sakalā purī ||21||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ dūtamukhācchrutvā sindhurjṛmbhāmathākarot |
khādanniva mṛgānsiṃhaḥ parvatendravinirgataḥ ||22||
uvāca durdharā senā dṛśyate kiṃ bhaviṣyati |
didīpe krodhānalato grasanniva triviṣṭapama ||23||
siṃhasyāgre kiṃ śaśakaḥ kariṣyati parākramam |
meroḥ kiṃ śalabhaḥ kuryāccalanaṃ jambukoʼpi vā ||24||
kālasya mama kiṃ kuryādbāloʼyaṃ mayūrarāṭ |
ityuktvā śveḍitenāsau nādayanvidiśo diśaḥ ||25||
cintāturaḥ kṣaṇaṃ tasthau munirmaunamivāsthitaḥ |
tato vīrā sindhudaityaṃ prābruvankrodhamūrchitāḥ ||26||
ājñāṃ dehi mayūreśaṃ jeṣyāmaḥ kṣaṇamātrataḥ |
tava pratāpāttrailokyaṃ vaśaṃ neṣyāma sāmpratama ||27||
sindhuruvāca |
samyaguktaṃ mahāvīrā mamāpi ripudarśane |
mana utkaṇṭhitaṃ yasmātsainyena yuddhyatāmiha ||28||
tataḥ sarve puro yātā garjanto vīrasattamāḥ |
asaṃkhyātāḥ śastradharāścaturaṃgabalānvitāḥ ||29||
hayārūḍhaḥ sindhudaityaḥ krodhaharṣasamanvitaḥ |
vamankrodhānalaṃ vaktrānnetrābhyāṃ raṇadurmadaḥ ||30||
yayāvaśvaṃ purodhāya manovegaṃ mahādhanam ||31||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe dvādaśādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||112||

<adhyāya 113 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
padātayaḥ puro yānti kampayanto vasundharām |
vikarālamukhā vīrāḥ sindūrāruṇamastakāḥ ||1||
kecinnibaddhakavacā muktakeśāḥ sudāruṇāḥ |
kecinnibaddhakaṭayaḥ keciccandanacarcitāḥ ||2||
nānāśastrapraharaṇā nānāyuddhaviśāradāḥ |
puro yānti gajā mattā dhātucitrā ivādrayaḥ ||3||
dīrghadantā vibhānti sma bhūṣaṇairvividhairapi |
ghaṇṭānādairjanaṃ sarvaṃ badhiraṃ cakruraṃjasā ||4||
cūrṇayanto girīnvakṣānyayuḥ śuṇḍāganaṃprahārataḥ |
lasatkumbhāḥ patākāḍhayāścitradhvajavirājitāḥ ||5||
tatoʼśvāśca khurāghātaiścūrṇayanto vasundharām |
heṣitairnādayanto vai gaganaṃ ca diśo daśa ||6||
suvarṇaratnamuktābhirbhūṣitā vyomacāriṇaḥ |
adhiṣṭhitā mahāyodhaistanutrāṇapraśobhibhiḥ ||7||
nānāśastradharairvīraiḥ parasenāvimardibhiḥ |
tatastu rathino jagmuḥ śastrāstrai ripughātinaḥ ||8||
sahitā yuddhamārgajñairvaraiḥ sārathibhirmṛdhe |
muktāmaṇimayā mālā vahanto gandhamālinaḥ ||9||
dhanuskandhāstūṇabhṛto bhallahastāḥ sakhaḍgakāḥ |
śastraprabhābhirnetrāṇi chāndayanto janasya ha ||10||
evaṃ senā samāyātā garjayantī diśo daśa |
bhūtarājena sā dṛṣṭā puṣpadantena vāhinī ||11||
mayūreśamubhau yātau tato bhītyā palāyitau |
tāvūcatuḥ |
tavājñayā puro yātāvāvābhyāṃ nihatā camūḥ ||12||
gulmasthā nagaradvāri dagdhaṃ ca nagaraṃ bahu |
pratāpaṃ nau samākarṇya sindhuḥ svayamupāgataḥ ||13||
caturaṃgāṃ camūṃ gṛhaya nānāśastrāstradhāriṇīm |
tato bhītyā bhavantaṃ ca samāyātau mahābala ||14||
ka uvāca |
sindhusainyasamudroʼtha vadatorevametayoḥ |
dadṛśe pūrvādigbhāge dustaraḥ śastrayodhibhiḥ ||15||
tato harṣayuto jñātvā daityavṛttaṃ samāsataḥ |
nīlakaṇṭhaṃ samāruhya mayūreśo yayau puraḥ ||16||
āyudhāni ca catvāri caturdoṣu pragṛhaya saḥ |
sannaddhā gaṇakoṭyastā dhanurbāṇāsipāṇayaḥ ||17||
parivārya sthitā devaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ vṛṣasthitam |
munīśvarāḥ stuvanti sma dadurāśīrvacoʼpi tam ||18||
ubhayoḥ senayorvādyaghoṣakolāhalo mahān |
parasparaṃ saṃpadaṃ tāṃ vīkṣya sene suvismite ||19|| 
asaṃkhyātaṃ balaṃ vīkṣya tutoṣa sa mayūrarāṭ |
yaduktaṃ nandinā pūrvaṃ taddṛṣṭaṃ sarvameva hi ||20||
tato nandī mayūreśaṃ natvā garjanmuhuryayau |
nabhomārgeṇa sainyeṣu ciccheda vīramastakān ||21||
śṛṃgāghātena mahatā hayamasyāhanaddṛḍham |
śīghramuḍḍīya ca yayau mayūreśaṃ yathāʼkṣataḥ ||22||
āścaryaṃ cakrire sarve gaṇāḥ sādhviti śaṃsire |
chatraṃ ca pātayāmāsa nandī śṛṃgaprahārataḥ ||23||
patiteʼśve ca chatre ca sindhuraśvāntare gataḥ |
dhārayāmāsa ca tadā chatramanyanmahādhanam ||24||
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ mene nandinaḥ karmaṇāʼsuraḥ |
cukrodha sa mahāvīrānkva gato vaḥ parākramaḥ ||25||
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ kaustubho maitra eva ca |
amātyau taṃ namaskṛtya senayā caturaṃgayā ||26||
mā cintāṃ kuru sindho tvaṃ vighāsyāvo ripūkṣayam |
anyathā na mukhaṃ nātha darśayāvaḥ kathaṃcana ||27||
evamuktvā pracalitau parasainyanibarhaṇau |
padātīnāṃ gaṇā yātā brahmāṇḍasphoṭatatparāḥ ||28||
nyubjatāmicchavo netuṃ bhūgolaṃ raṇadurmadāḥ |
kaustubhoʼtha mayūreśaṃ yāto maitraśca pātituma ||29||
śaravarṣaiḥ paricchādya yātāvasyāntikaṃ yadā |
vīrabhadrastadāyāto yoddhuṃ sa ca ṣaḍānanaḥ ||30||
asaṃkhyasenayā yuktau garjayantau diśo daśa |
nijaghnaturubhau roṣātsindhuṃ nānāprahārataḥ ||31||
kaustubhopyatha maitraśca jaghnatustau parasparam |
sahasva mārayasveti vyāharantaḥ parasparam ||32||
śastrairastrairanekaiśca tābhyāṃ yuddhamakurvatām |
śaravrātairbhiṇḍipālaiḥ parighairmusalairapi ||33||
nānāsenācarā jaghnuḥ parasparavadhepsavaḥ |
naṣṭeṣu sarvaśastreṣu śareṣu ca parasparam ||34||
mallayuddhaṃ pracakruste jīvagrāhamamārayan |
vyasavaḥ patitāḥ kecidvemū raktaṃ mukhādbahu ||35||
pādaṃ pādena jaghnuste skandhaṃ skandhena ttāpare |
pṛṣṭhaṃ pṛṣṭhena kecicca hastaṃ hastena cāpare ||36||
mastakaṃ mastakenaiva kūrparaṃ kūrpareṇa ca |
patitāḥ pātyamānaśca mṛtā bhagnāśca cūrṇitāḥ ||37||
chinnakaṇṭhā bhinnabhujā bhagnajaṅghorubāhavaḥ |
evaṃ naṣṭeṣu sainyeṣu sindhusenā jayaṃ yayau ||38||
jayaśabdaiḥ stūyamānairbandibhirvādyanisvanaiḥ |
sindhudaityeśvaraṃ yātau amātyau mitrakaustubhau ||39||
mayūreśaṃ samāyātau vīrabhadraṣaḍānanau |
tatoʼstamagamatsūryastato yuddhamupāramat ||40||
apare niścayudhyanta devasainyena dānavāḥ |
palāyamānāṃ tāṃ senāṃ nijaghnuḥ śarasaṃcayaiḥ ||41||
punarāveśamāpannau vīrabhadraṣaḍānanau |
garjantau garjayantau ca gaganaṃ ca diśo daśa ||42||
nighnantau ripusenāṃ tāṃ nidarhantau kṛtāntavat |
andhakāre mahāghore na prājñāyata kiṃcana ||43||
svānparānapi jaghnuste yudhyamānā mahābalāḥ |
bhagnāyāṃ sindhusenāyāṃ cakraturjayanisvanam ||44||
daityāmātyau punaryātau maraṇe kṛtaniścayau |
prerayāmāsaturubhau nijāśvau paravāhinīm ||45||
kirantau śarajālāni jaghnatuḥ karavālataḥ |
tataḥ ṣaḍānano dṛṣṭvā tayoratiparākramam |
muṣṭighātena tau hatvā nijasenāmagājjavāta ||46||
mitrastu bhūmimagamadvamanraktaṃ mukhādbahu |
tathāvidhaṃ ca taṃ dṛṣṭvā kaustubho yoddhumāgataḥ ||47||
ṣaḍānanamatho roṣānnijaghne karavālataḥ |
tasminmūrcchāmavāhe tu vīrabhadropyayudhyata ||48||
kaustubhaḥ prāhanadroṣāccharāghātairanakaśaḥ |
soʼpi taṃ prāhanadmuṣṭyā bhūmau cainamaghātayat ||49||
hate tasminnanaṃdāsau vīrabhadro mahābalaḥ |
papāla daityasenā sā hate mitre ca kaustubhe ||50||
tamudaṃtaṃ śastrahatāḥ sravaṃto rudhiraṃ bahū |
nyavedayandaityarājasiṃdhave devaśatrave ||51||
vācā vispaṣṭayā duḥkhācchastrāghātakṛtādbhṛṣam |
jayamākāṃkṣate nityaṃ mitrakaustubhayorbhṛśam ||52||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe mitrakaustubhavadho nāma trayodaśādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||113||

<adhyāya 114 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vīrā ūcuḥ |
nānāśastrapraharaṇau mṛtau tau mitrakaustubhau |
asaṃkhyātā hatā senā tābhyāṃ niśi divāpi ca ||1||
tato ripubalāddṛṣṭau vīrau dvau samupasthitau |
tābhyāṃ balibhyāṃ nihatā nijasenā vimarditā ||2||
yayoḥ karaprahārābhyāṃ gatau sau yamasādanam |
dṛṣṭā nānāvidhā senā na dṛṣṭau tādṛśau surau ||3||
sindhuruvāca |
yau dṛṣṭvā sa bibheti sma kṛtānto bhūtahārakaḥ |
tau kathaṃ śaraghātena mṛtau śaṃsatha sainikāḥ ||4||
parākramamidānīṃ me paśyantu sarvasainikāḥ |
raṇabhūmau nartayiṣye śiraḥ śatrorna saṃśayaḥ ||5||
cakrayuddhaṃ kariṣyāmi cakrapāṇeryataḥ sutaḥ ||
ityuktvā hayamāruhya mayūreśaṃ yayau khalaḥ ||6||
asaṃkhyakoṭyo daityānāṃ puro yātā mahābalāḥ |
yoddhukāmāḥ surendreṇa śivaputreṇa durdharāḥ ||7||
tato nandīpuṣpadantau yoddhuṃ yātau mahābalau |
bhūtarājaśca vikaṭo daśalakṣaiśca yodhibhiḥ ||8||
capalopyardhalakṣeṇa yoddhukāmoʼbhiniryayau |
ṣaḍānano vīrabhadroʼsaṃkhyasenāsamāyutau ||9||
īyaturyuddhabalinau nānāśastradharāvubhau |
saptavyūhānsamālokya prāvyūhandaityasainikāḥ ||10||
gandhāsuroʼtha madanakānto vīho dhvajastathā |
mahākāyaśca śārdūlo dhūrtanāmātha saptamaḥ ||11||
saptavyūhānpuraskṛtya yuyudhustānsamāgatān |
bāṇavṛṣṭyā samācchādya bhallāgraistānnijaghnire ||12||
bhiṇḍipālahatāḥ kecinnipeturdharaṇītale |
ṣaḍānanena yuyudhe balādgandhāsuro balī ||13||
vīrabhadreṇa madanakāntoʼpi yuyudhe raṇe |
nandī ca vīrarājaśca yuyudhāte parasparam ||14||
dhvajāsurapuṣpadantau śastrāstrerbāṇasaṃcayaiḥ |
bhūtarājo mahākāyo yuyudhāte parasparam ||15||
dhūrtaśca vikaṭaścaiva dvandvayuddhamakurvatān |
capalena hato roṣācchārdūlo nyapatadbhuvi ||16||
punaḥ saṃjñāmavāpyainaṃ sopi khaḍgena cāhanat |
mūrcchāmavāpa mahatīṃ capalaścapalo yudhi ||17||
pravṛtte dvandvayuddhe tu yuyudhāte bale ubhe |
jaghnatuḥ śastrasaṃghātairbāṇajālaiḥ parasparam ||18||
sahasva jahi mā bhaistvaṃ hataḥ svargaṃ sameṣyasi |
kṣīṇeṣu śastrabāṇeṣu lattābhirmuṣṭibhiḥ punaḥ ||19||
yuyudhuḥ sarvasainyāni nāśayanti parasparam |
kaścicca caraṇau dhṛtvā pothayāmāsa vairiṇam ||20||
kaściduḍḍīya nyapataccūrṇayāmāsa vai param |
kaścicciccheda ca śiraḥ kaścidbāhuṃ ripo raṇe ||21||
kaścitpādau jānunī ca gulphau ciccheda cāparaḥ |
śaravṛṣṭiṃ pare kecitparasparamavāsṛjan ||22||
sravadraktaḥ śastrahato maraṇāvadhyayudhyata |
kabandhā yuyudhustatra jaghnuḥ svāṃśca parānapi ||23||
apare sahasā peturvidīrṇavakṣaso narāḥ |
parasparāghātahatau mṛtau kecidraṇājire ||24||
ekāpsaraḥ prāptikṛte svargaṃ yuddhamakurvatām |
asṛgnadyabhavattatra keśaśaivalasaṃyutā ||25||
khaḍga matsyavatī kheṭamahākūrmā duratyayā |
pretakāṣṭhavahā ghorā cāmaratūṇasaṃyutā ||26||
kañcukagrāhaśobhāḍhyā mahākaṭakabhekikā |
śūramodakarā bhīrūmahābhayavivardhinī ||27||
medojalā duḥkhatarā māṃsakardamaśālinī |
tatoʼstamita āditye na prājñāyata kiṃcana ||28||
na hantavyā vayaṃ daityavāhinīcāriṇaḥ surāḥ |
na hantavyā vayaṃ devavāhinīcāriṇoʼsurāḥ ||29||
evaṃ kolāhalaistatra prāyudhyanta parasparam |
āśiṣoʼtha dadustatra śvāpadā bhūtarākṣasāḥ ||30||
sṛgālāḥ patagāḥ śyenāstṛptā pārvatinandanam |
dinatrayamabhūdevaṃ divārātrau mahāraṇaḥ ||31||
vādyaśabdairjayaṃ rātrau ghoṣayantaḥ parasparam |
evaṃ gandhāsuroʼyudhyatsenānyā bahuvistaram ||32||
madyapānena mattoʼsau tyaktvā śastrāṇi vegavān |
ahananmuṣṭighātena sahasā taṃ ṣaḍānanam ||33||
nyapatatsa tadā bhūmau vajrāhata ivācalaḥ |
adhāvatsoʼpi labdhvāśu sapakṣa iva parvataḥ ||34||
saṃjñāṃ dvādaśabhirhastairavidhyattaṃ mahāsuram |
śarajālānyanekāni visṛjanṣaḍdhanurvaraiḥ ||35||
nivārya śarajālāni chādayāmāsa taṃ śaraiḥ |
gandhāsuro mahāvegātṣaḍānanamanāmayam ||36||
tannivārya dadhārainaṃ caraṇe sa ṣaḍānanaḥ |
akasmādbhrāmayitvainaṃ pātayāmāsa bhūtale ||37||
tasyāṃga śatadhā jātaṃ gatāsordṛḍhaghātataḥ |
tasminhate tasya senā yayau vyagrā diśo daśa ||38||
pṛṣṭhalagno nindati sma senāṃ tāṃ sa ṣaḍānanaḥ |
senaśca krodhanaścaiva śataghnīṃ yoddhumāyayuḥ ||39||
yeṣāṃ śabdo mahānāsīdghanānāmiva garjatām |
ūcuḥ ṣaḍānanaṃ duṣṭa senā no nihanā bahu ||40||
idānīṃ yāhi bhavanaṃ hato ravisutasya ha |
tata ācchādayāmāsuryugapatte ṣaḍānanam ||41||
grāmasiṃhā yathā siṃhaṃ bahavo balavattarāḥ |
ṣaḍdhanurmocitairbāṇairnyahanattānṣaḍānanaḥ ||42||
tataste mūrchitāḥ peturmuhūrtādutthitā punaḥ |
pāśaṃ kṣiptvā skandagale ninyustaṃ paśuvattrayaḥ ||43||
jñātvā nītaṃ ṣaṇyukhaṃ tamādhāvaṃste trayo javāt |
hiraṇyagarbho balavāṃśyāmalo raktalocanaḥ ||44||
tribhiste muṣṭighātena hatāḥ peturdharātale |
tato madanakāntoʼpi vīrabhadro mahābalam ||45||
nyahanacchastraghātena sa mūrchā mahatīṃ yayo |
tyaktvā mūrchā vīrabhadro daṇḍaghātānnyapāyayat ||46||
mṛtaṃ madanakāntaṃ taṃ vīrarājo mahābalaḥ |
jñātvā prāyānnihantuṃ tāṃstrīnsurānbalavattarān ||47||
tatastānnyahanadroṣācchṛṃgābhyāṃ nandikeśvaraḥ |
asravadrudhiraṃ vaktrācchatadhā bhūtaleʼpatat ||48||
taṃ hataṃ tu vilokyāśu catvāroʼsurasattamāḥ |
āyayurnandinaṃ hantuṃ śārdūloʼtha dhvajāsuraḥ ||49||
mahākāyo dhṛtaścaiva sarvadevajito raṇe |
nānāśastrairnijaghnuste nandinaṃ balavattaram ||50||
patite nandini tataścatvāro yuddhadurmadāḥ |
puṣpadanto bhūtarājo vikaṭaścapaloʼpi ca ||51||
te tu śailānsamādāya tolayitvā nicikṣipuḥ |
sarveṣāṃ mastakā bhinnāścūrṇībhūtāśca kecana ||52||
śarāghātena te sarve dharāyāṃ patitā raṇe |
kecicca sindhumagamaṃśastraghātaprapīḍitāḥ ||53||
parājayaṃ svasainyānāṃ prāhurdevajayaṃ tathā |
jayaṃ prāptā devasenā bahuvādyānyavādayat ||54||
mayūreśa mayūreśa jaya tvaṃ jaya sarvadā |
ityabravīddharṣayutā svānandena nanarta ca ||55||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe caturdaśottaraśatatamodhyāyaḥ ||114||

<adhyāya 115 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
śrutvā senājayaṃ daityau yayau cintāṃ mahattarām |
atyantamlānavadano nimagno duḥkhasāgare ||1||
manasā parituṣṭena cintayāmāsa daityarāṭ |
sindhuruvāca |
viparītamidaṃ kasmājjātaṃ na jñāyate mayā ||2||
pipīlikā grasetkiṃ na brahmāṃḍaṃ lokasaṃkulam |
yasyāgre maśakā devā āsannindrādayo mama ||3||
śivārbhakena tasyādya kathaṃ senā vinirjitā |
ka uvāca |
ityuktvā hayamāruhya dhanurbāṇāsicarmabhṛt ||4||
ahatvā taṃ mayūreśaṃ darśayiṣye mukhaṃ na ca |
ityuktvāʼbhiyayau sindhū raṇabhūmau pataṃgavat ||5||
sajjaṃ cakāra ca dhanurnādayangaganaṃ diśaḥ |
vyasṛjadbāṇajālāni tīkṣṇānyagnimukhāni saḥ ||6||
cakampe pṛthivī sarvā mūrchitā jantavoʼkhilāḥ |
prākṣipaddevasenāyāmasaṃkhyātāśca patriṇaḥ ||7||
chinnā bhinnāḥ surāḥ petustacchastrāghātapīḍitāḥ |
palāyantaḥ surāḥ petusteʼpi pṛṣṭhe śarairhatāḥ ||8||
tataḥ sarvā mayūreśavāhinī vyagratāmiyāt |
dvidhābhūtā jānupādāḥ keṣāṃcinmastakā api ||9||
śaravṛṣṭyā sindhuhastamuktayā raṇamūrdhani |
abhavattumulaṃ yuddhamanivārya bhayāvaham ||10||
sindhoścaiva gaṇeśasya senayorubhayorapi |
kolāhale mahāghore dhvānte gāḍhe rajaḥkṛte ||11||
purogāṃ jaghnire sarve parānsvānapi te tadā |
śastrāghātairhatā senā devasenācaraiḥ parā||||16||
tataḥ sindhurhayāttasmādavaruhya padeʼgrahīt |
pātayāmāsa sahasā vīrabhadraṃ ca vegataḥ ||13||
ahananmastake soʼtha nandinaṃ balavattarama |
bhūtarājaṃ ca khaḍgena mārayansahasā kaṭau ||14||
puṣpadantasyodaraṃ ca bibheda balavattaram |
śikhāmākramya prākarṣaddhiraṇyagarbhamojasā ||15||
śyāmalasya lalāṭaṃ sa bibheda śaraghātataḥ |
capalasya hanū roṣājjaghāna vikaṭaṃ raṇe ||16||
lambakarṇasya kaṇṭhaṃ sa bibheda bāṇasaṃcayaiḥ |
dhṛtvā tu caraṇau raktalocanaṃ ca nyapātayat ||17||
hastānnistīrya sahasā samukhaḥ prāpalajjavāt |
somaṃ lattāprahāreṇa raṇabhūmāvapātayat ||18||
jaṭharaṃ dārayāmāsa bhṛṃgiṇoʼsiprahārataḥ |
dānavānalasya ca śiro bhedayāmāsa sāyakaiḥ ||19||
paṃcānanasya pṛṣṭhe soʼvadhīcca capalo yudhi |
evaṃ sarve mahāvīrā nihatā raṇamūrdhani ||20||
drutaṃ palāyitāścānye tataḥ sindhuḥ puro yarau |
jagarja ghanavadghoraṃ nādayanbhuvanatrayam ||21||
sa vivyādha mayūreśaṃ śarajālairanekadhā |
tasya dehaṃ samālokya vikarālaṃ bhayānakam ||22||
virūpākṣādayaḥ sarve palāyanamakurvata |
muniyukto gaṇeśaśca yuyudhe saṃyuge tadā ||23||
sa cakampe kariśiśuḥ siṃhasyevāsya darśanāt |
tatoʼbravīdroṣavaśātsindhustaṃ tu guṇeśvaram ||24||
sindhuruvāca |
dūrātteʼśrāvi bahuśaḥ puruṣārthaḥ śivārbhaka |
pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyase re tvaṃ jambūka iva durbalaḥ ||25||
kathaṃ me sahase mūḍha talāghātaṃ śilābhidam |
tvayā mātuḥ stanaṃ pītvā nartavyaṃ hi gṛhāṃgaṇe ||26||
śakrādayo jitā yena tasya te gaṇanāʼtra kā |
nivārayati māṃ mūḍha kṛpā tvāṃ hantumutsukam ||27||
kathaṃ te komalāṃgeṣu vidhye tīkṣṇatarāñśarān |
deva uvāca |
kimarthaṃ valgase mūḍhabhāvenādhama pāmara ||28||
nāvatāraṃ gṛhīṣyeʼhaṃ yadi jāneʼlpakaṃ vapuḥ |
tavedānīṃ haniṣyāmi kṣaṇena dehamalpakam ||29||
sūryadattādvarādduṣṭa duṣkarmanirato bhavān |
idānīṃ sa gataḥ kālo mṛtyukāla upasthite ||30||
valganākuśalastvaṃ kiṃ yuddhamadya kariṣyasi |
tvā hantuṃ mocituṃ devāndhṛtaṃ rūpamidaṃ mayā ||31||
antakāle sannihite puruṣārtho vṛthā bhavet |
mayā hate mahāsthānaṃ mama prāpsyasi durlabham ||32||
sindhuruvāca |
yāvanna cchedmi te mūḍha komalaṃ dehamuttamam |
tāvadvalgasi yo yasya bhaktaḥ syātsa mṛto vrajet ||33||
tasya lokaṃ vṛthā hi tvaṃ na praśaṃsāṃ vadātmanaḥ |
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā mahādaityaḥ sāyakaṃ parabhedinam ||34||
na kadācitprakaṭitamatitīkṣṇaṃ jayāvahama |
smṛtvā sūryaṃ dhanurgṛhya sajjaṃ kṛtvā nyayojayat ||35||
ṭaṇatkāreṇa dhanuṣo nāditaṃ bhuvanatrayam |
mumoca bāṇaṃ taṃ deve mayūreśe balātkhalaḥ ||36||
sa jajvāla diśaḥ sarvā gaganaṃ vidiśoʼpi ca |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sa mayūreśastatyāja paraśuṃ nijam ||37||
vajrātsārataraṃ sarvavairidarpaharaṃ param |
adahatsopi trīṃllokānpralayānalasannibhaḥ ||38||
paraśuḥ khaṇḍayāmāsa nabhasyeva ca taṃ śaram |
dharāyāṃ pātya śatadhā daityahastamathācchinat ||39||
dhanurbhārasahaṃ ghoraṃ nabhasyaiva gataṃ karāt |
tataḥ krodhena daityaḥ sa cakraṃ tatyāja śatrave ||40||
śatahūdeva tacchabdaṃ cakāra garjayandiśaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā tatra mayūreśaḥ śūlamugraṃ mumoca ha ||41||
dadāha bhuvanaṃ tacca papāta daityamastake |
mukuṭaṃ kuṇḍale karṇau khaṇḍayitvā yayau punaḥ ||42||
mayūreśakaraṃ śīghraṃ yathā na tyājitaṃ tathā |
tataḥ krodhāduvācainaṃ chinnakarṇo mahāsuraḥ ||43||
pauruṣaṃ darśitaṃ teʼdya darśayiṣye tavāpi ca |
idānīṃ śaraghātena chinadmi nāsikāṃ tava ||44||
ityuktvā khaḍgahastoʼsau taṃ jagāma guṇeśvaram |
mayūreśastadā cakre nānārūpāṇi sarvataḥ ||45||
āyudhāni ca catvāri bibhrankaracatuṣṭaye |
tataḥ sa vismayāviṣṭo daśadikṣu vyalokayat ||46||
dadarśa tatra taṃ devaṃ caturāyudhabhūṣitam |
tataḥ sa lajjito gehaṃ nijaṃ gantuṃ manodadhe ||47||
tathāpi puratoʼpaśyanmayūreśaṃ tathāvidham |
ācchādya netre sa yayau hṛdyenaṃ samalokayan ||48||
unmīlya netre soʼpaśyatpura enaṃ śikhaṇḍini |
tato daityo bubodhainaṃ svānte raviprasādata, ||49||
raṇasthalātatpurīṃ yāta ācchādya vadanāmbujam |
hataśeṣaiśca vīraiśca tādṛśaiḥ parivāritaḥ ||50||
paryaṃke nyapataccintāparyākulitamānasaḥ |
virāste svagṛhayātāścintāvyākulacetasaḥ ||51||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe sindhuvairūpyakaraṇaṃnāma paṃcadaśādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||115||

<adhyāya 116 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
jayaṃ prāpte mayūreśe yayustatra munīśvarāḥ |
śivoʼpi girijāyukto yayau taṃ ca nirīkṣitum ||1||
autsukyātpārvatī cainaṃ samāliṃgyābravīttadā |
pārvatyuvāca |
atiśrāntoʼsi daityasya kaṭhinasya raṇaṃ suta ||2||
komalāni tavāṃgāni kathaṃ śastrāṇi sehire |
kṛtāntasadṛśo daityo māyāvī balavattaraḥ ||3||
sa kathaṃ komalāṃgena tvayā yudhi vinirjitaḥ |
pārudatkaruṇaṃ devī dhṛtvāṃgaṃ nijabālakam ||4||
tato munigaṇāḥ śambhurvārayāmāsa tāṃ śivām |
śiva uvāca |
na jānīṣe mayūreśaṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam |
anādinidhanaṃ devaṃ vedāntāgocaraṃ vibhum |
trayastriṃśatkoṭidevairnutaṃ devāridāraṇam ||6||
sṛṣṭisthitijñānahetuṃ layahetumanāmayam |
bhūbhārottāraṇārthāya nānārūpadharaṃ param ||7||
brahmāṇḍavyāptaromāṃkamekānekasvarūpiṇam |
tapobalasamāyuktaṃ yogaśāstrasamanvitam ||8||
māyāvibhedakaṃ devaṃ māyināmapi māyinam |
bālabhāveʼpi daityānāṃ hantāraṃ kiṃ na vetsi tam ||9||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ stutiṃ samākarṇya dṛṣṭā sā pārvatī tadā |
tato dṛṣṭo mayūreśo munīnprāha śivaṃ śivāma ||10||
mayūreśa uvāca |
na bhayaṃ vajrahastānme anyasmāttatkathaṃ bhavet |
sarveṣāṃ namanānmātarmunīnāmāśiṣoʼpi ca ||11||
śivasya varadānena sindhudaityo jito mayā |
skandādayo jitā yena yudhyante na hi tena te ||12||
asaṃkhyātā hatā devā daityasenācarairnaraiḥ |
asṛgnadyaḥ saṃpravṛttā vīraharṣakarā bahu ||13||
raṇe nipatitāndevānvicinvāmo munīśvarāḥ |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ mayūreśavaco niśamya prāha dharmavit ||14||
vasiṣṭho munivaryastaṃ yāmaḥ sarve calantu vai |
tato yayau raṇe draṣṭuṃ munibhistairmayūrarāṭa ||15||
majjāraktavasāgandho ghrātuṃ yatra na śakyate |
atibhīṣaṇakāyāṃ stāṃśchinnabhinnānanekaśaḥ ||16||
dṛṣṭvā bībhatsarūpāṃstānpraphullānkiṃśukāniva |
vikṛtiṃ pariyātāni manāṃsi dānavadviṣām ||17||
paśyatāṃ vīrarāśīṃstānvīropari pratiṣṭhitān |
kāṃścicca jīvataḥ kāṃścinmūrchitāñśastravātataḥ ||18||
kāṃścicca praviśīrṇāṃgāndevāndevā vyakarṣayan |
sahasā na vidurdevā devānvā dānavānapi ||19||
abruvaṃstatra kecicca tvadardaṃ mṛtyumāgatāḥ |
ante te darśanaṃ prāptamityuktvā vyasṛjannasūn ||20||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ śrutvā tu tadvākyaṃ sāśrukaṇṭho mayūrarāṭ |
bhramanbhramandadarśāgre mūrchitaṃ tu ṣaḍānanam ||21||
patadaśrurmayūreśaḥ snehāttaṃ cābravīditi |
uttiṣṭhotiṣṭha bhadraṃ te kiṃ śeṣe prākṛto yathā ||22||
śrāntoʼsi daityahananādyacchāliṃganamadya me |
pramārjitastu hastena tataḥ sa prodatiṣṭhata ||23||
apaśyannikaṭe devapādapadmaṃ bhayāpaham |
uvāca ca mayūreśaṃ darśanātte gataṃ mama ||24||
sarvaduḥkhaṃ śramaścaiva viśrāntaścāsmi sāmpratam |
āliliṃga mayūreśaṃ dorbhirdvāśabhistadā ||25||
ubhau yātau raṇaṃ draṣṭuṃ gṛhītvā ca parasparam |
apaśyatāṃ puraḥ suptaṃ vīrabhadramiṣukṣatam ||26||
nandinaṃ ca śarāghātaiḥ pīḍitaṃ balavattaram |
bhūtarājaṃ ca purato vibhinnamastakaṃ tadā ||27||
krandantaṃ vikaṭaṃ caiva puṣpadantaṃ bhṛśārditam |
lalāṭe dṛḍhaviddhaṃ ca hiraṇyagarbhameva ca ||28||
mṛtaprāyaṃ ca capalaṃ mṛtaṃ śyāmalameva ca |
lambakarṇo na cājñāsīdbhrāntyā kaṃcana saṃgare ||29||
udānavāyunākrāntaḥ sumukhastatra cābhavat |
paramaṃ khedamāpannaḥ somaśca raktalocanaḥ ||30||
śete sma mūrchayāḥ bhṛṃgī paṃcāsyaśca mumūrṣayā |
śaṃkhaḥ pretatvamāpanno bhayakṛtsarvayodhinām ||31||
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvavīrānhataprāyānhatānapi |
cintayā parayāviṣṭo papraccha taṃ ṣaḍānanam ||32||
mayūreśa uvāca |
asurāśca svakīyāśca mahāvīrā hatā mṛdhe |
nijadhāmagatāḥ sarve jīvatāmatra kā gatiḥ ||33||
skanda uvāca |
anantakoṭibrahmāṇḍanāyakastvaṃ guṇeśvara |
caturdaśānāṃ vidyānāṃ sarvāsāṃ ca svayaṃ nidhiḥ ||34||
kalānāṃ tatra ki praśnaṃ karoṣi tvaṃ mamāntike |
nāhaṃ mantraṃ vijānāmi vettha tvaṃ hi na vai surāḥ ||35||
vivardhayasi kīrtiṃ tvaṃ nānādaityavibhaṃjanāt |
yathāmati bravīmi tvāmājñayā ca smṛterbalāt ||36||
pūrvaṃ tu tripuravadhe śivena yuddhamadbhutaṃ |
kṛtaṃ tatra mṛtāndevāndroṇādrivallijaṃ rasam ||37||
devakṣateṣu saṃyojya jīvayāmāsa tatkṣaṇāt |
ka uvāca |
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ devo jagāda taṃ ṣaḍānam ||38||
idānīṃ daityakoṭyastu yuddhārthaṃ balavattarāḥ |
āyāsyanti tadā koʼtra yuddhettābhiḥ ṣaḍānana ||39||
kaścānetuṃ vrajedadriṃ vallīrasamanuttamam |
evaṃ bruvanmayūreśo māyāmāviścakāra ha ||40||
ujjīvayāmāsa tadā nijāṃgavāyumaṃgataḥ |
tataste harṣasaṃpannāḥ praṇemustaṃ guṇeśvaram ||41||
āliliṃguḥ punaruyuddhaṃ kartuṃ taṃ ca vijijñapuḥ |
tvaddṛṣṭivinipātena tejo no dviguṇīkṛtam ||42||
guṇeśvara uvāca |
brahmādayo devagaṇāḥ stuvanti vaḥ parākramama |
tārakāsuramukhyāśca nihatā daityadānavāḥ ||43||
ka uvāca |
tatastaiḥ sahitā devairmayūreśaḥ śivaṃ yayau |
namaskṛtya śivaṃ gaurīṃ yuddhāya gantumudyataḥ ||44||
āliliṃga mudā gaurī śivastaṃ ca guṇeśvaram |
tataḥ ṣaḍānanādyāste śivamāliṃgire mudā ||45||
vayaṃ raṇe nipatitāḥ punaḥ saṃjīvitā hara |
mayūreśāṃga saṃbhūtavāyunāʼdbhūtakāriṇā ||46||
punaryuddhāya yāsyāmoʼnena sārddhaṃ śivādhunā |
prasādāttava jeṣyāmaḥ sarvānevāsurānyudhi ||47||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa krīḍākhaṇḍe raṇaśodhanaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśādhika śatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||116||

<adhyāya 117 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
sindhustalpagataścintāmavāpa mahatīṃ tadā |
agnisaṃtaptahṛdayo vicāra nānvapadyata ||1||
nirharṣo mlānavadano nistejaḥ niṣkaloʼpi saḥ |
tata ugrātmajā durgā lāvaṇyalaharī śubhā ||2||
cintāśāviṣadagdhaṃ taṃ śrutvāʼgānmaṃcakasthitam |
asaṃkhyabhūṣaṇaṃ ryāssyiāḥ kabarī bhāti mastake ||3||
kastūrītilakaṃ yasyā lalāṭe bhāti sundaram |
kaṇṭhe hāraṃ kaṭau kāṃñci bibhratīṃ ratnasaṃyutām ||4||
sarvālaṃkārasaṃyuktāṃ sarvāvayava sundarām |
āgatāṃ tu nirīkṣyaiva maṃcakābhyāśamutsukām ||5||
bahistatsevakā yātāstataḥ sā patimabravīt  |
durgovāca |
kiṃ cintāṃ kuruṣe svāminyadbhāvyaṃ tadbhaviṣyati ||6||
īśvarasya vaśe loko na svatantraḥ kadācana |
sarveṣāṃ mānasaṃ tvaṃ kimudvejayasi meʼpi ca ||7||
ko hetuste vada vibho tato yuktiṃ vadāmi te |
ka uvāca |
priyā vākyaṃ samākarṇya gaṇḍakīnāyakastadā |
sāvadhānamanā bhūtvā vṛttāntaṃ tāmathābravīt |
sindhuruvāca |
kiṃ vadāmi priye ślāghye manaḥkhedakaraṃ param ||9||
yudhtato me raṇe karṇau mayūreśena sāditau |
saptakoṭimitā senā nihatā pauruṣeṇa ha ||10||
skandādayo mahāvīrāḥ patitāḥ śaravṛṣṭibhiḥ |
akasmānme hatau karṇau triśūlaṃ preṣya śatruṇā ||11||
ācchādya mukhamāyāto vāsasā nijamandiram |
yenopāyena me śatruvadhaḥ syāttaṃ vadasva me ||12||
durgovāca |
vīradharmaḥ kṛtaḥ samyaṅnihatāḥ sarvasainikāḥ |
na yaśaḥ prāpyate svāmingobrāhmaṇasuradviṣām ||13||
taddveṣāccaiva kalyāṇaṃ kasyāpi hi na jāyate, sevanādvandanāddhyānātsmaraṇātpūjanādapi ||14||
devairindrādibhiḥ sthānānyāptāni ca sthirāṇi ca |
yaḥ samaḥ sarvabhūteṣu śubhāśubhaphalapradaḥ ||15||
tatsevayāʼbhiṣṭasiddhiḥ kāmadhenorivābhavat |
aṅkuro jāyate tādṛgyādṛgbījaṃ hi upyate ||16||
aśubhātkarmaṇo duḥkhaṃ sukhaṃ syācchubhakarmaṇaḥ |
ataḥ santaḥ prakurvanti śubham karmaṃ sadādarāt ||17||
hitaṃ ca sarvajantūnāṃ kāyena manasā girā |
bhavatā puruṣārthena pīḍitāste surarṣayaḥ ||18||
puruṣārthaḥ sa vijñeyo yaścaturṇāṃ hi sādhakaḥ |
yanmanonārthalubdhaṃ syātparadāreṣu na kvacit ||19||
sa eva puruṣārthaḥ syādyaścānindyaṃ na nindati |
śaraṇāgatarakṣāyāṃ dṛḍho dharmaparāyaṇaḥ ||20||
samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu puruṣārthaḥ sa gamyate |
svāminme paramaṃ vākyaṃ śṛṇu te hitakārakam ||21||
aduṣṭavāksatyaśīlaḥ svaguṇānāmakīrtakaḥ |
paropakaraṇe saktaḥ parapaiśūnyavarjitaḥ ||22||
tatkuruśva mahābhāga mama prītiṃ yadīcchasi |
mocayasva surānsarvānprabaddhāñśakrasaṃyutān ||23||
prayāsyati mayūśoʼkhilalokaprapālakaḥ |
vatsyāvaḥ susukhaṃ svāminnānyathā tu sukhaṃ bhavet ||24||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ vākyāmṛtaṃ tasyāstadabhūdviṣasannibham |
mumūrṣorauṣadhaṃ yadvadgraharogāplutasya ha ||25||
krodhasaṃraktanayano durgāṃ tāṃ sindhurabravīt |
na dadhre hṛdi tasyāḥ sa upadeśaṃ hitāvaham ||26||
sindhuruvāca |
samyaguktaṃ tvayā bhadre lokanindākaraṃ vacaḥ |
cāturyaṃ tava vijñātaṃ kāryākāryaṃvidā mayā ||27||
na śatruṃ bhajate koʼpi ripurmānaparāyaṇaḥ |
nārabhetkṛtyamanyāyyamārabdhaṃ cenna tattyajet ||28||
tato duḥkhaṃ sukhaṃ vā syādayaśo yaśa eva ca |
lābho vāpyathavāʼlābho jīvanaṃ mṛtyureva vā ||29||
pūrvaṃ bhadre mayā sandhiḥ sāmnā tena na vai kṛtaḥ |
idānīṃ bhavitā yadyattadbhavatyeva sarvathā ||30||
pūrvameva hi yajjantorlikhitaṃ sādhvasādhu vā |
kaścinna puruṣārthena kartuṃ śaktoʼnyathā sati ||31||
raṇe mṛtasya svargotha khyātiḥ syādviṣṭapatraye |
yātasya śaraṇaṃ śatrāreyaśo narakeʼpi ca ||32||
pūrvajaiḥ sahitasyāśu jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
jānāmi taṃ mayūreśaṃ devadevaṃ jagadgurum ||33||
saṃbhūtaṃ mama nāśāya rāvaṇe rāghavaṃ yathā |
pātayiṣye śiroʼsyāhamiti me niścitā mati ||34||
tyajanti jīvitaṃ śūrā nābhimānaṃ kadācana |
na meʼsti gaṇanā subhru kṛtāntasyāpi karhicit ||35||
tatra kā gaṇanāʼnyeṣāṃ lajjā me karṇataḥ kṛtā |
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā bhūṣaṇāni vastrāṇi ca dadhāra saḥ ||36||
keyūraṃ mukuṭaṃ ratnayuktaṃ hāraṃ ca kuṇḍale |
tūṇau khaḍgaṃ ca kheṭaṃ ca cāpaṃ sajjaṃ ca śastrikām ||37||
ācchādya karṇau raukmīyavastreṇoṣṇīṣagena ca |
āyayāvupaviṣṭaḥ sa bhadrāsanamuttamam ||38||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe durgāvākyaṃ nāma saptadaśādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||117||

<adhyāya 118 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
yuddhāveśadurādharṣaḥ upaviṣṭo mahāsane |
sa tadā vīramukhyāṃstānupaviṣṭānathābravīt ||1||
sindhuruvāca |
gatau mama pradhānau tau trailokyākarṣaṇakṣamau |
na tādṛśoʼsti vīroʼtra yo me hanyādripuṃ balāt ||2||
naitādṛśau buddhimantau parākramajayaiṣiṇau |
vīrā ūcustataḥ sindhuṃ mā cintāṃ kuru sarvathā ||3||
yāvadvayaṃ prāṇavanto laghukṛtya upasthite |
kimarthaṃ cintākulatāṃ tāvadvrajasi daityarāṭ ||4||
svāpaṃ kuru mahābhāga paryaṃke puṣyasaṃstare |
kṛtāntastāḍito yābhyāṃ tayoḥ kasmādbhayaṃ bhavet ||5||
evamuktvā kalaḥ sindhuṃ vikalaśca mahābalau |
śālakau tasya daityasya niryātau yuddhadurmadau ||6||
nānāyuddheṣu kuśalau nānāyudhadharāvubhau |
nānābhūṣaṇavastrāḍhyau nānāsenāsamanvitau ||7||
nānāgarjanato vyoma cakraturnādasaṃyutam |
devakoṭinihantārau asaṃkhyavāhinīyutau ||8||
yayustatpurato vīrāḥ sindūrāruṇamastakāḥ |
agniśastradharā muktakeśapāśāḥ sacandanāḥ ||9||
lasacchastradharāḥ sarve yamamattumivodyatāḥ |
tataḥ karighaṭā yātā nānādhātuvicitritāḥ ||10||
adhiṣṭhātā mahāmātrairnānābhūṣāvirājitāḥ |
tīkṣṇadantāḥ sinduritā ghaṇṭānādanināditāḥ ||11||
tatoʼśvasādino yātāstanutrāṇavirājitā |
dhanurbāṇadharāḥ sarve lasacchatravibhūṣitāḥ ||12||
anekayuddhasambhāra rathā yātāstataḥ param |
anekavīrasaṃyuktā nānāsūtavirājitāḥ ||13||
evaṃ tau śālakau yātau raṇasthānaṃ ruṣānvitau |
dadṛśāte mayūreśamāśīviṣabhujiḥsthitam ||14||
ṣaḍānanādibhirvīrairanyainyaśca parivāritam |
dṛṣṭvā taistu tayoḥ senā ghorā sā caturaṃgiṇī ||15||
asaṃkhyātaṃ balaṃ tasya samāyātaṃ vicitritām |
preṣito devapravarairdūto nītiviśāradaḥ ||16||
jñātvā vṛttāntamāyātaḥ sa jagāda surānprati |
dūta uvāca |
kalaśca vikalaścaiva nānāsenāsamāyutau ||17||
jetuṃ surānsamāyātau nānāyuddhaviśāradau |
