śṛṇu prahlāda yenāsya vāhanaṃ mūṣakoʼbhavat |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śravaṇātsarvakāmadam ||6||
purā śakrasabhāmadhye krauñcau gandharvasattamaḥ |
vāmadevamuniṃ soʼtha tvarāvānprapadāʼhanat ||7||
sa śaśāpa ca gandharvaṃ mūṣakastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi |
kalpayiṣyati vāhatve tvāmākramya guṇeśvaraḥ ||8||
sa eva karuṇāyukto mokṣaṃ neṣyati viśvakṛt |
parāśarāśrame soʼtha papāta mūṣakākṛtiḥ ||9||
giriśṛṃganibho ghoro mahāromā mahānasvaḥ |
mahāvīryo mahādaṃṣṭo mahāsvanavirājitaḥ ||10||
tasyāśrame mahāghoramupadravamathākarot |
bhakṣayāmāsa dhānyāni bhaṅaktvā mṛtpātrasaṃcayān ||11||
pustakāni ca vastrāṇi valkalāni mahāntyapi |
sa sārabhūtaṃ yatsarvaṃ bhakṣayāmāsa mūṣakaḥ ||12||
pucchāghātena vṛkṣānsa pātayāmāsa bhūtale |
cuñcukāreṇa śabdena garjayāmāsa viṣṭapam ||13||
jñātvā tathāvidhaṃ taṃ tu cintāmāpa munīśvaraḥ |
durjanasya balātkāraiḥ sthānaṃ tyājyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||14||
adhunā kvānugantavyaṃ kasminsthāne sukhaṃ bhavet |
prāṇatyāge mahaddoṣaṃ vadanti śāstracintakāḥ ||15||
kathaṃ mukhāśrame duḥkhe prāptaṃ kamaprasaṃgataḥ |
kasya vā smaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ ko vā duḥkhātpramokṣyati ||16||
ka enaṃ nāśayedatra kaḥ samartho bhaved vadhe |
kamadya śaraṇaṃ yāyāṃ ko nastrātā bhaviṣyati ||17||
evaṃ pitṛvacaḥ śrutvā bālakoʼnantavikramaḥ |
uvāja madhuraṃ vākyaṃ cintāṃ mā kuru sarvathā ||18||
mayi tiṣṭhati duṣṭānāṃ saṃhāriṇi mahāprabhau |
putratāṃ te prayātoʼsmi vidhāsye te priyaṃ tu yat ||19||
yasya me kranditeneyaṃ pṛthivī śīrṇatāmiyāt |
parvatāścūrṇaṃtāmāpuḥ pādāghātena me mune ||20||
paśya me kautukaṃ tāta nayāmyenaṃ tu vāhatām |
evamuktvāʼsṛjatpāśaṃ koṭisūryanibhaṃ śiśuḥ ||21||
megheṣviva taḍitpuñjaḥ śuśubhe soʼntarikṣagaḥ |
tatyajurbhayato devā svāni dhiṣṇyāni tatkṣaṇāt ||22||
sa yayau vahnivadano daśadigbhramaṇe rataḥ |
pātāle mūṣakaṃ kaṇṭhe baddhvā bahirakāśayat ||23||
mūrchāmavāpa mahatīmākhuḥ pāśabalārditaḥ |
śuśoca paramakruddho niruddhaśvāsamārutaḥ ||24||
kathaṃ kālasya maraṇamāgataṃ daivanirmitam|||
bhavitavyaṃ bhavatyeva puruṣārtho nirarthakaḥ ||25||
yena daṃṣṭrāgramātreṇa dāritāḥ parvatā bahu |
na mayā gaṇitā devā nāsurā rākṣasā narāḥ ||26||
tasya me kena pāśena kaṇṭhoʼkrāmi gatāyuṣaḥ
ka uvāca |
mūṣake prabruvatyevaṃ yāvattāvadgajānanaḥ ||27||
cakarṣa manasā taṃ sa soʼgānmūṣakasaṃyutaḥ |
yathā gārūḍaśāstrajñaḥ sarpaṃ karṣati tatkṣaṇāt ||28||
sa taṃ dṛṣṭavā pāśakaṇṭho mūṣako labdhadhīḥ kṣaṇāt |
namaskṛtya vibhuṃ devaṃ gajānanamanāmayam ||29||
tuṣṭāva parayā bhaktyā cidānandaghanaṃ prabhum |
tvameva jagatāṃ nāthaḥ kartā hartā prapālakaḥ ||30||
guṇatrayavihīnaśca guṇatrayasahāyakṛt |
māyātītoʼpi māyāvī māyināmapi mohakṛta ||31||
brahmādīnāmagamyastvaṃ munihṛtpadmasaṃsthitaḥ |
karaṇa karaṇaṃ kartā kāraṇānāṃ ca kāraṇam ||32||
soʼdya dṛṣṭaḥ svacakṣurbhyāṃmato dhanyaṃ janurmama |
dhanyā me pitarau netre tapo vidyā vrataṃ japaḥ ||33||
ka uvāca |
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ prītoʼsau dviradānanaḥ |
prāha bhaktiṃ dṛḍhāṃ jñātvā bhaktaṃ taṃ mūṣakaṃ vibhuḥ ||34||
tavaiva puruṣārthena devadvijadruhoʼnagha |
nirguṇoʼhaṃ guṇī jāto duṣṭanāśanahetave ||35||
sādhūnāṃ rakṣaṇārthāya yatastvaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
ataste dattamabhayaṃ varaṃ śṛṇu yamicchasi ||36||
mūṣaka uvāca |
nāhaṃ vṛṇe gajāsya tvāṃ tvameva vṛṇu vāṃchitam |
ka uvāca |
ityukto mūṣakenāsau garvitena gajānanaḥ ||37||
vāhatāṃ mama yāhi tvaṃ yadi satyaṃ vacastava |
tathetyukte sa pigākṣo rurohākramya tatkṣaṇāt ||38||
bhāreṇa cūrṇayaṃstvaṃ tu punardeva sa yācata |
ahaṃ te vāhanaṃ jāto laghubhūto bhava prabho ||39||
yācatastasya vacasā laghubhāroʼbhavadvibhuḥ |
etaddṛṣṭavā mahāścaryaṃ praṇamya munirabravīt ||40||
nā bāle pauruṣaṃ kvāpi mayā dṛṣṭaṃ jagattraye |
yacchabdātparvatāḥ śīrṇā lokapālāścyutasthalāḥ ||41||
sa tvayā kṣaṇamātreṇa nīto vāhanatāṃ balāt |
tata āgatya mātāʼsya vatsalā gṛhya taṃ śiśum ||42||
prasnutau pāyayāmāsa stanau mātā mudā yutā |
uvāca na ca jāne te svarūpaṃ ca parākramam ||43||
janmajanmāntarīyeṇa puṇyena nau gṛhaṃ gataḥ |
tataḥ sa mūṣakaṃ baddhvā cikrīḍe prākṛto yathā ||44||
evaṃ te kathitā cāsya yathā mūṣakavāhatā ||45||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe catustriṃśādhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||134||

<adhyāya 135 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
kimanena kṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ ca padmaja |
yenāyaṃ mauṣakaṃ janma pāpa devasya vāhatām ||1||
ka uvāca |
samyakpṛṣṭaṃ tvayā vatsa mamāpi manasaḥ priyam |
bravīmi sakalaṃ tacca tadihaikamanāḥ śṛṇu ||2||
sumeruśikhare ramyaḥ saubharerāśramo mahāna |
abhūdvakṣasamākīrṇo nānāpakṣigaṇānvitaḥ ||3||
vasiṣṭhādyā munigaṇā devā indrapurogamāḥ |
āyanti saubharastasya darśanārthaṃ dine dine ||4||
mahattapovṛddhidīpteratyagniravitejasaḥ |
khyātasya sarvalokeṣu dhyānaniṣṭhasya ceśvare ||5||
tasya patnī mahābhāgā manomayīti viśrutā |
pativratāsu sarvāsu vikhyātā bhartṛvallabhā ||6||
ramyatā tu ratestasyā jitā ramyatayāʼkhilā |
śacīprabhṛtayaḥ sarvā na prāpuḥ samatālavam ||7||
kadācitsaubharirhomaśālāyāṃ prātarutthitaḥ |
kṛtvā homaṃ vanaṃ yātaḥ samidarthaṃ gṛhe sthitā ||8||
manomayī suśīlā sā gṛhakāryaratāʼbhavat |
tadā duṣṭastu gandharvaḥ krauṃcanāmā samāgataḥ ||9||
viśrāntirabhavattasya dṛṣṭvāśramamanuttamam |
nānāśālāsamākīrṇaṃ bahucchāyaṃ suśītalam ||10||
etādṛśo ramyatara āśramo yasya sa prabhuḥ |
dhanyaṃ japastapastasya kṣaṇenāyaṃ sukhapradaḥ ||11||
cirasthānānmuktidaḥ syādityuktvā tadgṛhaṃ yayau |
dadarśa mukhacandraṃ sa manomayyā manoramam ||12||
yasyā darśanamātreṇa haroʼpi smarasādbhavet |
tasyāḥ kaṭākṣapātena madanānilavihvalaḥ ||13||
sa babhūva tadāsakto dadhāra sahasā kare |
tatkarasparśamātreṇa cakampe sā mumūrcha ca ||14||
manomayī na śaśāpa bhartṛsmaraṇatatparā |
mlānā ca śuṣkakaṇṭhā ca sasvedākṣisravajjalā ||15||
babhūva paramodvignā mumoha ca śuśoca ca |
kamadya śaraṇaṃ yāmi koʼsmādduṣṭātpramokṣyate ||16||
na kṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ kiṃcidihajanmani saṃsmare |
janmāntarīyapāpena sukhaduḥkhamupasthitam, ||17||
duṣṭabhāvaṃ tu taṃ jñātvā sāntvayantyabravīdidam |
ahaṃ duhitṛtulyā te janakena samoʼsi me ||18||
jñānavānasi pāpe tvaṃ mā vartasva gatatrapa |
pāpino yānti narakaṃ varṣakoṭigaṇānbahūn ||19||
tasmānmuṃca mahābhāga kṛpaṇāṃ putrikopamā |
no cedahaṃ kariṣyāmi prāṇatyāgaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||20||
strīhatyādoṣasaṃbhūtaṃ pāpaṃ te syānmahattaram |
mama bhartā mahābhāgo vanādāyāsyateʼdhunā ||21||
tasya krodhānalastvāṃ hi bhasmatvaṃ neṣyate kṣaṇāt |
vijñānāṃ tasya no kiṃcitkāryaṃ kārya mayā laghu ||22||
no cettvāṃ bhasmasātkuryā sṛṣṭiṃ ca brahmaṇoʼpi ca |
evaṃ vadantyāṃ tasyāṃ tu saubhariḥ samupāgataḥ ||23||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā svāṃgaṇagata madhyāhnaravisannibham |
mumoca tejasā tatra dharṣitoʼsau karaṃ tadā ||24||
adhoʼpaśyaccakampe ca mamlāya ca vibhāya ca |
muniḥ provāca sahasā pralayāgniriva jvalan ||25||
utsasarja tadā śāpaṃ gandharvaṃ prati duḥsaham |
muniruvāca |
asamakṣaṃ yato me tvaṃ patnīṃ dharṣitavānasi ||26||
asamakṣacaro mūḍha mūṣakastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi |
cauravatpṛthivī dāryaṃ svodaraṃ pūrayiṣyasi ||27||
krauṃca uvāca |
na mayā buddhipūrvaṃ te dharṣiteyaṃ manomayī |
prasaṃgādabhavatsaṃgo dṛṣṭvemāṃ cārarūpiṇam ||28||
karamātradhṛtā ceyaṃ tāvattvamapi dṛṣṭavān |
tejasā te prabhītena mayā muktāʼnaghā mune ||29||
atoʼparādhaṃ me kṣantumarhasi tvaṃ kṛpānidhe |
kuruṣvānugrahaṃ mahayaṃ śaraṇāgatavatsala ||30||
ahaṃ tvāṃ śaraṇaṃ yāmi kṛpāṃ kuru mamopari |
trailokye nedṛśī dṛṣṭā pativratyaguṇānvitā ||31||
muniruvāca |
plavate sāgare merurudiyātpaścime raviḥ |
na me vacoʼnyathā bhūyādvahniḥ śītatvamāpnuyāt ||32||
athāpi prabravīmyadya duṣṭa tacchṛṇu sādaram |
parāśaragṛhe devī dvāpareʼvatariṣyati ||33||
gajānana iti khyātastasya vāhatvameṣyasi |
tadā brahmādayo devā mānayiṣyanti sādaram ||34||
tasya hastagataḥ svargaṃ punareṣyasi satvaram |
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ duḥkhaharṣasamanvitaḥ ||35||
ka uvāca |
papāta pṛthivīpṛṣṭhe mahāmūṣakarūpadhṛk |
parāśarasyāśrame tu dvāpare samupasthite ||36||
mahābalo mahāvīryo mahāparvatasannibhaḥ |
munerāśīrbalenāsau gajānanasamīpagaḥ ||37||
abhavadvāharūpeṇa mūṣakoʼsau mahābalaḥ |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yanme pṛṣṭaṃ tvayāʼnagha ||38||
gajānanasya vāhatvaṃ mūṣakasya yathāʼbhavat |
muniruvāca |
sindūrasya vadhaṃ brahmankathaṃ sa kṛtavānvibhuḥ ||39||
gaṇeśastanmamācakṣva vistārāccaturānana |
śrutvā vāco na me tṛptirjāyateʼmṛtapānavat ||40||
bhaktyā śṛṇomi deveśa sarvajñastvaṃ vadasva me ||41||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe krauñcaśāpavarṇanaṃ nāma paṃcatriśottaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||135||

<adhyāya 136 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
ekasmindivase devo jagāda munisattamam |
parāśaraṃ mahābhāgyaṃ paraduḥkhaprapīḍitaḥ ||1||
gajānana uvāca |
sindūreṇa tu duṣṭena pīḍitaṃ sakalaṃ jagat |
svāhāsvadhāvaṣaṭkārairvarjitaṃ vedaghoṣataḥ ||2||
sthānabhraṣṭā devagaṇā bhraṣṭā ṛṣigaṇāstathā |
gajānanasvarūpeṇa kariṣye duṣṭanāśanam ||3||
sādhūnāṃ pālanaṃ caiva bhūbhāraharaṇaṃ tathā |
sthāpanaṃ sarvadevānāmānandaṃ jagatoʼpi ca ||4||
nidhehi mastake tāta pāṇiṃ meʼbhayadaṃ śubham |
anugrahaprasādātte haniṣye duṣṭadānavān ||5||
muniruvāca |
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ bāla bālabhāvātprabhāṣase |
bālakaḥ kautukāviṣṭo yācate candramaṇḍalam ||6||
asādhyaṃ tridaśaiḥ sarvaiḥ karma kartuṃ tathecchasi |
yasya śvāsena girayaḥ patanti śataśo bhuvi ||7||
pādāghātena yasyāśu kampate bhuvanatrayam |
kathaṃ tvaṃ yudhyase tena bisatantuśarīravān ||8||
caritvā navavarṣāṇi pūrṇāni tava sāṃpratam |
mamānugrahamātreṇa tvaṃ cecchakto bhaviṣyasi ||9||
tadāʼyaṃ nihito hasto mastakeʼbhayadāyakaḥ |
ka uvāca |
tato harṣayuto devo namaskṛtya muniṃ tadā ||10||
jananīmapi durgāṃ ca śrīhariṃ harameva ca |
yayau yoddhuṃ mūṣakaṃ tamāruhya sa gajānanaḥ ||11||
aṅkuśaṃ paraśuṃ pāśaṃ kamalaṃ ca caturbhujaiḥ |
dadhāno garjanenāśu kampayanbhuvanatrayam ||12||
prajvalaṃstejasā svena pralayānalasannibhaḥ |
kṣaṇena prāpa sindūranagaraṃ sa gajānanaḥ ||13||
ghūsṛṇeśvarasānnidhyātsiddhasindūravāḍakam |
tatrasthaḥ praśaśāsāsau sindūro bhuvanatrayam ||14||
taduttare sthito devo jagarja garjayandiśaḥ |
cukṣubhuḥ sāgarāḥ sapta viśīrṇā girayoʼpi ca ||15||
śrutvā ninādaṃ tasyāśu sarvadaityāścakampire |
mumūrcchurbhīravaḥ sarve kecinmṛtyumupāyayuḥ ||16||
sindūroʼpi mumūrcchāśu kṣaṇena prakṛtiṃ yayau |
uvāca sevakānsarvānkoʼyaṃ garjati dṛśyatām ||17||
mūrchā prāptā mayā kasmādyacchabdena puraḥ katham |
sthāsyāmi tatparaḥ sthātuṃ kasya śaktirbhaviṣyati ||18||
tataste sevakā jagmustadā bālasamīpataḥ |
tadrūpaṃ te nirīkṣyaiva siṃhasya kariṇo yathā ||19||
bibhuścaiva cakampuśca keciddhairyāttamabruvan |
kasya tvaṃ kuta āyātaḥ kimarthaṃ tava nāma kim ||20||
sindūro viśvasaṃhārarasikaḥ krodhasaṃyutaḥ |
śrutvā te kranditaṃ sarvaṃ kampitaṃ ca sasinduram ||21||
bāloʼpi balavānbhāsi catuḥpaṃcādvikoʼpi ca |
dṛśyase sarvasaṃhārakāraṇāya kṣamaḥ kṣaṇāt ||22||
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ provāca sa ca tānvibhuḥ |
ahaṃ śivasutaḥ khyātaḥ parāśaragṛhe vasan ||23||
dṛṣṭasaṃhārarasiko bhaktapālanatatparaḥ |
nānāvatārakuśalo brahmādīnāmagocaraḥ ||24||
gajānaneti me nāma yuddhaṃ kartuṃ samāgataḥ |
svāminaṃ voʼtha gacchantu tasmai sarvaṃ bruvantu ca ||25||
tasminhate mānasaṃ me toṣameṣyati durjaye |
ka uvāca |
tataste niryayuḥ śīghraṃ sindūraṃ prābruvanvacaḥ ||26||
dūtā ūcuḥ |
vayaṃ śīghrataraṃ yātāḥ puruṣa bhavadājñayā |
kṛtāntasadṛśaṃ taṃ tu dṛṣṭvā sarve ca kampitā ||27||
tatoʼbhavatpratāpena tadagre prābruvāma ca |
tatastena samākhyātaṃ nijaṃ sarvaṃ samāsataḥ ||28||
gajānananeti nāmāsya śivaputratvameva ca |
duṣṭadaityavināśāya sādhūnāṃ pālanāya ca ||29||
sāṃpratam tu tvayā sārddhaṃ yuddhāya samupāgataḥ |
sa caturvārṣiko bālo valgate bahuvistaram ||30||
idānīṃ svasvabhāvena jahi śatruṃ nijaṃ laghuṃ |
tava tu śvāsamātreṇa dūre gacchedayaṃ śiśuḥ ||31||
yasya te darśanātsvāmiṃścakaṃpaḥ śivakādayaḥ |
tasya te gaṇanā kāsti bālakasyālpamedhasaḥ ||32||
ka uvāca |
evaṃ dūtavacaḥ śrutvā siṃdūraścintayāturaḥ |
babhūva mlānatuṇḍaśca krodhāruṇavilocanaḥ ||33||
udvamannayanādvahniṃ pralayānalasannibham |
uvāca dūtānkrodhena harṣeṇa ca samanvitaḥ ||34||
sa uvāca |
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ dūtā bhavadvacasi meʼbhavata |
siṃhena saha yoddhuṃ vai maśakaḥ kathameṣyati ||35||
caturvarṣeṇa bālena yoddhavyaṃ kiṃ mayā saha |
yūyaṃ tataḥ kathaṃ bhītā na jāne tatra kāraṇam ||36||
mayi kruddhe jagannaśyettatra kā gaṇanāʼsya ha |
jagarja garjayanvyoma diśoʼpi vidiśoʼpi ca ||37||
dhṛtvā śastrāṇi cātrāṇi niryayau yuddhalālasaḥ |
tata ūcuramātyāstaṃ namaskṛtyāsurādhipam ||38||
satsu baleṣvamātyeṣu kathaṃ grāsi svayaṃ raṇam |
vayaṃ taṃ hi haniṣyāmaḥ pratāpāttava daityapa ||39||
samayārthaṃ mahatsainyaṃ pālyate soʼyamāgataḥ |
prāṇāṃstyakṣyāmahe sarve tava hetormahāprabho ||40||
anujānīhi yāsyāmo hantuṃ bālaṃ ripuṃ tava |
sindūra uvāca |
yāmi sarve prapaśyantu pauruṣaṃ mama sainikā ||41||
ityuktvā prayayo śīghraṃ bālaṃ hantuṃ sumutsukaḥ |
sa prāpa kṣaṇamātreṇa yatra bālaḥ sthitoʼbhavat ||42||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe bā… sindūranirgamo nāma ṣaṭtriṃśottaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||136||

<adhyāya 137 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
taṃ dṛṣṭavā bālakaṃ prāha sindūro madagarvitaḥ |
lajjā me jāyate manda yudhyato bālakena ha ||1||
yāhi śīghraṃ gṛhaṃ bāla piba mātuḥ stanaṃ mudā |
vṛthā maccharapātena sadyo mṛtyumupaiṣyasi ||2||
palāyante hi māṁ dṛṣṭvā keśādyāstri divaukasaḥ |
mṛte tvayi tava snehātpitarau te mariṣyataḥ ||3||
ato gaccha na me svāsyaṃ darśayasva kiśoraka |
yasya me talaghātena brahmāṇḍaṃ śatadhā bhavet ||4||
deva uvāca |
satyamuktaṃ tvayā duṣṭa na cetasi vicāritam |
na jñātaṃ mama sāmarthyaṃ rūpaṃ vā vividhaṃ tvayā ||5||
yasya me krodhadṛṣṭyaiva bhraṣṭāḥ syuḥ suranāyakāḥ |
svecchayāʼhaṃ sṛje bhāvaistribhireva harāmi ca ||6||
viśvaṃ carācaraṃ nānārūpavāṃstu yuge yuge |
bhūmi bhāraṃ mahāghoraṃ harāmi duṣṭanāśanāt ||7||
na laghurlaghutāmeti parākramayutoʼpi ceta |
aṇumātro dahedvahniḥ sakalaṃ nagaraṃ mahat ||8||
jijīviṣuścedgaccha tvaṃ natvā māṃ nijamandiram |
mānino naiva nighnanti nataṃ ca śaraṇāgatam ||9||
no cenmacchastrapātena svargalokaṃ gamiṣyasi |
hate tvayi sukhaṃ yāsye tvayā yatpīḍitaṃ jagat ||10||
brahmaṇo varadānena mā garvaṃ kuru daityarāṭ |
viparītaṃ bhavetsarvaṃ viparītaṃ yathā purā ||11||
stambheʼvatīrṇa nyahanaddhiraṇyakaśipuṃ vibhuḥ |
sugrīveṇa hato bandhū rāmamāśritya kālataḥ ||12||
viparītā matirjātā tavāpi kālayogataḥ |
atisthūlo laghutaro bhāsase mama sāṃpratam ||13||
dhṛtvā dhairyaṃ ca lajjāṃ ca yuddhaṃ kuru mayā saha |
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā dadhārāśu virāḍrūpaṃ gajānanaḥ ||14||
brahmāṇḍādūrdhvamagamanmastakaścaraṇau tathā |
pātālāni ca saptāni bhittvā yātau śrutī diśaḥ ||15||
paśyati sma yadā daityo bālaṃ taṃ viśvarūpiṇam |
sahasraśirasaṃ devaṃ sahasrākṣaṃ divo bhuvam ||16||
sahasrapādaṃ vyāpyāśu sthitaṃ vyāpya diśo daśa |
divyāmbaraṃ divyagandhaṃ divyālaṃkārabhūṣitam ||17||
asaṃkhyasūryasadṛśamasaṃkhyarūpiṇaṃ vibhum |
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā virāḍrūpaṃ cakampe daityamānasam ||18||
dhṛtvā dhairyaṃ punardaityo yayau devasya sannidhau |
jagarja garjayanvyoma diśoʼpi vidiśoʼpi ca ||19||
udyamya khaḍgaṃ sahasā devaṃ hantumiyeṣa saḥ |
yayau tatpurataḥ krodhātpataṃgo jvalanaṃ yathā ||20||
deva uvāca |
nāyaṃ mūḍho vijānīte svarūpaṃ mama durlabham |
ahameva vimokṣyāmītyuktvā kaṇṭhe dadhāra tam ||21||
mamarda sinduraṃ taṃ sa karābhyāṃ balavattaram |
tatastadasṛjāṃgāni vililimpāruṇena sa jaḥ ||22||
tataḥ sindūravadanasindūrapriya eva ca |
abhavajjagatīkhyāto bhaktakāmaprapūrakaḥ ||23||
sindūre nihate devā mudā puṣpāṇyavākiran |
avādyañjayavādyāni nanṛtuścāpsarogaṇāḥ ||24||
āyayustatra munayo vasiṣṭhapramukhāḥ surāḥ |
brahmādyāḥ sakalāḥ śakrapurogā vastupāṇayaḥ ||25||
jayaśabdairnamaḥ śabdaiḥ pūrayanto diśo daśa |
tataḥ sarve bhūmipālā yayustatra mudānvitāḥ ||26||
ṣoḍaśairupacāraistamānarcuḥ parameśvaram |
gajānanaṃ caturbāhuṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ||27||
divyāmbaraṃ divyagandhamākhuvāhanamīśvaram |
prārthayāmāsurindrādyāḥ stotuṃ tvāṃ na hi śaknumaḥ ||28||
yatra kuṇṭhāścaturvedā brahmādyāśca munīśvarāḥ |
tvaṃ kartā kāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ rakṣakaḥ poṣakoʼpi ca ||29||
saṃhartā mohanaścāsya viśvasya jñānadaḥ kvacit |
saritaḥ sāgarā vṛkṣāḥ parvatāḥ paśavoʼkhilāḥ ||30||
vāyurākāśa pṛthivī vahnirvāri tvameva ca |
brahmā viṣṇuḥ śivaḥ śakro maruto munayoʼpi ca ||31||
gandharvāścāraṇāḥ siddhā yakṣarākṣasapannagā |
apsaraḥkinnarā deva tvameva sa carācaram ||32||
vayaṃ dhanyāyato dṛṣṭaḥ pratyakṣaṃ mokṣasādhanaḥ |
sindūre tu hate deva sukhaṃ prāptāḥ surottamāḥ ||33||
rājāno munayo lokāḥ svasvakārye mudāratāḥ |
bhaviṣyanti svadhāsvāhāvaṣaṭkārāśritāḥ kriyāḥ ||34||
nānāvatāraiḥ kuruṣe pālanaṃ tvaṃ viśeṣataḥ |
duṣṭānāṃ nāśanaṃ sadyo bhaktānāṃ kāmapūrakaḥ ||35||
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā surāstaṃ tu vyadadhuḥ sarva eva te |
prāsādaṃ ramyaśikharaṃ sthāpayitvā gajānanam ||36||
yasya darśanamātreṇa mucyate pātakājjanaḥ |
kulāni tārayetsapta smaraṇādasya mānavaḥ ||37||
pūjayitvā praṇamyainaṃ paritṛptiṃ yayuḥ surāḥ |
tatastaṃ munayaḥ sarve pupūjuḥ paramādarāt ||38||
sindūraheti nāmāsya cakruḥ sindūranāśanāt||
atisaṃtuṣṭamanaso natvā jagmuḥ svamāśramam ||39||
tataḥ sarve munivarā natvā devaṃ sabhaktitaḥ |
pupūjuḥ paramātmānaṃ nānādravyairanekaśaḥ ||40||
rājasadanamiti te kṣetraṃ khyātimupaiṣyati |
vareṇyastaṃ vilokyaiva nijaputramabudhyata ||41||
yato rājñāṃ padānyāśu hatvā daityaṃ sudāruṇam |
dattāni bhavatā nātha sindūraṃ lokakaṇṭakam ||42||
daityavimardana iti nāma khyātimupaiṣyati |
pupūja tanayaṃ snehāttadā dṛṣṭaparākramam ||43||
mumoca snehajaṃ cāśru na śaśāka prabhāṣitum |
atigadgadakaṇṭhatvādruroda bhṛśaduḥkhitaḥ ||44||
vareṇya uvāca |
mūḍhabhāvena saṃtyakto vighnabhītena pāpinā |
yaṃ tvāṃ pūjayituṃ yātā brahma śakrapurogamāḥ ||45||
anantakoṭibrahmāṇḍanāyakaṃ tvāṃ na vidmahe |
kāmadhenuṃ nidhiṃkalpavṛkṣaṃ mūḍho yathā tyajet ||46||
māyayā mohitasteʼhamatyajaṃ tvāṃ tathā gṛhāt |
ka uvāca |
iti tacchucamākarṇya kṛpayā parameśvaraḥ ||47||
āliliṃga vareṇyaṃ taṃ caturbāhubhirādarāt |
uvāca parayā bhaktyā vareṇyanṛpatiṃ vibhuḥ ||48||
deva uvāca |
purākalpe mahāraṇye tapastaptaṃ sudāruṇam |
ubhābhyāṃ pakvaparṇāni bhakṣayitvā vaṭasya ha ||49||
divyavarṣasahasreṇa prasannoʼhaṃ tadāʼbhavam |
bhavadbhyāṃ hi vṛtaḥ putro na mokṣo mūḍhabhāvataḥ ||50||
soʼhaṃ putratvamāpanno bhūbhāraharaṇāya ca |
sindūrasya vadhaṃ kartuṃ sādhūnāṃ pālanaṃ tathā ||51||
anyathā na śarīraṃ me nirākārasya sākṣiṇaḥ |
vacanaṃ tu kṛtaṃ satyaṃ trailokyasyāpi toṣaṇam ||52||
yāsyeʼdhunā nijaṃ dhāma na tvaṃ śoke manaḥ kṛthāḥ |
vareṇya uvāca |
saṃsāre bahuduḥkhāni dṛṣṭāni duḥsahāni ca ||53||
idānīṃ mokṣamārgaṃ me kṛpayā diśa duḥkhahan |
sākṣātkāre kathaṃ bandhastava syāddviradānana ||54||
yenopadeśena muktiṃ yāsyāmi tadvadasva me |
yogaṃ taṃ yena saṃtyakṣe kāmaṃ krodhaṃ mṛterbhayam ||55||
ka uvāca |
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ kṛpayā sa gajānanaḥ |
upaveśya svāsane taṃ hastaṃ śirasi cādadhat ||56||
gaṇeśagītāṃ tasmai sa upadeṣṭuṃ pracakrame |
nirasya sarvasaṃdehānviśvarūpaṃ pradarśya ca ||57||
gītāsāraṃ sa yuddhvaivaḥ gaṇeśasyopadeśataḥ |
sthāpya rājyamamātyeṣu tapase vanamāyayau ||58||
ativairāgyasaṃyukto dadhyau taṃ ca gajānanam |
āvartayatsadā gītāmananyaviṣayakṛtī ||59||
yathā jalaṃ jale kṣiptaṃ jalameva hi jāyate |
tathā taddhyānataḥ soʼpi tanmayatvamupāyayau ||60||
muniruvāca |
caturānana deveśa kṛpayā parayā vada |
gītāṃ tāṃ tu gaṇeśasya sarvājñānavināśanīm ||61||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe vareṇyopadeśo nāma saptatriṃśadadhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||137||

<adhyāya 138 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
evameva purā pṛṣṭaḥ śaunakena mahātmanā |
sa sūtaḥ kathayāmāsa gītāṃ vyāsamukhācchrutām ||1||
sūta uvāca |
aṣṭādaśapurāṇoktamamṛtaṃ prāśitaṃ mayā |
tatoʼtirasavatpātumicchāmyamṛtamuttamam ||2||
yenāmṛtamayo bhūtvā pumānbrahmā mṛtaṃ yataḥ |
yogāmṛtaṃ mahābhāga tanme karuṇayā vada ||3||
vyāsa uvāca |
atha gītāṃ pravakṣyāmi yogamārgaprakāśinīm |
niyuktā pṛcchate sūta rājñe gajamukhena yā ||4||
vareṇya uvāca |
vighneśvara mahābāho sarvavidyāviśārada |
sarvaśāstrārthatatvajña yogaṃ me vaktumarhasi ||5||
gajānana uvāca |
samyagvyavasitā rājanmatisteʼnugrahānmama |
śṛṇu gītāṃ pravakṣyāmi yogāmṛtamayīṃ nṛpa ||6||
na yogaṃ yogamityāhuryogo yogo na ca śriyaḥ |
na yogo viṣayairyogo na ca mātrādibhistathā ||7||
yogo yaḥ pitṛmātrāderna sa yogo narādhipa |
yo yogo bandhuputrāderyaścāṣṭa bhūtibhiḥ saha ||8||
na sa yogaḥ striyā yogo jagadudbhūtarūpayā |
rājyayogaśca no yogo na yogo gajavājibhiḥ ||9||
yogo nendrapadasyāpi yogo yogārthinaḥ priyaḥ |
yogo yaḥ satyalokasya na sa yogo mato mama ||10||
śaivasya yogo no yogo vaiṣṇavasya padasya ca |
na yogo bhūpa sūryatvaṃ candratvaṃ na kuberatā ||11||
nānilatvaṃ nānalatvaṃ nāmaratvaṃ na kālatā  |
vāruṇyaṃ na nairऋtyaṃ yogo na sārvabhīmatā ||12||
yogaṃ nānāvidhaṃ bhūpa yujjanti jñāninastu tam |
bhavanti vitṛṣā loke jitāhārā viretasaḥ ||13|| 
pānayantyakhilāllokānvaśīkṛtajagattrayāḥ |
karuṇāpūrṇahṛdayā bodhayanti ca kāṃścana ||14||
jīvanmuktā hṛde magnāḥ paramānandarūpiṇi |
nimīlyākṣīṇi paśyantaḥ paraṃ brahma hṛdi sthitam ||15||
dhyāyantaḥ paramaṃ brahma citte yogavaśīkṛtam |
bhūtāni svātmanā tulyaṃ sarvāṇi gaṇayanti te ||16||
yena kenacidācchinnā yena kena cidāhatāḥ |
yena kena cidākṛṣṭā yena kenācidāśitāḥ ||17||
karuṇāpūrṇahṛdayā bhramanti dharaṇītale |
anugrahāya bhūtānāṃ jitakrodhā jitendriyāḥ ||18||
dehamātrabhṛto bhūpa samaloṣṭhāśmakāṃcanā |
etādṛśā mahābhāgāḥ syuścakṣurgocarāḥ priya ||19||
tamidānīmahaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu yogamanuttamam |
śrutvā yaṃ mucyate jantuḥ pāpebhyo bhavasāgarāt ||20||
śive viṣṇau ca śaktau ca sūrye mayi narādhipa |
yāʼbhedabuddhiryogaḥ sa samyagyogo mato mama ||21||
ahameva jagadyasmātsṛjāmi pālayāmi ca |
kṛtvā nānāvidhaṃ veṣaṃ saṃharāmi svalīlayā ||22||
ahameva mahāviṣṇurahameva sadāśivaḥ |
ahameva mahāśaktirahamevāryamā priya ||23||
ahameko nṛṇāṃ nātho jātaḥ paṃcavidhaḥ purā |
ajñānānmāṃ na jānanti jagatkāraṇakāraṇam ||24||
mattoʼgnirāpo dharaṇī matta ākāśamārutau |
brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca lokapālā diśo daśa ||25||
vasavo manavo gāvo munayaḥ paśavoʼpi ca |
saritaḥ sāgarā yakṣā vṛkṣāḥ pakṣigaṇā api ||26||
tathaikaviṃśatisvargā nāgāḥ saptavanāni ca |
manuṣyāḥ parvatāḥ sādhyāḥ siddhā rakṣogaṇāstathā ||27||
ahaṃ sākṣī jagaccakṣuraliptaḥ sarvakarmabhiḥ |
avikāroʼprameyoʼhamavyakto viśvagoʼvyayaḥ ||28||
ahameva paraṃ brahmāvyayānandātmakaṃ nṛpa |
mohayatyakhilānmāyā śreṣṭhānmana narānamūn ||29||
sarvadā ṣaḍravikāreṣu tāni yaṃ yojayedbhṛśam |
hitvājāpaṭalaṃ janturanekairjanmabhiḥ śanaiḥ ||30||
virajya vindati brahma viṣayeṣu subodhataḥ |
acchedyaṃ śastrasaṅaghātairadāhyamanalena ca ||31||
akledyaṃ pa ca vanairaśoṣyaṃ mārutena ca |
avadhyaṃ vadhyamāneʼpi śarīreʼsminnarādhipa ||32||
yāmimāṃ puṣpitāṃ vācaṃ praśaṃsanti śrutīritām |
trayīvādaratā mūḍhāstatoʼnyanmanvatoʼpi na ||33||
kurvanti satataṃ karma janmamṛtyuphalapradam |
svargaiśvaryaratā dhvastacetanā bhogabuddhayaḥ ||34||
saṃpādayanti te bhūyaḥ svātmanā nijabandhanam |
saṃsāracakraṃ yuṃjanti jaḍāḥ karmaparā narāḥ ||35||
yasya yadvihitaṃ karma tatkartavyaṃ madarpaṇam |
tatoʼsya karmabījānāmucchinnāḥ syurmahāṃkurāḥ ||36||
cittaśuddhiśca mahatī vijñānasādhakā bhavet |
vijñāne na hi vijñātaṃ paraṃ brahma munīśvaraiḥ ||37||
tasmātkarmāṇi kurvīta buddhiyukto narādhipa |
na tvakarmā bhavetkoʼpi svadharmatyāgavāṃstathā ||38||
jahāti yadi karmāṇi tataḥ siddhiṃ na vindati |
ādau jñāne nādhikāraḥ karmaṇyeva sa yujyate ||39||
karmaṇā śuddhahṛdayoʼbhedabuddhimupaiṣyati |
sa ca yogaḥ samākhyātoʼmṛtatvāya hi kalpate ||40||
yogamanyaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu bhūpa tamuttamam |
paśau putre tathā mitre śatrau bandhau suhṛjjane ||41||
bahirdṛṣṭyā ca samayā hṛtsthayā lokayetpumān |
sukhe duḥkhe tathāmarṣe harṣe bhītau samo bhavet ||42||
rogāptau caiva bhogāptau jaye vā vijayeʼpi ca |
śriyoʼyoge ca yoge ca lābhālābhe mṛtāvapi ||43||
samo māṃ vastujāteṣu paśyannantarbahisthitam |
sūrye some jale vahnau śive śaktau tathāʼnile ||44||
dvije hṛde mahānadyāṃ tīrthe kṣetreʼghanāśini |
viṣṇau ca sarvadeveṣu tathā yakṣorageṣu ca ||45||
gandharveṣu manuṣyeṣu tathā tiryagbhaveṣu ca |
satataṃ māṃ hi yaḥ paśyetsoʼyaṃ yogaviducyate ||46||
saṃparīhṛtya svārthebhya indriyāṇi vivekataḥ |
sarvatra samatābuddhiḥ sa yogo bhūpa me mataḥ ||47||
ātmanātmavivekena yā buddhirdaivayogataḥ |
svadharmāsaktacittasya tadyogo yoga ucyate ||48||
dharmādharmau jahātīha tathā tyukta ubhāvapi |
ato yogāya yuñjīta yogo vaidheṣu kauśalam ||49||
dharmādharmaphale tyaktvā manīṣī vijitendriyaḥ |
janmabandhavinirmuktaḥ sthānaṃ saṃyātyanāmayam ||50||
yadā hyajñānakāluṣyaṃ jantorbuddhiḥ kramiṣyati |
tadāʼsau yāti vairāgyaṃ vedavākyādiṣu kramāta ||51||
trayīvipratipannasya sthāṇutvaṃ yāsyate yadā |
parātmanyacalā buddhistadā sa yogamāpnuyāt ||52||
mānasānakhilānkāmānyadā dhīmāṃstyajetpriya |
svātmani svena saṃtuṣṭaḥ sthirabuddhistadocyate ||53||
vitṛṣṇaḥ sarvasaukhyeṣu nodvigno duḥkhasaṃgame |
gatasādhvasaruḍrāgaḥ sthirabuddhistadocyate ||54||
yathāʼyaṃ kamaṭhoʼṅagāni saṃkocayati sarvataḥ |
viṣayebhyastathā khāni saṃkarṣedyogatatparaḥ ||55||
vyāvartanteʼsya viṣayāstyaktāhārasya varṣmiṇaḥ |
vinā rāgaṃ ca rāgoʼpi dṛṣṭvā brahma vinaśyati ||56||
vipaścidyatate bhūpa sthitimāsthāya yoginaḥ |
manthayitvendriyāṇyasya haranti balato manaḥ ||57||
yuktastāni vaśe kṛtvā sarvadā matparo bhaveta |
saṃyatānīndriyāṇīha yasya sa kṛtadhīmataḥ ||58||
cintayānasya viṣayānsaṃgasteṣūpajāyate |
kāmaḥ saṃvardhate tasmāttataḥ krodhoʼbhivarddhate ||59||
krodhādajñānasaṃbhūtirvibhramastu tataḥ smṛteḥ |
bhraṃśātsmṛtermaterdhvaṃsastaddhvaṃsātsoʼpi naśyati ||60||
vinā dveṣaṃ ca rāgaṃ ca gocarānyasta khaiścaret |
svādhīnahṛdayo vaśyaiḥ saṃtoṣaṃ sa samṛcchati ||61||
trividhasyāpi duḥkhasya saṃtoṣe kṣepaṇaṃ bhavet |
prajñayā saṃsthitaścāyaṃ prasannahṛdayo bhavet ||62||
vinā prasādaṃ na matirvinā matyā na bhāvanā |
vinā tāṃ na śamo bhūpa vinā tena kutaḥ sukham ||63||
indriyāśvānvicarato viṣayānanuvartate |
yanmanastanmatiṃ hanyādaśva nāvaṃ marudyathā ||64||
yā rātriḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ tasyāṃ nidrāti naiva saḥ |
na svapantīha te yatra sā rātristasya bhūmipa ||65||
saritāṃ patimāyānti vanāni sarvato yathā |
āyānti taṃ tathā kāmāḥ sa nā śāntiṃ sadā labhet ||66||
atastānīha saṃruddhya sarvataḥ khāni mānavaḥ |
svasvārthebhyaḥ pradhāvanti buddhirasya sthirā tadā ||67||
mamatāʼhaṃkṛtī tyaktvā sarvānkāmāṃśca yastyajet |
nityaṃ jñānarato bhūyājjñānānmuktiṃ prayāsyati ||68||
enāṃ brahmadhiyaṃ bhūpa yo vijānāti daivataḥ |
turīyāvasthāṃ prāpyāpi śīlānmuktiṃ sa ṛcchati ||69|| 
oṁtatsaditi śrīmad gaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārthaṃ śāstre śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṃḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde 'sāṃkhyayogasārārthaṃ' yogonāma prathamodhyāya ||1|| ||138||

<adhyāya 139 prāraṃbhaḥ>
vareṇya uvāca |
jñānaniṣṭhā karmaniṣṭhā dvayaṃ proktaṃ tvayā vibho |
avadhārya vadaikaṃ me niśreyasakaraṃ nu kim ||1||
gajānana uvāca |
asmiṃścarācare sthityau purokte dve mayā priya |
sāṃkhyānāṃ buddhiyogena vaidhayogena karmaṇām ||2||
anārabhbheṇa vaidhānāṃ niṣkriyaḥ puruṣo bhavet |
na siddhiṃ yāti saṃtyāgātkevalātkarmaṇān nṛpa ||3||
kadācidakriyaḥ koʼpi kṣaṇaṃ naivāvatiṣṭhate |
asvatantraḥ prakṛtijairguṇaiḥ karma ca kāryate ||4||
karmakārīndriyagrāmaṃ niyamyāste smaranpumān |
tadgocarānmandacitto dhigācāraḥ sa bhaṇyate ||5||
tadgrāmaṃ saṃniyamyādau manasā karma cārabhet |
indriyaiḥ karmayogaṃ yo vitṛṣṇaḥ sa paro nṛpa ||6||
akarmaṇaḥ śreṣṭhatamaṃ karmānīcchākṛtaṃ tu yat |
varṣmaṇaḥ sthitirapyasyākarmaṇo naiva setsyati ||7||
asamarpya nibadhyante karma tena janā mayi |
kurvīta satataṃ karmāṃnāśoʼsaṃgo madarpaṇam ||8||
madarthaṃ yāni karmāṇi tāni badhnanti na kvacit |
savāsanamidaṃ karma badhnāti dehinaṃ balāt ||9||
varṇānsṛṣṭvāʼvadaṃ cāhaṃ sayajñāṃstānpurā priya |
yajñena ṛdhyatāmeṣa kāmadaḥ kalpavṛkṣavat ||10||
surāṃścānena prīṇadhvaṃ surāste prīṇayantu naḥ |
labhadhvaṃ paramaṃ sthānanyonyaprīṇanātsthiram ||11||
iṣṭā devāḥ pradāsyanti bhogāniṣṭānsutarpitāḥ |
tairdattāṃstānnara
stebhyoʼdattvā bhuṃkte sa taskaraḥ ||12||
hutāvaśiṣṭa bhoktāro muktāḥ syuḥ sarvapātakaiḥ |
adantyeno mahāpāpā ātmahetoḥ pacanti ye ||13||
ūrjā bhavanti bhūtāni devādannasya saṃbhavaḥ |
yajñāṃcca devasaṃbhūtistadutpattiśca vaidhataḥ ||14||
brahmaṇo vaidhamutpannaṃ matto brahmasamudbhavaḥ |
ato yajñe ca viśvasminsthitaṃ māṃ viddhi bhūmipa ||15||
saṃsṛtīnāṃ mahācakraṃ krāmitavyaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
sa mudā prāṇiti bhūpendriyakrīḍoʼdhamo janaḥ ||16||
antarātmani yaḥ prīta ātmārāmoʼkhilapriyaḥ |
ātmatṛpto naro yaḥ syāttasyārtho naiva vidyate ||17||
kāryākāryakṛtīnāṃ sa naivāpnoti śubhāśubhe |
kiṃcidasya na sādhyaṃ syātsarvajantuṣu sarvadā ||18||
atoʼsaktanayā bhūpa kartavyaṃ karma jantubhiḥ |
saktoʼgatimavāpnoti māmavāpnoti tādṛśaḥ ||19||
paramāṃ siddhimāpannāḥ purā rājarṣayo dvijāḥ |
saṃgrahāya hi lokānāṃ tādṛśaṃ karma cārabhet ||20||
śreyānyatkurute karma tatkarotyakhilo janaḥ |
manute yatpramāṇaṃ sa tadevānusaratyasau ||21||
viṣṭape me na sādhyoʼsti kaścidartho narādhipa |
anālabdhaśca labdhavyaḥ kurve karma tathāpyaham ||22||
na kurveʼhaṃ yadā karma svatantroʼlasabhāvitaḥ |
kariṣyanti mama dhyānaṃ sarve varṇā mahāmate ||23||
bhaviṣyanti tato lokā ucchinnāḥ saṃpradāyinaḥ |
hantā syāmasya lokasya vidhātā saṃkarasya ca ||24||
kāmino hi sadā kāmairajñānātkarmakāriṇaḥ |
lokānāṃ saṃgrahāyaitadviddhānkuryādasaktadhīḥ ||25||
vibhinnatvamatiṃ jahyādajñānāṃ karmacāriṇām |
yogayuktaḥ sarvakarmāṇyarpayenmayi karmakṛta ||26||
 avidyāguṇasācivyātkurvakarmāṇyatandritaḥ |
ahaṃkārānmandabuddhirahaṃ karteti yoʼbravīt ||27||
yastu vettyātmanastattvaṃ vibhāgād guṇakarmaṇoḥ |
karaṇaṃ viṣaye vṛttamiti matvā na sajjate ||28||
kuvanti saphalaṃ karma guṇaistribhirvimohitāḥ |
aviśvajñāsvātmadruho viśvavinnaiva laṅaghayeta ||29||
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ tasmānmayi karmārpayedbudhaḥ |
tyaktvāʼhaṃmamatābuddhiṃ parāṃ gatimavāpnuyāt ||30||
anīrṣyanto ye mayoktamidaṃ śubham |
anutiṣṭhanti ye sarve muktāsteʼkhilakarmabhiḥ ||31||
ye caiva nānutiṣṭhanti aśubhā hatacetasaḥ |
īrṣyamāṇānmahāmūḍhānnaṣṭāṃstānviddhi me ripūn ||32||
tulyaṃ prakṛtyā kurute karma yajjñānavānapi |
anuyāti ca tāmeva grahastatra mudhā mataḥ ||33||
kāmaścaiva tathā krodhaḥ khānāmartheṣu jāyate |
naitayorvaśyatāṃ yāyādasya vidhvaṃsakau yataḥ ||34||
śastoʼguṇo nijo dharmaḥ sāṃgadānyasya dharmataḥ |
nije tasminmṛtiḥ śreyaḥ paratra bhayadaḥ paraḥ ||35||
vareṇya uvāca |
pumānyatkurute pāpaṃ sa hi kena niyujyate |
akāṃkṣannapi heramba preritaḥ prabalādiva ||36||
śrīgajānana uvāca |
kāmakrodhau mahāpāpau guṇadvayasamudbhavau |
nayantau vaśyatāṃ lokānviddhyetau dveṣiṇau varau ||37||
āvṛṇoti yathā māyā jagadvāṣpo jalaṃ yathā |
varṣāmegho yathā bhānuṃ tadvatkāmoʼkhilāṃśca ruṭ ||38||
pratipattimato jñāna chāditaṃ satataṃ dviṣā |
icchātmakena tarasā duṣpūreṇa ca śuṣmiṇā ||39||
āśritya buddhimanasī indriyāṇi sa tiṣṭhati |
tairevācchāditaprajño jñāninaṃ mohayatyasau ||40||
tasmānniyamya tānyādau samanāṃsi naro jayet |
jñānavijñānayoḥ śāntikaraṃ pāpaṃ manobhavam ||41||
aṃtastāni parāṇyāhustebhyaśca paramaṃ manaḥ |
tatoʼpi hi parā buddhirātmā buddheḥ paro mataḥ ||42||
buddhvaivamātmanātmānaṃ saṃstabhyātmānamātmanā |
hatvā śatruṃ kāmarūpaṃ paraṃ padamavāpnuyāt ||43||
oṁtatsaditi śrīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārtha śāstre śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṇḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde 'karmayogo' nāma dvitīyodhyāyaḥ ||2|| ||139||

<adhyāya 140 prāraṃbhaḥ>

śrīgajānana uvāca |
purā sargādisamaye traiguṇyaṃ tritanūraham |
nirmāya cainamavadaṃ viṣṇave yogamuttamam ||1||
aryamṇe soʼbravītsoʼpi manave nijasūnave |
tataḥ paramparāyātaṃ vidurenaṃ maharṣayaḥ ||2||
kālena bahunā cāyaṃ naṣṭaḥ syāccarame yuge |
aśraddheyo hyaviśvāsyo vigītavyaśca bhūmipa ||3||
enaṃ purābhavaṃ yogaṃ śrutavānasi manmukhāt |
guhayādguhayatamaṃ vedaharasyaṃ paramaṃ śubham ||4||
vareṇya uvāca |
sāṃprataṃ cāvatīrṇoʼsi garbhatastvaṃ gajānana |
proktavānkathamenaṃ tvaṃ viṣṇave yogamuttamama ||5||
śrīgajānana uvāca |
anekāni ca te janmānyatītāni mamāpi ca |
saṃsmare tāni sarvāṇi na smṛtistava vartate ||6||
matta eva mahābāho jātā viṣṇvādayaḥ surāḥ |
mayyeva ca layaṃ yānti pralayeṣu yuge yuge ||7||
ahameva paro brahmā mahārudroʼhameva ca |
ahameva jagatsarvaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ ca yat ||8||
ajoʼvyayoʼhaṃ bhūtātmāʼnādirīśvara eva ca |
āsthāya triguṇāṃ māyāṃ bhavāmi bahuyoniṣu ||9||
adharmopacayo dharmāpacayo hi yadā bhavet |
sādhūnsaṃrakṣituṃ duṣṭāṃstadāttuṃ saṃbhavāmyaham ||10||
ucchidyādharmānicayaṃ dharmasaṃsthāpayāmyaham |
hanmi duṣṭāṃśca daityāṃśca nānālīlākaro mudā ||11||
varṇāśramānmunīnsādhūnpālaye bahurūpadhṛk |
evaṃ yo vetti saṃbhūtīmmama divyāṃ yuge yuge ||12||
tattatkarma ca vīryaṃ ca mama rūpaṃ samāsataḥ |
tyaktvāhaṃ mamatābuddhiṃ na punarbhūḥ sa jāyate ||13||
nirīhā nirbhayāroṣā matparā madyapāśrayāḥ |
vijñāna tapasā śuddhā aneke māmupāgatāḥ ||14||
yena yena hi bhāvena saṃsevante narottamāḥ |
tathā tathā phalaṃ tebhyaḥ prayacchāmyavyayaḥ sphuṭam ||15||
janāḥ syuritare rājanmama mārgānuyāyinaḥ |
tathaiva vyavahāraṃ te sveṣu cānyeṣu kurvate ||16||
kurvanti devatāprītiṃ vāñchantaḥ karmaṇāṃ phalam |
prāpnuvantīha te loke śīghraṃ siddhiṃ hi karmajām ||17||
catvāro hi mayā varṇā rajaḥsattvatamoṃśataḥ |
karmāṃśataśca saṃsṛṣṭā mṛtyuloke mayā nṛpa ||18||
kartāramapi māṃ teṣāmakartāraṃ vidurbudhāḥ |
anādimīśvaraṃ nityamaliptaṃ karmajairguṇaiḥ ||19||
nirīhaṃ yoʼbhijānāti karma badhnāti naiva tam |
cakruḥ karmāṇi buddhvaivaṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ mumukṣavaḥ ||20||
vāsanāsahitādādyātsaṃsārakāraṇāddṛḍhāt |
ajñānabandhanājjanturbuddhvāyaṃ mucyateʼkhilāt ||21||
tadakarmaṃ ca karmāpi kathayāmyadhunā tava |
yatradā maunaṃ gatā mohādṛṣayo buddhiśālinaḥ ||22||
tattvaṃ mumukṣuṇā jñeyaṃ karmākarmavikarmaṇām |
trividhānīha karmāṇi suninnaiṣāṃ gatipriya ||23||
kriyāyāmakriyājñānamakriyāyāṃ kriyāmatiḥ |
yasya syātsa hi martyeʼsmiṃlloke muktoʼkhilārthadṛk ||24||
karmāṃkuraviyogena yaḥ karmāṇyārabhennaraḥ |
tattvadarśananirdagdhakriyamāhurbudhā budham ||25||
phalatṛṣṇāvihāyasyātsadā tṛpto visādhanaḥ |
udyuktoʼpi kriyāṃ kartuṃ kiṃcinnaiva karoti saḥ ||26||
nirīho nigṛhītātmā parityaktaparigrahaḥ |
kevalaṃ vai gṛhaṃ karmācarannāyāti pātakam ||27||
advandvoʼmatsaro bhūtvā siddhausiddhyauḥ samaśca yaḥ |
yathāprāptyīha saṃtuṣṭaḥ kurvankarma na badhyate ||28||
akhilairviṣayairmukto jñānavijñānavānapi |
yajñārthaṃ
tasya sakalaṃ kṛtaṃ karma vilīyate ||29||
ahamagnirhavirhotā hutaṃ yanmayi cārpitam |
brahmāptavyaṃ ca tenātha brahmaṇyeva yato rataḥ ||30||
yoginaḥ kecidapare diṣṭaṃ yajñaṃ vadanti ca |
brahmāgnireva yajño vai iti kecana menire ||31||
saṃyamāgnau paraṃ bhūpa indriyāṇyupajuhvati |
khāgniṣvanye tadviṣayāñśabdādīnupajuhvati ||32||
prāṇānāmindriyāṇāṃ ca pare karmāṇi kṛtatsnaśaḥ |
nijātmarasirūpeʼgnau jñānadīpte prajuhvati ||33||
dravyeṇa tapasā vāpi svādhyāye nāpi kecana |
tīvravratena yatino jñānenāpi yajanti mām ||34||
prāṇeʼpānaṃ tathā prāṇamapāne prakṣipanti ye |
ruddhvā gatīṃścobhayoste prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ ||35||
jitvā prāṇānprāṇagatīrupajuhvati teṣu ca |
evaṃ nānā yajñaratā yajñadhvaṃsitapātakāḥ ||36||
nityaṃ brahma prayāntyete yajñāśeṣāmṛtāśinaḥ |
ayajñakāriṇo loko nāyamanyaḥ kuto bhavet ||37||
kāyikāditridhābhūtānyajñānvede pratiṣṭhitān |
jñātvā tānakhilānbhūpa mokṣaseʼkhilabandhanāt ||38||
sarveṣāṃ bhūpa yajñānāṃ jñānayajñaḥ paro mataḥ |
akhilaṃ līyate karma jñāne mokṣasya sādhane ||39||
tajjñeyaṃ puruṣavyāghrapraśnena natitaḥ satām |
śuśrūṣayā vadiṣyanti santastattvaviśāradāḥ ||40||
nānāsaṃgānjanaḥ kurvannaikaṃ sādhusamāgamam |
karoti tena saṃsāre bandhanaṃ samupaiti saḥ ||41||
satsaṃgādguṇasaṃbhūtirāpadāṃ laya eva ca |
svahitaṃ prāpyate sarvairiha loke paratra ca ||42||
itaratsulabhaṃ rājansatsaṃgoʼtīvadurlabhaḥ |
yajjñātvā na punarbandhameti jñeyaṃ tato yataḥ ||43||
tataḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni svātmanyevābhipaśyati |
atipāparato jantustatastasmātpramucyate ||44||
vividhānyapi karmāṇi jñānāgnirdahati kṣaṇāt |
prasiddhoʼgniryathā sarvaṃ bhasmatāṃ nayati kṣaṇāt ||45||
na jñānaṃ samatāmeti pavitramitarannṛpa |
ātmanyevāvagacchanti yogātkālena yoginaḥ ||46||
bhaktimānindriyajayī tatparo jñānamāpnuyāt |
labdhvā tatparamaṃ mokṣaṃ svalpakālena yātyasau ||47||
bhaktihīno ʼśraddadhānaḥ sarvatrasaṃśayī tu yaḥ |
tasya śaṃ nāpi vijñātamiha loke na cāpare ||48||
ātmajñānarataṃ jñānanāśitākhilasaṃśayama |
yogāstākhilakarmāṇaṃ badhnanti bhūpa tāni na ||49||
jñānakhaḍgaprahāreṇa saṃbhūtāmajñatāṃ balāt |
chittvāʼntasaṃśayaṃ tasmādyogayukto bhavennaraḥ ||50||
oṃ tatsaditi śrīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārthaṃ śāstre śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṃḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde brahmārpaṇayogo nāma tṛtīyodhyāyaḥ ||3|| ||140||

<adhyāya 141 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vareṇya uvāca |
saṃnyastiścaiva yogaśca karmaṇāṃ varṇyate tvayā |
ubhayorniścitaṃ tvekaṃ śreyo yadvada me prabho ||1||
śrīgajānana uvāca |
kriyāyogo viyogaścāpyubhau mokṣasya sādhane |
tayormadhye kriyāyogastyāgāttasya viśiṣyate ||2||
dvandvaduḥkhasahoʼdveṣṭā yo na kāṃkṣati kiṃcana |
mucyate bandhanātsadyo nityaṃ saṃnyāsavānsukham |
vadanti bhinnaphalakau karmaṇastyāgasaṃgrahau |
mūḍhālpajñāstayorekaṃ saṃyujīta vicakṣaṇaḥ ||4||
yadeva prāpyate tyāgāttadeva yogataḥ phalam |
saṃgrahaṃ karmaṇo yogaṃ yo vindati sa vindati ||5||
kevalaṃ karmaṇāṃ nyāsaṃ saṃnyāsaṃ na vidurbudhāḥ |
kurvannanicchayā karma yogī brahmaiva jāyate ||6||
nirmalo yatacittātmā jitasvo yogatatparaḥ |
ātmānaṃ sarvabhūtasthaṃ paśyankurvanna lipyate ||7||
tattvavidyogayuktātmā karomīti na manyate |
ekādaśānīndriyāṇi kurvanti karmasaṃkhyayā ||8||
tatsarvamarpayedbrahmaṇyapi karma karoti yaḥ |
na lipyate puṇyapāpairbhānubhānuryathārthagaḥ ||9||
kāyikaṃ vācikaṃ bauddhamaindriyaṃ mānasaṃ tathā |
tyaktvāśāṃ karma kurvanti yogajñāśvittaśuddhaye ||10||
yogahīno naraḥ karma phalehayā karotyalam |
baddhyate karmabījaiḥ sa tato duḥkhaṃ samaśnute ||11||
manasā sakalaṃ karma tyaktvā yogī sukhaṃ vaset |
na kurvankārayanvāpi nandañśvabhre supattane ||12||
na kriyā na ca kartṛtvaṃ kasyacitsṛjyate mayā |
na kriyābījasaṃparkaḥ śaktyā tatkriyateʼkhilama ||13||
kasyacitpuṇyapāpāni na spṛśāmi vibhurnupa |
jñānamūḍhā vimuhayanti mohenāvṛtabuddhayaḥ ||14||
vivekenātmano jñānaṃ yeṣāṃ nāśitamātmanā |
teṣāṃ vikāśamāyāti jñānamādityavatparam ||15||
manniṣṭhā maddhiyoʼtyantaṃ maccittā mayi tatparāḥ |
apunarbhavamāyānti vijñānanāśitainasaḥ ||16||
jñānavijñānasampannaṃ dvije gavi gajādiṣu |
samekṣaṇā mahātmānaḥ paṇḍitāḥ śvapace śuni ||17||
vaśyaḥ svargo jagatteṣāṃ jīvanmuktāḥ samekṣaṇāḥ |
yatoʼdoṣaṃ brahmasamaṃ tasmāttaistadvaśīkṛtam ||18||
priyāpriye prāpya harṣadveṣau ye prāpnuvanti na |
brahmāśritā asaṃmūḍhā brahmajñāḥ samabuddhayaḥ ||19||
vareṇya uvāca |
kiṃ sukhaṃ triṣu lokeṣu devagandharvayoniṣu |
bhagavankṛpayā tanme vada vidyāviśārada ||20||
gaṇeśa uvāca |
ānandamaśnute saktaḥ svātmārāmo nijātmani |
avināśyaṃ sukhaṃ taddhi na sukhaṃ viṣayādiṣu ||21||
viṣayotthāni saukhyāni duḥkhānāṃ tāni hetavaḥ |
utpattināśayuktāni tatrāsakto na tattvavit ||22||
kāraṇe sati kāmasya krodhasya sahate ca yaḥ |
tau jetuṃ varṣmavirahātsa sukhaṃ ciramaśnute ||23|| 
antarniṣṭhoʼntaḥprakāśoʼntaḥsukhoʼntoratirlabhet |
asaṃdigdhoʼkṣayaṃ brahma sarvabhūtahitārthakṛt ||24||
jetāraḥ ṣaḍarīṇāṃ ye śamino daminastathā |
teṣāṃ samaṃ tato brahma svātmajñānāṃ vibhātyaho ||25||
āsaneṣu samāsīnastyaktvemānviṣayānbahiḥ |
saṃstabhya bhṛkuṭīmāste prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇaḥ ||26||
prāṇāyāmaṃ tu saṃrodhaṃ prāṇāpānasamudbhavam |
vadanti munayastaṃ ca tridhābhūtaṃ vipaścitaḥ ||27||
pramāṇaṃ bhedato viddhi laghumadhyamamuttamama |
daśabhidvayaṃdhikairvarṇaiḥ prāṇāyāmo laghuḥ smṛtaḥ ||28||
caturviṃśatyakṣaro yo madhyamaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ |
ṣaṭtriṃśallaghuvarṇo ya uttamaḥ soʼbhidhīyate ||29||
siṃhaṃ śārdūlakaṃ vāpi mattebhaṃ mṛdutāṃ yathā |
nayanti prāṇinastadvatprāṇāpānau susādhayet ||30||
pīḍayanti mṛgāṃste na lokānvaśyaṃ gatā nṛpa |
dahatyenastathā vāyuḥ saṃstabdho na ca tattanum ||31||
yathā yathā naraḥ kaścitsopānāvalimākramet |
tathā tathā vaśīkuryātprāṇāpānau hi yogavit ||32||
pūrakaṃ kumbhakaṃ caiva recakaṃ ca tatobhyaset |
atītānāgatajñānī tataḥ syājjagatītale ||33||
prāṇāyāmairdvādaśabhiruttamairdhāraṇā matā |
yogastu dhāraṇe dve syādyogīśasta sadāʼbhyaset ||34||
evaṃ yaḥ kurute rājaṃstrikālajñaḥ sa jāyate |
anāyāsena tasya syādvaśyaṃ lokatrayaṃ nṛpa ||35||
brahmarūpaṃ jagatsarvaṃ paśyati svāntarātmani |
evaṃ yogaśca saṃnyāsaḥ samānaphaladāyinau ||36||
jantūnāṃ hitakartāraṃ karmaṇāṃ phaladāyim |
māṃ jñātvā muktimāpnoti trailokyasyeśvaraṃ vibhum ||37||
oṁtatsaditi ṅīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārtha śāstre śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṃḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde karmasaṃnyāsa' yogo nāma caturthoʼdhyāyaḥ ||4|| ||141||

<adhyāya 142 prāraṃbhaḥ>

śrī gajānana uvāca |
śrautasmārtāni karmāṇi phalaṃ necchansamācareta |
śastaḥ sa yogī rājendra akriyādyogamāśritāt ||1||
yogaprāptyai mahābāho hetuḥ karmaiva me matam |
siddhayogasya saṃsiddhyai hetū śamadamau matau ||2||
indriyārthāśca saṃkalpya kurvansvasya ripurbhavet |
etānanicchanyaḥ kurvansiddhiṃ yogī sa siddhati ||3||
suhrattve ca riputve ca uddhāre caiva bandhane |
ātmanaivātmano hyātmāʼnātmā bhavati kaścana ||4||
māneʼpamāne duḥkhe ca sukhe suhṛdi sādhuṣu |
mitreʼmitreʼpyudāsīne dveṣye loṣṭhe ca kāṃcane ||5||
samo jitātmā vijñānī jñānīndriyajayāvahaḥ |
abhyasetsatataṃ yogaṃ tadā yuktatamo hi saḥ ||6||
taptaḥ śrānto vyākūlo vā kṣudhitoʼvyagracittakaḥ |
kāleʼniśītetyuṣṇe vānilāgnyambusamākule ||7||
sadhvanāvatijīrṇe gosthāne sāgnau jalāntike |
kūpakūle śmaśāne ca nadyāṃ bhittau ca marmare ||8||
caitye savalmike deśe piśācādisamāvṛte |
nābhyasedyogavidyogaṃ yogīdhyānaparāyaṇaḥ ||9||
smṛtilopaśca mūkatvaṃ bādhiryamandhatā jvaraḥ |
jaḍatā jāyate sadyo doṣājñānāddhi yoginaḥ ||10||
ete doṣāḥ parityājyā yogābhyāsenaśālinā |
anādare hi caiteṣāṃ smṛtilopādayo dhruvam ||11||
nātibhuñjansadā yogo nābhuṃ jannātinidritaḥ |
nātijāgratsiddhimeti bhūpa yogaṃ sadāʼbhyasan ||12||
saṃkalpajāṃstyajetkāmānniyatāhārajāgaraḥ |
niyamya khagaṇaṃ buddhyā virameta śanaiḥ śanaiḥ ||13||
tatastataḥ kṛṣedetadyatra yatrānugacchati |
dhṛtyātmavaśagaṃ kuryāccittaṃ caṃcalamādṛtaḥ ||14||
evaṃ kurvansadā yogī parāṃ nirvṛtimṛcchati |
viśvasminnijamātmānaṃ viśvaṃ ca svātmanīkṣate ||15||
yogena yo māmupaiti tamupaimyahamādarāt |
mocayāmi na muṃcāmi tamahaṃ māṃ sa na tyajet ||16||
sukhe duḥkhe tare dveṣe kṣudhi toṣe samastṛṣi |
ātmasāmyena bhūtāni sarvagaṃ māṃ ca vetti yaḥ ||17||
jīvanmuktaḥ sa yogīndraḥ kevalaṃ mayi saṃśritaḥ |
brahmādīnāṃ ca devānāṃ sa vandyaḥ syājjagattraye ||18||
vareṇya uvāca |
dvividhoʼpi hi yogoʼyamasaṃbhāvyo hi me mataḥ |
yatoʼntaḥkaraṇaṃ duṣṭaṃ caṃcalaṃ durgrahaṃ vibho ||19||
śrī gajānana uvāca |
yo nigrahaṃ durgrahasya manasāḥ saṃprakalpayet |
ghaṭīyantrasamādasmānmuktaḥ saṃsṛticakrakāt ||20||
viṣayaiḥ krakacairetatsaṃsṛṣṭaṃ cakrakaṃ dṛḍham |
janaśchettuṃ na śaknoti karmakīlaiḥ susaṃvṛtam ||21||
atiduḥkhaṃ ca vairāgyaṃ bhogādvaitṛṣṇyameva ca |
guruprasādaḥ satsaṃga upāyāstajyaye amī ||22||
abhyāsādvā vaśīkuryānmano yogasya siddhaye |
vareṇya durlabho yogo vināʼsya manaso jayāt ||23||
vareṇya uvāca |
yogabhraṣṭasya ko lokaḥ kā gatiḥ kiṃ phalaṃ bhavet |
vibho sarvajña me chindhi saṃśayaṃ buddhicakrabhṛt ||24||
śrī gajānana uvāca |
divyadehadharo yogādbhraṣṭaḥ svarbhogamuttamam |
bhuktvā yogikule janma labhedṛddhimatāṃ kule ||25||
punaryogī bhavatyeṣa saṃskārātpūrvajanmajāt |
na hi puṇyakṛtāṃ kaścinnarakaṃ pratipadyate ||26|| 
jñānaniṣṭhāttaponiṣṭhākarmaniṣṭhānnarādhipa |
śreṣṭho yogī śreṣṭhatamo bhaktimānmayi teṣu yaḥ ||27||
oṁ tatsaditi… śrīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārtha śāstre śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṃḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde 'yogāvṛtti praśaṃsano' yogonāma paṃcamodhyāya||5|| ||142||

<adhyāya 143 prāraṃbhaḥ>

gajānana uvāca |
īdṛśaṃ viddhi me tattvaṃ madgatenāntarātmanā |
yajjñātvā māmasaṃdigdhaṃ vetsi mokṣyasi sarvagam ||1||
tatteʼhaṃ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā |
asti jñeyaṃ yato nānyanmukteśca sādhanaṃ nṛpa ||2||
jñeyā matprakṛtiḥ pūrvaṃ tataḥ syājjñānagocaraḥ |
tato vijñānasaṃpattirmayi jñāte nṛṇāṃ bhavet ||3||
kvanalo khamahaṃkāraḥ kaṃ cittaṃ dhīsamīraṇaḥ |
ravīndū yāgakṛccaikā daśadhā prakṛtirmama ||4||
anyāṃ matprakṛtiṃ vṛddhāṃ munayaḥ saṃgiranti ca |
tathā triviṣṭapaṃ vyāptaṃ jīvatvaṃ gatayāʼnayā ||5||
ābhyāmutpādyate sarvaṃ carācaramayaṃ jagat |
saṃgādviśvasya saṃbhūtiḥ paritrāṇaṃ layopyaham ||6||
tattvametanniboddhuṃ me yatate kaścideva hi |
varṇāśramavatāṃ pusāṃ purā cīrṇena karmaṇā ||7||
sākṣātkaroti māṃ kaścidyatnavatsvapi teṣu ca |
mattoʼnyannekṣate kiṃcinmayi sarvaṃ ca vīkṣate ||8||
śritau sugandharūpeṇa tejorūpeṇa cāgniṣu |
prabhārūpeṇa puṇyabje rasarūpeṇa cāpsu ca ||9||
dhītapo balināṃ cāhaṃ dhīstapo balameva ca |
trividheṣu vikāreṣu madutpanneṣvahaṃ sthitaḥ ||10||
na māṃ vindanti pāpīyān māyāmohitacetasaḥ |
trivikārā mohayati prakṛtirme jagattrayam ||11||
yo me ttattvaṃ vijānāti mohaṃ tyajati soʼkhilam |
anekairjanmabhiścaivaṃ jñātvā māṃ mucyate tataḥ ||12||
anye nānāvidhāndevānbhajante tānvrajanti te |
yathā yathā matiṃ kṛtvā bhajate māṃ janoʼkhilaḥ ||13||
tathā tathāʼsya taṃ bhāvaṃ pūrayāmyahameva tam |
ahaṃ sarvaṃ vijānāmi māṃ na kaścidvibudhyate ||14||
avyaktaṃ vyaktimāpannaṃ na viduḥ kāmamohitāḥ |
nāhaṃ prakāśatāṃ yāmi ajñānāṃ pāpakarmaṇām ||15||
yaḥ smṛtvā tyajati prāṇamante māṃ śraddhayānvitaḥ |
sa yātyapunarāvṛttiṃ prasādānmama bhūbhuja ||16||
yaṃ yaṃ devaṃ smaranbhaktyā tyajati svaṃ kalevaram |
tattatsālokyamāyāti tattadbhaktyā narādhipa ||17||
ataścāharniśaṃ bhūpa smartavyoʼnekarūpavān |
sarveṣāmapyahaṃ gamyaḥ srotasāmarṇavo yathā ||18||
brahmaviṣṇuśivendrādyāṃllokānprāpya punaḥ patet |
yo māmupaitya saṃdigdhaḥ patanaṃ tasya na kvacit ||19||
ananyaśaraṇo yo māṃ bhaktyā bhajati bhūmipa |
yogakṣemau ca tasyāhaṃ sarvadā pratipādaye ||20||
dvividhā gatiruddiṣṭā śuklā kṛṣṇā nṛṇāṃ nṛpa |
ekayā paramaṃ brahma parayā yāti saṃsṛtim ||21||
oṁtatsaditi śrīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārtha śāstre śrīmadādi śrīnmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṇḍe bālacarite śrīgajānavareṇyasaṃvāde 'yogāvṛttibuddhiyogo nāma ṣaṣṭhoʼdhyāyaḥ ||6|| ||143||

<adhyāya 144 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vareṇya uvāca |
kā śuklā gatiruddiṣṭā kā ca kṛṣṇā gajānana |
kiṃ brahmasaṃsṛtiḥ kā me vaktumarhasyanugrahāt ||1||
gajānana uvāca |
agnirjyotirahaḥśuklā karmārhamayanaṃ gatiḥ |
cāndraṃ jyotistathā dhūmo rātriśca dakṣiṇāyanam ||2||
kṛṣṇaite brahmasaṃsṛtyoravāptaiḥ kāraṇaṃ gatiḥ |
dṛśyādṛśyamidaṃ sarvaṃ brahmaivetyavadhāraya ||3||
kṣaraṃ paṃcātmakaṃ viddhi tadantarakṣaraṃ smṛtam |
ubhābhyāṃ yadatikrāntaṃ śuddhaṃ viddhi sanātanam ||4||
anekajanmasaṃbhūtiḥ saṃsṛtiḥ parikīrtitā |
saṃsṛtiṃ prāpnuvantyete ye tu māṃ gaṇayanti na ||5||
ye māṃ samyagupāsante paraṃ brahma prayānti te |
dhyānādyairupacārairmāṃ tathā paṃcāmṛtādibhiḥ ||6||
snānavastrādyalaṃkārasugandhadhūpadīpakaiḥ |
naivedyaiḥ phalatāmbūladakṣiṇābhiśca yorcayet ||7||
bhaktyaikacetasā caiva tasyeṣṭaṃ pūrayāmyaham |
evaṃ pratidinaṃ bhaktyā madbhakto māṃ samarpayet ||8||
athavā mānasīṃ pūjāṃ kurvīta sthiracetasā |
athavā phalapatrādyaiḥ puṣpamūlajalādibhiḥ |
pūjayenmāṃ prayatnena tattadiṣṭaphalaṃ labhet ||9||
trividhāsvapi pūjāsu śreyasī mānasī matā |
sāpyuttamā matā pūjāʼnicchayā yā kṛtā mama ||10||
brahmacārī gṛhastho vā vānaprastho yatiśca yaḥ |
ekāṃ pūjāṃ prakurvāṇopyano vā siddhimṛcchati ||11||
madanyaṃ devaṃ yo bhaktyā dviṣanmāmanyadevatām |
soʼpi māmeva yajate paraṃtvavidhito nṛpa, ||12||
yo hyanyadevatāṃ māṃ ca dviṣannanyāṃ samarcayet |
yāti kalpasahasraṃ sa nirayānduḥkhabhāk sadā ||13||
bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāyādau prāṇānāṃ sthāpanaṃ tathā |
ākṛṣya cetaso vṛtiṃ tato nyāsamupakramet ||14||
kṛtvāntarmātṛkānyāsaṃ bahiścātha ṣaḍaṅkakam |
nyāsaṃ ca mūlamantrasya tato dhyātvā japenmanum ||15||
sthiracitto japenmantraṃ yathā gurumukhodgatam |
japaṃ nivedya devāya stutvā stotrairanekadhā ||16||
evaṃ māṃ ya upāsīta sa labhenmokṣamavyayam |
ya upāsanayā hīno dhiṅanaro vyarthajanmabhāk ||17||
yajñoʼhamauṣadhaṃ mantroʼgnirājyaṃ ca havirhutam |
dhyānaṃ dhyeyaḥ stutiḥ stotraṃ natirbhaktirupāsanā ||18||
trayījñeyaṃ pavitraṃ ca pitāmaha pitāmahaḥ |
oṁkāraḥ pāvanaḥ sākṣī prabhurmitraṃ gatirlayaḥ ||19||
utpattiḥ poṣako bījaṃ śaraṇaṃ vāsa eva ca |
asanmṛtyuḥ sadamṛtamātmā brahmāhameva ca ||20||
dānaṃ homastapo bhaktirjapaḥ svādhyāya||eva ca |
yadyatkaroti tatsarvaṃ sa me mayi nivedayet ||21||
yoṣitoʼtha durācārāḥ pāpāstraivarṇikāstathā |
madāśrayā vimucyante ki madbhaktyā dvijādayaḥ ||22||
na vinaśyati madbhakto jñātvemā madvibhūtayaḥ |
prabhavaṃ me vibhūtīśca na devā ṛṣayo viduḥ ||23||
nānāvibhūtibhirahaṃ vyāpya viśvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
yadyacchreṣṭhatamaṃ loke sā vibhūtirnibodha me ||24||
oṁ tatsaditi śrīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārtha śāstre śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṃḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde ' upāsanā yogo' nāma saptamodhyāya||7|| ||144||

<adhyāya 145 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vareṇya uvāca |
bhagavannārado mahayaṃ tava nānāvibhūtayaḥ |
uktavāṃstā ahaṃ veda na sarvāḥ soʼpi vetti tāḥ ||1||
tvameva tattvataḥ sarvā vetsi tā dviradānana |
nijaṃ rūpamidānīṃ me vyāpakaṃ cāru darśaya ||2||
śrīgajānana uvāca |
ekasminmayi paśya tvaṃ viśvametaccarācaram |
nānāścaryāṇi divyāni purā dṛṣṭāni kenacit ||3||
jñānacakṣurahaṃ teʼdya sṛjāmi svaprabhāvataḥ |
carmacakṣuḥ kathaṃ paśyenmāṃ vibhuṃ ajamavyayam ||4||
vyāsa uvāca |
tato rājā vareṇyaḥ sa divyacakṣuravaikṣata |
īśituḥ paramaṃ rūpaṃ gajāsyasya mahādbhutam ||5||
asaṃkhyavaktraṃ lalitamasaṃkhyāṃghrikaraṃ mahat |
anuliptaṃ sugandhena divyabhūṣāmbarasrajam ||6||
asaṃkhyanayanaṃ koṭisūryaraśmidhṛtāyudham |
tadvarṣmāṇi trayo lokā draṣṭāstena pṛthagvidhāḥ, ||7||
dṛṣṭvaiśvaraṃ paraṃ rūpaṃ praṇamya sa nṛpobravīt |
vareṇya uvāca |
vīkṣeʼhaṃ tava deheʼsmindevānṛṣigaṇānpitṝn ||8||
pātālānāṃ samudrāṇāṃ dvīpānāṃ caiva bhūbhṛtām |
vanarṣīṇāṃ saptakaṃ ca nānārthaḥ saṃkulaṃ vibho ||9||
bhuvoʼnnarikṣaṃ svargāśca manuṣyoragarākṣasān |
brahmaviṣṇumaheśendrāndevāṃjantūnanekadhā ||10||
anādyanantaṃ lokādimanantabhujaśīrṣakam |
pradīptānalasaṃkāśamaprameyaṃ purātanam ||11||
kirīṭakuṇḍaladharaṃ durnirīkṣyaṃ mudāvaham |
etādṛśaṃ ca vīkṣe tvāṃ viśālavakṣasaṃ prabhum ||12||
suravidyādharairyakṣaiḥ kinnarairmunimānuṣaiḥ |
nṛtyadbhirapsarobhiśca gandharvairgānatatparaiḥ ||13||
vasurudrādityagaṇaiḥ siddhaiḥ sādhyairmudā yutaiḥ |
sevyamānaṃ mahābhaktyā vīkṣyamāṇaṃ suvismitaiḥ ||14||
vettāramakṣaraṃ vedyaṃ dharmagoptāramīśvaram |
pātālāni diśaḥ svargānbhuvaṃ vyāpyākhilaṃ sthitam ||15||
bhītā lokāstathā cāhamevaṃ tvāṃ vīkṣya rūpiṇam |
nānādaṃṣṭākarālaṃ ca nānāvidyāviśāradam ||16||
pralayānaladīptāsyaṃ jaṭilaṃ ca nabhaḥspṛśam |
dṛṣṭvā gaṇeśa te rūpamahaṃ bhrānta ivābhavam ||17||
devā manuṣyā nāgādyāḥkhilāstvadudareśayāḥ |
nānāyonibhujaścānte tvayyeva praviśanti ca ||18||
abdherutpadyamānāste yathā jīmūtabindavaḥ |
tvamindroʼgniryamaścaiva nirऋtirvaruṇo maruta ||19||
guhyakeśastatheśānaḥ somaḥ sūryoʼkhilaṃ jagat |
namāmi tvāmataḥ svāminprasādaṃ kuru meʼdhunā ||20||
darśayasva nijaṃ rūpaṃ saumyaṃ yatpūrvamīkṣitam |
ko veda līlāste bhūmankriyamāṇā nijecchayā ||21||
anugrahānmayā dṛṣṭamaiśvaraṃ rūpamīdṛśam |
jñānacakṣuryato dattaṃ prasannena tvayā mama ||22||
śrīgajānana uvāca |
nedaṃ rūpaṃ mahābāho mama paśyantyayoginaḥ |
sanakādyā nāradādyāḥ paśyanti madanugrahān ||23|| 
caturvedārthatattvajñāścatuḥśāstraviśāradāḥ |
yajñadānataponiṣṭhā na me rūpaṃ vidanti te ||24||
śakyoʼhaṃ vīkṣituṃ jñātuṃ praveṣṭuṃ bhaktibhāvataḥ |
tyaja bhītiṃ ca mohaṃ ca paśya māṃ somyarūpiṇam ||25||
madbhakto matparaḥ sarvasaṃgahīno madarthakṛt |
niṣkrodhaḥ sarvabhūteṣu samo māmeti bhūbhuja ||26||
oṁ tatsaditi śrīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārtha śāstre śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṃḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde 'viśvarūpavīkṣaṇayogo' yogo nāma aṣṭamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||8|| ||145||

<adhyāya 146 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vareṇya uvāca |
ananyabhāvastvāṃ samyaṅmatimantamupāsate |
yoʼkṣaraṃ paramaṃ vyaktaṃ tayoḥ kaste matoʼdhikaḥ ||1||
asi tvaṃ sarvavitsākṣī bhūtabhāvana īśvaraḥ |
atastvāṃ paripṛcchāmi vada me kṛpayā vibho ||2||
śrīgajānana uvāca |
yo māṃ mūrtidharaṃ bhaktyā madbhaktaḥ parisevate |
sa me mānyoʼnanyabhaktirniyujya hṛdayaṃ mayi ||3||
khagaṇaṃ svavaśaṃ kṛtvāʼkhilabhūtahitārthakṛt |
madhyeyamakṣarāvyaktaṃ sarvagaṃ kūṭagaṃ sthiram ||4||
soʼpi māmetya nirdeśyaṃ matparo ya upāsate |
saṃsārasāgarādasmāduddharāmi tamapyaham ||5||
avyaktopāsanāduḥkhamadhikaṃ tena labhyate |
vyaktasyopāsanāsādhyaṃ tadevāvyaktabhaktitaḥ ||6||
bhaktiścaivādaraścātra kāraṇaṃ paramaṃ matam |
sarveṣāṃ viduṣāṃ śreṣṭho hyakiṃcijjñoʼpi bhaktimān |
bhajanbhaktyā vihīno yaḥ sa cāṇḍāloʼbhidhīyate |
cāṇḍāloʼpi bhajanbhaktyā brāhmaṇebhyoʼdhiko mama ||8||
śukādyāḥ sanakādyāśca purā muktā hi bhaktitaḥ |
bhaktyaiva māmanuprāptā nāradādyāścirāyuṣaḥ ||9||
ato bhaktyā mayi mano nidhehi buddhimeva ca |
bhaktyā yajasva māṃ rājaṃstato māmeva yāsyasi ||10||
asamarthoʼrpituṃ svāntaṃ dhruvaṃ mayi narādhipa |
abhyāsena ca yogena tato gantuṃ yajasva mām ||11||
tatrāpi tvamaśaktaścetkuru karma madarpaṇam |
mamānugrahataścaiva parāṃ nirvṛtimeṣyasi ||12||
athaitadapyanuṣṭhātuṃ na śaknoṣi tadā kuru |
prayatnataḥ phalatyāgaṃ trividhānāṃ hi karmaṇām ||13||
śreyasī buddhirāvṛttestato dhyānaṃ varaṃ matam |
tatoʼkhilaparityāgastataḥ śāntirgarīyasī ||14||
nirahaṃmamatābuddhiradveṣaḥ karuṇaḥsamaḥ |
lābhālābhe sukheduḥkhe mānāmāne sa me priyaḥ ||15||
yaṃ vīkṣya na bhayaṃ yāti janastasmānna ca svayam |
udvegabhīḥ kopamadbhī rahito yaḥ sa me priyaḥ, ||16||
ripau mitreʼtha garhyāyāṃ stutau śoke samaḥ samut |
maunī niścaladhībhaktirasaṃgaḥ sa ca me priyaḥ ||17||
saṃśīlayati yaścainamupadeśaṃ mayā kṛtam |
sa vandyaḥ sarvalokeṣu muktātmā me priyaḥ sadā ||18||
aniṣṭāptau ca na dveṣṭīṣṭaprāptau na ca tuṣyati |
kṣetratajjñau ca yo vetti sa me priyataro bhavet ||19||
vareṇya uvāca |
kiṃ kṣetraṃ kaśca tadvetti ki tajjñānaṃ gajānana |
etadācakṣva mahayaṃ tvaṃ pṛcchate karuṇāmbudhe ||20||
śrīgajānana uvāca |
paṃcabhūtāni tanmātrāḥ paṃcakarmendriyāṇi ca |
ahaṃkāro mano buddhiḥ paṃcajñānendriyāṇi ca ||21||
icchāvyaktaṃ dhṛtidveṣau sukhaduḥkhe tathaiva ca |
cetanāsahitaścāyaṃ samūhaḥ kṣetramucyate ||22||
tajjñaṃ tvaṃ viddhi māṃ bhūpa sarvāntaryāmiṇaṃ vibhum |
ayaṃ samūhoʼhaṃ cāpi yajjñānaviṣayau nṛpa ||23||
ārjavaṃ guruśuśrūṣā viraktiścendriyārthataḥ |
śaucaṃ kṣāntiradambhaśca janmādidoṣavīkṣaṇam ||24||
samadṛṣṭiइrdṛḍhā bhaktirekāntitvaṃ śamo damaḥ |
etairyacca yutaṃ jñānaṃ tajjñānaṃ viddhi bāhuja ||25||
tajjñānaviṣayaṃ rājanbravīmi tvaṃ śaṇuṣva me |
yajjñātvaiti ca nirvāṇaṃ muktvā saṃsṛtisāgaram ||26||
yadanādīndriyairhīnaṃ guṇabhugguṇavarjitam |
avyaktaṃ sadasadbhinnamindriyārthāvabhāsakam ||27||
viṣvabhṛccākhilavyāpi ekaṃ nāneva bhāsate |
bāhyābhyantarataḥ pūrṇamasaṃgaṃ tamasaḥ param ||28||
durjñeyaṃ cātisūkṣmatvāddīptīnāmapi bhāsakam |
jñeyametādṛśaṃ viddhi jñānagamyaṃ purātanam ||29||
etadeva paraṃ brahma jñeyamātmā paroʼvyayaḥ |
guṇānprakṛtijānbhuṅkte puruṣaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ ||30||
guṇaistribhiriyaṃ dehe badhnāti puruṣaṃ dṛḍham |
yadā prakāśaḥ kṣāntiśca viddhiḥ sattvaṃ tadādhikam ||31||
lobhoʼśamaḥ spṛhārambhaḥ karmaṇāṃ rajasoguṇaḥ |
mohoʼpravṛttiścājñānaṃ pramādastamaso guṇāḥ ||32||
sattvādhikaḥ sukhaṃ jñānaṃ karmasaṃgo rajodhikaḥ |
tamodhikaśca labhate nidrālasyaṃ sukhetarat ||33||
eṣu triṣu pravṛddheṣu muktisaṃsṛtidurgatiḥ |
prayānti mānavā rājaṃstasmātsattvayuto bhava ||34||
tataśca sarvabhāvena bhaja tvaṃ māṃ nareśvara |
bhaktyā cāvyabhicāriṇyā sarvatraiva ca saṃsthitam ||35||
agnau sūrye tathā some yacca tārāsu saṃsthitam |
viduṣī brāhmaṇe teje viddhi tanmāmakaṃ nṛpa ||36||
ahamevākhilaṃ viśvaṃ sṛjāmi visṛjāmi ca |
oṣadhīstejasā sarvā viśvaṃ cāpyāyayāmyaham ||37||
sarvendriyāṇyadhiṣṭhāya jāṭharaṃ ca dhanañjayam |
bhunajmi cākhilānbhogānpuṇyapāpāvivarjitaḥ ||38||
ahaṃ viṣṇuśca rudraśca brahmā gaurī guṇeśvaraḥ |
indrādyā lokapālāśca mamaivāṃśasamudbhavāḥ ||39||
yena yena hi rūpeṇa jano māṃ paryupāsate |
tathā tathā darśayāmi tasmai rūpaṃ subhaktitaḥ ||40||
iti kṣetraṃ tathā jñātā jñānaṃ jñeyaṃ mayeritam |
akhilaṃ bhūpate samyagupapannāya pṛcchate ||41|| 
oṁtatsaditi śrīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtartha śāstre śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare uttarakhaṇḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde 'kṣetrakṣetrajñavivekayogo' navamodhyāyaḥ ||9|| ||146||

<adhyāya 147 prāraṃbhaḥ>

śrīgajānana uvāca |
daivyāsurī rākṣasī ca prakṛtistrividhā nṛṇām |
tāsāṃ phalāni cihnāni saṃkṣepātteʼdhunā bruve ||1||
ādyā saṃsādhayenmuktiṃ dve pare bandhanaṃ nṛpa |
cihnaṃ bravīmi cādyāyāstanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu ||2||
apaiśunyaṃ dayā krodhaścāpalaṃ dhṛtirārjavam |
tejoʼbhayahiṃsā ca kṣamā śaucamamānitā ||3||
ityādicihnamādyāyā āsuryāḥ śṛṇu sāṃpratam |
ativādoʼbhimānaśca darpoʼjñānaṃ sakopatā ||4||
āsuryā evamādyāni cihnāni prakṛternṛpa |
niṣṭhuratvaṃ mado mohoʼhaṃkāro garva eva ca ||5||
dveṣo hiṃsā dayā krodha auddhatyaṃ durvinītatā |
ābhicārikakartṛtvaṃ krūrakarmaratistathā ||6||
aviśvāsaḥ satāṃ vākyeʼśucitve karmahīnatā |
nindakatvaṃ ca vedānāṃ bhaktānāmasuradviṣām ||7||
muniśrotriyaviprāṇāṃ tathā smṛtipurāṇayoḥ |
pākhaṇḍavākye viśvāsaḥ saṃgatirmalinātmanām ||8||
sadambhe karmakartṛtvaṃ spṛhā ca paravastuṣu |
anekakāmanāvattvaṃ sarvadāʼnṛtabhāṣaṇam ||9||
parotkarṣāsahiṣṇutvaṃ parakṛtyaparāhatiḥ |
ityādyā bahavaścānye rākṣasyāḥ prakṛterguṇāḥ ||10||
pṛthivyā svargaloke ca parivṛtya vasanti te |
madbhaktirahitā lokā rākṣasīṃ prakṛtiṃ śritāḥ ||11||
tāmasīṃ ye śritā rājanyānti te rauravaṃ dhruvam |
anirvācyaṃ ca te duḥkhaṃ bhuñjate tatra saṃsthitāḥ ||12||
daivānniḥsṛtya narakājjāyante bhuvi kubjakāḥ |
jātyandhāḥ paṃgavo dīnāhīnajātiṣu te nṛpa ||13||
punaḥ pāpasamācārā mayyabhaktāḥ patanti te |
utpatanti hi madbhaktā yāṃ kāṃcidyonimāśritāḥ ||14||
labhante svargatiṃ yajñairanyairdharmaiśca bhūmipa |
sulabhā sā sakāmānāṃ mayi bhaktiḥ sudurlabhā ||15||
vimūḍhā mohajālena baddhāḥ svena ca karmaṇā |
ahaṃ hartā ahaṃ kartā ahaṃ bhokteti vādinaḥ ||16||
ahameveśvaraḥ śāstā ahaṃ vettā ahaṃ sukhī |
etādṛśī matirnṛṇāmadhaḥpātayatīha tān ||17||
tasmādetatsamutsṛjya daivīṃ prakṛtimāśraya |
bhaktiṃ kuru madīyāṃ tvamaniśaṃ dṛḍhacetasā ||18||
sāpi bhaktistridhā rājansātvikī rājasītarā |
yaddevānbhajate bhaktyā sāttvikī sā matā śubhā ||19||
rājasī sā tu vijñeyā bhaktirjanmamṛtipradā |
yadyakṣāṃccaiva rakṣāṃsi yajante sarvabhāvataḥ ||20||
vedenāvihitaṃ krūraṃ sāhaṃkāraṃ sadambhakam |
bhajante pretabhūtādīnkarma kurvanti kāmikam ||21||
śoṣayanto nijaṃ dehamantasthaṃ māṃ dṛḍhā grahāḥ |
tāmasyetādṛśī bhaktirnṛṇāṃ sā nirayapradā ||22||
kāmo lobhastathā krodho dambhaścatvāra ityami |
mahādvārāṇi vīcīnāṃ tasmādetāṃstu varjayet ||23||
oṁtatsaditi śrīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārtha śāstre śrīmadādi śrīnmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṃḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde ' upadeśayogo' nāma daśamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||10|| ||147||

<adhyāya 148 prāraṃbhaḥ>

śrīgajānana uvāca |
tapoʼpi trividhaṃ rājankāyikādiprabhedataḥ |
ṛjutārjavaśaucāśca brahmacaryamahiṃsanam ||1||
guruvijñadvijātīnāṃ pūjanaṃ cāsuradviṣām |
svadharmapālanaṃ nityaṃ kāyikaṃ tapaईdṛśam ||2||
marmāṃspṛkca priyaṃ vākyamanudvegaṃ hitaṃ ṛtam |
adhītirvedaśāstrāṇāṃ vācikaṃ tapa īdaśam ||3||
antaḥprasādaḥ śāntatvaṃ maunamindriyanigrahaḥ |
nirmalāśayatā nityaṃ mānasaṃ tapa īdṛśam ||4||
akāmataḥ śraddhayā ca yattapaḥ sāttvikaṃ tu tat |
satkārapūjārthaṃ sadambhaṃ rājasaṃ tapaḥ ||5||
tadasthiraṃ janmamṛtī prayacchati na saṃśayaḥ |
parātmapīḍakaṃ yacca tapastāmasamucyate ||6||
vidhivākyapramāṇārthaṃ satpātre deśakālataḥ |
śraddhayā dīyamānaṃ yaddānaṃ tatsātvikaṃ matam ||7||
upakāraṃ phalaṃ vāpi kāṃkṣadbhirdīyate naraiḥ |
kleśatoʼdīyamānaṃ vā bhaktyā rājasamucyate ||8||
akāladeśatoʼpātrevajñayā dīyate tu yat |
asatkārācca yaddattaṃ taddānaṃ tāmasaṃ smṛtam ||9||
jñānaṃ ca trividhaṃ rājanśṛṇuṣva sthiracetasā |
tridhā karma ca kartāraṃ bravīmi te prasaṃgataḥ ||10||
nānāvidheṣu bhūteṣu māmekaṃ vīkṣyate tu yaḥ |
nāśavatsu ca nityaṃ māṃ tajjñānaṃ sāttvikaṃ nṛpa ||11||
teṣu vetti pṛthagbhūtaṃ vividhaṃ bhāvamāśritaḥ |
māmavyayaṃ ca tajjñānaṃ rājasaṃ parikīrtitam ||12||
hetuhīnamasatyaṃ ca dehātmaviṣayaṃ ca yat |
asadalpārthaviṣayaṃ tāmasaṃ jñānamucyate ||13||
bhedatastrividhaṃ karma viddhi rājanmayeritam |
kāmanādveṣadambhairyadrahitaṃ nityakarma yata ||14||
kṛtaṃ vinā phalecchāṃ yatkarma sāttvikamucyate |
yadbahukleśataḥ karma kṛtaṃ yacca phalecchayā ||15||
kriyamāṇaṃ nṛbhirdambhātkarma rājasamucyate |
anapekṣya svaśaktiṃ yadarthakṣayakaraṃ ca yat ||16||
ajñānātkriyamāṇaṃ yatkarma tāmasamīritam |
karttāraṃ trividhaṃ viddhi kathyamānaṃ mayā nṛpa ||17||
dhairyotsāhī samoʼsiddhau siddhau cāvikriyastu yaḥ |
ahaṃkāravimukto yaḥ sa kartā sāttviko nṛpa ||18||
kurvanharṣaṃ ca śokaṃ ca hiṃsā phalaspṛśam ca yaḥ |
aśucirlubdhako yastu rājasoʼsau nigadyate ||19||
pramādājñānasahitaḥ parīcchedaparaḥ śaṭhaḥ |
alasastarkavānyastu kartāʼsau tāmaso mataḥ ||20||
sukhaṃ ca trividhaṃ rājanduḥkhaṃ ca kramataḥ śṛṇu |
sāttvikaṃ rājasaṃ caiva tāmasaṃ ca mayocyate ||21||
viṣavadbhāsate pūrvaṃ duḥkhasyāntakaraṃ ca yata |
icchyamānaṃ tathā vṛtyā vadante mṛtavadbhavet ||22||
prasādātsvasya buddheryatsāttvikaṃ sukhamīritam |
viṣayāṇāṃ tu yo bhogo bhāsateʼmṛtavatpurā ||23||
hālāhālamivānte yadrājasaṃ sukhamīritam |
tandripramādasaṃbhūtamālasyaprabhavaṃ ca yat ||24||
sarvadā mohakaṃ svasya sukhaṃ tāmasamīdṛśam |
na tadasti yadetairyamuktaṃ syāttrividhairguṇaiḥ ||25||
rājanbrahmāpi trividhamoṃtatsaditi bhedataḥ |
trilokeṣu tridhābhūtamakhilaṃ bhūpa vartate ||26||
brahmakṣatriyaviṭśūdrāḥ svabhāvādbhinnakarmiṇaḥ |
tāni teṣāṃ tu karmāṇi saṃkṣepātteʼdhunā vade ||27||
antarbāhyendriyāṇāṃ ca vaśatvamārjavaṃ kṣamā |
nānātapāṃsi śaucaṃ ca dvividhaṃ jñānamātmanaḥ ||28||
vedaśāstrapurāṇānāṃ smṛtināṃ jñānameva ca |
anuṣṭhānaṃ tadarthānāṃ karma brāhmamudāhṛtam, ||29||
dārḍhyaṃ śauryaṃ ca dākṣyaṃ ca yuddhe pṛṣṭhapradarśanam |
śaraṇyapālanaṃ dānaṃ dhṛtistejaḥ svabhāvajam ||30||
prabhutā manaऔnnatyaṃ sunītirlokapālanam |
paṃcakarmādhikāritvaṃ kṣātrakarma samīritam ||31||
nānāvastukrayo bhūmeḥ karṣaṇaṃ rakṣaṇaṃ gavām |
tridhākarmādhikāritvaṃ vaiśyānāṃ karma īdṛśam ||32||
dānaṃ dvijānāṃ śuśrūṣā sarvadā śivasevanam |
etādṛśaṃ naravyāghra karma śaudramudīritam ||33||
svasvakarmaratā ete mayyārpyākhilakāriṇaḥ |
matprasādātsthiraṃ sthānaṃ yānti te paramaṃ nṛpa ||34||
iti te kathito rājanprasādādyoga uttamaḥ |
sāgopāṃga savistāroʼnādisiddhau mayā priya ||35||
yuṅkṣva yogaṃ mayākhyātaṃ nākhyātaṃ kasyacinnṛpa |
gopayainaṃ tataḥ siddhiṃ parāṃ yāsyasyanuttamām ||36||
ka uvāca |
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā prasannasya mahātmanaḥ |
gaṇeśasya vareṇyaḥ sa cakāra ca yathoditam ||37||
tyaktvā rājyaṃ kuṭuṃbaṃ ca kāntāraṃ prayayau syāt |
upadiṣṭaṃ yathāyogamāsthāya muktimāpnuyāt ||38||
imaṃ yopyuttamaṃ yogaṃ śṛṇoti śraddhayā tu yaḥ |
soʼpi kaivalyamāpnoti yathā yogī tathaiva saḥ ||39||
ya imaṃ śrāvayedyogaṃ kṛtvāʼsyārthaṃ subuddhimān |
yathā yogī tathā soʼpi paraṃ nirvāṇamṛcchati ||40||
yo gītāṃ samyagabhyasya jñātvācārthaṃ gurormukhāta |
kṛtvā pūjāṃ gaṇeśasya pratyahaṃ paṭhate tu yaḥ ||41||
ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ vāpi yaḥ paṭhet |
brahmībhūtasya tasyāpi darśanānmucyatetaraḥ ||42||
na yajñairna vratairdānairnāgnihotrairmahādhanaiḥ |
na vedaiḥ samyagabhyastaiḥ samyagjñātaiḥ sahāṃgakaiḥ ||43||
purāṇaśravaṇairnaiva na śāstraiḥ sādhu cintanaiḥ |
prāpyate brahma paramamanayā prāpyate naraiḥ ||44||
brahmaghno madyapaḥ steno gurutalpagamoʼpi yaḥ |
caturṇāṃ yastu saṃsargī mahāpātakakāriṇām ||45||
strīhiṃsā govadhādīnāṃ kartāro ye ca pāpinaḥ |
te sarve pratimucyante gītāmetāṃ paṭhanti cet ||46||
yaḥ paṭhetprayato nityaṃ sa gaṇeśo na saṃśayaḥ |
caturthyāṃ yaḥ paṭhedbhaktyā soʼpi mokṣāya kalpate ||47||
tataḥtkṣetraṃ samāsādya snātvāʼbhyarcya gajānanam |
sakṛdgītāṃ paṭhedbhaktyā brahmabhūyāya jāyate ||48||
bhādramāse site pakṣe caturthyāṃ bhaktimānnaraḥ |
kṛtvā mahīmayīṃ mūrtiṃ gaṇeśasya caturbhujām ||49||
savāhanāṃ sāyudhāṃ ca samabhyarcya yathāvidhi |
yaḥ paṭhetsaptakṛtvastu gītāmetāṃ prayatnataḥ ||50||
dadāti tasya tuṣṭosau gaṇeśo bhogamuttamam |
putrānpautrāndhanaṃ dhānyaṃ paśuratnādisaṃpadaḥ ||51||
vidyārthino labhedvidyā sukhārthī sukhamāpnuyāt |
kāmānanyāṁllabhetkāmī muktimante prayānti te ||52||
oṁtatsaditi śrīmadgaṇeśagītāsūpaniṣadarthagarbhāsu yogāmṛtārtha śāstre śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe uttarakhaṇḍe bālacarite śrīgajānanavareṇyasaṃvāde
'guṇatrayakṛtiyogo' nāmaikādaśodhyāyaḥ ||11|| ||148||

<adhyāya 149 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
evaṃ pūjāṃ saṃpragṛhaya tatraiva nyavasadvibhuḥ |
bhaktānāṃ pūrayankāmānsadane rājapūjite ||1||
muniruvāca |
vināyakasya māhātmye kathā bhinnā nirūpitāḥ |
avatīrya vareṇyasya gṛhe vighnāsuro hataḥ ||2||
gajāsuramukhaprāptirvarṇitā caturānana |
atraiva pārvatī garbhādāvirbhāvo nirūpitaḥ ||3||
gajānanatayā deva saṃśayoʼto mamābhavat |
tvadanyaḥ saṃśayecchettā nāstyeva bhuvanatraye ||4||
ka uvāca |
na kāryaḥ saṃśayo vyāsa nānāśaktiyute vibhau |
nijecchāvaśatoʼnekarūpakaḥ kalpabhedataḥ ||5||
śambhuvaktrācca krodhācca prādurbhūto gajānanaḥ |
gauryāśca tejaso jāta udarācca kvacinmune ||6||
kvacidgaurīmalāccāpi dvimukho dvikaraḥ kvacit |
kvacitpaṃcamukhaḥ kvāpi ṣaṇmukho daśabāhukaḥ ||7||
kvaciddvādaśahastaśca sahasrabhujamaṇḍitaḥ |
āgameṣu samasteṣu nānādhyānāni santi hi ||8||
na cātra saṃśayaḥ kāryo vismayo vā mune prabhau |
śivādbrahmā ca viṣṇuśca laiṃge jātau nirūpitau ||9||
skānde vidhāturnetrācca śivotpattirnirūpitā |
viṣṇuḥ śivaṃ dhyāyati ca śivoʼpi viṣṇumeva ca ||10||
paṃcākṣaraṃ samantraṃ ca tārakaṃ vadati prabhuḥ |
śatakoṭimitaṃ rāmacaritaṃ ca tridhākṛtam ||11||
martyaloke tārakaṃ sa sthāpayāmāsa śaṃkaraḥ |
kvacicchivātsṛṣṭiruktā brahmaṇaśca kvaciddhareḥ ||12||
bhagavatyāḥ kvacitproktā kvacitsūryādgajānanāt |
kvacicca brahmaṇaḥ proktā sarvaṃ tacchāstrasaṃmatam ||13||
saṃśayaṃ kurute yastu sa yāti narakaṃ dhruvam |
ka uvāca||
evaṃ tretāyugakathā proktā sarvā mune tava ||14||
idānīmabhidhāsyāni kathāṃ kaliyugāśritām |
kaliprānte mune lokā bhraṣṭācārāstathānṛtāḥ ||15||
brāhmaṇā vedarahitāḥ snānasandhyāvivarjitāḥ |
yajanaṃ yājanaṃ dānaṃ na kariṣyanti karhicit ||16||
pratigrahaṃ ca duṇṭānāṃ kariṣyanti sudurlabham |
parāpavādaṃ tannindāṃ parasrīdharṣaṇaṃ tathā ||17||
kariṣyanti janāḥ sarve vidhilopaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
viśvāsaghātaṃ sarvatra kurvanti ca janā dhruvam ||18||
vidhilope ca śapathaṃ kariṣyanti dharātale |
na kariṣyanti meghāśca vṛṣṭiṃ kvāpi munīśvara ||19||
mahānadītīradeśe kariṣyanti kṛṣiṃ janāḥ |
yo bali sopyabalino hariṣyanti dhanaṃ balāt ||20||
hariṣyatastato dvau ca trayastābhyāṃ balātpunaḥ |
śūdrā vedaṃ paṭhiṣyanti brāhmaṇāḥ śūdrakarmiṇaḥ ||21||
kṣatriyā vaiśyakarmāṇo vaiśyāśca śūdrakarmiṇaḥ |
pratigrahaṃ kariṣyanti cāṇḍālasya dvijātayaḥ ||22||
daridrāśca bhaviṣyanti hāhābhūtā vicetasaḥ |
vadiṣyanti ca na mayā gṛhītaṃ dhanamaṇvapi ||23||
gṛhītvāpi ca kṛtyāya yācitāro dhanaṃ parāt |
utkocaṃ ca gṛhītvaiva vṛthā sākṣyaṃ bruvanti ca ||24||
satāṃ nindā kariṣyanti maitrīṃ cāsadbhireva ca |
vṛthā māṃsasya bhakṣyaṃ ca kariṣyanti dvijātayaḥ ||25||
ucchedaḥ sajjanānāṃ syāditareṣāṃ ca vaibhavam |
hitvā devānbhaviṣyanti janāḥ sarve tathaindrikān ||26||
bhūtapretapiśācānāṃ bhajane sakta mānasāḥ |
veṣānnānāvidhānkṛtvā bhakṣayiṣyanti vāḍavāḥ ||27||
kecicca kṣatriyā bhikṣāṃ cariṣyantyakulocitām |
vratāni nirmamāṃścāpi nācariṣyanti karhicit ||28||
varṇasaṃkarakārīṇi kartā karmāṇi bhūjanaḥ |
pativratā bhraṣṭavratā bhaviṣyanti kalau yuge ||29||
mlecchaprāyāḥ sarvalokāḥ paradravyāpahāriṇaḥ |
nirdayāḥ kupathāsaktāḥ sarvadā satyavarjitāḥ ||30||
dharitrī sasyahīnā ca rasahīnā mahīruhāḥ |
paṃcame vātha ṣaṣṭhe vā varṣe kanyāṃ prasūyate ||31||
ṣoḍaśābdaparaṃ cāyurbhavipyati tadā yuge |
tīrthāni guptarūpāṇi devatāyatanāni ca ||32||
evaṃ pāpe pravṛddhe tu dharme viśvārthamātrakaḥ |
bhaviṣyanti tadā devā upavāsaparāyaṇāḥ ||33||
svadhāsvāhāvaṣaṭkārairvajitābhayavihvalāḥ |
yāsyanti śaraṇaṃ devaṃ gajānanamanāmayam ||34||
stutiṃ nānāvidhāṃ tasya kariṣyanti natiṃ bahu |
prārthayiṣyanti deveśaṃ sarvavighnavināśanam ||35||
evaṃ sarvaṃ vicāryāsau āvirbhāvaṃ kariṣyati |
gajānanaḥ śūrpakarṇo dhūmravarṇoʼpi nāmataḥ ||36||
nīlamaśvaṃ samāruhya khaḍgahasto ruṣā jvalan |
kalpayiṣyati senāṃ sa nānārūpāṃ nijecchayā ||37||
śastrāṇi ca mahāstrāṇi kalpayiṣyatyayatnataḥ |
vadhiṣyati mahāmlechānsenayā svena tejasā ||38||
mlecchabhūtāṃstathā lokānvadhiṣyati tadāʼkhilān |
līlānkandaradeśeṣu brāhmaṇānvanyabhakṣiṇaḥ ||39||
samānāyya prapūjyaitānbhūmiṃ dāsyati devarāṭ |
satkarmakāriṇaḥ sarvānvicālyati ca sajjanān ||40||
tadā dharmamayaṃ viśvaṃ bhaviṣyati kṛte yuge |
evaṃ kṛtvā dhūmravarṇastatontardhānameṣyati ||41||
evaṃ caturyuge vyāsa vighneśasya mahātmanaḥ |
kathitāni ca nāmāni rūpāṇi ca tavānagha ||42||
karmāṇyadbhutarūpāṇi tasya coktāni sarvaśaḥ |
yeṣāṃ śravaṇamātreṇa mucyante sarvajantavaḥ ||43||
ananyasyāṇi rūpāṇi na vaktuṃ śaktirasti me |
yatra vedāśca catvāro vikuṇṭhāstatra kā kathā ||44||
mama vā madvidhasyāpi mahimnastasya varṇane |
anuṣṭhānāya gaccha tvaṃ vyavasāyaṃ karomyaham ||45||
bhṛguruvāca |
evaṃ śrutvā somakānta vyāso brahmamukhātkathām |
atiprītamanāḥ prāha sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm ||46||
muniruvāca |
na tṛptimadhigacchāmi, śrutvākhyānaṃ mahādbhutam |
dhanyoʼsmyanugṛhītoʼsmi yattvayā sarvasaṃśayāḥ ||47||
dūrīkṛtāḥ kathāsaṃgādgaṇeśacaritā śrayāt |
amṛtasyeva me tṛptiḥ śrutvā śrutvā na jāyate ||48||
asya śravaṇamātreṇa janmajanmāntarārjitam |
vṛjinaṃ nāśamāyāti sarve kāmā bhavanti ca ||49||
pūrvapuṇyabalādasya śravaṇaṃ jāyate nṛṇām |
na śrāvyaṃ durjanāyedaṃ purāṇaṃ sarvasiddhidam ||50||
evamuktvā namaskṛtya parikramya svayaṃ bhuvam |
gṛhītvājñāṃ yayau vyāsastapase vanamuttamam ||51||
yatra kandaphalānyāsannuttamāni jalāni ca |
na vāyuḥ prabalo yatra nātapo nāgnireva ca ||52||
tatropaviṣṭaikaniṣṭho japannekākṣaraṃ manum |
vyatīyurddhādaśābdāni munestasya tapasyataḥ ||
tataḥ prādurabhūttasya puratastu gajānanaḥ ||53||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe munivisarjanaṃ nāmaikonapaṃcāśaduttaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||149||

<adhyāya 150 prāraṃbhaḥ>

somakānta uvāca |
kathaṃ prādurabhūddevaḥ purastasya mahātmanaḥ |
bhṛgo tatsarvamākhyāhi śrutvā pāpakṣayo yataḥ ||1||
bhṛguruvāca |
yathā tatpurataḥ prādurāsīddevo gajānanaḥ |
tatsarvaṃ kathayiṣyeʼhaṃ sādaraṃ śṛṇu bhūmipa ||2||
raktamālyāmbaradharo raktagandhānulepanaḥ |
gajānanoʼbjanayanaḥ sindūrāruṇamastakaḥ ||3||
anekasūryasaṃkāśo divyakuṇḍalamaṇḍitaḥ |
aṃgade mukuṭaṃ bibhratkaṃkaṇe śeṣameva ca ||4||
muniritthaṃ nirīkṣyainaṃ nimīlya nayane punaḥ |
cakampe bhayasaṃtrastaḥ smṛtvā mantraṃ mumūrcha ca ||5||
tatoʼbravīnnāgamukho mā bhaistvaṃ munisattama |
yaṃ dhyāyasi divārātrau soʼhaṃ tvāṃ samupāgataḥ ||6||
brahmādīnāmagamyo yo varaṃ dātuṃ mune tava |
bhṛguruvāca |
śrutvetthaṃ madhurāṃ vāṇīṃ jaharṣa munipuṃgavaḥ ||7||
mūrdhānaṃ nyasya tatpāde dhanyoʼhaṃ pitarau tapaḥ |
dhanyoʼhaṃ dharaṇī vṛkṣāḥ punaḥ punaravīvadat ||8||
yato dṛṣṭaḥ sarvarūpo guṇātītoʼkhilāśrayaḥ |
cidānandaghanoʼnantoʼkhilakāraṇakāraṇam ||9||
evaṃ stutvā prārthayansa varadaṃ taṃ gajānanam |
muniruvāca |
nirasya bhrāntiṃ sarvā me yaccha bhaktiṃ dṛḍhāṃ tvayi ||10||
aṣṭādaśapurāṇānāṃ yathā syāṃ karaṇe kṣamaḥ |
tavāpi stavane śaktiḥ syādyathā tatkuruṣva bhoḥ ||11||
hṛdaye vasa deveśa prasannaścenmamānagha |
gajānana uvāca |
tvayā yatprārthitaṃ vatsa sarvamadya bhaviṣyati ||12||
sarvamānyo vyāsa nāmā sarvapāvanapāvanaḥ |
aparokṣajñānavāṃśca bhaviṣyasi gurorguruḥ ||13||
nārāyaṇaparaḥ khyātyā guṇaiḥ kīrtyā śriyāpi ca |
aṣṭādaśapurāṇānāṃ kartā tvaṃ hi bhaviṣyasi ||14||
tathaivopapurāṇānāṃ tāvatāṃ ca munīśvara |
tvayā pūrvaṃ dambhavaśānna kṛtaṃ mama pūjanam ||15||
smaraṇaṃ vā tataḥ stambho jāto vāṇyā mahādbhutaḥ |
dhāturmukhācchruto yasmānmahimā tena meʼnagha ||16||
vilīnaṃ sarvamadhunā praviśāmi tavodaram |
bhṛguruvāca |
evamuktvā prāviśatsa tanmunerudaraṃ vibhuḥ ||17||
prakāśamatulaṃ lebhe sūryakoṭisamaṃ muniḥ |
dīpayāmāsa kakubhastato mūrtiṃ vidhāya saḥ ||18||
gajānanasya mahatīṃ prāsādeʼsthāpayacca saḥ |
pūjayāmāsa vividhairupacāraiḥ pṛthakpṛthak ||19||
siddhikṣetramidaṃ sarvalokānāṃ pāvanaṃ dṛśoḥ |
anuṣṭhānavatāṃ mantranānāsiddhipradaṃ śubham ||20||
evamuktvāpyanujñāpya vyāso nārāyaṇastadā |
purāṇaṃ tadgaṇeśasya yacchrutaṃ brahmaṇomukhāt ||21||
prāha vaiśampāyanāya tatastadbhuvi paprathe |
tadetadakhilaṃ teʼdya mayāpi kathitaṃ nṛpa ||22||
nānena sadṛśaṃ kiṃcitpāvanaṃ triṣu vidyate |
lokeṣu paramānandadāyakaṃ nṛpasattama ||23||
somakānta na cākhyemamidaṃ duṣṭajane tvayā |
vinīteṣu ca bhakteṣu tvayākhyeyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||24||
mayā nirantaramidaṃ japyate smaryateʼpi ca |
tavāpi karuṇāyogātkathitaṃ rājasattama ||25||
gajānanadhyānaparo nityaṃ japaparobhava ||26||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe vyāsānugrahasiddhikṣetravarṇanaṃ nāma paṃcāśaduttaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||150||

<adhyāya 151 prāraṃbhaḥ>

sūta uvāca |
evaṃ śrutvā kathāṃ nityaṃ somakānto munīśvarāt |
śuṣkāstramūle tatpuṇyaṃ nyakṣipajjalato nṛpaḥ ||1||
gate saṃvatsare cūtaḥ pallavaiḥ kusumaiḥ phalaiḥ |
śuśubheʼtīvaruciraḥ purāṇaśrutipuṇyataḥ ||2||
somakānto divyakāntistyaktvā kuṣṭhabhavāṃ rujam |
akṣataścābhavatpūtigandhaṃ tyaktvā sugandhavān ||3||
niḥkhedaḥ surabhergandhāddaśadikṣu prasāriṇaḥ |
divyadehaṃ sūryakāntiṃ koṭicandranibhaṃ nṛpam ||4||
dṛṣṭvā visismire lokāḥ kathamīdṛk ca tādṛśaḥ |
gaṇeśākhyapurāṇasya mahimnā doṣahāriṇā ||5||
bhṛguruvāca |
siddhiste nṛpaśārdūla purāṇaśravaṇādabhūt |
vrajedānīṃ nijaṃ putra sāmātyaṃ janameva ca ||6||
cirotkaṇṭhaṃ varṣamadhye dṛṣṭvā harṣamavāpsyasi |
sūta uvāca |
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ pādayornyapatanmuneḥ ||7||
ānandārṇavamagnaḥ sa somakāntoʼvadadbhṛgum |
rājovāca |
adbhutastapasasteʼdya mahimaikṣi mayā mune ||8||
purāṇaśravaṇasyāpi yenāhaṃ patitoʼpi ca |
galatkuṣṭho divyadeho jāto mātā pitā mama ||9||
asyadehasya nirmātā tvameva munisattama |
nīrasaścūtavṛkṣoʼpi phalapuṣpayutoʼbhavat ||10||
tyaktuṃ necchāmyatoʼhaṃ tvāṃ kiṃ me putrajanairbhavet |
evaṃ niśamya tadvākyaṃ bhṛguḥ punarathābravīt ||11||
bhṛguruvāca |
janmāntarīyapuṇyena purāṇaśravaṇaṃ tava |
saṃjātaṃ manmukhāddhanyaṃ duritaṃ yena teʼnaśat ||12||
gaṇeśasya purāṇasya mahimā kena varṇyate |
na māvismara gaccha tvaṃ janeśa nagaraṃ nijam ||13||
evaṃ tayoḥ saṃvadatostejasojvaladadbhutam |
vimānaṃ dadṛśe tābhyāṃ sūryakoṭinibhaṃ mahat ||14||
bhṛguruvāca |
tvadarthaṃ yānamāyātamidaṃ nṛpa mahādbhutam |
idamāruhaya gaccha tvaṃ gaṇanāthasya sannidhim ||15||
na prāpnuvantyanuṣṭhānādvimānaṃ yanmunīśvarāḥ |
tatpurāṇaśravādrājaṃstvayā prāptaṃ gaṇeśvarāt ||16||
vimānaṃ vadatorevaṃ bhūmaṇḍalamagātkṣaṇāt |
vināyakagaṇairyuktaṃ caturbāhuvirājitaiḥ ||17||
mukuṭāṃgadahārādi bhūṣaṇairupaśobhitaiḥ |
divyacandanavastrādipadmaparaśuśobhibhiḥ ||18||
nṛtyatkinnarakanyābhirmṛdaṃganālapāṇibhiḥ |
kinnarairgāyanairyuktaṃ dṛṣṭvā rājāʼbravītpunaḥ ||19||
rājovāca |
śrutāsīdbahudhā vārtā vimānānāṃ munīśvara |
taddṛṣṭaṃ purato brahmaṃstapovīryabalāttava ||20||
mahimā śrūyate karṇairbahudhā bahuvaktrataḥ |
anubhūto mayā soʼpi purāṇaśravaṇāttava ||21||
evaṃ bruvati bhūpe tu dūtā uttīrya yānataḥ |
praṇipatyākhilāḥ procuḥ somakāntaṃ nṛpaṃ tadā ||22||
parātpareṇa devena tava puṇyātsmṛtiḥ kṛtā |
gajānanena śuddhātmanpurāṇaśravaṇādinā ||23||
vayaṃ dūtā ājñayāʼsya vimānena samāgatāḥ |
netuṃ tvāṃ puṇyakīrtiṃ ca yāhi lokamanuttamam ||24||
taddarśanājjanmamṛtermuktastatsannidhiṃ gataḥ |
sukhamātyantikaṃ tatra prāpsyase tatprasādataḥ ||25||
niśamya dūtavākyaṃ taddehātīta ivābhavat |
romāṃcāṃcitasarvāṃga prāha gadgadayā girā ||26||
parātpareṇa devena carācarakṛtāʼmunā |
nirguṇena guṇakṣobhakāriṇā viśvayoninā ||27||
dīnanāthena bho dūtāḥ ||smṛtoʼhaṃ kṛpayāʼnaghāḥ |
brahmādyā na viduryaṃ hi dhyātvā ca sanakādayaḥ ||28||
anantagaṇapūrṇaḥ soʼpreṣayadyānamuttamam |
nānāvatāravānnānārūpī paramamāyikaḥ ||29||
tena me smaraṇaṃ kasmātkṛtamāścaryakārakama |
evamuktvā namaskṛtya bhṛguṃ taṃ prāha bhūmipaḥ ||30||
ājñayā te gamiṣyāmi dūtaiḥ saha vimānagaḥ |
prasādātte ca devasya na māṃ vismartumarhasi ||31||
sūta uvāca |
niśamya gaditaṃ tasya bhṛguḥ saṃbhramavihvalaḥ |
ānandāśruḥ saromāṃcaḥ kaṇṭhe dhṛtvā nṛpasya saḥ ||32||
prāhāhaṃ nṛpate viproʼbhāgyavāṃstvaṃ mahāphalaḥ |
gaṇeśapurago bhūtvā na māṃ vismara karhicit ||33||
evamukatvā bahiryātau dhṛtvā hastāvubhāvapi |
namaskṛtya vimānaṃ tannṛpo munivaraṃ ca tam ||34||
āruhatparamaprīto dūtavākyāttvarānvitaḥ |
dvāvamātyau sudharmā ca ruruhuryānamuttamam ||35||
tato dūtāśca vādyānāṃ ghoṣairvyāptaṃ digantaram |
vimānaśobhāṃ vīkṣyaiva somakānto nananda ha ||36||
janmāntarīyapuṇyena prāptametadamanyata |
tatoʼsminpaśyati bhṛgau vimānaṃ yānamambare ||37||
sarvottamapadaṃ prāptaṃ martyadehena vīkṣya ca |
vismayaṃ paramaṃ prāpa natvā dūtānyayau padaṃ ||38||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe somakāntavimānaprāptivarṇanaṃ nāmaikapaṃcāśadadhikaśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||151||

<adhyāya 152 prāraṃbhaḥ>

sūta uvāca |
upariṣṭāddadarśātha nṛpo daivapuraṃ nijam |
jaharṣa gopurādālaśobhāṃ dṛṣṭvātha kāñcanīm ||1||
tataḥ sudharmā sasmāra sabhāṃ ramyāṃ samīkṣya ha |
nijaṃ putraṃ snehavaśātprāha gadgadabhāṣiṇī ||2||
hemakaṇṭho mama suto bhadrāsanagato bhavet |
pitrormārgaṃ pratīkṣansa jāte saṃvatsareʼdhunā ||3||
svāminkṛpānidhe tasya darśanaṃ dātumarhasi |
striyo vākyaṃ niśamyaivaṃ śuśoca nṛpatistadā ||4||
nṛpa uvāca |
hemakaṇṭho mama suto jīvansyādvā mṛto bhavet |
uktamāsīnmayā cāsmai vatsarāddarśanaṃ bhavet ||5||
anṛtātkutra gaccheyaṃ noktamāsītkadācana |
na tatra gantuṃ no hātuṃ śaktoʼhaṃ paramaṃ padam ||6||
viruddhaṃ kathametaddhi ghaṭetetyarudadbhṛśam |
dṛṣṭvā dūtāstayoḥ śokamabruvanrājasattamam ||7||
yuvayo rodanaṃ śrutvā kṛpā naḥ samupāgatā |
kṣaṇamuttārayāmoʼtra dṛṣṭvā taṃ yāta satvaram ||8||
jāte bhavatsamādhāne saṃtoṣo naḥ paro bhavet |
tata uttārayāmāsurdūtā devapurādudak ||9||
vimānaṃ tadgaṇeśasya purāṇaśravaṇāgatam |
nagaraṃ prabhayā vyāptaṃ vādyaghoṣaiśca nāditam ||10||
subalo jñānagamyaśca namaskṛtya nṛpaṃ gaṇān |
īyaturhemakaṇṭhaṃ taṃ sarvavṛttāntamīrituma ||11||
kṣaṇādbhadrāsanagataṃ hemakaṇṭhamapaśyatām |
sannaddhairvīramukhyaiśca parivītaṃ mahābalaiḥ ||12||
amātyanāgarairyuktamīkṣantaṃ nṛtyamuttamam |
tadamātyo jñānagamyaṃ subalaṃ samapaśyatām ||13||
uttasthuḥ sarvavīrāśva sāmātyo hemakaṇṭhakaḥ |
āliṃganāya te sarve tayoḥ prāpuḥ sasaṃbhramama ||14||
teṣāmāliṃgane jāte tato nṛpasuto yayau |
harṣeṇa mahatā yuktaḥ saromāṃcoʼbravīcca tau ||15||
mama mātā pitāʼmātyau kṣemayuktau na vā kvacit |
tyaktvā tau pitarau yāto yuvāmekākinau katham ||16||
evamuktvā svāsane tāvupaveśya prapūjya ca |
vastracandanabhūṣādiphalatāmbūlakāṃcanaiḥ ||17||
tayorvinā mama prāṇāḥ kaṇṭhamātrāvalambinaḥ |
aharniśaṃ tayordhyānaṃ nānyasya mama mānase ||18||
pitā me prāvadatpūrvamekadā darśanaṃ punaḥ |
dāsyāmīti kathaṃ taccānṛtaṃ vākyaṃ kariṣyati ||19||
tāvūcatuḥ |
mā kuruṣva vṛthā cintāṃ pitroste kuśalaṃ nṛpa |
tayoḥ puṇyaprabhāvaṃ ko jānāti bhuvanatraye ||20||
anujñāṃ bhavato gṛhaya nirgatā nagarādvayam |
catvāroʼpi pitusteʼbhūtsaukumāryādikaṃ mahat ||21||
kṣudhayā pīḍitoʼtyantaṃ raktasrāvi padāmbujam |
kandamūlaiḥ phalaistṛptirna cāsīttasya bhūpateḥ ||22||
mātusteʼpi tathāvasthā celaturna padaṃ tadā |
bhramadbhirbahudhā paścāddṛṣṭhaṃ cāru saro mahat ||23||
śītalaṃ vṛkṣavallībhistatra suṣvāpa bhūmipaḥ |
sudharmā pādasaṃvāhaṃ cakāra śramakarṣitā ||24||
āvāṃ tu kandamūlārthe yātastatrāyayau muniḥ |
cyavano muniśārdūlo jalārthe tau dadarśa ha ||25||
tayorhārdaṃ sa gṛhyaiva nijamāśramamāyayau |
tata āvāṃ samāyātau gṛhya mūlaphalaṃ bahu ||26||
sarve tadāśrame yātā muninā pūjitā bhṛśam |
bhojitāḥ ṣaḍrasaiścānnairviśrāntiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ ||27||
tataḥkathāprasaṃgoʼbhūnmuninā ca nṛpeṇa ca |
sarvaṃ cākathayadrājā vṛttāntaṃ munaye tadā ||28||
tato munirdhyānabalātpūrva janmāsya saṃjagau |
duritaṃ tacchamopāyamabravītkaruṇāyutaḥ ||29||
duritaśravaṇe cāsya saṃtrāso nau nṛpābhavat |
sa saṃdigdhamanā yāvattāvaddehādvinirgatāḥ ||30||
pakṣiṇaḥ śvetavarṇāstamārabdhā bhakṣituṃ nṛpam |
teṣāṃ caṃcuprahāreṇa vihvalaḥ patito nṛpaḥ ||31||
rakṣa rakṣeti kāruṇyātproktaḥ prārtanayā muniḥ |
tasya prekṣaṇamātreṇa pakṣiṇoʼntardadhuḥ kṣaṇāt ||32||
sa tu hastayugaṃ baddhvā tasthau munimukho nṛpaḥ |
mahajjñātvā nṛpainaḥ sa śrāvayāmāsa bhaktitaḥ ||33||
gaṇeśasya purāṇaṃ yadvarṣamātraṃ kṛpānidhiḥ |
aṣṭottaraśatenādau devanāmnā prasicya ca ||34||
tadaṃgātpuruṣaṃ ghoraṃ niṣkāśyatapaso balāt |
vyomakeśo lalajjihvo vyākulaḥ kṣudhayā bhṛśam ||35||
bhakṣyaṃ yayāce sa bhṛguṃ soʼvadattaṃ purasthitam |
bhakṣayasvāmravṛkṣaṃ tvaṃ mahāntaṃ śuṣkameva ca ||36||
