tena spṛṣṭo āmravṛkṣo bhasmasādabhavatkṣaṇāt |
tatastaṃ dhīvaraṃ prāha bhakṣayasvetyasau muniḥ ||37||
tato nṛpaṃ muniḥ prāha purāṇaśravaṇaṃ mahat |
śreyo nikṣipa nityaṃ tvamasminbhasmani bhūmipa ||38||
yāvaccūtasya vṛkṣoʼtra bhavedanyo yathā purā |
tatastvamapi rājendra divyadeho bhaviṣyasi ||39||
tathaiva sa nṛpaścakre snātvā śrutvā kathāṃ bahu |
nyakṣipattyahaṃ śreyastasminmasmāni bhaktitaḥ ||40||
saṃvatsarānte vṛkṣoʼsau bhavannṛpa yathā purā |
gaṇeśasya purāṇe tu samāpte phalapuṣpavān ||41||
nṛpoʼpi divyakāntiḥ sa śaśisūryanibhoʼbhavat |
yāvannṛpo muniṃ stauti vimānaṃ tāvadāgatam ||42||
nṛtyagītasamāyuktaṃ vādyaghoṣanināditam |
gaṇeśadūtasaṃyuktamāśramāntikamāsthitam ||43||
yacchobhāṃ paśyatornātha dṛśo nau saphalābhavan |
atipuṇyātpituste te dūtā devājñayā nṛpam ||44||
nyakṣipanyānamadhye nau nṛpekṣaṇapraṇoditāḥ |
sudharmā ca munerājñāṃ gṛhya yātā vimānagā ||45||
dṛṣṭaṃ devapuraṃ yāvattāvatvatsmṛtirāgatā |
ata uttāritaṃ yānaṃ uttare nagarānnṛpa ||46||
didṛkṣayā pitṛbhyāṃ te tau yātau tvāṃ niveditum |
tayoḥ śīghraṃ darśanāya yāhi no cedgamiṣyati ||47||
tayorvākyaṃ niśamyetthaṃ hemakaṇṭhatvarānvitaḥ |
romāṃcāśrusamāyukto dhāvamānaḥ śrutākhilaḥ ||48||
yayau taddarśanotsukyātpuraskṛtya tadā tu tau |
viśīrṇabhūṣaṇagaṇo nāgaraiḥ sevakairvṛtaḥ ||49||
galadaśruḥ kṣaṇātprāpa vimānaṃ gaṇasaṃkulam ||50||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe hemakaṇṭhadarśanaṃ nāma dvipaṃcāśaduttaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||152||

<adhyāya 153 prāraṃbhaḥ>

sūta uvāca |
tadamātyāśca catvāraḥ somakāntaṃ puro yayuḥ |
praṇipatyākhilānprocurāgataste suto nṛpa ||1||
sarvavīrairnāgarikairindraḥ suragaṇairiva |
vṛto harṣeṇa śokena dṛṣṭvānyānameva ca ||2||
evaṃ niveditoʼmātyaiḥ soʼpi śīghraṃ samāgataḥ |
ābālavanitāvṛddhaiḥ sevakaiśca samanvitaḥ ||3||
dṛṣṭvā śobhāṃ vimānasya sarva ānandanirbharāḥ |
tanmadhye somakāntaṃ te dṛṣṭvā nemurdharātale ||4||
yuktaṃ dūtairgaṇeśasya kāntayāpyatikāntayā |
tata uttīrya yānātsa āliliṃga sutaṃ mudā ||5||
ānandāśrūṇi muṃcaṃtau saromāṃcāvubhāvapi |
videhāviva saṃjātau na kiṃcidūcatuḥ kṣaṇam ||6||
tata ūce pitā putraṃ snehena vatsarāgatam |
tvāmeva nityaṃ dhyāyāmi satputraṃ vaṃśabhūṣaṇam |
na tvayā sadṛśaṃ paśye putreṣurjanavartiṣu |
tataḥ sudharmā sasnehā dhāvamānā sutaṃ yayau |
āliliṃga mudā yuktā galadaśrū ubhāvapi |
tata ūce sutaṃ mātā ciradṛṣṭoʼsi bālaka |
na me sukhaṃ kiṃcidabhūttādutkaṇṭhitacetasaḥ |
idānīṃ te mukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā sānandaṃ me manoʼbhavat |
viyogena ca te deho mama jātaḥ kṛśo bhṛśam |
tata āliṃganaṃ cakrurnāgarā nāmadhārakāḥ |
kecitpradakṣiṇāṃ cakruḥ kecitpādau tadāʼspṛśan |
kecicca darśanādeva pratiyātāḥ puraṃ prati |
tata ūce nṛpaḥ sarvānbhṛguṇāʼnugrahānmama |
gaṇeśasya purāṇaṃ yacchrāvitaṃ pāpanāśanam ||7||
abhavaṃ tena niṣpāpo divyadeho vimānagaḥ |
sarveṣāṃ darśanaṃ jātaṃ punaryāsyāmi yānagaḥ ||8||
kṛpāvatā gaṇeśena yānaṃ me preṣitaṃ bhuvi |
rājyaṃ ca paribhuktaṃ me svecchayā saṃpadaḥ suta ||9||
dhāma yāsyāmi paramaṃ prasādādbhṛgusaṃbhavāt |
evaṃ te śuśruvurvāṇī somakāntasamīritām ||10||
ruruduḥ susvaraṃ sarve patitā bhuvi kecana |
śreṇīmukhyāstataḥ procuḥ somakāntaṃ kṛpānidhim ||11||
no vinā jagatīpāla kathaṃ yāsyasi dhāma tat |
prāṇāṃstyaktvā vayaṃ sarve yāsyāmoʼpyanu te nṛpa ||12||
hatyā tvanmastake rājansarveṣāṃ no bhaviṣyati |
nanaḥ puṇyaṃ tathā yena drakṣyāmastaṃ guṇeśvaram ||13||
tava prasādāddevasya yāsyāmo lokamuttamam |
na saṃsāre sukhaṃ kiṃcidāyunāśo vṛthāʼtra hi ||14||
asmākaṃ na bhavetkiṃcitsādhanaṃ dhāmasādhanam |
tatoʼvadaddhemakaṇṭho janakaṃ vākyamādṛtaḥ ||15||
kathaṃ tyaktvāʼrbhakaṃ tāta yāsi draṣṭuṃ gajānanam |
mama rājyena ko heturājñayā tava vatsaram ||16||
pālitaṃ rājyamakhilaṃ necchedānīṃ mamāpi ca |
śapathasta tathā rājannato netuṃ mamārhasi ||17||
somakānta uvāca |
sarveṣāmeva saṃdraṣṭumicchā devapadāmbujam |
ahaṃ ca paravānatra kathaṃ kāryaṃ mayā janāḥ ||18||
bhavatsnehena śocāmi putrasnehādviśeṣataḥ |
ataएvordhvagaṃ yānamuttārya draṣṭumāgataḥ ||19||
sūta uvāca |
tataḥ śocatsu lokeṣu somakānte rudatyapi |
putre ca devadūtānāṃ dayā prādurabhūttadā ||20||
te ūcuḥ somakāntaṃ taṃ dhanyo rājanyato janāḥ |
tvayi saṃkalpitaprāṇā yathā tvaṃ jagadīśvare ||21||
ataḥ sarvāngṛhītvaiva yāmaḥ śīghraṃ gajānanam |
rājovāca |
yadi sarvā purī nītā tadā kīrtirdṛḍhā mama ||22||
bhaviṣyati rasāyāṃ me nānyathā tu bhaviṣyati |
sūta uvāca |
evamuktvā purāṇasya gaṇeśasya śravodbhavam ||23||
pūjyaṃ haste jale naiṣāṃ dadau rājā tadājñayā |
toye haste gate lokā niṣpāpāḥ puṇyamūrtayaḥ ||24||
ābālavanitāvṛddhā ruruhuryānamuttamam |
gaṇeśasya tu ye dūtāstatoʼgurūrdhvamārgataḥ ||25||
tataḥ kecinnarāḥ pāpā yairnāpi ca jalaṃ kare |
te punarnagare yātāḥ śaśaṃsuḥ śeṣitaṃ janam ||26||
tato dadṛśire sarve urdhvagaṃ nagaraṃ bahu |
ūcuḥ parasparaṃ śeṣā dravyalābhena toṣiṇaḥ ||27||
nānāvyavasāyaratā babhūvurhaṣṭamānasāḥ |
kāṃścitpalāyanaparāndūtā daṇḍaprahāriṇaḥ ||28||
balādgṛhītvā prākṣipanyānamadhyetikautukāt |
nānāmiṣeṇa jagmurye tāṃstathaiva nicikṣipuḥ ||29||
upaviṣṭā madhyadeśe rājapatnīsutādayaḥ |
amātyāḥ parito lokā nāgarā viviśuḥ sukham ||30||
mayūreśamayūreśa jaya tvamiti ghoṣiṇaḥ |
vādyatsu devavādyeṣu nṛtyatyapsarasāṃ gaṇe ||31||
pratinādena garjanti maṇḍalāni diśāmapi |
ativegena te prāptā gaṇeśasya padaṃ śubham ||32||
suṣamāṃ paripaśyanto vismayenābruvanniti |
aho mahatpuṇyamasya yena dṛṣṭo gajānanaḥ ||33||
samīpatāṃ yayuḥ sarve rājā sāyujyamāpa ca |
sūta uvāca |
evaṃ vaḥ kathitā viprā gaṇeśasya purāṇanā ||34||
kathā sarvajanasyāpi śravaṇātpāpanāśani |
śṛṇudhvaṃ devadūtebhyo vimāne pṛṣṭavānnṛpaḥ |
tatsarvaṃ bhavatāṃ viprāḥ kathayāmi kathānakam ||35||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe somakāntasya devapadaprāptivarṇanaṃ nāma tripaṃcāśaduttaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||153||

<adhyāya 154 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ṛṣaya ūcuḥ |
kiṃ pṛṣṭaṃ somakāntena vimānavartināʼnagha |
tadvayaṃ śrotumicchāmo vada sarvamaśeṣataḥ ||1||
sūta uvāca |
gacchatā vyomamārgeṇa pṛṣṭā dūtā mahātmanā |
vārāṇasīsthitānāṃ tu gaṇeśānāṃ mahātmanām ||2||
nāmāni parivārāṇāṃ tāni naḥ śṛṇutānaghāḥ |
rājovāca |
dūtā vadantu kṛpayā viśveśaparivāragān ||3||
gaṇeśānmama kārtsnyena smaraṇātsarvasiddhidān |
dūtā ūcuḥ |
kathayāmaḥ samāsena viśveśāvaraṇe gatān ||4||
gaṇeśānkramaśo rājañśṛṇu sarvabhayāpahān |
durgā vināyakoʼthārka bhīmacaṇḍī vināyakaḥ ||5||
dehalīgaṇapaścātha tathoddaṇḍavināyakaḥ |
pāśapāṇiḥ sarvavighna haraṇoʼtha vināyakaḥ ||6||
prathamāvaraṇe siddhasiddhirūpo vināyakaḥ |
lambodaraḥ kūṭadantaḥ śūlaṭaṃka vināyakaḥ ||7||
kūṣmāṇḍākhyaścaturthaśca paṃcamo muṇḍasaṃjñakaḥ |
vikaṭadvijasaṃjñastu rājaputravināyakaḥ ||8||
praṇavākhyoʼthāparaśca dvitīyāvaraṇe sthitāḥ |
vakratuṇḍa ekadantatrimukhaśca vināyakaḥ ||9||
paṃcāsyaścāparaścātra herambaśca vināyakaḥ ||10||
modakapriya ityeva tṛtīyāvaraṇe sthitāḥ |
vināyakoʼbhayapradaḥ siṃhatuṇḍavināyakaḥ ||11||
kuṇitākṣaścāparaśca kṣipraprasādasaṃjñakaḥ |
cintāmaṇiriti khyāto dantahastavināyakaḥ ||12||
pracaṃḍaścāpyaparaścoddaṇḍamuṇḍavināyakau |
caturthāvaraṇe jñeyā aṣṭāvete vināyakāḥ ||13||
sthūladanto dvitīyastu kalipriyavināyakaḥ |
caturdanto dvituṇḍākhyo jyeṣṭho gajavināyakaḥ ||14||
kālākhyaśca tathā jñeyo mārgeśālayoparoʼpi ca |
paṃcamāvaraṇe jñeyā aṣṭāvete vināyakāḥ ||15||
maṇikarṇivināyaka āśāsṛṣṭivināyakaḥ |
yakṣākhyo gajakarṇākhyaścitraghaṇṭavināyakaḥ ||16||
sumaṃgalaśca mitrākhya ete ṣaṣṭhe vināyakāḥ |
modaḥ pramodaḥ sumukho durmukhaśca vināyakaḥ ||17||
gaṇapākhyothāparaśca tathā jñāna vināyakaḥ |
saptamāvaraṇe jñeyā tasthā dvāravināyakāḥ ||18||
avimuktoʼṣṭamo yatra mokṣadoʼtha vināyakaḥ |
anyo bhagīrathākhyo hi hariścandravināyakaḥ ||19||
kapardīti paro jñeyastathā binduvināyakaḥ |
eteṣāṃ smaraṇaṃ nityaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam ||20||
prātarutthāya yaścaitānpaṭhate śuddhamānasaḥ |
na tasya vighnā vighnaṃ hi kurvanti sarvakarmasu ||21||
ekaikanāmnā rājendra dūrvābhistandulairapi |
tilaiḥ śamīdalaiścaitānpūjayedbhaktimānnaraḥ ||22||
asādhyaṃ sādhayetkāryaṃ sarvatra vijayī bhavet |
āyuṣyaṃ puṣṭimārogyaṃ prāpnuyāddhanamuttamam ||23||
idaṃ te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yaddhi pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā nṛpa |
tūṣṇīṃ yayurdevadūtāstato dhāma nijaṃ mudā ||24||
sūta uvāca |
idaṃ tatkathitaṃ viprā viśveśāvaraṇaṃ mayā |
idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi purāṇasya phalaśrutim ||25||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe ṣaṭpaṃcāśadvināyakavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥpaṃcāśaduttaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||154||

<adhyāya 155 prāraṃbhaḥ>

sūta uvāca |
janmamṛtiḥ na ca syātāṃ purāṇasyāsya saṃśrayāt |
ekaśobahuśaścetsyādyadyasya śravaṇaṃ dvijāḥ ||1||
na śaknutāmalaṃ vaktuṃ śeṣapadmāsanāvapi |
yo dadāti kurukṣetre somasūryagrahe naraḥ ||2||
hemabhārasahasrāṇi viprebhyo bhaktibhāvataḥ |
tadasya śravaṇātpuṇyaṃ prāpnuyādbhaktimānnaraḥ ||3||
yajñānāṃ sāṃgajātānāṃ sarveṣāṃ dakṣiṇāvatām |
phalaṃ kastulayāmāsa purāṇaśravaṇasya ca ||4||
purāṇaśraṇasyāsya puṇyaṃ tasmādviśeṣavat |
vratānāṃ jaladānānāṃ tatra kā gaṇanā matā ||5||
koṭikanyāpradānānāṃ godānānāṃ sahasraśaḥ |
tatpuṇyaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ śravaṇādasya jāyate ||6||
caturṇāmapi vedānāṃ sāṃgānāṃ paṭhanātsadā |
śāstrāṇāṃ vyākṛtervāpi sadā satsevanādapi ||7||
tatpuṇyaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ tadasya śravaṇādbhavet |
bhāratasya purāṇasya saṃpūrṇasya śravādapi |
yatpuṇyaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ tadasya śravaṇādbhavet ||8||
yasya gehe gaṇeśasya purāṇaṃ likhitaṃ bhavet |
na tatra rākṣasā bhūtāḥ pretāśca pūtanādayaḥ ||9||
grahā bālagrahā naiva pīḍāṃ kurvanti karhicit |
tadgṛhaṃ hi gaṇeśena rakṣyate sarvadā svayam ||10||
idaṃ purāṇaṃ śṛṇuyātpūjayedvā samāhitaḥ |
tasya darśanataḥ pūtā bhavanti patitā narāḥ ||11||
brahmādīnāṃ munīnāṃ sa mānyo bhavati mānavaḥ |
kruddho dahetsa bhuvanaṃ tuṣṭaścendrapadaṃ dadet ||12||
aṣṭasiddhīḥ samāpnoti śravaṇātpratyahaṃ naraḥ |
na dāridryaṃ samāpnoti na saṃkaṣṭaṃ naraḥ kvacit ||13||
īpsitaṃ samavāpnotī padmādīṃśca nidhīnapi |
kalpadrumaḥ kāmadhenurnidhiścintāmaṇistathā ||14||
tasya vaśyatvamabhyeti soʼpi vandyo bhavatyapi |
jāraṇaṃ māraṇaṃ stambhaṃ uccāṭanamathāpi ca ||15||
asya smaraṇamātreṇa nirastaṃ syātkṣaṇena ha |
gaṇeśanikaṭaṃ sthitvā purāṇaṃ śṛṇuyānnaraḥ ||16||
mahāpāpādvinirmuktaḥ strībālahatyayāpi ca |
sānnidhyamāpnuyādante gaṇeśasya na saṃśayaḥ ||17||
śūdroʼpi madhye saṃsthāpya brāhmaṇānśṛṇuyādidam |
krameṇa labhate varṇānvaiśyakṣatradvijāhyām ||18||
nityanaimittikarmabhyaścyuto yaḥ śṛṇuyādidam |
karmasādguṇyamāpnoti gaṇeśasya prasādataḥ ||19||
bhādre śuklacaturthyāṃ yaḥ kṛtvā mūrtiṃ mahīmayīm |
maṇḍape toraṇe ramye saṃpūjya paramādarāt ||20||
purāṇaṃ śṛṇuyātsadyastasya tuṣṭo vināyakaḥ |
dadāti nikhilānkāmānante mokṣaṃ ca vighnapaḥ ||21||
stotrāṇyatra ca khyā yāvanti tāvanti pratyahaṃ naraḥ |
paṭhate yadi yo bhavatyā sa siddhaḥ syānna saṃśayaḥ ||22||
asādhyamapi yatkiṃcitteṣāṃ pāṭhāllabhennaraḥ |
anuṣṭhānavidhānena māsamātraṃ japettu yaḥ ||23||
brāhmaṇānbhojayedbhaktyā soʼpi tanmayatāmiyāt |
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ |
kathamasya śravātpuṇyaṃ kena prāptaṃ purāʼnadha ||24||
tannaḥ śaṃsa mahābhāga pṛcchatāṃ sūtanandana |
sūta uvāca |
śṛṇvantu munayaḥ pūrvaṃ kaścinmūkoʼbhavanmuniḥ ||25||
brahmalokaṃ gatoʼkasmāllomaśoʼpi yadṛcchayā |
namaskṛtya ca lokeśamupaviṣṭastadājñayā ||26 ||
pūjitaḥ parayā bhaktyā lomaśoʼthābravīdvidhima |
lomaśa uvāca |
vyāsāya kathitaṃ deva purāṇaṃ puṇyavarddhanam ||27||
gaṇeśasya mahābhāga tanme vaktumihārhasi |
brahmovāca |
śṛṇu lomaśa yatnena sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham ||28||
purāṇaṃ hi gaṇeśasya kāmamokṣapradaṃ nṛṇām |
sūta uvāca |
tataḥ sa kathayāmāsa brahmā svīyena yāmataḥ ||29||
lomaśāya gaṇeśasya purāṇaṃ kāmamokṣadam |
mūkena nādalubdhena śrutaṃ bhaktyāʼkhilaṃ tu tat ||30||
tataḥ sa vāgīśvaravatprovāca bhāratīṃ muniḥ |
adhītya pāṭhataḥ kṛtvā śrāvayāmāsa cetarām ||31||
bhuktvā bhogānyathā kāmānputrānpautrānavāpya ca |
ante jagāma paramaṃ dhāma gāṇeśvaraṃ śubham ||32||
punaḥ śṛṇvantu munaya itihāsaṃ purātanam |
ikṣvākukulasaṃbhūto rājā śuddhātmakaḥ śuciḥ ||33||
yajvā dānaprado nityaṃ svādhyāyaparamorihā |
ākhyātā sarvadharmāṇāṃ prajānāṃ pālane rataḥ |
ṣaṣṭhāṃśabhāgī lokānāṃ mānyaḥ priyataropi ca ||34||
vikhyātastriṣu lokeṣa goptā saṃvaraṇābhidhaḥ |
anapatyaḥ sa putrārthe putrīyāmiṣṭimāharat ||35||
sāṃgā sadakṣiṇāmannadānaiḥ suvihitāṃ tataḥ |
na lebhe saṃtatiṃ rājā harivaṃśamathāśṛṇot ||36||
pūjayitvā dvijānante vācakaṃ paritoṣya ca |
vastradhenuhiraṇyādyai ratnamūlaphalādibhiḥ ||37||
tatoʼpi nābhavatputro daivānmūkastu tadgṛham |
samāyātastu vikhyāto gaṇeśākhyapurāṇavita ||38||
sthāpitaṃ prārthayitvā ca rājñā saṃvaraṇena saḥ |
śrutvā purāṇaṃ tadvaktrādgaṇeśākhyaṃ mudā nṛpaḥ ||39||
tadante toṣayāmāsa mūkaṃ taṃ dvijapuṃgavam |
ratnamuktāphalairvastrairbhūṣaṇairhemanirmitaiḥ ||40||
lebhe tataḥ kumāraṃ sa gaṇeśe tatparoʼbhavat |
bhuktvānekasukhānyāpa gāṇeśaṃ padamuttamam ||41||
bhaginī tasya vandhyāsīttriṃśadvarṣā rajasvalā |
yathākālaṃ bhavatyava śrutvā tasyātha saṃtatim ||42||
gaṇeśākhyapurāṇasya śravājjātāṃ ca taṃ munim |
ākāryaṃ pariśuśrāva purāṇaṃ tanmukhācchubham ||43||
lebhe putraṃ mahāśūraṃ gaṇeśabhajane ratā |
putrānpautrānsamāvāpya bhuktvā bhogānmanoharān ||44||
jagāma sā gaṇeśasya nijadhāmovasānataḥ |
sagarasya tu putrāṇāmekaḥ paṅagurabhūtsutaḥ ||45||
tenāpīdaṃ śrutaṃ puṇyaṃ purāṇaṃ lomaśātpurā |
bhaktyā dvādaśavarṣeṇa vinītenātha bhaktitaḥ ||46||
saṃtoṣya lomaśaṃ dravyaistatoṅghri samavāpa saḥ |
vijayaṃ puṣṭimāyuṣyaṃ prāṇānte dhāmatatparaḥ ||47||
aṣṭādaśapurāṇānāṃ śravaṇe yatphalaṃ labhet |
tadekasya purāṇasya gaṇeśasya śruterbhavet ||48||
kākavandhyā bahusutā śravaṇādasya jāyate |
sravadgarbhā bhavedviprā dṛḍhagarbhā bhavatyapi ||49||
mūko vāgīśatāṃ yāti śravaṇānmunipuṃgavāḥ |
vedādhyayanasaṃpanno mānyoʼpi dvijapuṃgavaḥ ||50||
śūdro vaiśyatvamāpnoti vaiśyaḥ kṣatriyatāmiyāt |
kṣatriyo dvijatāṃ yāti smaraṇādapyamuṣya ha ||51||
kanyāṃ tu labhate cāsyāpyekādhyāyasya saṃśravāt |
guṇavantaṃ kulīnaṃ ca patiṃ sādhuṃ dhanānvitam ||52||
jātyandho labhate dṛṣṭiṃ purāṇasyāsya saṃśrayāt |
sarvatīrthaṣu yaḥ snāyāddadyāddānāni sarvaśaḥ ||53||
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti bhaktyā yaḥ śṛṇuyādidam |
paṃcāgnisādhanaṃ grīṣme hemante jalavāsanam ||54||
varṣāsvākāśavāsaṃ yaḥ kurute bahuvatsaram |
tatphalaṃ prāpnuyānmartya saṃśruteʼdhyāyapaṃcake ||55||
agnihotraṃ sadā yastu sevate bhaktimānnaraḥ |
tatphalaṃ hi purāṇasya śravaṇādasya jāyate ||56||
ekāṃgupṭhena yastiṣṭhedvarṣāṇāmayutaṃ naraḥ |
daśādhyāyaśruterasya purāṇasya tathā phalam ||57||
labhate mānavo bhaktyā niścitaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
ājanmamaraṇānnityaṃ śṛṇuyānmānavo bhuvi ||58||
purāṇaṃ tu gaṇeśasya cakravartī bhavettu saḥ |
ājanmamaraṇādyastu kāśīvāsaṃ karoti yaḥ ||59||
tatpuṇyaṃ labhate martyo gaṇeśasya purāṇataḥ |
sahasraṃ māghamāsānyaḥ prayāge snāti mānavaḥ ||60||
tatphalaṃ labhate martyaḥ purāṇasyāsya saṃśravāt |
gomatīsaṃgame tadvatsnānaṃ bhaktyā karoti yaḥ ||61||
tatphalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ labhate cāsya saṃśravāt |
gaṇeśasya purāṇaṃ yaḥ śṛṇuyādbhaktimānnaraḥ ||62||
na bhayaṃ tasya śūlāttu vajrācca cakricakrataḥ |
idaṃ vaḥ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ purāṇaṃ ca savistaram ||63||
saṃpūrṇo mahimā vaktuṃ na śakyo varṣakoṭibhiḥ |
brāhmaṇā ṣaṇmukhenāpi śeṣeṇāpi munīśvarāḥ ||64||
yuṣmābhiḥ paripṛṣṭaṃ yatsarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
sarvaṃ kāmapradaṃ bhuktimuktidaṃ puṇyavardhanam ||65||
gaṇeśasya pareśasya nānālīlādharasya va |
vaktṛsrosroraghaharaṃ kimanyacchrotumicchatha ||66||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe krīḍākhaṇḍe phalaśrutinirūpaṇaṃ nāma paṃcapaṃcāśaduttaraśatatamoʼdhyāyaḥ ||155||
||śrīgajānanārpaṇamastu||
||śubhaṃ bhavatu||
